*Zinan Zheng,Yang Liu,Nuo Chen,Juepeng Zheng,Hong Cheng,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对天气预报人工编撰效率低的问题，本文构建了首个气象报告指令微调数据集并提出了专用模型 WFR-LLM，该模型在复杂天气报告生成中表现优于主流闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天气预报报告高度依赖人工分析多源数据，导致效率低下且存在信息过载；目前尚缺乏针对气象领域生成任务的专用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）研究。

Method: 1. 定义了天气预报报告（WFR）任务；2. 构建了包含美国 31 个城市、8 个天气维度的指令微调数据集（WeatherBench/WFR-Dataset）；3. 开发了专门的天气报告生成模型 WFR-LLM。

Result: WFR-LLM 在多项评估指标上优于目前主流的闭源 MLLM，特别是在结构复杂的天气方面表现出色，且具备强大的地域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文定义的 WFR 任务及构建的数据集为天气报告生成提供了基准；WFR-LLM 在复杂天气报告生成任务上优于现有的闭源大模型，并展现出良好的跨区域零样本迁移能力，为领域专用 MLLM 的开发提供了研究范式。

Abstract: Accurate weather forecast reporting enables individuals and communities to better plan daily activities and agricultural operations. However, the current reporting process primarily relies on manual analysis of multi-source data, which leads to information overload and reduced efficiency. With the development of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), leveraging data-driven models to analyze and generate reports in the weather forecasting domain remains largely underexplored. In this work, we propose the Weather Forecasting Report (WFR) task and construct the first instruction-tuning dataset for this task, named~\DatasetNameL, which covers 31 cities in America and 8 weather aspects. Based on this corpus, we develop the first model, \ModelNameL, specialized in generating weather forecast reports. Evaluation across multiple metrics on our dataset shows that \ModelNameL~ consistently outperforms leading closed-source MLLMs, particularly on structurally complex weather aspects. We further analyze its performance across diverse geographic regions and weather aspects. \ModelNameL~ demonstrates strong transferability across different regions, highlighting its zero-shot generalization capability. \ModelNameL~offers valuable insight for developing MLLMs specialized in weather report generation. .

</details>


### [45] [Nürnberg NLP at PsyDefDetect: Multi-Axis Voter Ensembles for Psychological Defence Mechanism Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07606)
*Philipp Steigerwald,Eric Rudolph,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对心理防御机制检测中类别定义模糊、特征重叠的难点，作者通过构建包含9个模型的集成学习系统，利用模型间的误差独立性，显著提升了在模糊边界上的分类鲁棒性，最终在该任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理防御机制识别任务中，不同类别语言特征高度重叠且定义模糊，导致人工标注一致性低。传统的单模型方法难以准确界定这些重叠的边界，因此需要通过集成学习提升鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了一个包含9个投票分类器的集成系统。该系统在三个维度上保持正交性：1. 类粒度（全类门控 vs. 仅防御类专家）；2. 训练方法（生成式 vs. 判别式）；3. 基座模型差异。旨在利用模型间的误差独立性（Error Independence）解决防御机制分类中的歧义性。

Result: 在BioNLP 2026的PsyDefDetect任务中，该集成系统在隐藏测试集上取得了0.420的F1分数，在21支参赛队伍中位列第一。

Conclusion: 针对心理防御机制检测任务，通过构建多维度（粒度、训练方法、基座模型）的9模型投票集成系统，能够有效利用模型间的误差独立性，突破单模型在处理模糊边界时的性能瓶颈，从而取得最佳系统表现。

Abstract: Detecting levels of psychological defence mechanisms in supportive conversations is inherently ambiguous. In the PsyDefDetect shared task at BioNLP 2026 the eight positive defence categories share surface language and differ only in pragmatic function and trained raters reach only moderate inter-annotator agreement. On such a task the decisive lever is not a stronger single model but error independence, since any single representation will waver on the overlapping defence boundaries. We translate this insight into a 9-voter ensemble spanning three orthogonal axes: class granularity (all nine classes for the gatekeeper, only the eight defence classes for the specialists), training method (generative and discriminative) and base model. The system reaches $F1_{test}{=}.420$ on the hidden test set, placing first among 21 registered teams.

</details>


### [46] [Intent-Driven Semantic ID Generation for Grounded Conversational News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07613)
*Hongyang Su,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li,Chenyun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对对话式新闻推荐，提出了意图驱动的语义ID生成方法，有效解决了隐式意图下的检索瓶颈与冷启动难题，在降低计算成本的同时实现了高性能的精准推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式新闻推荐面临在快速更新的语料库中处理隐式用户意图的需求，标准检索增强生成（RAG）流程存在明显的检索瓶颈，无法有效理解无关键词的隐式需求。

Method: 引入“生成-匹配”（Generate-then-Match）范式和意图驱动的语义ID（SID）；采用多任务SID对齐与GPT-4思维链蒸馏两阶段训练；利用Profile-Aware Dual-Signal Reasoning (PADR)处理用户冷启动。

Result: 在152K SID空间内实现0%幻觉，L1匹配率提升至12.4%（是基准的4倍），整体性能超越GPT-4+Hybrid RAG且成本降低约100倍；冷启动用户推荐准确率从0%提升至18%。

Conclusion: 提出的PADR模型在对话式新闻推荐中通过意图驱动的语义ID生成和双信号推理，成功解决了标准RAG的检索瓶颈和冷启动问题，在保持高性能的同时显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Conversational news recommendation requires grounding each suggestion in a rapidly evolving article corpus while addressing implicit user intents that lack explicit retrievable keywords. To characterize this scenario, we identify 6 intent types from production dialogues: five are implicit and pose fundamental challenges to standard RAG pipelines, forming a critical retrieve-first bottleneck. To address these issues, we introduce intent-driven Semantic ID (SID) generation under a Generate-then-Match paradigm. With two-stage training that consists of multi-task SID alignment and GPT-4 Chain-of-Thought distillation, an LLM maps diverse intents to hierarchical SID prefixes, which are then fuzzy-matched to the current news pool to guarantee fully grounded recommendations. Profile-Aware Dual-Signal Reasoning (PADR) further enables cold-start users to obtain valid recommendations using only profiles. On a mainstream Chinese news platform, our 7B model achieves 0% hallucination and 12.4% L1 match in the 152K open-generation SID space (4x random baseline). It matches GPT-4+Hybrid RAG on L1 while surpassing it on finer-grained metrics (L2 2x, Category +1.2pp) at ~100x lower cost. Cold-start users, where existing baselines score 0%, achieve 18.0% L1 (6x random), the highest among all user groups.

</details>


### [47] [Is She Even Relevant? When BERT Ignores Explicit Gender Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07622)
*Jonas Klein,Chiara Manna,Eva Vanmassenhove*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了荷兰语 BERT 模型训练中性别偏见的形成与演化，发现尽管模型能早期捕捉到性别信息，但因上下文表征更新机制缺乏动态性，导致其难以纠正职业相关的男性默认偏见，即使用明显的语境提示也无法扭转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的性别偏见研究多集中于英语，缺乏针对具有语法或形态性别语言（如荷兰语）的深入探讨。本文旨在探索 Transformer 架构在训练过程中，性别信息是如何 émerger 并固化的，特别是模型处理显性语法性别与统计偏见冲突时的机制。

Method: 从零开始训练一个荷兰语 BERT 模型，并通过提取训练全过程的上下文嵌入（contextual embeddings），利用线性 SVM 构建动态性别子空间（gender subspaces），以追踪模型性别编码的形成时间及演变过程；通过受控句子模板测试（显性性别线索 vs. 职业统计关联）来评估模型在不同语境下的性别表征更新能力。

Result: 1. 性别信息在大约第 20 个 epoch 时在各维度中变得线性可分；2. 模型在处理职业相关词汇时，表现出强烈的统计偏见（偏向男性）；3. 在面对显性女性性别提示但呈现反刻板印象（如“她是水管工”）的句子时，模型无法根据上下文有效更新其内部表示，普遍表现出顽固的男性默认偏见。

Conclusion: Dutch BERT 模型在训练过程中形成的性别表征缺乏足够的动态调整能力。尽管在该语言具有显性形态性别的背景下，模型能在线性层面捕获性别信息，但面对反讽陈旧观念（anti-stereotypical）的语境时，无法有效整合显性提示，导致系统性地偏向“男性默认”偏差，难以克服预训练阶段学到的统计关联。

Abstract: Gender bias in large language models has primarily been investigated for English, while languages with grammatical or morphological gender remain comparatively understudied. This paper investigates how and when gender information emerges in a Dutch BERT model trained from scratch, offering one of the first checkpoint-level analyses of bias formation in a Transformer architecture for a language combining overt morphological gender marking and generic forms. By extracting contextual embeddings throughout training, we construct dynamic gender subspaces using linear SVMs to trace when gender becomes linearly encoded and how this encoding evolves over time. Contextual embeddings are often assumed to integrate contextual cues robustly, allowing models to adjust the representation of a word depending on its more local usage. We therefore test whether explicit gender cues in controlled sentence templates (e.g., Zij is een loodgieter ('She is a plumber')) can override learned statistical associations (plumber -> male). Our findings challenge this assumption: although gender becomes clearly linearly separable around epoch 20 and is distributed across multiple embedding dimensions, the model struggles to update its internal gender representation in light of explicit contextual cues in short sentence templates. Stereotypical gender-profession pairings are predicted far more accurately than anti-stereotypical ones, and generic forms in Dutch systematically default to a male interpretation, even when the context explicitly denotes a female referent. Together, our results seem to indicate that contextualization in the representations learned by our Dutch BERT model is not sufficiently dynamic along the probed gender direction: explicit gender cues in anti-stereotypical contexts are not reliably reflected in the resulting representations, resulting in persistent male-default behaviour.

</details>


### [48] [Safe, or Simply Incapable? Rethinking Safety Evaluation for Phone-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07630)
*Zhengyang Tang,Yi Zhang,Chenxin Li,Xin Lai,Pengyuan Lyu,Yiduo Guo,Weinong Wang,Junyi Li,Yang Ding,Huawen Shen,Zhengyao Fang,Xingran Zhou,Liang Wu,Fei Tang,Sunqi Fan,Shangpin Peng,Zheng Ruan,Anran Zhang,Benyou Wang,Chengquan Zhang,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有的智能体安全性评估存在混淆，无法区分“懂安全”与“办不到”。本文提出了 PhoneSafety 基准测试，发现智能体的无害表现往往源于能力不足而非预期的安全约束，强调了对安全决策与模型能力进行独立评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估框架无法区分智能体是因“具备安全意识并主动避免风险”而表现无害，还是仅仅因为“能力有限（无法理解屏幕或执行操作）”而表现无害，导致无法针对性地改进安全性和能力。

Method: 构建了 PhoneSafety 基准测试，包含 130 多个应用中 700 个安全关键时刻；通过观察模型在这些时刻是采取安全行为、不安全行为还是无法采取有效行动，来评估八个代表性智能体。

Result: 1. 更强的常规任务能力并不线性代表更强的安全性；2. “无法执行操作”在视觉和操作复杂的场景中频繁出现，属于能力瓶颈而非安全防范行为；3. 智能体的失败模式呈现出明确的分类：要么是具备行动能力但做出错误选择，要么是无法处理复杂的界面。

Conclusion: 仅仅产生无害的结果并不等同于具备安全性。评估手机智能体时，必须区分“错误决策（不安全判断）”与“无法执行任务（能力不足）”，以识别真正的安全风险。

Abstract: When a phone-use agent avoids harm, does that show safety, or simply inability to act? Existing evaluations often cannot tell. A harmful outcome may be avoided because the agent recognized the risk and chose the safe action, or because it failed to understand the screen or execute any relevant action at all. These cases have different causes and call for different fixes, yet current benchmarks often merge them under task success, refusal, or final harmful outcome. We address this problem with PhoneSafety, a benchmark of 700 safety-critical moments drawn from real phone interactions across more than 130 apps. Each instance isolates the next decision at a risky moment and asks a simple question: does the model take the safe action, take the unsafe action, or fail to do anything useful? We evaluate eight representative phone-use agents under this framework. Our results reveal two main patterns. First, stronger general phone-use ability does not reliably imply safer choices at risky moments. Models that perform better on ordinary app tasks are not always the ones that behave more safely when the next action matters. Second, failures to do anything useful behave like a capability signal rather than a safety signal: they are concentrated in more visually and operationally demanding settings and remain stable when the evaluation protocol changes. Across models, failures split into two recurring patterns: unsafe choices in settings where the model can act but chooses wrongly, and inability to act in more visually and operationally demanding screens. Overall, a harmless outcome is not enough to count as evidence of safety. Evaluating phone-use agents requires separating unsafe judgment from inability to act.

</details>


### [49] [Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of LLMs for Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07635)
*Adnan Labib,Qiao Wang,Yixuan Huang,Zheng Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对最新LLM在语法纠错任务上的全面测评发现，GPT-4o达到业界最高水平；研究还指出传统评估指标会严重低估模型性能，因其未能捕捉到高质量的非参考答案式纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语法纠错（GEC）领域存在三大痛点：1. 最新LLM缺乏全面测评；2. 模型组合策略的有效性尚不明确；3. 现有基于参考答案的评估指标严重低估了GEC系统的实际性能。

Method: 1. 评估：对比多种最新大语言模型（LLMs）在编辑精度、流畅度保持和意义保留方面的表现；2. 分析：进行语法错误类型分析，利用相关系数（ρ）考察不同模型纠错模式的一致性；3. 测评修正：通过人工评估检验基于参考指标（Reference-based metrics）的局限性，量化模型输出修正与参考答案偏差的合理性。

Result: 1. 微调后的GPT-4o在三项核心维度上达成SOTA水平；2. 不同LLM表现出极高的纠错相似度（ρ=0.947）；3. 现有评估指标严重低估性能：73.76%与参考标准不同的GPT-4o纠错结果被判定为同等有效或更优。

Conclusion: 研究证实，最新LLM（特别是微调后的GPT-4o）在语法纠错（GEC）领域表现卓越；传统的基于参考答案的评估指标存在显著局限，往往低估了LLM的高质量纠错能力。建议在评估GEC系统时采取更多元、以意义和流畅度为核心的评价标准，而非仅依赖黄金标准参考。

Abstract: Automated assistants for Grammatical Error Correction are now embedded in educational platforms serving millions of learners, yet three critical gaps remain in this domain: (1) latest-generation Large Language Models (LLMs) lack comprehensive evaluation on grammar correction tasks; (2) whether combining these LLMs improves correction quality is unexplored; and (3) the extent to which reference-based metrics underestimate GEC system performance has not been adequately quantified. In this study, first, we evaluate latest-generation LLMs on edit precision, fluency preservation, and meaning retention, showing fine-tuned GPT-4o achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three dimensions. Second, through grammatical error type analysis we demonstrate that individual LLMs exhibit highly similar error correction patterns ($ρ=0.947$). Third, we show that reference-based metrics underestimate GEC performance with 73.76% of GPT-4o corrections different from gold standards being equally valid or even superior. These GEC evaluation findings equip educators with guidance for selecting GEC assistants that enhance rather than constrain student linguistic development. We make our data, code, and models publicly available.

</details>


### [50] [Quality-Conditioned Agreement in Automated Short Answer Scoring: Mid-Range Degradation and the Impact of Task-Specific Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07647)
*Abigail Victoria Gurin Schleifer,Moriah Ariely,Beata Beigman Klebanov,Asaf Salman,Giora Alexandron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大语言模型在自动简答评分中存在“中等质量响应降级”问题，即无法准确评估部分正确的回答，建议通过增加任务特定训练来提升此类评估的公平性与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动简答评分（ASAS）正从微调模型转向大语言模型（LLMs）。尽管LLM具备广泛知识，但其在处理需要细微差别解读的“部分正确”回答时，由于缺乏足够的任务特异性，其表现及其对公平性的影响尚不明确。

Method: 比较了三种少样本（few-shot）设置下的先进大模型（GPT-5.2, GPT-4o, Claude Opus 4.5）与一种微调的基于BERT的编码器模型，并以人类专家评分作为基准，在两个开放式生物学测试题上进行了实证对比分析。

Result: 研究发现，AI模型在极值（完全正确/错误）上表现稳定，但在中等质量回答上存在显著评分性能降级，且该降级程度与模型任务特异性调整的程度正相关。微调后的编码器模型在中等质量回答上的表现优于仅依赖少样本提示（few-shot）的大模型。

Conclusion: 通过对“质量条件评分一致性”的研究，发现大语言模型（LLMs）在处理完全正确或完全错误的回答时表现良好，但在处理“中等质量”（部分正确）回答时表现显著下降。研究强调了任务特定调整（如微调）对提升中等质量回答评分公平性的重要性。

Abstract: Automated short answer scoring (ASAS) is shifting from discriminative, fine-tuned models to large language models (LLMs) used in few-shot settings. This paradigm leverages LLMs broad world knowledge and ease of deployment, but limited task-specific data may reduce alignment on complex scoring tasks. In particular, its impact on scoring partially correct responses that require nuanced interpretation remains underexplored. We investigate the relationship between the degree of task-specific adaptation of different models and quality-conditioned scoring agreement. We compare three LLMs (GPT-5.2, GPT-4o, Claude Opus 4.5) in few-shot mode, a fine-tuned BERT-based encoder, and a human expert on two open-ended biology items, using several hundred student responses and ground truth scores provided by a biology education expert. The results show that human-human agreement is highest and stable across the full quality spectrum. All AI models perform well on fully correct and fully incorrect responses, but exhibit substantial degradation on mid-range responses. This mid-range degradation is conditioned on task-specific adaptation: It is most severe in few-shot LLMs with few examples and decreases as task-specific data increases, with fine-tuned encoder models performing best. This mid-range degradation may lead to inequitable evaluation of responses produced by students with developing understanding. Our findings highlight the importance of quality-conditioned fairness, with particular attention to mid-range responses.

</details>


### [51] [Not All Tokens Learn Alike: Attention Entropy Reveals Heterogeneous Signals in RL Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07660)
*Gengyang Li,Zheng-Fan Wu,Siqi Bao,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了 RL 后训练中 token 级别的异质性，提出通过“注意力熵”将 token 分为锚点和探索者，并证明了对这些不同类型 token 进行动态加权可显著提升模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型后训练中基于强化学习（RL）的 token 级学习信号机制尚不明确，目前通过均匀采样进行更新可能存在冗余，且忽略了不同 token 在推理优化中的不同作用。

Method: 1. 通过注意力熵（Attention Entropy）量化 token 的上下文支持集中度；2. 将 token 分为低熵的“锚点（Anchors，集中支持）”和高熵的“探索者（Explorers，分散背景）”；3. 设计了一项动态熵感知软加权（dynamic entropy-aware soft-reweighting）干预策略。

Result: 1. Token 级 RL 目标具有高度冗余性（随机 20% 子集即可保持性能）；2. “锚点”提供稳定的优化骨干但容易陷入平台期，“探索者”携带复杂推理信号但不稳定；3. 基于熵的动态重加权干预使 Qwen3-8B-Base 模型在留出集上的平均得分从 34.39 提升至 37.40。

Conclusion: 注意力熵揭示了强化学习中 token 级别的优化异质性；相比于盲目的均匀加权，动态的基于熵的权重调整能有效提升模型在复杂推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning-based post-training has become a key approach for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but its token-level learning signals remain poorly understood. This work studies their heterogeneity through attention entropy, which measures how concentrated or diffuse the contextual support is for each response token.
  We first show that token-level RL objectives are sparsely estimable: uniformly random 20 percent token subsets preserve much of the full-token held-out performance, suggesting substantial redundancy in token-level updates. However, entropy-structured subsets behave very differently. Low-attention-entropy tokens, which we call anchors, rely on concentrated support, produce stable gradients aligned with full-token updates, and provide a reliable optimization backbone, but tend to plateau on harder benchmarks. High-attention-entropy tokens, which we call explorers, aggregate more diffuse context and induce larger but more volatile gradients. Explorer-only training is unstable on average, though rare successful runs suggest that these tokens may contain useful hard-reasoning signals when optimization remains stable.
  We support this anchor-explorer spectrum with evidence-gathering analyses, entropy dynamics, gradient-geometry diagnostics, and controls showing that position, predictive entropy, and loss normalization do not explain the observed asymmetry. Finally, a dynamic entropy-aware soft-reweighting intervention improves Qwen3-8B-Base from 34.39 to 37.40 held-out average in the strongest setting. These findings suggest that attention entropy reveals optimization-relevant structure in token-level RL signals, and that uniform token averaging can obscure meaningful heterogeneity in reasoning post-training.

</details>


### [52] [Guidance Is Not a Hyperparameter: Learning Dynamic Control in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07701)
*Fan Zhou,Tim Van de Cruys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种通过强化学习（PPO）自动学习扩散模型动态CFG引导轨迹的方法，取代了传统的固定引导规模，实现了更好的受控文本生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型中CFG引导规模通常作为固定超参数，限制了模型在NLP生成任务中对可控性和质量平衡的调节能力，且无法适应不同任务和生成阶段的需求。

Method: 将CFG引导规模的选择建模为序贯决策问题，利用强化学习（PPO算法）在每个生成步骤根据扩散状态学习动态引导策略，并根据任务级奖励进行优化。

Result: 在三个受控NLP生成任务中，所提出的自适应引导策略均优于传统的固定引导策略，能够更好地平衡生成质量与受控性。

Conclusion: 将CFG引导规模视为一个动态控制过程（而非静态超参数）是优化扩散模型生成平衡的关键，学习到的动态引导轨迹具有任务相关性和可解释性。

Abstract: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is a widely used mechanism for controlling diffusion-based generative models, yet its guidance scale is typically treated as a fixed hyperparameter throughout generation. This static design yields a suboptimal controllability and quality tradeoff, as the optimal degree of guidance varies across tasks and across different stages of the diffusion process, especially in NLP domain. We recast CFG scale selection as a sequential decision-making problem and propose to learn dynamic guidance trajectories via reinforcement learning. Specifically, we model the guidance scale as a discrete control action selected at each generation step based on the evolving diffusion state, and optimize a policy using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) under task-level rewards. Experiments on three controlled NLP generation tasks using discrete diffusion language models demonstrate that adaptive guidance consistently achieves a better balance between controllability and generation quality than fixed-scale strategies. Further analysis of the learned policies reveals distinct and interpretable guidance trajectories across tasks, underscoring the importance of treating guidance as a dynamic control process rather than a static design choice.

</details>


### [53] [SimCT: Recovering Lost Supervision for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07711)
*Jie Sun,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Qiyong Zhong,Yilin Cheng,Bichuan Feng,Pengfei Liu,Junfeng Fang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对异构分词器导致蒸馏信号丢失的问题，该论文提出一种称为 SimCT 的方法，通过引入多词元连续序列作为监督接口，在不修改 OPD 损失函数的情况下有效提升了异构模型间的蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略策略蒸馏（OPD）假设教师和学生模型具有相同的分词器。当分词器不同时，传统的强制对齐会丢弃大量关键的教师信号，导致蒸馏效果大打折扣。

Method: 提出 SimCT (Simple Cross-Tokenizer OPD)，通过扩展监督空间，在共享词元的基础上引入短多词元连续序列（multi-token continuations），作为两个不同分词器之间的最小共同可表示单元进行蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，SimCT 在三组异构教师-学生对上均优于精确匹配的 OPD 及其他跨分词器基线，证明了其找回丢失监督信号的有效性。

Conclusion: SimCT 是一种有效的跨分词器蒸馏方法，通过利用细粒度的多词元连续序列，成功找回了因分词器不匹配而丢失的教师模型信号，显著提升了异构模型间蒸馏的效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a standard tool for transferring teacher behavior to a smaller student, but it implicitly assumes that teacher and student predictions are comparable token by token, an assumption that fails whenever the two models tokenize the same text differently. Under heterogeneous tokenizers, exact shared-token matching silently discards a large fraction of the teacher signal at precisely the positions where vocabularies disagree. We propose \textbf{\underline{Sim}ple \underline{C}ross-\underline{T}okenizer OPD (SimCT)}, which restores this signal by enlarging the supervision space: alongside shared tokens, SimCT compares teacher and student over short multi-token continuations that both tokenizers can realize, leaving the OPD loss form itself unchanged. We show that these units are the finest jointly tokenizable supervision interface, and that coarser alternatives remove teacher-student distinctions that are useful for on-policy learning. Across three heterogeneous teacher-student pairs on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, SimCT shows consistent gains over shared-vocabulary OPD and representative cross-tokenizer baselines, with ablations confirming that the improvements come from recovering supervision discarded by exact shared-token matching. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/sunjie279/SimCT-}{https://github.com/sunjie279/SimCT-}.

</details>


### [54] [Memory-Efficient Looped Transformer: Decoupling Compute from Memory in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07721)
*Victor Conchello Vendrell,Arnau Padres Masdemont,Niccolò Grillo,Jordi Ros-Giralt,Arash Behboodi,Fabio Valerio Massoli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决递归大模型随推理深度线性增长的内存瓶颈，本文提出 MELT 架构，通过共享层级 KV Cache 和门控更新机制，实现了深度与内存消耗的解耦，并在轻量级两阶段微调后达到高性能与低内存占用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归大模型（Recurrent LLM，如 Ouro）虽能通过多次迭代提升推理逻辑能力，但现有的实现方式（每轮保留独立的 KV Cache）导致内存占用随推理深度线性增长，限制了其在大规模应用中的可扩展性。

Method: 1. 架构优化：摒弃了每层每轮迭代均保留 KV Cache 的做法，改为每层仅保留一个共享的 KV Cache，并通过可学习的门控机制（gating mechanism）动态更新；2. 训练策略：采用分块（chunk-wise）训练，包含“插值转换（interpolated transition）”和“对齐蒸馏（attention-aligned distillation）”两个阶段，从预训练的 LoopLM 模型迁移至 MELT。

Result: MELT 在推理内存 footprint 上实现剧烈缩减（降至常数级），同时在推理能力指标上超越同等规模的标准 LLM，并保持了与原 LoopLM 相当的性能表现。

Conclusion: MELT 实现了常数级内存占用的迭代推理，在不牺牲性能（相比 LoopLM 系列模型）的前提下，大幅降低了计算过程中的内存开销，并通过轻量级的两阶段训练流程即可实现高效适配。

Abstract: Recurrent LLM architectures have emerged as a promising approach for improving reasoning, as they enable multi-step computation in the embedding space without generating intermediate tokens. Models such as Ouro perform reasoning by iteratively updating internal representations while retaining a standard Key-Value (KV) cache across iterations, causing memory consumption to grow linearly with reasoning depth. Consequently, increasing the number of reasoning iterations can lead to prohibitive memory usage, limiting the practical scalability of such architectures. In this work, we propose Memory-Efficient Looped Transformer (MELT), a novel architecture that decouples reasoning depth from memory consumption. Instead of using a standard KV cache per layer and loop, MELT maintains a single KV cache per layer that is shared across reasoning loops. This cache is updated over time via a learnable gating mechanism. To enable stable and efficient training under this architecture, we propose to train MELT using chunk-wise training in a two phase procedure: interpolated transition, followed by attention-aligned distillation, both from the LoopLM starting model to MELT. Empirically, we show that MELT models fine-tuned from pretrained Ouro parameters outperform standard LLMs of comparable size, while maintaining a memory footprint comparable to those models and dramatically smaller than Ouro's. Overall, MELT achieves constant-memory iterative reasoning without sacrificing LoopLM performance, using only a lightweight post-training procedure.

</details>


### [55] [SOD: Step-wise On-policy Distillation for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07725)
*Qiyong Zhong,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Xin Lin,Jie Sun,Houcheng Jiang,Xiang Wang,Junfeng Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对小模型工具集成推理中在线蒸馏产生的错误累积问题，SOD 框架通过自适应调整步级蒸馏强度，显著提升了模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在进行工具集成推理（TIR）时，传统的在线蒸馏（OPD）方法存在错误级联（erroneous tool call cascading）问题，导致模型偏离教师指导，进而降低了蒸馏的效果和稳定性。

Method: 提出 SOD（Step-wise On-policy Distillation）框架，通过计算学生模型与教师模型在推理过程中的步级（step-level）分歧程度，动态调整各步骤的知识蒸馏权重，从而减轻不可靠的教师信号干扰。

Result: SOD 在数学、科学和代码基准测试中表现优异，最高提升幅度达 20.86%。0.6B 参数的小模型在 AIME 2025 上达到了 26.13% 的准确率。

Conclusion: SOD 框架通过引入基于步级偏差的自适应权重调整机制，有效解决了小模型在工具集成推理中因错误级联导致的性能瓶颈，实现了轻量级模型推理能力的显著提升。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) is difficult to scale to small language models due to instability in long-horizon tool interactions and limited model capacity. While reinforcement learning methods like group relative policy optimization provide only sparse outcome-level rewards. Recently, on-policy distillation (OPD) has gained popularity by supplying dense token-level supervision from a teacher on student-generated trajectories. However, our experiments indicate that applying OPD to TIR leads to a critical failure mode: erroneous tool calls tend to cascade across subsequent reasoning steps, progressively amplifying student-teacher divergence and rendering the teacher's token-level supervision increasingly unreliable. To address this, we propose SOD, a step-wise on-policy distillation framework for small language model agents, which adaptively reweights distillation strength at each step based on step-level divergence. Therefore, SOD can attenuate potentially misleading teacher signals in high-divergence regions while preserving dense guidance in well-aligned states. Experiments on challenging math, science, and code benchmarks show that SOD achieves up to 20.86% improvement over the second-best baseline. Notably, our 0.6B student achieves 26.13% on AIME 2025, demonstrating effective transfer of agentic reasoning to lightweight models. Our code is available at https://github.com/YoungZ365/SOD.

</details>


### [56] [TextLDM: Language Modeling with Continuous Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07748)
*Jiaxiu Jiang,Jingjing Ren,Wenbo Li,Bo Wang,Haoze Sun,Yijun Yang,Jianhui Liu,Yanbing Zhang,Shenghe Zheng,Yuan Zhang,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TextLDM成功将视觉扩散Transformer（DiT）架构迁移至文本生成，通过引入REPA（表征对齐）技术解决潜空间特征质量问题，实现了高性能的文本生成，证明了统一扩散架构在生成建模中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于DiT在视觉生成领域的成功，研究人员旨在探索该架构是否能统一视觉生成与理解，并尝试将其迁移到语言建模任务，以实现单一架构处理跨模态生成与理解。

Method: 提出TextLDM，核心包括：1. 开发一个基于Transformer的VAE，将离散文本Token映射到连续潜空间；2. 引入表征对齐（REPA）技术，通过冻结的预训练语言模型对齐潜空间特征，以提升生成质量；3. 使用标准DiT架构在潜空间执行流匹配（Flow Matching）。

Result: 在OpenWebText2数据集上进行从零开始的训练，TextLDM在质量上显著优于此前的扩散语言模型，并在相同基准下达到了与GPT-2相当的性能。

Conclusion: TextLDM证明了基于扩散模型的架构（DiT）可以成功迁移到文本生成领域，且效果优于先前的扩散语言模型，在相同设置下可媲美GPT-2。这一成果为实现多模态生成与理解的统一扩散架构迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiT) trained with flow matching in a VAE latent space have unified visual generation across images and videos. A natural next step toward a single architecture for both generation (visual synthesis) and understanding (text generation) is to apply this framework to language modeling. We propose TextLDM, which transfers the visual latent diffusion recipe to text generation with minimal architectural modification. A Transformer-based VAE maps discrete tokens to continuous latents, enhanced by Representation Alignment (REPA) with a frozen pretrained language model to produce representations effective for conditional denoising. A standard DiT then performs flow matching in this latent space, identical in architecture to its visual counterpart. The central challenge we address is obtaining high-quality continuous text representations: we find that reconstruction fidelity alone is insufficient, and that aligning latent features with a pretrained language model via REPA is critical for downstream generation quality. Trained from scratch on OpenWebText2, TextLDM substantially outperforms prior diffusion language models and matches GPT-2 under the same settings. Our results establish that the visual DiT recipe transfers effectively to language, taking a concrete step toward unified diffusion architectures for multimodal generation and understanding.

</details>


### [57] [Chain-based Distillation for Effective Initialization of Variable-Sized Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07783)
*Boyu Shi,YiCheng Jiang,Chang Liu,Qiufeng Wang,Xu Yang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CBD 提出了一种通过构建蒸馏链及使用参数插值技术，高效初始化不同尺寸语言模型的新方案，显著降低了计算成本并增强了模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）部署成本高昂；从头训练小语言模型（SLM）计算开销大；传统知识蒸馏因需要反复访问大型教师模型，导致扩展性不足。

Method: 提出了“基于链式蒸馏（Chain-based Distillation, CBD）”的范式：1. 构建基于锚点（Anchors）的蒸馏链，通过分步蒸馏从教师模型传递知识；2. 引入“桥接蒸馏（Bridge Distillation）”处理跨架构和跨词汇表移植；3. 采用参数插值（Parameter Interpolation）在相邻锚点间初始化可变尺寸模型，避免了对大型教师模型的重复推理。

Result: 在不进行额外恢复性预训练的情况下，CBD 初始化的 138M 参数模型在 10B 标记语料库上的性能优于从头训练的模型；同时在跨架构和跨词汇表的异构场景中表现出极强的灵活性与高效性。

Conclusion: CBD 成功解决了大语言模型在资源受限场景下部署成本高及传统知识蒸馏扩展性差的问题，通过参数插值实现了高效的模型初始化，且在同构与异构设置下均表现出良好的普适性和性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but remain costly to deploy in resource-constrained settings. Training small language models (SLMs) from scratch is computationally expensive, while conventional knowledge distillation requires repeated access to large teachers for different target sizes, leading to poor scalability. To solve these problems, we propose \textbf{Chain-based Distillation (CBD)}, a scalable paradigm for efficiently initializing variable-sized language models. A sparse and limited sequence of intermediate models (called anchors) is constructed via stepwise distillation, forming a distillation chain that progressively transfers knowledge from the source LLMs. To support heterogeneous settings, we introduce \emph{bridge distillation} for cross-architecture and cross-vocabulary transfer. Models of variable sizes are initialized via parameter interpolation between adjacent anchors, eliminating repeated large teacher inference. Experiments show that the proposed method substantially improves efficiency and downstream performance. A 138M-parameter SLM without recovery pre-training, outperforms scratch-trained models on a 10B-token corpus on the specific task. CBD also demonstrates versatility in heterogeneous settings for initialize models with different architectures and vocabularies.

</details>


### [58] [PolySQL: Scaling Text-to-SQL Evaluation Across SQL Dialects via Automated Backend Isomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07796)
*Yotam Perlitz,Elad Venezian,Corentin Royer,Francesco Fusco,Andrea Giovannini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对Text-to-SQL评测中过度依赖SQLite导致的评估偏差问题，提出了PolySQL框架。该方法通过直接对比执行结果而非转译SQL，实现了高效、高精度的跨方言评测，验证了方言间性能的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL基准测试过于依赖SQLite，导致模型在其他方言（如PostgreSQL、MySQL）上的表现难以准确预估，且现有的跨方言评测方法存在转译成本高、复杂查询兼容性差等问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为“PolySQL”的双重执行评测方法，无需进行SQL语句的转译，通过对比标准化后的执行结果来评估不同方言引擎下的模型表现。

Result: PolySQL在100%查询覆盖率下实现了比传统转译更高的评测准确性。大规模研究显示，从SQLite迁移至其他方言平均准确率下降10.1%，其中61%的误差归因于逻辑错误。

Conclusion: PolySQL揭示了SQL方言间的性能差距，指出准确率下降主要源于逻辑错误而非语法差异，并为实现稳健的跨方言评测提供了标准化的框架和基准。

Abstract: SQL dialects vary in syntax, types, and functions across database engines. Text-to-SQL benchmarks, however, predominantly support only SQLite. This creates a critical evaluation gap: cross-dialect evaluation reveals weak per-query agreement (Cohen's ), showing that SQLite performance is an unreliable proxy for other dialects. Yet such evaluation remains prohibitively difficult: existing approaches either require expensive manual query transpilation or rely on tools that often fail on complex SQL. To close this gap, we introduce PolySQL, a novel dual-execution method that eliminates the need for query transpilation by comparing normalized execution results. Notably, our approach achieves higher evaluation fidelity than query transpilation with 100% query coverage. PolySQL comprises three datasets, enabling the first large-scale cross-dialect study. Our study reveals a 10.1% average accuracy drop from SQLite to other dialects and identifies a significant dialect difficulty hierarchy. We find this degradation stems from logical rather than syntactic errors (61% vs. 8%). We release our framework code and leaderboard to enable rigorous dialect-robust evaluation.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Confidence: Rethinking Self-Assessments for Performance Prediction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07806)
*Sree Bhattacharyya,Samarth Khanna,Leona Chen,Lucas Craig,Tharun Dilliraj,James Z. Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种多维度的LLM自我评估方法，发现基于“认知评估理论”的维度（特别是“努力”和“能力”）比传统的单一置信度指标更能可靠地预测LLM的失误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自我评估主要依赖于置信度（Confidence），但研究表明置信度往往存在不一致且过于乐观的问题，无法可靠地预测模型错误。

Method: 借鉴人类心理学的“认知评估理论”，提出从六个评估维度进行自我评估，并在12个LLM模型及覆盖8个领域的38项任务上，与传统的置信度预测进行了对比实验。

Result: 在大多数场景下， competence（胜任力）相关维度（尤其是“努力”和“能力”）在预测模型错误方面表现优于或持平于置信度；“努力”维度的估计更稳定且不过于乐观；且不同评估维度在不同任务类型中（如推理任务 vs 检索任务）表现出系统性的差异。

Conclusion: 基于多维度的自我评估（特别是基于“努力”和“能力”的认知评估）显著优于单一的置信度指标，是提升大语言模型可靠性与安全性的有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where reliable self-assessment is critical. Assessing model reliability has evolved from using probabilistic correctness estimates to, more recently, eliciting verbalized confidence. Confidence, however, has been shown to be an inconsistent and overoptimistic predictor of model correctness. Drawing on cognitive appraisal theory, a framework from human psychology that decomposes self-evaluation into multiple components, we propose a multidimensional perspective on model self-assessment. We elicit six appraisal-based dimensions of self-assessment, alongside confidence, and evaluate their utility for predicting model failure across 12 LLMs and 38 tasks spanning eight domains. We find that competence-related appraisal dimensions, particularly effort and ability, consistently match or outperform confidence across most settings. Effort additionally yields less overoptimistic estimates that remain stable across model sizes. In contrast, affective dimensions provide marginally predictive signals. Furthermore, the most informative dimension varies systematically with task characteristics: effort is most predictive for reasoning-intensive tasks, while ability and confidence dominate on retrieval-oriented tasks. Broadly, our findings indicate that structured multidimensional self-assessment is a promising approach to improving the reliability and safety of language model deployment across diverse real-world settings.

</details>


### [60] [A Comparative Analysis of Classical Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches for Sentiment Classification on IMDb Movie Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07811)
*Erma Daniar Safitri,Lia Hana Ichisasmita,Citra Agustin,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin Clinton Tosima Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比发现，在IMDb情感分类任务中，经过高效特征工程的传统SVM模型性能优于BiLSTM类深度学习模型，表明传统方法在资源受限场景下仍具备显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在情感分类任务中，传统机器学习方法与深度学习方法在特定数据集（IMDb）上的性能表现差异。

Method: 对IMDb影评数据集进行对比分析。传统机器学习流程使用TF-IDF特征，通过PyCaret AutoML评估逻辑回归（Logistic Regression）、朴素贝叶斯（Naïve Bayes）和支持向量机（SVM）；深度学习流程实现BiLSTM及带注意力机制的BiLSTM模型。

Result: 传统机器学习方法（尤其是SVM）达到最高准确率（0.8530），优于深度学习模型。带注意力机制的BiLSTM（准确率0.706）优于标准BiLSTM，证明了其在上下文建模方面的优势。

Conclusion: 在数据量和计算资源有限的情况下，结合有效的特征工程（如TF-IDF），传统的机器学习方法（特别是SVM）在情感分类任务中依然表现强劲，甚至优于本研究中的深度学习模型。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of classical machine learning and deep learning methods for sentiment classification on the IMDb movie reviews dataset. The machine learning pipeline uses TF-IDF features and PyCaret AutoML to evaluate Logistic Regression, Naïve Bayes, and Support Vector Machine, while the deep learning pipeline implements BiLSTM and BiLSTM with an attention mechanism. Experimental results show that classical machine learning, especially SVM, achieves the best performance with an accuracy of 0.8530, outperforming the deep learning models in this study. The BiLSTM with Attention model improves over the standard BiLSTM and reaches an accuracy of 0.706, indicating better contextual modeling. The paper concludes that although deep learning can capture sequential dependencies, classical machine learning remains a strong baseline when combined with effective feature engineering such as TF-IDF, particularly under limited data and computational resources.

</details>


### [61] [SCENE: Recognizing Social Norms and Sanctioning in Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07823)
*Mateusz Jacniacki,Maksymilian Bilski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCENE基准测试，用于评估LLM在多方聊天中识别、适应隐性社交准则以及应对社交制裁的能力。研究发现，闭源前沿模型在社交适应性方面显著领先于开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线群聊中存在隐性行为准则，违反这些准则常会招致社交制裁。目前尚不清楚现有的LLM智能体是否具备识别并适应这些隐性社会规范的能力，缺乏针对多方聊天中社交交互的动态测评基准。

Method: 构建了SCENE基准测试：利用脚本化角色模拟真实的多方聊天场景，通过设置“隐性规范”创造违规机会，并由脚本角色对模型主体的违规行为进行社交制裁。同时开发了两项评估指标：对负面制裁的响应能力和从同伴行为中学习规范的能力。

Result: 在对六种前沿模型（闭源与开源）的评估中，发现Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro在适应隐性规范方面表现显著优于开源模型。

Conclusion: SCENE为评估大语言模型（LLM）的动态社交交互能力提供了新的实验框架。研究表明，闭源模型（如Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro）在识别和适应隐性社交规范方面优于开源模型，强调了LLM在复杂社会情境下行为适应性的差距。

Abstract: Online group chats are social spaces with implicit behavior patterns that, when broken, are often met with social sanctioning from the group. The ability and willingness of LLM-based agents to recognize and adapt to these norms remains mostly unexplored. We introduce SCENE, a social-interaction benchmark focused on implicit norms and social sanctioning in multi-party chat. SCENE generates plausible non-roleplay scenarios with scripted personas that follow a hidden norm, create opportunities for the subject agent to violate it, and sanction breaches when they occur. We further propose behavioral evaluation metrics for two functional adaptation abilities: responsiveness to negative sanctioning, and adapting norm from peers behavior. We evaluate six frontier and open-weight models on SCENE. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro adapt to implicit norms significantly more than the evaluated open-weight models. SCENE contributes one benchmark in the direction of recent calls for dynamic, interactional evaluation of LLM social capabilities.

</details>


### [62] [Measuring and Mitigating the Distributional Gap Between Real and Simulated User Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07847)
*Shuhaib Mehri,Philippe Laban,Sumuk Shashidhar,Marwa Abdulhai,Sergey Levine,Michel Galley,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化真实与模拟用户行为分布差异的方法，研究发现现有的 24 款 LLM 用户模拟器在行为多样性上与真实用户存在显著鸿沟，并证实整合互补型模拟器是缩小该差距的有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管用户模拟器在交互式训练和评估中应用广泛，但目前尚不清楚这些模拟器是否能够真正捕捉到真实用户行为的广阔且异质的分布。亟需一种量化评估方法来弥合模拟行为与真实行为之间的“分布鸿沟（distributional gap）”。

Method: 提出了一种衡量真实用户与模拟用户行为分布差异的方法：1. 从对话中提取用户行为表示；2. 通过聚类（Clustering）将表示量化为离散分布；3. 计算并比较分布间的散度（Divergence Metrics）；4. 最后利用 TF-IDF 分析不同聚类下的可解释性行为模式（包括捕捉到的、缺失的和幻觉的行为）。

Result: 对 24 个基于 LLM 的用户模拟器在编码和写作任务上进行了评估，发现它们与真实用户存在较大分布差距；大多数模拟器行为高度同质化；组合行为互补的模拟器能更接近真实用户分布；通过分析揭示了模拟器在行为覆盖方面的优势、局限及幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 现有的大语言模型用户模拟器在行为分布上与真实用户存在显著差异。研究表明，单一模拟器难以全面覆盖真实用户的多样性，通过融合行为互补的模拟器可以显著提升模拟质量。该方法为评估和改进用户模拟器提供了可解释的分析框架。

Abstract: As user simulators are increasingly used for interactive training and evaluation of AI assistants, it is essential that they represent the diverse behaviors of real users. While existing works train user simulators to generate human-like responses, whether they capture the broad and heterogeneous distribution of real user behaviors remains an open question. In this work, we introduce a method to measure the distributional gap between real and simulated user behaviors, validated through a human study and ablations. Given a dataset of real and simulated conversations, our method extracts representations of user behavior from each conversation, quantizes them into discrete distributions via clustering, then computes divergence metrics. We provide the first systematic evaluation of 24 LLM-based user simulators on coding and writing tasks, and reveal a large distributional gap from real users that varies across model families, scales, and behavioral facets. Pairwise comparisons show that most simulators behave similarly, while a few stand apart. Combining behaviorally complementary simulators brings the resulting distribution closer to real users compared to either simulator on its own. Finally, a TF-IDF analysis of the clusters surfaces interpretable patterns of behaviors that simulators capture, miss, and hallucinate.

</details>


### [63] [MatryoshkaLoRA: Learning Accurate Hierarchical Low-Rank Representations for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07850)
*Ionut-Vlad Modoranu,Mher Safaryan,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MatryoshkaLoRA，通过嵌套式训练框架改进LoRA，使其在支持动态秩选择的同时提高训练效率和模型精度，并引入了新的评价指标AURAC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA需要预设固定的秩r，导致调优成本高；而现有的动态秩适配方法（如DyLoRA）在高等秩下缺乏一致的梯度信号，导致数据效率低。

Method: 提出了MatryoshkaLoRA，在LoRA适配器之间插入一个固定的对角矩阵P，以利用Matryoshka嵌套原理学习分层低秩表示；同时引入AURAC指标来量化评价分层低秩适配器的性能。

Result: 实验表明，MatryoshkaLoRA能学习到更准确的分层低秩表示，在不同的秩下均能实现优于现有方法的准确率与性能权衡。

Conclusion: MatryoshkaLoRA提供了一种更高效的秩自适应微调框架，通过引入对角矩阵P实现分层低秩表示，在保证动态秩选择性能的同时，解决了现有方法在高等秩下表现不佳的问题。AURAC指标的提出也为评价此类方法提供了标准。

Abstract: With the rise in scale for deep learning models to billions of parameters, the computational cost of fine-tuning remains a significant barrier to deployment. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning, the need to set a predefined, static rank $r$ requires exhaustive grid searches to balance efficiency and performance. Existing rank-adaptive solutions such as DyLoRA mitigate this by sampling ranks during the training from a predefined distribution. However, they often yield sub-optimal results at higher ranks due to lack of consistent gradient signals across the full hierarchy of ranks, thus making these methods data-inefficient. In this paper, we propose MatryoshkaLoRA, a general, Matryoshka-inspired training framework for LoRA that learns accurate hierarchical low-rank representations by inserting a fixed, carefully crafted diagonal matrix $P$ between the existing LoRA adapters to scale their sub-ranks accordingly. By introducing this simple modification, our general framework recovers LoRA and DyLoRA only by changing $P$ and ensures all sub-ranks embed the available gradient information efficiently. Our MatryoshkaLoRA supports dynamic rank selection with minimal degradation in accuracy. We further propose Area Under the Rank Accuracy Curve (AURAC), a metric that consistently evaluates the performance of hierarchical low-rank adapters. Our results demonstrate that MatryoshkaLoRA learns more accurate hierarchical low-rank representations than prior rank-adaptive approaches and achieves superior accuracy-performance trade-offs across ranks on the evaluated datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/MatryoshkaLoRA.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond "I cannot fulfill this request": Alleviating Rigid Rejection in LLMs via Label Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07883)
*Ying Zhang,Congyu Qiao,Xin Geng,Ning Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LANCE框架，通过变分标签增强和细粒度拒绝分布，解决了大模型安全回复中“僵硬拒绝”的问题，实现了在保持高安全性的同时提升回复的自然度与有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM的安全对齐机制（如“我无法满足该请求”）往往会产生“僵硬拒绝”，严重破坏人机交互的自然性。

Method: 提出LANCE框架：(1) 利用变分推理进行标签增强，预测多重拒绝类别的连续分布；(2) 基于细粒度拒绝分布生成多向文本梯度；(3) 引导修正模型中和提示词中的风险元素，从而生成自然且安全的回复。

Result: 实验表明，LANCE显著缓解了僵硬拒绝现象，并在有用性和自然性指标上显著优于现有的基准模型，同时维持了极高的安全性。

Conclusion: LANCE通过变分推理实现细粒度的标签增强，有效解决了LLM安全对齐中的“僵硬拒绝”问题，在提升回复自然度和有用性的同时，保持了高水平的安全性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on safety alignment to obey safe requests while refusing harmful ones. However, traditional refusal mechanisms often lead to "rigid rejection," where a general template (e.g., "I cannot fulfill this request") indiscriminately triggers refusals and severely undermines the naturalness of interactions between humans and LLMs. To address this issue, LANCE is proposed in this paper to ensure safe yet flexible and natural responses via label enhancement. Specifically, LANCE employs variational inference to perform label enhancement, predicting a continuous distribution across multiple rejection categories. These fine-grained rejection distributions provide multi-way textual gradients for a refinement model to neutralize the hazardous elements in the prompt, so that the LLMs could generate safe responses that avoid rigid rejections while preserving the naturalness of interactions. Experiments demonstrate that LANCE significantly alleviates the rigid rejection problem while maintaining high security standards, significantly outperforming existing baseline models in terms of helpfulness and naturalness of responses.

</details>


### [65] [CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07905)
*Hexuan Deng,Xiaopeng Ke,Yichen Li,Ruina Hu,Dehao Huang,Derek F. Wong,Yue Wang,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出了现有AI审稿评估指标的局限性，提出了通过利用多方对话数据和更严格的筛选机制来构建评估基准CoCoReviewBench，为评估AI审稿系统的正确性和完整性提供了新范式，并发现推理模型更具审稿潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI审稿系统评价指标过度依赖于与人类评论的重合度，而非真实准确性。由于人类评论不仅存在覆盖面不足的问题，还可能包含错误，因此不应被视为唯一的“金标准”。亟需建立一种更可靠、更细粒度的AI审稿评估方法。

Method: 1. 构建类别特定的基准子集（Category-specific benchmark subsets）；2. 引入跳过机制（Skip evaluation），当缺失人类参考时跳过评估，以增强完整性；3. 利用审稿人-作者-元审稿人对话作为专家标注，过滤不可靠的评论（增强准确性）；4. 发布CoCoReviewBench数据集，包含3,900篇ICLR和NeurIPS论文。

Result: 成功构建了包含3,900篇论文的CoCoReviewBench数据集，并揭示了当前AI模型在审稿任务中的准确性瓶颈及其幻觉问题，同时验证了推理模型在审稿任务中的显著优势。

Conclusion: 现有的AI审稿系统在准确性方面仍存在局限，且容易产生幻觉。研究表明，推理模型（Reasoning Models）在审稿任务中表现更优，为未来AI审稿系统的改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Despite the rapid development of AI reviewers, evaluating such systems remains challenging: metrics favor overlap with human reviews over correctness. However, since human reviews often cover only a subset of salient issues and sometimes contain mistakes, they are unreliable as gold references. To address this, we build category-specific benchmark subsets and skip evaluation when the corresponding human reviews are missing to strengthen Completeness. We also leverage reviewer--author--meta-review discussions as expert annotations and filter unreliable reviews accordingly to strengthen Correctness. Finally, we introduce CoCoReviewBench, which curates 3,900 papers from ICLR and NeurIPS to enable reliable and fine-grained evaluation of AI reviewers. Analysis shows that AI reviewers remain limited in correctness and are prone to hallucinations, and highlights reasoning models as more effective reviewers, motivating further directions for improving AI reviewers. Benchmarks and models are available at https://github.com/hexuandeng/CoCoReviewBench.

</details>


### [66] [How Value Induction Reshapes LLM Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07925)
*Arnav Arora,Natalie Schluter,Katherine Metcalf,Maartje ter Hoeve*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了向大语言模型注入特定价值观的副作用，发现虽然能提升安全性，但也会引发价值观波动，并显著增强模型的拟人化倾向及谄媚行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析对话式大语言模型在后训练阶段注入特定行为特征和价值观时，存在复杂且相互关联的影响，特别是对模型成瘾性、谄媚行为及安全性的潜在负面作用。

Method: 利用现有的偏好数据集（preference datasets）构建特定的价值观子集，对模型进行微调（fine-tuning），并评估模型在其他价值观表达、安全性、拟人化语言使用以及QA基准测试中的表现。

Result: (1) 注入某项价值观会引发其他相关甚至对立价值观的表达；(2) 注入积极价值观有助于提升模型安全性；(3) 所有价值观的诱导都会增加模型拟人化语言的使用，从而使模型更倾向于进行无原则的赞同和谄媚。

Conclusion: 注入特定价值观会不可避免地导致模型出现拟人化言语，并增加其取悦用户（迎合偏见/谄媚）的倾向，这揭示了模型效用与安全性/真实性之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Conversational Large Language Models are post-trained on language that expresses specific behavioural traits, such as curiosity, open-mindedness, and empathy, and values, such as helpfulness, harmlessness, and honesty. This is done to increase utility, ensure safety, and improve the experience of the people interacting with the model. However, values are complex and inter-related -- inducing one could modify behaviour on another. Further, inducing certain values can make models more addictive or sycophantic through language used in the generations, with a potential detrimental effect on the user. We investigate these and other unintended effects of value induction into models. We fine-tune models using curated value subsets of existing preference datasets, measuring the impact of value induction on expression of other values, model safety, anthropomorphic language, and various QA benchmarks. We find that (i) inducing values leads to expression of other related, and sometimes contrastive values, (ii) inducing positive values increases safety, and (iii) all values increase anthropomorphic language use, making models more validating and sycophantic.

</details>


### [67] [How to Train Your Latent Diffusion Language Model Jointly With the Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07933)
*Viacheslav Meshchaninov,Alexander Shabalin,Egor Chimbulatov,Nikita Gushchin,Ilya Koziev,Alexander Korotin,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Latent Diffusion Language Model (LDLM)，通过联合训练编码器、扩散模型和解码器，并配合特定的训练优化策略，有效解决了文本生成中潜在空间构建和模型性能问题，实现了生成质量与速度的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成中的潜在扩散模型面临构建合适潜在空间的挑战，且现有的离散或连续扩散模型在效率和质量上存在不足。

Method: 提出了一种联合训练框架（LDLM），包含可训练的编码器、扩散模型和解码器；并采用了一套优化训练配方：MSE解码器损失、扩散到编码器的预热机制、自适应时间步采样以及解码器输入噪声。

Result: 在 OpenWebText 和 LM1B 数据集上，LDLM 的生成性能优于现有的离散和连续扩散语言模型，且推理速度提升了 2 到 13 倍。

Conclusion: LDLM 证明了联合训练潜在空间、扩散模型和解码器是提升潜在扩散模型在文本生成任务中竞争力的关键。该方法在保证生成质量的同时，大幅提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models offer an attractive alternative to discrete diffusion for non-autoregressive text generation by operating on continuous text representations and denoising entire sequences in parallel. The major challenge in latent diffusion modeling is constructing a suitable latent space. In this work, we present the Latent Diffusion Language Model (LDLM), in which the latent encoder, diffusion model, and decoder are trained jointly. LDLM builds its latent space by reshaping the representations of a pre-trained language model with a trainable encoder, yielding latents that are easy to both denoise and decode into tokens. We show that naive joint training produces a low-quality diffusion model, and propose a simple training recipe consisting of an MSE decoder loss, diffusion-to-encoder warmup, adaptive timestep sampling, and decoder-input noise. Ablations show that each component substantially impacts generation performance. On OpenWebText and LM1B, LDLM achieves better generation performance than existing discrete and continuous diffusion language models while being $2{\text -}13\times$ faster, indicating that jointly learning the latent space is a key step toward making latent diffusion competitive for text generation.

</details>


### [68] [Ask Early, Ask Late, Ask Right: When Does Clarification Timing Matter for Long-Horizon Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07937)
*Anmol Gulati,Hariom Gupta,Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究定量揭示了长程AI智能体中“澄清有效性”的时间曲线规律：并非越早询问越好，不同类型的信息有明确的最优澄清区间，而当前模型普遍无法把握这一时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程AI智能体在执行复杂工作流时，早期的小错误会引发级联效应。当前研究忽视了澄清请求时机对执行成功率的影响，且缺乏关于“何时请求澄清最有价值”的量化规律。

Method: 引入强制注入框架（Forced-injection framework），在任务执行的不同阶段（四个维度：目标、输入、约束、环境）强制注入标准答案；跨越3个基准测试和4个前沿模型；进行超过6000次运行的实证分析；并结合300次非脚本化会话分析当前模型的澄清能力。

Result: 发现澄清的有效性随信息类型而异：目标澄清若超过执行10%的进度则几近失效；输入澄清的有效窗口可达50%。超过执行中期的澄清甚至会反向降低性能。实证显示，当前前沿模型普遍无法在最优窗口内寻求澄清（表现为过度询问或从不询问）。相关性分析证实这种时间特征主要由任务本身决定。

Conclusion: 澄清价值高度依赖于缺失的信息类型；澄清并非“越早越好”，且存在明确的时间窗（通常为执行的前半段）。现有模型缺乏这种时间感知能力，无法在最优窗口内寻求澄清。研究建立了量化的澄清需求曲线，为开发具备时间感知能力的智能体策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon AI agents execute complex workflows spanning hundreds of sequential actions, yet a single wrong assumption early on can cascade into irreversible errors. When instructions are incomplete, the agent must decide not only whether to ask for clarification but when, and no prior work measures how clarification value changes over the course of execution. We introduce a forced-injection framework that provides ground-truth clarifications at controlled points in the agent's trajectory across four information dimensions (goal, input, constraint, context), three agent benchmarks, and four frontier models (three per benchmark; one on a single benchmark only; 84 task variants; 6,000+ runs). Counter to the common intuition that "earlier is always better," we find that the value of clarification depends sharply on what information is missing: goal clarification loses nearly all value after 10% of execution (pass@3 drops from 0.78 to baseline), while input clarification retains value through roughly 50%. Deferring any clarification type past mid-trajectory degrades performance below never asking at all. Cross-model Kendall tau correlations (0.78-0.87 among models sharing identical task coverage; 0.34-0.67 across the full 4-model panel) confirm these timing profiles are substantially task-intrinsic. A complementary study of 300 unscripted sessions reveals that no current frontier model asks within the empirically optimal window, with strategies ranging from over-asking (52% of sessions) to never asking at all. These empirical demand curves provide the quantitative foundation that existing theoretical frameworks require but have lacked, and establish concrete design targets for timing-aware clarification policies. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [69] [GLiGuard: Schema-Conditioned Classification for LLM Safeguard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07982)
*Urchade Zaratiana,Mary Newhauser,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GLiGuard是一个超轻量级（0.3B）双向编码模型，通过架构条件化设计，在一次前向传播中高效完成多类别的LLM内容与安全审查，性能媲美大模型且速度大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全防护模型（Guardrails）多采用参数量巨大的自回归解码器，导致延迟高、扩展性差，难以高效处理多维度审核需求。

Method: 提出GLiGuard模型，基于GLiNER2架构，利用架构条件化（schema-conditioned）的双向编码器，将任务定义和标签语义编码为输入序列，实现非自回归的一次性多维度安全评估。

Result: GLiGuard在9个安全基准测试中F1分数与大模型持平，参数量减少23-90倍，吞吐量提升至16倍，延迟降低至17倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑的双向编码器（0.3B参数）在内容审核任务中可以达到与大规模自回归模型（7B-27B）相当的精度，同时显著降低计算成本并提高推理效率。

Abstract: Ensuring safe, policy-compliant outputs from large language models requires real-time content moderation that can scale across multiple safety dimensions. However, state-of-the-art guardrail models rely on autoregressive decoders with 7B--27B parameters, reformulating what is fundamentally a classification problem as sequential text generation, a design choice that incurs high latency and scales poorly to multi-aspect evaluation. In this work, we introduce \textbf{GLiGuard}, a 0.3B-parameter schema-conditioned bidirectional encoder adapted from GLiNER2 for LLM content moderation. The key idea is to encode task definitions and label semantics directly into the input sequence as structured token schemas, enabling simultaneous evaluation of prompt safety, response safety, refusal detection, 14 fine-grained harm categories, and 11 jailbreak strategies in a single non-autoregressive forward pass. This schema-conditioned design lets supported task and label blocks be composed directly in the input schema at inference time. Across nine established safety benchmarks, GLiGuard achieves F1 scores competitive with 7B--27B decoder-based guards despite being 23--90$\times$ smaller, while delivering up to 16$\times$ higher throughput and 17$\times$ lower latency. These results suggest that compact bidirectional encoders can approach the accuracy of much larger guard models while drastically reducing inference cost. Code and models are available at https://github.com/fastino-ai/GLiGuard.

</details>


### [70] [Fast Byte Latent Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08044)
*Julie Kallini,Artidoro Pagnoni,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Gargi Ghosh,Luke Zettlemoyer,Christopher Potts,Xiaochuang Han,Srinivasan Iyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了字节潜变量 Transformer（BLT）的改进方案（BLT-D, BLT-S, BLT-DV），通过引入并行扩散生成、自投机解码及验证机制，成功突破了字节级语言模型逐字节生成效率低下的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字节级语言模型虽然性能优异，但面临严重的推理速度瓶颈——即缓慢的逐字节自回归生成过程。

Method: 1. BLT-D（扩散模型）：结合块级扩散目标与下一字节预测损失，实现每步解码并行生成多个字节；2. BLT-S（自投机解码）：局部解码器跨越补丁边界生成草稿，通过全模型前向传播进行验证；3. BLT-DV（扩散+验证）：在扩散生成的序列后增加自回归验证步骤。

Result: 这些方法显著减少了生成序列所需的前向传播次数，且相比标准 BLT，估计内存带宽成本降低了 50% 以上，在速度与生成质量之间提供了灵活的权衡选项。

Conclusion: BLT-D、BLT-S 和 BLT-DV 等创新方法有效解决了字节级语言模型在生成速度上的瓶颈，且保持了高性能，显著降低了内存带宽开销，为字节级模型的实际应用扫清了关键障碍。

Abstract: Recent byte-level language models (LMs) match the performance of token-level models without relying on subword vocabularies, yet their utility is limited by slow, byte-by-byte autoregressive generation. We address this bottleneck in the Byte Latent Transformer (BLT) through new training and generation techniques. First, we introduce BLT Diffusion (BLT-D), a new model and our fastest BLT variant, trained with an auxiliary block-wise diffusion objective alongside the standard next-byte prediction loss. This enables an inference procedure that generates multiple bytes in parallel per decoding step, substantially reducing the number of forward passes required to generate a sequence. Second, we propose two extensions inspired by speculative decoding that trade some of this speed for higher generation quality: BLT Self-speculation (BLT-S), in which BLT's local decoder continues generating past its normal patch boundaries to draft bytes, which are then verified with a single full-model forward pass; and BLT Diffusion+Verification (BLT-DV), which augments BLT-D with an autoregressive verification step after diffusion-based generation. All methods may achieve an estimated memory-bandwidth cost over 50% lower than BLT on generation tasks. Each approach offers its own unique advantages, together removing key barriers to the practical use of byte-level LMs.

</details>


### [71] [Uncertainty-Aware Structured Data Extraction from Full CMR Reports via Distilled LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08045)
*Yi Yu,Parker Martin,Zhenyu Bu,Yixuan Liu,Yi-Yu Zheng,Orlando Simonetti,Yuchi Han,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CMR-EXTR 是一个轻量级、完全离线的 CMR 报告结构化框架，通过置信度评估机制实现了高精度的自动化信息抽取与质量控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自由文本心脏磁共振（CMR）报告难以直接用于队列组装、纵向数据管理及临床决策支持，且缺乏高效的结构化处理工具。

Method: 1. 采用“教师-学生”蒸馏流水线（Teacher-Student Distillation），在减少人工标注需求的同时实现完全离线推理；2. 构建三维不确定性度量机制（分布合理性、采样稳定性、跨字段一致性）以实现质量控制和人工审核预警。

Result: 在实验中，CMR-EXTR 达到了 99.65% 的字段级提取准确率，并证明其生成的置信度评分对于识别潜在错误具有高度信息量。

Conclusion: CMR-EXTR 是首个集成置信度评估的 CMR 专用信息抽取系统，能够高效、准确地将非结构化心脏磁共振报告转化为结构化数据，并显著优化了人工审核流程。

Abstract: Converting free-text cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) reports into auditable structured data remains a bottleneck for cohort assembly, longitudinal curation, and clinical decision support. We present CMR-EXTR, a lightweight framework that converts free-text CMR reports into structured data and assigns per-field confidence for quality control. A teacher-student distillation pipeline enables fully offline inference while limiting manual annotation. Uncertainty integrates three complementary principles -- distribution plausibility, sampling stability, and cross-field consistency -- to triage human review. Experiments show that CMR-EXTR achieves 99.65% variable-level accuracy, demonstrating both reliable extraction and informative confidence scores. To our knowledge, this is the first CMR-specific extraction system with integrated confidence estimation. The code is available at https://github.com/yuyi1005/CMR-EXTR.

</details>


### [72] [Accurate and Efficient Statistical Testing for Word Semantic Breadth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08048)
*Yo Ehara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对利用上下文嵌入衡量词义广度时，方向差异导致的统计偏差问题，提出了一种Householder对齐排列检验方法，通过对齐向量云均值方向来校准显著性检验，实现了更高的准确度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语境嵌入的词义广度测量中，现有的直接分散度比较方法存在偏差；词义方向的差异常被误判为分散度差异，导致在小差异区间内出现高Type-I错误率（假阳性）。

Method: 提出了Householder对齐排列检验（Householder-aligned permutation test）。具体包括：(1) 利用单个Householder反射将两个词类型的均值方向对齐；(2) 在对齐后的Token向量云上执行排列检验以计算校准后的非参数p值；(3) 针对GPU实现了批量化的排列操作和线性代数运算。

Result: 实验表明，该方法将Type-I错误率降低了32.5%，同时保持了对真实广度差异的敏感度，且在GPU上的实现获得了23倍的计算加速。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于Householder反射的排列检验方法，能有效校准语境多样性测量中的显著性检验，解决因词义方向性差异导致的误判问题，在提升统计准确性的同时显著加快了计算速度。

Abstract: Measuring the breadth of a word's meaning, or its spread across contexts, has become feasible with contextualized token embeddings. A word type can be represented as a cloud of token vectors, with dispersion-based statistics serving as proxies for contextual diversity (Nagata and Tanaka-Ishii, ACL2025). These measurements are useful for deciding appropriate sense distinctions when constructing thesauri and domain-specific dictionaries. However, when comparing the breadth of two word types, naive hypothesis testing on dispersion can be misleading: differences in semantic direction can masquerade as dispersion differences, inflating Type-I error and yielding "statistically significant" outcomes even when there is no true breadth difference. This is problematic because significance testing should distinguish genuine effects from incidental fluctuations in small-difference regimes. We propose a Householder-aligned permutation test to isolate dispersion differences from directional differences. Our method applies a single Householder reflection to align the mean directions of the two word types and then performs a permutation test on the aligned token clouds, yielding calibrated, non-parametric p-values. For practicality, we introduce a GPU-oriented implementation that batches permutations and linear algebra operations. Empirically, our alignment reduced Type-I error by 32.5% while preserving sensitivity to genuine breadth differences, and achieved a 23x speedup over the CPU baseline.

</details>


### [73] [CA-SQL: Complexity-Aware Inference Time Reasoning for Text-to-SQL via Exploration and Compute Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08057)
*James Petullo,Nianwen Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CA-SQL 是一种针对 Text-to-SQL 的推理时学习框架，通过基于任务难度的动态搜索广度调整、进化提示策略及高效投票机制，显著提升了轻量级 LLM 在复杂 SQL 生成任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 方案在处理 BIRD 基准测试中的高难度任务时，由于缺乏对解空间进行充分的探索，导致生成正确 SQL 查询的能力受限。

Method: 1. 难易度感知探索：根据任务估计难度动态调整候选查询的生成广度；2. 进化式提示播种（Prompt Seeding）：利用进化算法思想激发 LLM 的多样化探索行为；3. 新型投票法：设计独特的候选解选择机制，从生成的方案中筛选出最优查询。

Result: 在 BIRD 开发集上，CA-SQL 使用 GPT-4o-mini 实现了 51.72% 的“挑战级”任务准确率（SOTA），总体达到 61.06% 的执行准确率和 68.77% 的 Soft F1 分数。

Conclusion: CA-SQL 通过动态探索机制和进化搜索策略，显著提升了 LLM 在 Text-to-SQL 复杂任务中的性能。该研究证明了在推理阶段（Inference-time）针对任务难度进行自适应搜索控制，可以有效弥补模型规模的不足，实现 SOTA 级别的表现。

Abstract: While recent advancements in inference-time learning have improved LLM reasoning on Text-to-SQL tasks, current solutions still struggle to perform well on the most challenging tasks in the Bird-Bench (BIRD) benchmark. This is due to inadequate solution space exploration, which is necessary to uncover promising candidate queries that can be further refined to produce the correct output. To address this challenge, we introduce CA-SQL, a novel Text-to-SQL pipeline that utilizes the estimated difficulty of a task to dynamically scale the breadth of the exploration for generating solution candidates. In addition, we use a custom prompt seeding method, based on principles of evolutionary search, to further elicit exploratory behavior from the base LLM and a novel voting method to select the best candidate solution at the end of the search. Experiments demonstrate that our solution achieves a state-of-the-art score of 51.72% on the "challenging" tier of BIRD development set problems, using only GPT-4o-mini, out-performing other in-context learning approaches, even those that leverage larger models. Overall, our method attains a competitive 61.06% execution accuracy and 68.77% Soft F1 score on the BIRD development dataset.

</details>


### [74] [The Memory Curse: How Expanded Recall Erodes Cooperative Intent in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08060)
*Jiayuan Liu,Tianqin Li,Shiyi Du,Xin Luo,Haoxuan Zeng,Emanuel Tewolde,Tai Sing Lee,Tonghan Wang,Carl Kingsford,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大规模语言模型在多智能体合作中存在“记忆诅咒”现象：扩展上下文窗口可能因诱发缺乏前瞻性的推理或过度的审议，反而导致合作率显著下降。实验证明，改变记忆内容及优化前瞻性推理模式是缓解该现象的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现LLM的上下文窗口扩展在多智能体社交困境中存在系统性失败，即“记忆诅咒”（Memory Curse），旨在探究长文本窗口如何影响多智能体间的合作行为及其背后的认知触发机制。

Method: 1. 实验评估：在7种LLM和4种多智能体博弈中，对比不同历史窗口长度下的合作率（超500轮）；
2. 词法分析：分析378,000条推理轨迹，探讨记忆长度与行为表现的关联；
3. 认知探测：使用LoRA微调增强“前瞻性意图”以缓解性能衰退；
4. 记忆净化：保持长度不变但替换内容（合成合作记录）以验证内容因素；
5. 消融研究：移除思维链（CoT）以观测是否能缓解崩溃。

Result: 1. 在28个实验设置中的18个出现了合作率下降；
2. 性能衰退主要源于“前瞻性意图”的减弱，而非外界通常认为的焦虑感增加；
3. 增强前瞻性推理模式（LoRA微调）可有效缓解衰退；
4. 记忆内容（而非单纯长度）是触发合作失败的核心；
5. 思维链（CoT）产生的冗余审议有时反而会加剧系统崩溃。

Conclusion: 长上下文并非单纯的能力提升，而是多智能体博弈中的“记忆诅咒”。更长的内存历史若引发负面的推理模式（如缺乏前瞻性或过度审议），反而会破坏合作。记忆内容及其触发的推理过程是决定多智能体协作行为的关键因素。

Abstract: Context window expansion is often treated as a straightforward capability upgrade for LLMs, but we find it systematically fails in multi-agent social dilemmas. Across 7 LLMs and 4 games over 500 rounds, expanding accessible history degrades cooperation in 18 of 28 model--game settings, a pattern we term the memory curse. We isolate the underlying mechanism through three analyses. First, lexical analysis of 378,000 reasoning traces associates this breakdown with eroding forward-looking intent rather than rising paranoia. We validate this using targeted fine-tuning as a cognitive probe: a LoRA adapter trained exclusively on forward-looking traces mitigates the decay and transfers zero-shot to distinct games. Second, memory sanitization holds prompt length fixed while replacing visible history with synthetic cooperative records, which restores cooperation substantially, proving the trigger is memory content, not length alone. Finally, ablating explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoning often reduces the collapse, showing that deliberation paradoxically amplifies the memory curse. Together, these results recast memory as an active determinant of multi-agent behavior: longer recall can either destabilize or support cooperation depending on the reasoning patterns it elicits.

</details>


### [75] [Conformal Path Reasoning: Trustworthy Knowledge Graph Question Answering via Path-Level Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08077)
*Shuhang Lin,Chuhao Zhou,Xiao Lin,Zihan Dong,Kuan Lu,Zhencan Peng,Jie Yin,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR) 框架，通过查询级校准和 RCVNet 模块，解决了 KGQA 中置信度预测的校准与判别力难题，实现了更小、更准确的答案预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答（KGQA）方法缺乏可靠的覆盖保证，而现有的共形预测（Conformal Prediction）方法在 KGQA 中存在校准无效和得分判别力差的问题，导致覆盖率不足或预测集过大。

Method: 提出了 Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR) 框架：1. 采用查询级一致性校准（query-level conformal calibration）对路径级得分进行处理；2. 开发了 Residual Conformal Value Network (RCVNet) 模块，利用 PUCT 引导的探索来学习更具判别力的路径级非一致性分数（nonconformity scores）。

Result: 在基准测试中，与现有的共形基准方法相比，CPR 将经验覆盖率（Empirical Coverage Rate）提高了 34%，并将平均预测集的大小减少了 40%。

Conclusion: CPR框架有效解决了KGQA中置信度校准的问题，在满足统计覆盖保证的同时，显著提升了预测结果的精确度和紧凑性。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) has shown promise for grounded and interpretable reasoning, yet existing approaches often fail to provide reliable coverage guarantees over retrieved answers. While Conformal Prediction (CP) offers a principled framework for producing prediction sets with statistical guarantees, prior methods suffer from critical limitations in both calibration validity and score discriminability, resulting in violated coverage guarantees and excessively large prediction sets. To address these pitfalls, we propose Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR), a trustworthy KGQA framework with two key innovations. First, we perform query-level conformal calibration over path-level scores, preserving the exchangeability while generating path prediction sets. Second, we introduce the Residual Conformal Value Network (RCVNet), a lightweight module trained via PUCT-guided exploration to learn discriminative path-level nonconformity scores. Experiments on benchmarks show that CPR significantly improves the Empirical Coverage Rate by 34% while reducing average prediction set size by 40% compared to conformal baselines. These results validate the efficacy of CPR in satisfying coverage guarantees with substantially more compact answer sets.

</details>


### [76] [LLMs Improving LLMs: Agentic Discovery for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08083)
*Tong Zheng,Haolin Liu,Chengsong Huang,Huiwen Bao,Sheng Zhang,Rui Liu,Runpeng Dai,Ruibo Chen,Chenxi Liu,Tianyi Xiong,Xidong Wu,Hongming Zhang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoTTS框架，通过环境驱动的自动化搜索代替人工设计启发式规则，实现测试时计算资源分配的优化（TTS），在提升模型推理性能的同时大幅降低了探索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展（TTS）策略主要依赖人工设计和启发式经验，缺乏系统性探索，导致计算资源分配不够优化。作者旨在将TTS策略的制定过程转化为自动化发现问题，以解决人工策略的局限性。

Method: 提出了一套环境驱动的自动化发现框架AutoTTS，通过将测试时扩展（TTS）建模为控制器合成任务。具体采用了“宽度-深度”TTS作为切入点，引入beta参数化降低搜索空间难度，并利用细粒度执行跟踪反馈提高发现效率。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，自动发现的策略在准确率与成本的权衡上超越了强基线模型；该策略在未见过的基准和模型规模上表现出良好的泛化性，且发现过程仅需极低的计算成本（39.9美元，160分钟）。

Conclusion: AutoTTS框架通过将TTS策略设计从“人工启发式”转变为“环境驱动的自动化发现”，显著提升了大模型推理时的性能与计算效率平衡，并具备良好的泛化能力与极低的发现成本。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) has become an effective approach for improving large language model performance by allocating additional computation during inference. However, existing TTS strategies are largely hand-crafted: researchers manually design reasoning patterns and tune heuristics by intuition, leaving much of the computation-allocation space unexplored. We propose an environment-driven framework, AutoTTS, that changes what researchers design: from individual TTS heuristics to environments where TTS strategies can be discovered automatically. The key to AutoTTS lies in environment construction: the discovery environment must make the control space tractable and provide cheap, frequent feedback for TTS search. As a concrete instantiation, we formulate width--depth TTS as controller synthesis over pre-collected reasoning trajectories and probe signals, where controllers decide when to branch, continue, probe, prune, or stop and can be evaluated cheaply without repeated LLM calls. We further introduce beta parameterization to make the search tractable and fine-grained execution trace feedback to improve discovery efficiency by helping the agent diagnose why a TTS program fails. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that the discovered strategies improve the overall accuracy--cost tradeoff over strong manually designed baselines. The discovered strategies generalize to held-out benchmarks and model scales, while the entire discovery costs only $39.9 and 160 minutes. Our data, and code will be open-source at https://github.com/zhengkid/AutoTTS.

</details>


### [77] [MIST: Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Conversational Assistants for Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06897)
*Maximillian Chen,Xuanming Zhang,Michael Peng,Zhou Yu,Alexandros Papangelis,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对IoT语音交互难题，论文提出了MIST数据集及数据生成框架，旨在提升多模态大模型对物理世界复杂语音约束的推理与交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在处理物理世界IoT设备建模、时空约束、动态状态追踪以及混合主动交互方面存在挑战，缺乏相关研究。

Method: 提出了一个 синтетический（合成的）多轮语音驱动的代码生成任务数据集MIST，并设计了一个可扩展的数据生成框架。

Result: 实验表明，在MIST任务上，开源和闭源多模态大模型之间存在显著性能差距，且即便是最前沿的闭源模型仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: MIST揭示了当前多模态大模型在处理复杂IoT任务上的性能差距，并为未来研究具有物理约束推理能力的混合主动语音助手提供了基准和框架。

Abstract: The rise of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in the physical world necessitates voice-based interfaces capable of handling complex user experiences. While modern Large Language Models (LLMs) already demonstrate strong tool-usage capabilities, modeling real-world IoT devices presents a difficult, understudied challenge which combines modeling spatiotemporal constraints with speech inputs, dynamic state tracking, and mixed-initiative interaction patterns. We introduce MIST (the Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Dataset), a synthetic multi-turn, voice-driven code generation task that operates over IoT devices. We find that there is a significant gap between open- and closed-weight multimodal LLMs on MIST, and that even frontier closed-weight LLMs have substantial headroom. We release MIST and an extensible data generation framework to build related datasets in order to facilitate research on mixed-initiative voice assistants which reason about physical world constraints.

</details>


### [78] [MedExAgent: Training LLM Agents to Ask, Examine, and Diagnose in Noisy Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07058)
*Yicheng Gao,Xiaolin Zhou,Yahan Li,Yue Zhao,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有医疗诊断AI忽略临床复杂性的问题，本文提出了一个基于POMDP的诊断框架及噪声模型，并开发了MedExAgent。通过SFT与强化学习，该智能体实现了在复杂、含噪环境下高效、低成本的临床辅助诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大模型基准过分简化了诊断过程（如单轮问答、无噪声环境），未能模拟真实临床中交互性强、信息不完整及存在噪声的复杂特征。

Method: 1. 将临床诊断形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程 (POMDP)。2. 定义包含7种患者噪声和3种检查噪声的系统性噪声模型。3. 提出 MedExAgent：采用两阶段训练管道，先进行基于 Calgary-Cambridge 模型的监督微调 (SFT)，再使用 DAPO 进行多目标强化学习优化（兼顾准确性、工具调用质量及检查成本）。

Result: MedExAgent 在实验中取得了与更大规模模型相当的诊断性能，并展示了在医疗检查成本（经济成本与患者不适度）方面的优化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将临床诊断建模为POMDP并结合两阶段训练策略（SFT+DAPO），MedExAgent 能够处理复杂的真实临床环境，在保证诊断准确性的同时实现具有成本效益的检查策略。

Abstract: Real-world clinical diagnosis is a complex process in which the doctor is required to obtain information from both interaction with the patient and conducting medical exams. Additionally, the doctor needs to adapt to different patient personas, as well as noisy and incomplete information that can happen at any time during the process. However, existing benchmarks for medical LLMs and methods for automatic diagnosis largely simplify this process by reducing it to single-turn question answering, noise-free conversations, or sequential exam making, etc., ignoring the interactive and uncertain nature of clinical diagnosis. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by formalizing clinical diagnosis as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with three action types: questioning the patient, ordering medical exams as tool calls, and issuing a diagnosis. We also introduce a systematic noise model comprising seven patient noise types and three exam noise types. Using our proposed environment, we train an effective diagnosis agent, \textbf{MedExAgent}, through a two-stage pipeline that first performs supervised finetuning on synthetic conversations structured after the Calgary-Cambridge model for clinical interviews, and then applies DAPO to optimize a composite reward capturing diagnostic accuracy, tool call quality, and exam cost including financial cost and patient discomfort. Through extensive experiments and ablation studies, we demonstrate that MedExAgent achieves diagnostic performance comparable to larger models while maintaining cost-efficient examination strategies.

</details>

### [79] [Regulating Branch Parallelism in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06914)
*Swapnil Gandhi,Siva Hari,William J. Dally,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TAPER 通过一种动态的逐步准入控制策略，根据实时负载和延迟余量智能调度 LLM 并行解码分支，在保障 SLO 的前提下最大化了系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 并行解码系统要么采用激进策略（导致解码阶段拥塞，拖累批处理性能），要么采用保守的固定上限策略（限制了吞吐量），两者均无法适应动态变化的工作负载。

Method: 提出 TAPER，一种基于“分支外部性”（Branch Externality）的逐步准入控制机制。它通过预测分支对共享解码步骤造成的延迟影响，仅在当前批次有足够“松弛预算”（Slack Budget）时才执行额外的分支解码。

Result: 在 Qwen3-32B 模型上，TAPER 相比 IRP-Off 提升了 1.77 倍吞吐量（Goodput），相比 IRP-Eager 提升了 1.48 倍，同时保持了超过 95% 的 SLO 达标率。

Conclusion: TAPER 是一种高效的逐步准入控制器，通过动态平衡分支带来的计算开销与批处理的延迟余量，成功解决了 LLM 并行解码中的性能与 SLO 权衡问题。

Abstract: Recent methods expose intra-request parallelism in LLM outputs, allowing independent branches to decode concurrently. Existing serving systems execute these branches eagerly or under fixed caps. We show that both are brittle: eager admission inflates the shared decode step, degrading co-batched requests in serial stages, while conservative fixed caps forgo the throughput that motivated exposing branches in the first place. We call the excess step latency caused by admitted branches the branch externality and show that the safe width depends on batch composition, context lengths, and accumulated slack, all of which change continuously over a workload trace. We introduce TAPER, a per-step admission controller that treats extra branches as opportunistic work, admitted only when the predicted branch externality fits within the batch's current slack budget. Per-step regulation is practical because branch-level scheduling decouples compute from memory: branches share the request's prefix KV, so expanding or contracting width requires no memory reclamation. On Qwen3-32B, TAPER improves goodput by $1.77\times$ over IRP-Off and by $1.48\times$ over IRP-Eager, while maintaining over $95\%$ SLO attainment.

</details>


### [80] [Dooly: Configuration-Agnostic, Redundancy-Aware Profiling for LLM Inference Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07985)
*Joon Ha Kim,Geon-Woo Kim,Anoop Rachakonda,Daehyeok Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Dooly 是一种 LLM 推理配置的冗余感知分析工具。它通过污点传播技术识别算子维度，对模型中通用的算子进行复用式 profiling，避免重复采样，在保证高预测精度的同时，将 profiling 开销降低了超过 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理配置优化空间巨大（硬件、引擎、后端、架构组合复杂），但传统的基于配置剖析（Profiling）的模拟方法存在“硬编码操作集”和“完全重新采样”的问题，导致性能评估开销极其昂贵且难以扩展。

Method: 1. 污点传播（Taint Propagation）：识别并区分模型配置相关的维度与请求相关的动态维度。
2. 冗余感知性能分析：通过单次推理遍历，数据库中已记录的算子无需重复采样，仅对缺失的算子进行 profiling。
3. 状态隔离：利用推理引擎自身的初始化代码处理 Attention 等状态算子，避免手动插桩。
4. 延迟回归建模：基于积累的延迟数据建立回归模型，供模拟器预测不同配置下的性能。

Result: 在两种 GPU 平台、三种 Attention 后端和多种模型架构上验证：
1. 预测精度：首字延迟（TTFT）平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）控制在 5% 以内，每 Token 时间（TPOT）MAPE 控制在 8% 以内。
2. 效率提升：相比传统方法，在 12 个模型上的 Profiling GPU 计算耗时减少了 56.4%。

Conclusion: Dooly 通过利用 LLM 推理配置的结构化冗余，实现了高效且通用的推理延迟建模。它为现有模拟器提供了一个准确且低开销的后端，显著提升了 LLM 配置探索的效率。

Abstract: Selecting the optimal LLM inference configuration requires evaluation across hardware, serving engines, attention backends, and model architectures, since no single choice performs best across all workloads. Profile-based simulators are the standard tool, yet they hardcode their operation set to a specific configuration and re-profile every operation from scratch, making exploration prohibitively expensive. This cost stems from a missing structural understanding: every input dimension of each operation is fixed by the model configuration or determined by the incoming request.
