Many model-configuration values (e.g., head size, layer count) recur across models, so the same operation runs in many configurations; a single sweep over the request-dependent dimensions can serve them all. We present Dooly, which exploits this structure to achieve configuration-agnostic, redundancy-aware profiling. Dooly performs a single inference pass, labels each input dimension with its origin via taint propagation, and selectively profiles only operations absent from its latency database; stateful operations such as attention are isolated by reusing the serving engine's own initialization code, eliminating manual instrumentation. It builds latency regression models based on the database, which becomes a drop-in backend for existing simulators. Across two GPU platforms, three attention backends, and diverse model architectures, Dooly achieves simulation accuracy within 5% MAPE for TTFT and 8% for TPOT while reducing profiling GPU-hours by 56.4% across 12 models compared to the existing profiling approach.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [An Efficient Hybrid Sparse Attention with CPU-GPU Parallelism for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07719)
*Feiyu Yao,Zhixiong Niu,Xiaqing Li,Yongqiang Xiong,Juan Fang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fluxion是一种针对CPU驻留KV缓存的长上下文推理优化系统，通过协同设计预算分配、稀疏配置与计算调度，显著解决了传统混合稀疏设计中的带宽和流水线瓶颈，在保证精度的同时大幅提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：长上下文推理中KV缓存常驻CPU，现有方案存在PCIe带宽瓶颈、元数据开销过大以及CPU-GPU协同效率低（导致GPU空闲）等问题。传统的块稀疏注意力无法在大规模跨设备环境中有效解决这些系统级瓶颈。

Method: 方法：Fluxion通过三项核心技术实现优化：1. 基于输出感知的KV预算分配；2. 基于注意力头和粒度感知的稀疏化配置；3. 跨设备协同调度机制。具体实现包括轻量级头属性预测器、粒度预算选择器和基于优先级的调度器，用以联合优化预算分配、配置策略及CPU-GPU执行流水线的重叠。

Result: 结果：在2个模型、3个基准测试和40个任务上的评估显示：与最强的固定稀疏基准（KV预算仅为0.05）相比，Fluxion实现了1.5倍至3.7倍的加速，且精度保持良好，最大平均性能下降仅为-0.26（相对于全量注意力）。

Conclusion: 结论：Fluxion实现了一种基于CPU驻留KV缓存的高效混合稀疏注意力机制。通过端到端的系统协同设计，它在长上下文推理任务中，能够在保持高模型精度的同时，显著提升推理速度，解决现有方法在PCIe带宽和计算调度上的瓶颈。

Abstract: Long-context inference increasingly operates over CPU-resident KV caches, either because decoding-time KV states exceed GPU memory capacity or because disaggregated prefill-decode systems place KV data in host memory. Although block-sparse attention reduces attention cost in this setting, sparsity alone is insufficient for end-to-end efficiency. GPU-only designs remain constrained by PCIe bandwidth and metadata memory overhead, while CPU-GPU hybrid designs still suffer from substantial GPU idle time and bottlenecks in CPU-side top-k selection and sparse attention computation.
  Fluxion is built on three key insights: output-aware KV budgeting, head-specific and granularity-aware sparse configuration, and cross-device coordinated execution for sparse attention over CPU-resident KV caches. Guided by these insights, Fluxion combines a lightweight head-property predictor, a granularity-budget selector, and a priority-based scheduler to jointly optimize budget allocation, sparse configuration, and CPU-GPU execution overlap. This co-design enables hybrid sparse attention to achieve both accuracy and system efficiency in long-context inference. Across 2 models, 3 benchmarks, and 40 tasks, Fluxion preserves quality well -- the worst average degradation is only -0.26 relative to FULL, while delivering 1.5$\times$-3.7$\times$ speedup over the strongest fixed sparse hybrid baseline, whose KV budget is only 0.05.

</details>


### [82] [From Canopy to Collision: A Hybrid Predictive Framework for Identifying Risk Factors in Tree-Involved Traffic Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06684)
*Abdul Azim,Ahmed Hossain,Soumyadip Maitra,Panick Kalambay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用CatBoost和SHAP分析工具，针对2020-2023年树木碰撞事故数据进行建模，发现系安全带、车辆状态和行驶行为是决定损伤严重性的核心因素，并揭示了不同环境与车辆因素间的复合风险效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木碰撞属于严重的冲出路面（ROR）事故，往往导致致命或重伤。研究旨在构建一个综合分析框架，识别并量化导致此类碰撞严重程度的关键风险因素，从而为安全预防策略提供支撑。

Method: 1. 数据来源：使用2020-2023年CRSS数据库；2. 分类建模：采用CatBoost模型对碰撞严重性（KA vs BC）进行分类；3. 可解释性分析：利用SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）量化特征重要性并分析变量交互作用；4. 模型验证：使用二元逻辑回归模型验证SHAP的重要性评估结果。

Result: 1. 系安全带是降低重伤风险的最关键因素（不系安全带的重伤风险是不系的三倍）；2. 车龄较长、超速及驾驶员受损显著增加了碰撞严重性；3. 揭示了关键交互作用，如照明条件与车龄、超速与灯光等组合会产生累加性风险。

Conclusion: 研究强调了受约束情况（不系安全带）、车龄、超速和驾驶员受损是树木碰撞严重性的关键影响因素。通过识别具体变量间的相互作用（如照明与车龄、超速与照明），提出应采取加强安全带执法、改善低能见度下的车速管理及车队现代化等针对性安全措施。

Abstract: Tree-involved crashes represent a critical subset of run-off-road (ROR) collisions, often resulting in fatal or severe injuries due to high-energy impacts. This study develops a comprehensive analytical framework to identify and quantify risk factors contributing to crash severity in tree-involved collisions using the Crash Report Sampling System (CRSS) database spanning 2020-2023. The modeling framework follows a multi-step process. First, a machine learning based classification model (CatBoost) identifies key factors associated with binary crash injury severity (KA: fatal or incapacitating injury versus BC: non-incapacitating or possible injury). Second, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) tool is used to quantify and visualize the marginal effects of top influential factors on crash severity. Third, a binary logistic regression model estimates factor effects and validates SHAP-derived importance measures. Finally, SHAP interaction plots examine the combined effects of key contributing factors. Results reveal restraint non-use as the most influential predictor, with unrestrained occupants nearly three times more likely to experience severe outcomes due to ejection risk. Vehicle age, speeding violations, and driver impairment demonstrate substantial effects, reflecting reduced crashworthiness, increased impact forces, and reduced control capabilities. Critical interactions emerge between lighting conditions and vehicle age, speeding and lighting conditions, restraint use and vehicle age, and road surface and speeding, demonstrating additive risk effects with specific interactions. These findings provide critical insights for targeted safe system-based interventions, including enhanced seat belt enforcement, speed management in reduced visibility conditions, and vehicle fleet modernization.

</details>


### [83] [Robustness of Refugee-Matching Gains to Off-Policy Evaluation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06686)
*Kirk Bansak,Elisabeth Paulson,Dominik Rothenhäusler,Jeremy Ferwerda,Jens Hainmueller,Michael Hotard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多种先进的离线评估统计方法，验证了Bansak et al. (2018)关于难民匹配能改善难民安置结果的结论具有高度的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证Bansak et al. (2018)提出的难民匹配模型在不同评估方法和设定下的稳健性，确保因果影响评估的可靠性。

Method: 采用了多种离线策略评估（Off-policy Evaluation）方法，包括逆概率加权（IPW）和多种增强型逆概率加权（AIPW）变体；同时对建模架构的替代方案和不同的分配程序进行了敏感性分析。

Result: 在所有设定的情境下，影响估计值在量级上保持一致，且在大多数情况下具有统计学显著性，证实了原始研究结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过多种离线策略评估方法验证，难民匹配对改善难民结果的因果影响评估具有高度的鲁棒性和稳定性，与原始研究（Bansak et al., 2018）的结论高度一致。

Abstract: Previous research has investigated the potential of refugee matching for boosting refugee outcomes, first considered by Bansak et al. (2018). This paper demonstrates the stability of counterfactual impact evaluation results in the context of refugee matching in the United States using a range of off-policy evaluation methods. In order to estimate counterfactual impact and test the robustness of our results, we employ several evaluation methods, including inverse probability weighting (IPW) and multiple variants of augmented inverse probability weighting (AIPW). We also consider various modifications, including alternative modeling architectures and different assignment procedures. The impact estimates remain consistent in magnitude in all scenarios as well as statistically significant in most cases. Furthermore, the estimates are also consistent with the results originally presented in Bansak et al. (2018).

</details>


### [84] [Conditional generation of antibody sequences with classifier-guided germline-absorbing discrete diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06720)
*Justin Sanders,Luca Giancardo,Lan Guo,Yue Zhao,Kemal Sonmez,Nina Cheng,Melih Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种“种系吸收扩散”模型，通过从抗体成熟的物理演化路径中学习，解决了pLMs在抗体设计中过度依赖种系序列的问题，显著提升了条件生成任务的精度与生物学相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型在抗体设计中存在两大痛点：一是容易盲目记忆种系序列（Germline bias），缺乏对真正躯体高频突变（Somatic variation）的建模能力；二是缺乏灵活的分类器引导下的条件生成能力。

Method: 1. 采用离散扩散微调（Discrete Diffusion Fine-tuning）实现条件生成；2. 提出“种系吸收扩散”（Germline Absorbing Diffusion）机制，将种系序列设为扩散过程的吸收态，强迫模型学习从种系到成熟抗体的演化轨迹，从而消除种系偏差。

Result: 1. 非种系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，逼近理论极限；2. 在疏水性和结合亲和力引导的条件生成任务中，表现优于现有的EvoProtGrad算法，实现了样本质量与条件依从性之间更好的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过引入“种系吸收扩散”（Germline Absorbing Diffusion）这一新型去噪过程，有效克服了传统蛋白质语言模型（pLMs）在抗体设计中过分依赖种系序列的问题，显著提升了非种系残基的预测精度，并实现了更具生物学意义和高灵活性的条件生成。

Abstract: Antibody therapeutics are among the most successful modern medicines, yet computationally designing antibodies with desirable binding and developability properties remains challenging. While protein language models (pLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for antibody sequence design, existing approaches largely suffer from two key limitations: they predominantly memorize germline sequences rather than modeling biologically meaningful somatic variation, and they offer limited support for flexible classifier-guided conditional generation. We address these challenges through two primary contributions. First, we demonstrate that discrete diffusion fine-tuning achieves strong language modeling performance on antibody sequences while allowing for generation conditioned on any off-the-shelf classifier. Second, we introduce germline absorbing diffusion, a novel modification of the discrete diffusion noise process in which the germline sequence - rather than a masked sequence - serves as the absorbing state. This biologically motivated inductive bias restricts the model to learning the trajectory from germline to observed sequence, effectively excluding genetic variation and V(D)J recombination statistics from the learned distribution and dramatically mitigating germline bias. We show that germline diffusion improves non-germline residue prediction accuracy from 26 percent to 46 percent, approaching the theoretical upper bound set by true biological variability. We then demonstrate the utility of our germline diffusion model on the conditional generation tasks of sampling antibodies with improved hydrophobicity and predicted binding affinity. On both tasks our model shows an improved tradeoff between class adherence and sample quality, significantly outperforming EvoProtGrad, a popular strategy to sample from pLMs with gradient-based discrete Markov Chain Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [85] [Enabling Unsupervised Training of Deep EEG Denoisers With Intelligent Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06724)
*Qiyu Rao,Haozhe Tian,Homayoun Hamedmoghadam,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对可穿戴 EEG 因缺乏纯净训练数据而难以去噪的痛点，论文提出了自监督框架 iPSD，通过将信号划分为多个噪声实现来训练去噪模型，实现了在无参考样本下的高性能 EEG 信号增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴 EEG 信号微弱且易受频谱重叠噪声干扰。传统信号处理方法难以应对时变噪声，而现有深度学习方法依赖难以获得的纯净训练数据，导致泛化性能及实际场景应用受限。

Method: 提出了一种名为智能分区（Intelligent Partitioning, iPSD）的自监督学习框架。该方法通过将输入的 EEG 片段划分为多个具有相同潜在信号的独立噪声实现（noisy realizations）来进行训练，从而无需原始纯净数据即可实现深度学习模型的去噪训练，并支持单样本的零样本（zero-shot）去噪。

Result: iPSD 在包含耳内传感器采集在内的多项实验中达到了行业领先水平；特别是在极低信噪比（低至 -10 dB）和强伪影（如肌电信号）环境下，表现出显著优于现有基准方法的频谱保真度。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的 iPSD 方法成功实现了无需纯净参考信号的深度学习 EEG 去噪，在极低信噪比和复杂伪影环境下表现稳健，证明了其在可穿戴 EEG 设备中的实用价值。

Abstract: Denoising wearable electroencephalogram (EEG) is inherently challenging since neural activity is not only subtle but also inseparable from spectrally overlapping noise artifacts. Classical signal processing methods, relying on fixed or heuristic rules, cannot handle the time-varying pervasive artifacts in wearable EEGs. Deep learning methods, on the other hand, show promise in decomposition-free EEG denoising using highly expressive neural networks, but the training requires artifact-free EEG, which is inherently unobtainable. To address this, we propose Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising (iPSD). Our method eliminates the need for clean references by learning to partition an input EEG segment into independent noisy realizations with the same underlying signal. This enables self-supervision of deep learning denoisers, even in zero-shot settings where only a single EEG segment to be denoised is available. We validate iPSD through extensive experiments, including validations on wearable EEG from in-ear sensors. The results show that iPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance, most notably under extremely low signal-to-noise ratios (down to -10 dB) and challenging artifacts (e.g., EMG), with spectral fidelity orders of magnitude higher than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Transformer-Based Wildlife Species Classification from Daily Movement Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06726)
*Obed Irakoze,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Transformer在利用野生动物GPS轨迹进行物种识别方面优于传统序列模型，通过特征工程和统一时间采样，显著提高了分类精度和跨场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依靠日常运动轨迹数据来辨别野生动物物种具有挑战性。研究旨在通过深度学习技术，探索利用高精度轨迹特征识别物种的可行性，并解决数据稀疏、跨区域泛化及时间分辨率选择等实际应用难题。

Method: 利用Movebank平台的大规模7种野生动物GPS轨迹数据集，训练并对比了Transformer、LSTM、CNN和TCN等序列模型。采用了排除特定研究或区域的严格测试协议，分析了不同时间分辨率（1小时 vs 30分钟）的影响，并对比了基础位移编码与包含速度、方向及转向行为的扩展特征编码。

Result: Transformer在所有测试中均表现出最强的性能，平衡准确率比基准模型（LSTM, CNN, TCN）高出8-22个百分点；在象类分类任务中，平衡准确率达到0.83，AUC为0.92。特征增强显著提升了稀疏物种的分类效果，且1小时的时间分辨率相比30分钟在整体鲁棒性和数据完整性上更具优势。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的序列模型能高效地从GPS移动数据中推断野生动物物种。通过特征增强和统一的时间分辨率，模型在面对稀疏数据和跨区域预测时表现出强大的鲁棒性和优越性，为生态研究中的物种识别提供了强有力的方法论。

Abstract: Inferring the identity of wildlife species from daily movement data alone is a challenging task. We train sequence models on large-scale, 7-species GPS trajectories from the Movebank platform. Trajectories models are evaluated using a protocol in which entire telemetry studies or regions are heldout during testing. We compare Transformer-based sequence models to LSTM, CNN, and Temporal Convolutional Networks, and find that Transformers consistently achieve higher balanced accuracy with gains of approximately 8 to 22 percentage points, depending on the species and experimental setting. In an elephant binary classification task with 1-hour resolution, the Transformer achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.83 and an AUC of 0.92, substantially outperforming all baseline models. We examine, under data-limited conditions, feature representations by analyzing the differences between a basic displacement-based encoding and an expanded range of movement descriptors that include speed, direction, and turning behavior. With feature augmentation, we see clear performance gains, especially for underrepresented and sparsely represented species, such as large carnivores, lions, and Zebras. Finally, experiments comparing 1-hour and 30-minutetemporal resolutions show that while finer sampling can capture short-term movement patterns for some species, a unified 1-hour resolution yields more promising performance across studies by reducing missing data and ensuring consistent temporal coverage.

</details>


### [87] [FLAM: Evaluating Model Performance with Aggregatable Measures in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07962)
*Fabian Stricker,Jose A. Peregrina,David Bermbach,Christian Zirpins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中分布式评估结果与中心化评估不一致的问题，提出了FLAM方法，通过利用可聚合度量标准，在不泄露本地数据的前提下，确保全局性能评估的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中由于数据分布在各参与方，传统的加权平均评估方法与中心化评估结果存在偏差（特别是在非准确率类指标上），导致全局模型质量评估不准确，不符合联邦学习的目标。

Method: 提出了FLAM（Federated Learning Aggregatable Measures），这是一种专注于可聚合度量指标的评估框架，通过重构评估过程以确保分布式计算结果与中心化评估结果数学一致。

Result: FLAM成功消除了分布式评估与中心化评估之间的不一致性，无需访问原始全局测试数据即可获得准确的全局评估结果。

Conclusion: FLAM方法能够通过可聚合度量标准，在无需全局测试集的前提下，实现与中心化评估完全一致的联邦学习性能评价，解决了传统加权平均法在非同源评估中的准确性偏误。

Abstract: Performance evaluation is essential for assessing the quality of machine learning (ML) models and guiding deployment decisions. In federated learning (FL), assessing the performance is challenging because data are distributed across participants. Consequently, the coordinator must rely on locally computed evaluation metrics and aggregate them to assess the global model. A key challenge is that common aggregation strategies, such as weighted averaging based on the local samples per participant, do not always produce the same results as centralized evaluation. Existing definitions of performance evaluation are largely tailored to accuracy and do not generalize to other metrics, leading to inconsistencies between participant-based and centralized evaluation. However, such discrepancies are inconsistent with the FL objective and lead to a wrong calculation of the metric.
  To address this issue, we examine the underlying reasons for these discrepancies and propose FLAM, a performance evaluation method based on aggregatable measures that yields the same results as centralized evaluation without the need for a global test dataset.

</details>


### [88] [On Training in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06732)
*Nadav Timor,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Micah Goldblum,Yann LeCun,David Harel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）进行了理论量化，阐明了模型误差对策略性能的影响，并为在有限预算下如何平衡动态样本、奖励准确性及训练效率提供了最优策略分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型的强化学习方法主要依赖从学习到的动态模型和奖励模型中生成的轨迹进行策略训练，但其对模型误差的容忍度及样本分配的科学性尚缺乏系统的理论指导，特别是在奖励噪声存在的情况下。

Method: 1. 扩展了Asadi et al. (2018)的理论框架，推导出了动态模型和奖励模型样本的最优分配比。2. 引入Lipschitz常数分析，与时间矫正（temporal-straightening）目标建立联系。3. 分析了REINFORCE算法在有噪声奖励下的梯度估计，将其建模为一个变分优化问题。

Result: 1. 发现样本最优分配比受限于动态模型、奖励模型及策略的Lipschitz常数。2. 证明了零均值奖励噪声不会引入梯度偏差，仅增加可控的方差。3. 提出了一维优化模型，解决了在有限预算下如何在“廉价多样本”与“昂贵高质量样本”之间进行权衡的问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）提供了实践指导：(1) 定义了模型样本的最优分配比例，并指出低Lipschitz常数对于提升性能是必要的；(2) 证明了使用廉价但有噪声的奖励函数在满足特定条件下是可行的，并给出了预算分配的最优策略。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods train policies on imagined rollouts. These rollouts are trajectories generated by a learned dynamics model and are scored by a learned reward model, but without querying the true environment during policy updates. We study this training paradigm by quantifying how errors in learned dynamics and reward models affect returns and policy optimization. First, we extend the analysis of Asadi et al. (2018) to MDPs with learned reward models, and derive the optimal sample allocation--the ratio of dynamics samples to reward samples that minimizes a bound on return error under power-law scaling assumptions. We identify lower Lipschitz constants of the learned dynamics, reward, and policy as a representation desideratum that tightens this bound, and we connect this perspective to the temporal-straightening objective of Wang et al. (2026). Second, we examine how policy optimization with REINFORCE tolerates noisy rewards, which are often cheaper to obtain. We show that zero-mean reward noise leaves the gradient estimator unbiased and adds at most a variance term that decreases with the number of rollouts. This introduces a practical tradeoff: given a fixed budget, should one buy more rollouts with cheaper but noisier rewards, or fewer rollouts with more expensive but less noisy rewards? We reduce this choice to a one-dimensional optimization problem and characterize the optimum.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Factor Aggregation: Gauge-Aware Low-Rank Server Representations for Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06733)
*Jinqian Chen,Chang Liu,Jihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有联邦 LoRA 聚合方法中权重分解的规范依赖性（Gauge dependency）问题，该论文提出 GLoRA，通过在共识子空间内进行坐标对齐聚合以及支持异构秩的读取方式，显著提升了联邦大模型微调的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 federated LoRA 直接对低秩因子求平均存在“语义失配”问题：LoRA 更新具有规范等价性（Gauge equivalence），即相同的权重更新可以通过无限多种不同的低秩因子组合表示，直接平均会导致因坐标选择不同而产生不一致的聚合结果。

Method: 提出了 GLoRA（Gauge-aware LoRA），核心思路包括：1. 放弃直接平均原始低秩因子，改为利用客户端投影矩阵估计共识更新子空间；2. 在共享的参考坐标系下聚合，确保语义一致性；3. 提供秩兼容的读取机制（rank-compatible readout），支持不同客户端具有异构秩的场景。

Result: 在 GLUE 和 SuperNI 基准测试中，GLoRA 在应对数据异构性、资源限制（异构秩）、稀疏参与度及大规模主干模型等场景下，表现均优于现有的 federated LoRA 基线方法，并实现了良好的训练效率与性能平衡。

Conclusion: 联合微调（Federated LoRA）的有效性不仅仅取决于低秩因子的平均，更重要的是定义一个语义上可解释的服务端表示空间以进行聚合。GLoRA 通过解决权重分解的规范依赖性问题，确立了一个更具鲁棒性和灵活性的 federated LoRA 范式。

Abstract: Federated LoRA enables parameter-efficient adaptation of large language models under decentralized data and limited client resources.However, directly averaging LoRA factors is representation-dependent: the same intrinsic update admits infinitely many gauge-equivalent factorizations, so factor-level aggregation can change under arbitrary coordinate choices while the underlying update remains unchanged. This reveals a semantic mismatch in existing federated LoRA aggregation rules. We propose \textbf{GLoRA}, a gauge-aware server representation for federated LoRA.Instead of aggregating raw factors, GLoRA estimates a consensus update subspace from client projectors and aggregates client updates in shared reference coordinates, thereby representing semantic update aggregation entirely in low-rank form. To support heterogeneous client capacities, GLoRA further provides a rank-compatible readout that instantiates adapters of different ranks from the same server state without dense update reconstruction. Experiments on GLUE and SuperNI show that GLoRA consistently outperforms federated LoRA baselines under data, resource, and task heterogeneity, including heterogeneous client ranks, sparse participation, larger backbones, and unseen-task evaluation. GLoRA also achieves a favorable efficiency--performance trade-off, suggesting that effective federated LoRA requires not merely averaging low-rank factors, but defining a semantically meaningful server-side representation for aggregation.

</details>


### [90] [Simple KNN-Based Outlier Detection Achieves Robust Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07130)
*Tianle Jiang,Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了简单高效的KNN距离启发式方法足以解决鲁棒k-Means问题，在理论保证与实际性能上均能匹配甚至超越复杂算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒k-Means（即包含离群点的k-Means）算法过于复杂，且理论与实践中对于经典离群点检测启发式方法的有效性研究不足，需探索更简单高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出了基于K-最近邻（KNN）距离的离群点移除策略，证明了在假设最优聚类大小合理的情况下，该方法可将鲁棒k-Means问题转化为标准k-Means问题，并保持常数因子近似比。

Result: 理论证明了该方法在特定假设下能达到与现有先进算法相当的近似保证，且无需额外中心点；实验表明在多个真实数据集上，该方法在聚类代价和运行时间上优于或等同于更复杂的现有算法。

Conclusion: 基于KNN的简单启发式方法在鲁棒k-Means问题中表现极其有效，证明了离群点检测技术与聚类分析结合的潜力，并简化了鲁棒聚类的流程。

Abstract: Being robust to the presence of outliers is crucial for applying clustering algorithms in practice. In the $\textit{robust $k$-Means}$ problem (i.e., $k$-Means with outliers), the goal is to remove $z$ outliers and minimize the $k$-Means cost on the remaining points. Despite the close connection between robust $k$-Means and outlier detection, both theoretical and empirical understanding of the effectiveness of $\textit{classic outlier detection heuristics}$ for robust $k$-Means remains limited. In this paper, we prove that under a practical assumption on the optimal cluster sizes, simply removing points with large $K$-Nearest-Neighbor distances achieves performance comparable to prior work in terms of approximation guarantees: it yields a constant-factor reduction from robust $k$-Means to standard $k$-Means, without introducing additional centers or discarding extra outliers, as is commonly required by existing approaches. Empirically, experiments on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms or matches several more sophisticated algorithms in terms of clustering cost and runtime. These results demonstrate that simple KNN-based heuristics can be surprisingly effective for robust clustering, highlighting new opportunities to bridge techniques from outlier detection and clustering.

</details>


### [91] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对feasibility region随时间收缩的在线凸优化问题（CONES），设计了能同时优化遗憾与移动成本的算法，并在强凸设置下实现了理论最优的$O(\log T)$移动成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标函数固定但可行域呈嵌套序列（$S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$）演变的在线优化问题（CONES）中，需要设计算法在保证每一步可行性的前提下，同时最小化相对于静态最优基准的遗憾（regret）以及算法在可行域内的总移动成本。

Method: 1. 针对凸损失函数，提出了一种能够平衡遗憾（regret）和移动成本（movement cost）的懒惰算法（lazy-algorithm），通过参数$\beta \in (0,1]$在$O(T^{1-\beta})$遗憾和$O(T^\beta)$移动成本之间进行权衡；2. 针对强凸或$\alpha$-尖损失函数，提出了Frugal算法，并利用下界分析证明了其在移动成本上的最优性。

Result: 1. 对于凸函数，实现了遗憾与移动成本的权衡，即对于任意$\beta \in (0,1]$，遗憾为$O(T^{1-\beta})$，移动成本为$O(T^\beta)$；2. 对于强凸或$\alpha$-尖函数，实现了零遗憾和$O(\log T)$的移动成本；3. 证明了任何实现$o(T)$遗憾的在线算法，其移动成本最低为$\Omega(\log T)$。

Conclusion: 作者提出了针对CONES问题的最优在线算法Frugal。对于强凸或$\alpha$-尖函数，Frugal实现了零遗憾和$O(\log T)$的移动成本，且被证明在移动成本上达到了理论最优下界。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$. When the loss function is strongly convex or $α$-sharp, we propose an algorithm Frugal that simultaneously achieves zero regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with $o(T)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω(\log{T})$ for both cases, proving optimality of Frugal.

</details>


### [92] [Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (GeoKAN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06740)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Giridas Maiti,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoKAN 是一种改进的 KAN，通过学习黎曼度量对输入坐标进行几何变换，实现了动态的分辨率分配，从而大幅提升了处理科学计算中复杂、非均匀问题时的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) 在固定的欧氏坐标下工作，难以高效处理科学计算中常见的函数剧烈变化、局部奇异性及非均匀分布问题。

Method: 引入自适应学习的对角黎曼度量来改变输入空间的几何结构（扭曲输入空间），在基函数展开和特征混合前进行空间变换。实现了包括 GeoKAN-NNMetric, GeoKAN-γ, 和 LM-KAN（含RBF、小波、傅里叶基）在内的多种变体。

Result: GeoKAN 能够自主学习如何通过拉伸剧烈变化区域和压缩平滑区域来重新分配表征能力，在处理科学机器学习及微分方程问题时，相比传统 KAN 具有更强的适应性和精度。

Conclusion: GeoKAN 通过学习黎曼度量引入几何归纳偏置，动态调整表示分辨率，在大梯度、局部化及强非均匀性的科学机器学习任务中表现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: We introduce Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (GeoKANs), a family of geometry-aware KAN-type models in which approximation is carried out in learned, geometry-adapted coordinates rather than in fixed Euclidean input coordinates. GeoKAN achieves this by learning a diagonal Riemannian metric that warps the input before basis expansion and feature mixing. The learned metric provides a geometric inductive bias through local length scaling and volume distortion, and in physics-informed settings it also affects the differential structure seen by the model. Within this framework, we develop three main variants, namely GeoKAN-NNMetric, GeoKAN-$γ$, and LM-KAN. For LM-KAN, we further consider three basis-specific versions, LM-KAN-RBF, LM-KAN-Wav, and LM-KAN-Fourier. These variants allow us to study geometry-aware KAN models both as general function approximators and as surrogates in physics-informed learning. By stretching regions with rapid variation and compressing smoother regions, GeoKAN reallocates representational resolution in a task-dependent manner, allowing the model to place capacity where it is most needed. As a result, GeoKAN is well suited to sharp, stiff, localized, and strongly non-uniform regimes arising in scientific machine learning and differential-equation problems.

</details>


### [93] [Curvature Beyond Positivity: Greedy Guarantees for Arbitrary Submodular Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07902)
*Yixin Chen,Alan Kuhnle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文推广了“曲率”概念，使贪婪算法能够首次为任何子模函数（包含非单调及负值函数）提供统一的乘法近似保证，在保持经典理论一致性的同时，提升了实际应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的子模函数优化理论主要依赖“单调性”和“非负性”假设。然而，实际应用中目标函数往往存在惩罚项（导致负值）或非单调特性，以往的研究仅能孤立处理这些问题，缺乏通用的结构化理论。

Method: 1. 将曲率（curvature）参数推广至所有子模函数，作为刻画非线性程度的统一工具；2. 提出一种基于“修剪（pruning）”技术的贪婪算法；3. 在非单调函数中引入multilinear-extension变体以处理一般组合约束。

Result: 1. 证明了曲率约束下的贪婪近似比，其在非单调领域（1 ≤ c_g < 2.2）的表现优于传统的最优均匀比率（0.401）；2. 该结果在单调函数下可还原为经典的 (1-e^{-c_g})/c_g 界；3. 通过实验验证了该方法在实验设计、特征选择等任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过通用化曲率概念（curvature），成功为所有子模函数（包括包含负值和非单调函数）提供了一套统一的贪婪算法乘法近似比理论，弥补了现有理论在处理非单调性和负值方面的局限性。

Abstract: Submodular functions -- functions exhibiting diminishing returns -- are central to machine learning. When the objective is monotone and non-negative, the greedy algorithm achieves a tight $63\%$ approximation. But many practical objectives incorporate costs that make them negative on some inputs, and all existing multiplicative guarantees require non-negativity. Prior work handles negativity through additive bounds for the special class of decomposable functions and non-monotonicity through partial-monotonicity parameters, but these address each difficulty in isolation and neither extends the classical structural theory. We extend \emph{curvature} -- a parameter measuring how far a function deviates from linearity -- to all submodular functions, handling both non-monotonicity and negativity through a single classical concept. A greedy algorithm with pruning achieves a curvature-controlled multiplicative ratio for \emph{any} submodular function, including those taking negative values -- the first such guarantee beyond monotonicity and non-negativity. In the non-monotone regime $1 \le c_g < 2.2$, the bound strictly beats the best known uniform ratio of $0.401$ (for non-negative $f$), and it recovers the classical $(1-e^{-c_g})/c_g$ guarantee for monotone functions. A multilinear-extension variant extends the framework to general combinatorial constraints via multilinear relaxation. Experiments on cost-penalized experimental design, coverage, feature selection, and a curvature sweep on Multi-News passage selection support the theory.

</details>


### [94] [A Closed-Form Upper Bound for Admissible Learning-Rate Steps in Belief-Space Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06741)
*Zixi Li,Youzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过自然梯度（KL/Bregman几何）模型推导出学习率的上限，将传统的超参数调节转化为基于几何契约性的理论计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习中学习率通常被视为需要手动调整的超参数，缺乏理论指导，本研究旨在探索学习率更新的内在几何约束与理论上限。

Method: 将参数更新过程建模为概率单纯形（probability simplex）上的投影前向步骤，并利用KL散度/Bregman几何构建契约性（contractivity）分析模型。

Result: 提出了一种基于几何约束的计算方法，成功地将学习率的设置从经验性的“调节口号”转化为可计算的数学公式。

Conclusion: 学习率不应仅仅是被视为超参数，其上限可以通过基于KL散度或Bregman散度的模型推导为具体的数学公式，从而实现理论上的最优收敛。

Abstract: Learning-rate steps are usually treated as hyperparameters. This paper isolates a local beliefspace calculation: when an update is modeled as a projected forward step on the probability simplex, admissibility means contractivity in the natural KL/Bregman geometry. Under this model, the upper bound of an admissible step is not a tuning slogan but a formula.

</details>


### [95] [Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06755)
*Ismam Nur Swapnil,Aranya Saha,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Mohammad Ariful Haque,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出的 GXPO 是一种低成本的策略优化方法，通过梯度外推模拟长路径前瞻，在不增加额外滚动采样成本的前提下，显著提升了 RL 对 LLM 推理能力的微调效果和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的 GRPO 仅使用单步更新，而全多步前瞻虽能提供更好的优化方向，但因计算成本过高（需要多次反向传播）难以应用。因此需要一种高效、廉价且能近似长距离前瞻的策略优化方法。

Method: 提出 GXPO（Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization）。该方法在 GRPO 框架下，利用两次快速优化步测量梯度变化，预测虚拟的 K 步前瞻点，并执行修正后的更新策略。同时引入自适应机制，在预测不稳定时回退至标准 GRPO。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 和 Llama 的数学推理实验中，相比 GRPO，pass@1 提升了 1.65-5.00 个百分点；相比 SFPO，提升了 0.14-1.28 个百分点。同时实现了高达 4 倍的迭代速度提升和 2.33 倍的挂钟时间缩短。

Conclusion: GXPO 通过基于梯度的外推方法，以极低的额外计算成本（仅需三次反向传播）有效地模拟了多步前瞻，显著提升了 LLM 在数学推理任务中的性能，并保持了极高的训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is widely used to improve the reasoning ability of large language models, especially when answers can be automatically checked. Standard GRPO-style training updates the model using only the current step, while full multi-step lookahead can give a better update direction but is too expensive because it needs many backward passes. We propose Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization (GXPO), a plug-compatible policy-update rule for GRPO-style reasoning RL. GXPO approximates a longer local lookahead using only three backward passes during an active phase. It reuses the same batch of rollouts, rewards, advantages, and GRPO loss, so it does not require new rollouts or reward computation at the lookahead points. GXPO takes two fast optimizer steps, measures how the gradients change, predicts a virtual K-step lookahead point, moves the policy partway toward that point, and then applies a corrective update using the true gradient at the new position. When the lookahead signal becomes unstable, GXPO automatically switches back to standard single-pass GRPO. We also give a plain-gradient-descent surrogate analysis that explains when the extrapolation is exact and where its local errors come from. Across Qwen2.5 and Llama math-reasoning experiments, GXPO improves the average sampled pass@1 by +1.65 to +5.00 points over GRPO and by +0.14 to +1.28 points over the strongest SFPO setting, while keeping the active-phase cost fixed at three backward passes. It also achieves up to 4.00x step speedup, 2.33x wall-clock speedup, and 1.33x backward-pass speedup in reaching GRPO's peak accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [Sparse Attention as a Range Searching Problem: Towards an Inference-Efficient Index for KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06763)
*Mohsen Dehghankar,Abolfazl Asudeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏注意力导致模型推理精度下降的问题，本文提出了 Louver。该方法通过将注意力计算转化为半空间范围搜索问题，实现了在保证关键 KV 键“零遗漏（zero false negatives）”的前提下，显著提升推理效率，优于 FlashAttention。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法在推理时会丢失关键 KV 片段，导致推理误差（尤其是长文本推理任务）。现有方法缺乏针对查询动态变化的零误检保证，且现有的范围搜索算法计算开销过大，不适用于现代 LLM 推理。

Method: 将稀疏注意力重构为半空间范围搜索问题（halfspace range searching problem），并构建了名为 Louver 的定制索引结构。该结构专为 KV 缓存设计，具备轻量化特性，并针对 CPU 和 GPU 进行了硬件感知优化。

Result: Louver 实现了在指定阈值下的理论和实测零误检，在保持高精度的同时，推理速度超过了高度优化的 FlashAttention。

Conclusion: Louver 为稀疏注意力机制提供了理论保证和高效的 KV 缓存检索方案。实验证明它在保持零误检（zero false negatives）的同时，实现了比 FlashAttention 更优的推理效率，证明了召回保证是开发高效稀疏注意力的关键维度。

Abstract: Sparse attention improves LLM inference efficiency by selecting a subset of key-value entries, but at the cost of potential accuracy degradation. In particular, omitting critical KV entries can induce substantial errors in model outputs. Existing methods typically operate under fixed or adaptive token budgets and provide empirical robustness or partial theoretical guarantees, yet they do not ensure zero false negatives in decoding steps, particularly since the set of relevant tokens is both query- and step-dependent. Our empirical observations confirm that missing even one critical key can lead to sharp error spikes, especially in long reasoning tasks where the set of important tokens varies throughout decoding. This observation motivates the need for indexing methods that dynamically adapt to these variations across decoding steps while guaranteeing a full recall of the relevant keys above a certain threshold. We address this challenge by reformulating sparse attention as the halfspace range searching problem. However, existing range searching indices are not suitable for modern LLM inference due to their computational and implementation overheads. To overcome this, we introduce Louver, a novel index structure tailored for efficient KV cache retrieval. Louver (i) guarantees zero false negatives with respect to a specified threshold in both theory and practice, (ii) is lightweight to integrate into existing LLM pipelines, and (iii) incorporates hardware-aware optimizations for both CPU and GPU executions. Our experiments demonstrate that Louver outperforms prior sparse attention methods in both accuracy and runtime, and is faster than highly optimized dense attentions such as FlashAttention. These results highlight that recall guarantees are a critical and overlooked dimension of sparse attention, and open a new direction for building theoretically grounded, efficient KV cache indices.

</details>


### [97] [A Rod Flow Model for Adam at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06821)
*Eric Regis,Sinho Chewi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将“杆流”（rod flow）技术扩展到Adam等主流自适应动量优化器，通过相空间建模，在“稳定性边缘”的高动态优化场景下实现了比传统模型更准确的轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对“稳定性边缘”的连续时间建模主要集中于基础梯度下降，而针对包含动量和自适应机制的高级优化器（如Adam）的数学建模尚处于发展不足的状态。

Method: 构建了包含参数和一阶矩的联合相空间 $(w, m)$，并将二阶矩 $\nu$ 视作平滑辅助变量，从而将“杆流”建模方法应用于Adam、重球法（Heavy Ball）、Nesterov动量以及RMSProp和NAdam的标量与按分量版本。

Result: 在八种主流优化器及典型机器学习架构上的实验表明，相比传统的稳定流（stable flow），“杆流”模型能够以更高的精度追踪优化器在“稳定性边缘”区域的行为。

Conclusion: 通过将“杆流”（rod flow）从梯度下降扩展至自适应动量方法（如Adam、RMSProp、NAdam等），本文证明了该框架能够更精确地追踪优化器在“稳定性边缘”（edge of stability）状态下的离散迭代轨迹，优于传统的稳定常微分方程建模。

Abstract: Cohen et al. (arXiv:2207.14484) observed that adaptive gradient methods such as Adam operate at the edge of stability. While there has been significant work on continuous-time modeling of gradient descent at the edge of stability, extending these models to momentum methods remains underdeveloped. In the gradient descent setting, Regis et al. (arXiv:2602.01480) introduced rod flow, which models consecutive iterates as an extended one-dimensional object -- a "rod." Here we extend rod flow to Adam by working in the joint phase space of parameters and first moment $(w, m)$ and treating the second moment $ν$ as a smooth auxiliary variable. We also develop rod flows for heavy ball momentum, Nesterov momentum, and scalar and per-component versions of RMSProp, Adam, and NAdam. For all eight optimizers, we empirically evaluate rod flow on representative machine learning architectures, where it tracks the discrete iterates through the edge-of-stability regime significantly more accurately than the corresponding stable flow.

</details>


### [98] [Revisiting Adam for Streaming Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06764)
*Florin Gogianu,Adrian Catalin Lutu,Razvan Pascanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究挑战了深度强化学习必须依赖重放缓冲区的传统观点，提出了基于方差自适应机制的 Adaptive Q(λ) 算法，实现了高效且稳定的在线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习长期依赖重放缓冲区（replay buffers）和并行采样来保持稳定性。研究旨在探索能否摆脱这些记忆机制，实现更高效、即时的在线强化学习。

Method: 1. 重新评估 DQN 和 C51 在在线学习场景（无重放缓冲区）下的表现；2. 分析 Adam 等优化器与在线更新的交互机制；3. 提出一种基于资格迹（eligibility traces）的方差自适应算法：Adaptive Q(λ)。

Result: 研究发现 C51 在在线设置下表现优异且具有竞争性。提出的 Adaptive Q(λ) 在 55 款 Atari 游戏中达到了人类基准的两倍，性能超越了现有的 StreamQ 等方法。

Conclusion: 证明了在线强化学习中，梯度有界和方差自适应更新是保持性能的关键。提出的 Adaptive Q(λ) 算法在 55 款 Atari 游戏中表现优异，性能大幅超过现有方法。

Abstract: Learning from a sequence of interactions, as soon as observations are perceived and acted upon, without explicitly storing them, holds the promise of simpler, more efficient and adaptive algorithms. For over a decade, however, deep reinforcement learning walked the contrary path, augmenting agents with replay buffers or parallel sampling routines, in an effort to tame learning instability. Recently, this topic has been revisited by Elsayed et al. (2024), focusing on update computation through eligibility traces and modifications to the optimisation routine, resulting in the StreamQ algorithm. In this work we take a step back, investigating the efficacy of established updates, such as those implemented by DQN and C51 within this online setting. Not only do we find that they perform well, but through analysing how the optimisation algorithm generally, and Adam in particular, interacts with these updates, we contend that two properties are essential for robust performance: i) the derivative of the objective is to be bounded and ii) weight updates are variance-adjusted. Rigorous and exhaustive experimentation demonstrates that C51, which exhibits both characteristics, is competitive with StreamQ across a subset of 55 Atari games. Using these insights, we derive a variance-adjusted algorithm based on eligibility traces, termed Adaptive Q$(λ)$, which approaches double the human baseline on the same subset, surpassing existing methods by all performance metrics.

</details>


### [99] [Distributional Process Reward Models: Calibrated Prediction of Future Rewards via Conditional Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06785)
*Rachel Ma,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Kristjan Greenewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PRM校准不佳的问题，本文首次引入条件最优传输（CondOT）学习单调条件分位数函数，实现了对成功概率的结构化校准，并显著优化了推理时扩展的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展方法高度依赖过程奖励模型（PRM），但现有PRM往往校准不佳且会高估成功概率，导致扩展效果受限。

Method: 提出了一种基于条件最优传输（Conditional Optimal Transport, CondOT）的校准方法。该方法修改了CondOT映射学习，通过PRM的隐藏状态作为条件，学习成功概率的单调条件分位数函数，从而实现对PRM输出的校准并提取置信区间，并将其集成到实例自适应扩展（IAS）框架中。

Result: 在MATH-500和AIME基准测试中，该方法显著提升了PRM的校准能力（优于未校准PRM和分位数回归），并在下游的Best-of-N实例自适应扩展（IAS）任务中取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 条件最优传输（CondOT）是校准过程奖励模型（PRM）的一种稳健且实用的方法，能够提供结构化的保证并实现灵活的不确定性估计，有效提升了推理时扩展的效果。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling methods rely on Process Reward Models (PRMs), which are often poorly calibrated and overestimate success probabilities. We propose, to our knowledge, the first use of conditional optimal transport for calibrating PRMs, modifying conditional OT (CondOT) map learning \cite{bunne2022supervised} to estimate a monotonic conditional quantile function over success probabilities estimated by the PRM, conditioned on PRM hidden states. This yields structurally valid quantile estimates and enables efficient extraction of confidence bounds at arbitrary levels, which we integrate into the instance-adaptive scaling (IAS) framework of \cite{park2025know}. We evaluate on mathematical reasoning benchmarks spanning moderate-difficulty problems (MATH-500) and harder out-of-distribution problems (AIME). For PRMs with reliable ranking signals, our method substantially improves calibration over both uncalibrated PRMs and quantile regression. On downstream Best-of-N IAS performance, our method generally improves over uncalibrated PRMs. These results establish conditional optimal transport as another principled and practical approach to PRM calibration, offering structural guarantees and flexible uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [100] [$f$-Divergence Regularized RLHF: Two Tales of Sampling and Unified Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06977)
*Di Wu,Chengshuai Shi,Jing Yang,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLHF中的通用f-divergence正则化问题，建立了统一的理论框架，并提出了两种高效的在线学习算法，实现了理论上的收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前RLHF主流采用反向KL散度作为正则化手段，尽管探索其他f-divergence（如前向KL、chi-squared）的实证研究已有出现，但缺乏统一的通用f-divergence正则化理论框架。

Method: 提出了一种针对在线RLHF的通用f-divergence正则化目标函数的理论框架，并设计了两种算法：1. 扩展了经典乐观原则（Optimism Principle）并引入专门的探索奖励（Exploration Bonus）；2. 基于f-divergence正则化下最优策略对奖励扰动的敏感性设计的新方法。

Result: 推导并证明了算法的遗憾（Regret）为O(log T)，次优间隙（Sub-optimality Gap）为O(1/T)，为通用f-divergence正则化的RLHF提供了首个可证明的性能保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为在线RLHF在通用f-divergence正则化下建立了首个性能界限，证明了所提出的两类算法均可实现O(log T)的累积遗憾和O(1/T)的次优间隙，确立了其理论上的高效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a cornerstone technique for post-training large language models. While most existing approaches rely on the reverse KL-regularization, recent empirical studies have begun exploring alternative divergences (e.g., forward KL, chi-squared) as regularizers in RLHF. However, a unified theoretical understanding of general $f$-divergence regularization remains under-explored. To fill this gap, this work develops a comprehensive theoretical framework for online RLHF with a general $f$-divergence regularized objective. Rather than treating each possible divergence function individually, we adopt a holistic perspective across the entire function class and propose two algorithms based on distinct sampling principles. The first extends the classical optimism principle with a carefully designed exploration bonus, while the second introduces a new method that exploits the sensitivity of the optimal policy to reward perturbations under $f$-divergence regularization. Theoretical analysis shows that $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(1/T)$ sub-optimality gap are achievable, establishing provable efficiency of both algorithms and, to the best of our knowledge, the first performance bounds for online RLHF under general $f$-divergence regularization.

</details>


### [101] [Conformal Agent Error Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06788)
*Naihe Feng,Yi Sui,Shiyi Hou,Ga Wu,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MAS系统难以归因和自动恢复的问题，本文提出了一种基于共形预测的框架，能够精准定位错误序列并支持系统回滚，具有模型无关特性且满足有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）产生的交互轨迹过长，导致自动故障恢复时难以精准定位根本错误（误差归因），现有方法缺乏理论上的不确定性保证。

Method: 引入了专门针对序列数据（智能体轨迹）设计的基于过滤的共形预测（Filtration-based CP）算法，通过预测连续序列集合来识别决定性误差，并支持回滚机制。

Result: 通过实验验证了所提算法在多种智能体和数据集上的有效性，证明了该方法能够精准识别错误，并利用预测集合成功回滚MAS至正确状态。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于共形预测（CP）的鲁棒性误差归因框架，为多智能体系统（MAS）的自动故障恢复提供了一种模型无关、理论上可靠且可操作的解决方案。

Abstract: When multi-agent systems (MAS) fail, identifying where the decisive error occurred is the first step for automated recovery to an earlier state. Error attribution remains a fundamental challenge due to the long interaction traces that large language model-based MAS generate. This paper presents a framework for error attribution based on conformal prediction (CP) which provides finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees. We introduce new algorithms for filtration-based CP designed for sequential data such as agent trajectories. Unlike existing CP algorithms, our approach predicts sets that are contiguous sequences to enable efficient recovery and debugging. We verify our theoretical guarantees on a variety of agents and datasets, show that errors can be precisely isolated, then use prediction sets to rollback MAS to correct their own errors. Our overall approach is model-agnostic, and offers a principled uncertainty layer for MAS error attribution. We release code at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/conformal-agent-error-attribution.

</details>


### [102] [MIND: Monge Inception Distance for Generative Models Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06797)
*Quentin Berthet,Yu-Han Wu,Clement Crepy,Romuald Elie,Klaus Greff,Michael Eli Sander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的生成模型评估指标 MIND，利用切片 Wasserstein 距离取代 FID 的高维统计量计算，实现了更高效、更快速且更抗干扰的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 FID 存在的样本复杂度高、计算开销大以及易受对抗攻击（矩匹配）影响的局限性，提出一种更高效、更稳健的评估标准。

Method: 利用切片 Wasserstein 距离（Sliced Wasserstein Distance）通过平均一维最优传输距离来比较分布，避免了 FID 中高维均值和协方差矩阵的估计。

Result: 相比 FID，MIND 的样本效率提升了一个数量级，计算速度提升了两个数量级，且对矩匹配等对抗攻击具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIND 是一种比 FID 更高效、稳健的生成模型评估指标。仅需 5k 样本即可达到 FID 在 50k 样本下的表现，且在极小样本下（1k-2k）仍具有高度信息量，非常适合快速模型迭代。

Abstract: We propose the Monge Inception Distance (MIND), a metric for evaluating generative models that addresses key limitations of the widely adopted Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). The MIND metric leverages the sliced Wasserstein distance to compare distributions by averaging one-dimensional optimal transport distances, efficiently computed via sorting. This approach circumvents the estimation of high-dimensional means and covariance matrices, which underlie FID's poor sample complexity and vulnerability to adversarial attacks. We empirically demonstrate three primary advantages: (i) it is more sample-efficient by one order of magnitude, (ii) it is faster to compute by two orders of magnitude, (iii) it is more robust to adversarial attacks such as moment-matching. We show that MIND with 5k samples can replace the evaluation performance of FID with 50k samples, providing high correlation with this standard benchmark and superior discriminative performance. We further demonstrate that even smaller sample sizes (e.g., 1k or 2k) remain highly informative for rapid model iteration.

</details>


### [103] [Conformal-Style Quantile Analyses for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07115)
*Chengyu Du,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对常规老虎机算法对均值优化不足以应对追求上尾表现任务的问题，本文提出了 ACP-UCB1，通过自适应共形预测机制实现对上分位数的直接优化，并证明了其对数增长的后悔界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机多臂老虎机算法通常关注均值奖励（mean-reward），但在许多实际问题中，上尾表现（upper-tail performance）更为重要。现有的均值优化目标与上尾目标存在错位，需要一种针对上尾目标直接优化且能处理不确定性的策略。

Method: 提出了一种称为 ACP-UCB1 的算法，该算法结合了自适应共形预测（Adaptive Conformal Prediction）来估计上分位数（upper endpoint），并将其与 UCB 类型的乐观加权机制相结合。利用奖励-分位数集中不等式、重新计算得分分位数的微扰论证以及自适应水平的确定性定位来处理技术难点。

Result: ACP-UCB1 实现了对数级别的上分位数后悔（upper-quantile regret），其各臂贡献度为 O(log n / Δ_j^ACP)；实验结果证明了该方法在处理特定指标下的有效性和相对于 UCB1 的改进幅度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 ACP-UCB1 算法在处理具有强上尾表现需求的随机多臂老虎机问题时，不仅能够有效估计上尾目标，还能够实现次线性的累积后悔（对数增长），优于传统的基于均值的 UCB 算法。

Abstract: Stochastic bandit algorithms are usually analyzed under a mean-reward criterion, yet many problems favor arms with strong upper-tail performance, which we study herein. For a fixed miscoverage level \(α\), the natural upper-tail target of arm \(j\) is the upper endpoint \(F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)\) of a central prediction interval. This target can rank arms differently from their means, creating a central mismatch with the classical bandit objective. To this end, we propose ACP-UCB1, a conformal-style policy that combines an adaptive conformal estimate of the upper endpoint with a UCB-type optimism bonus. The technical challenge is that the conformity scores used by ACP-UCB1 are recomputed from evolving empirical quantile estimates and evaluated at an adaptive level. We control this endpoint through reward-quantile concentration, a perturbation argument for recomputed score quantiles, and deterministic localization of the adaptive level. ACP-UCB1 achieves logarithmic upper-quantile regret with per-arm contribution \(O(\nicefrac{\log n}{Δ_j^{\mathrm{ACP}}})\). We also provide metric-specific regret decompositions comparing ACP-UCB1 with UCB1 and use numerical experiments to validate performance and improvement.

</details>


### [104] [When Symbol Names Should Not Matter: A Logistic Theory of Fresh-Symbol Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07120)
*Wenjie Guan,Jelena Bradic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了 Transformer 处理抽象模板任务的原理，证明了模型泛化能力受限于词汇重叠（碰撞）的特定几何分布，并提出了评估提示策略是否能辅助实现“符号重命名不变性”的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究 Transformer 是否真正具备基于抽象符号而非具体 Token 名称的推理能力，重点关注在训练集和测试集词汇不交集（disjoint vocabularies）的情况下，模型如何实现“符号重命名不变性”（symbol renaming invariance）。

Method: 研究了变换器核（transformer-kernel）机制下的正则化核逻辑回归；通过将习得预测器分解为“理想模板级分类器”和“有限样本扰动项”，并利用有色碰撞图建模词汇重叠造成的干扰。

Result: 建立了“保证金-碰撞”准则（margin-versus-collision criterion），量化了正则化强度、样本容量以及词汇重叠结构如何影响模型在新符号上的分类性能。实证实验验证了该理论框架对模型泛化行为的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 通过引入“有色碰撞图”（colored collision graph），论文证明了在词汇量分布不均或存在重叠时，分类器泛化能力的边界取决于“碰撞几何”而非单纯的词汇量大小；此外，该框架可用于评估提示工程（prompting strategies）对抽象推理能力的影响。

Abstract: Template tasks have emerged as a clean testbed for asking whether transformers reason with abstract symbols rather than concrete token names. We study the fixed-label classification version of this problem, where train and test examples share latent templates but may use disjoint vocabularies. Unlike next-token prediction, the model need not emit unseen symbols; it must learn a decision rule invariant to symbol renaming. We analyze regularized kernel logistic classification in the transformer-kernel regime. Our main result decomposes the learned predictor into an ideal template-level classifier and a finite-sample perturbation caused by accidental token overlaps in the training data. We encode these overlaps by a colored collision graph and prove high-probability margin-transfer guarantees for fresh-symbol classification. This perspective extends template-based analyses to logistic classification and refines scalar diversity conditions: vocabulary size controls the average rate of collisions, but collision geometry controls whether the ideal classification margin is preserved. More broadly, the same perturbation framework applies to abstraction-augmented inputs, yielding a general margin-versus-collision criterion for identifying when prompting strategies improve fresh-symbol generalization. Synthetic template experiments illustrate the predicted roles of regularization, sample size, and transformer-kernel structure.

</details>


### [105] [On Privacy Leakage in Tabular Diffusion Models: Influential Factors, Attacker Knowledge, and Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06835)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,D. B. Emerson,Behnoosh Zamanlooy,Elaheh Bassak,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Sara Kodeiri,Marcelo Lotif,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究量化了TDMs的隐私风险，证明了即便在攻击者资源或信息匮乏的情况下，针对TDMs的成员推理攻击依然高效，并指出了现有启发式隐私评估指标的弊端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着表格扩散模型在合成数据领域的广泛应用，为了降低隐私泄露风险，亟需量化并深入理解这些模型所带来的隐私安全挑战。

Method: 在黑盒与白盒攻击场景下，利用先进的成员推理攻击（Membership Inference Attacks）对表格扩散模型（TDMs）进行实验评估，量化训练设置、合成策略及攻击者先验知识对隐私泄露的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1. 即使攻击者缺乏完美的训练设置信息、数据分布先验或大规模算力，依然能够成功实施攻击；2. 揭示了现有常用的隐私度量方法（如distance-to-closest record）在评估扩散模型时的局限性和误导性。

Conclusion: 现有的启发式隐私度量指标（如“距离最近记录”法）存在缺陷，无法准确衡量表格扩散模型的隐私风险；研究强调了在处理敏感表格数据时，必须重新审视扩散模型的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Tabular data plays an important role in many fields and industries, including those with elevated privacy considerations and risks. As such, there is a rising interest in generating high-quality synthetic proxies for real tabular data as a means of reducing privacy risk and proprietary data exposure. With tabular diffusion models (TDMs) demonstrating leading performance in synthesizing such data, understanding and measuring the privacy risks associated with these models is imperative. Leveraging state-of-the-art membership inference attacks for TDMs in both black- and white-box settings, this work quantifies the impact of training setup, synthesis choices, and attacker knowledge on privacy leakage. Moreover, the results demonstrate that adversaries need not have perfect knowledge of the training setup, identical data distributions, or massive compute resources to construct successful attacks. Finally, the pitfalls associated with applying heuristic privacy metrics, such as distance-to-closest record, are revealed.

</details>


### [106] [Improved Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Smooth Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07218)
*Kun Long,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的核平滑模型基强化学习方法，通过引入伯恩斯坦探索奖励，在Lipschitz连续MDP中实现了更优的后悔界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩MDP理论虽然样本效率高但假设过于严格；传统的核平滑方法虽然依赖性较弱（仅需Lipschitz连续性），但在性能保证（尤其是后悔界限的优化）上仍有提升空间。研究旨在通过改进核平滑方法的探索机制来获得更紧的后悔界限。

Method: 提出了一种基于核平滑（kernel smoothing）的模型基在线强化学习算法，适用于连续状态-动作空间。核心创新在于将一种新型的伯恩斯坦风格（Bernstein-style）探索奖励纳入该框架，以更好地处理不确定性，并利用新的伯恩斯坦型鞅集中不等式进行理论分析。

Result: 该算法在有限水平（finite-horizon）设置下实现了比现有最先进方法更优的后悔界限，特别是在时间跨度（horizon）依赖性方面取得了提升。此外，推导出的新型伯恩斯坦型鞅集中不等式具有独立的理论价值。

Conclusion: 本研究通过引入伯恩斯坦风格的探索奖励，成功改进了核平滑模型基强化学习在有限水平任务中的后悔界限，特别是在对时间跨度（horizon）的依赖性上表现更优。证明了通过结合非参数估计与精细的集中不等式分析，可以在Lipschitz连续假设下实现更高效的在线学习。

Abstract: For continuous state-action space scenarios, classical reinforcement learning (RL) theory predominantly focuses on low-rank Markov decision processes (MDPs), which provide sample-efficient guarantees at the expense of restrictive structural assumptions. Kernel smoothing model-based approaches offer a promising alternative paradigm that instead leverages the smoothness of the MDP and employs non-parametric kernel smoothing estimates of transition dynamics. This paper proposes a new kernel-smoothing model-based approach for online reinforcement learning in finite-horizon settings under Lipschitz continuity assumptions on the MDP. By incorporating a Bernstein-style exploration bonus into the kernel smoothing framework, our method achieves a regret bound which improves upon the state-of-the-art regret bound in its dependence on the horizon. The theoretical advancement relies on a delicate analysis of the synergy between Bernstein-style bonuses and kernel smoothing, where a new tight Bernstein-type concentration inequality for martingales may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [107] [How to Compress KV Cache in RL Post-Training? Shadow Mask Distillation for Memory-Efficient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06850)
*Rui Zhu,Weiheng Bai,Qiushi Wu,Yang Ren,Haixu Tang,Yuchu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在线强化学习中，KV缓存压缩在缓解内存压力的同时引入了严重的离策略偏差，该偏差在RL训练中被放大，导致现有统计方法难以纠正，且存在高方差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型（LLM）长上下文推理任务中，在线强化学习 rollout 阶段因 KV 缓存占用过大导致的内存瓶颈，以及尝试压缩 KV 缓存时引入的离策略偏差及其与 RL 优化不稳定性带来的负面影响。

Method: 本文通过对比分析在线强化学习（RL）推理阶段的“记忆墙”问题，指出KV缓存压缩导致的离策略偏差（off-policy bias）会被RL的优化不稳定性放大，导致现有纠正方案（如重要性加权）失效。

Result: 揭示了尽管现代 KV 压缩技术在标准推理中表现优异，但在 RL 优化中，采样器（受限上下文）与学习者（全量上下文）之间的不一致会导致偏差显著放大，现有方法无法有效解决此问题。

Conclusion: 该研究指出了在线强化学习中KV缓存压缩与离策略偏差之间的根本矛盾，并指出当前统计校正方法在缓解该问题上存在高方差和样本效率低下的局限性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for unlocking the advanced reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), encompassing frameworks like RLHF and RLAIF. Regardless of the specific optimization algorithm (e.g., PPO, GRPO, or Online DPO), online RL inherently requires an exploratory trajectory generation (rollout) phase. However, for long-context reasoning tasks, this rollout phase imposes a severe ``memory wall'' due to the exorbitant Key-Value (KV) cache footprint. While applying KV cache compression during rollouts mitigates this memory overhead, it induces a critical off-policy bias. Although modern KV compression is often nearly lossless during standard inference, even minuscule approximation errors are drastically amplified by the inherent instability of RL optimization. Specifically, the sampler generates responses under a sparse context, whereas the learner updates parameters using the full, dense context. Existing statistical solutions, such as importance reweighting, struggle to correct this magnified bias, suffering from high gradient variance and severe sample inefficiency.

</details>


### [108] [Modulated learning for private and distributed regression with just a single sample per client device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07233)
*Praneeth Vepakomma,Amirhossein Reisizadeh,Samuel Horváth,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大量设备各仅持有一个样本的联邦学习场景，提出了一种传输隐私增强数据表征而非模型参数的协作学习框架，实现了在满足隐私约束下的准确全局模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景（如可穿戴设备、日志监测）中，终端设备常仅持有单一样本。传统的联邦学习在单样本和差分隐私噪声干扰下，本地更新极不稳定且无效，导致全局模型难以收敛。

Method: 提出了一种针对单样本客户端的隐私保护方法：在本地对样本注入精心校准的扰动，生成隐私增强的中间表征并上传至服务器，服务器通过聚合这些表征生成与非隐私中心化训练期望一致的无偏梯度更新。该方法不同于传统联邦学习传输模型参数的方式。

Result: 该方法在极度受限的单样本客户端设置下，实现了有效的协作学习，能够在保证数据隐私的前提下，获得与非隐私集中式训练相当的梯度更新效果。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了在极度受限（每个客户端仅一个样本）条件下进行联邦学习的难题，证明了在保护隐私的同时，无需大量本地数据即可协作训练高效的全局模型。

Abstract: This work focuses on the question of learning from a large number of devices with each device holding only a single sample of data. Several real-world applications exist to this one sample per client setup up including learning from fitness trackers, data/app usage aggregators, body-worn sensing devices, and daily event monitors to name a few. When a client has only one sample, the standard federated learning paradigm breaks down as a local update based on that single point is far from being useful, especially in the earlier rounds for estimation of the model coefficients. This utility is further weakened by the privacy-inducing noise applied at every round. This work caters to this problem to enable such clients to collaboratively contribute to effectively learn a global model without leaking the privacy of their data. The proposed approach injects a single, carefully calibrated noisy perturbation to transform the sample at each client, followed by a post-processed representation which is shared with the server. These representations aggregated at the server are processed to obtain an unbiased gradient update that in expectation matches the non-private centralized gradient while preserving data privacy. This approach is different than traditional private federated learning, where the communication payloads involve model coefficients as opposed to privately transformed data samples. This method enables devices with extremely limited data to collaborate and learn accurate, privacy-preserving models without requiring large local datasets or sacrificing individual privacy.

</details>


### [109] [Christoffel-DPS: Optimal sensor placement in diffusion posterior sampling for arbitrary distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06861)
*James Rowbottom,Nick Huang,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Ben Adcock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统传感器放置方法（OSP）无法应对复杂非高斯分布的问题，该论文提出了一种基于克里斯托费尔函数的模型无关框架（Christoffel-DPS），通过数学保证显著提升了生成模型在极少传感器约束下的状态重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的传感器放置（OSP）方法依赖于高斯假设，无法应对现实中复杂的非高斯分布系统；而新兴的基于生成模型的重建方法（如DPS）缺乏与其相匹配的、非高斯的传感器选择策略，导致无法充分发挥现代恢复模型的能力。

Method: 提出了一个基于克里斯托费尔函数的传感器放置框架，能够为任意传感器配置提供后验采样的恢复保证；开发了离线和在线版本的“Christoffel-DPS”算法，将克里斯托费尔采样集成到生成模型（如DPS和流匹配模型）中，并推导了传感器数量的非渐近界。

Result: Christoffel-DPS 在多个非高斯结构基准测试中表现优于现有的高斯OSP基准和生成模型放置方法，特别是在传感器数量稀缺的极端情况下，验证了其在理论上的严谨性和实践中的高效性。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种基于克里斯托费尔函数（Christoffel function）的无分布传感器放置框架（Christoffel-DPS），通过理论和实证证明了该方法在处理复杂、非高斯分布状态重建任务中，优于传统的基于高斯假设的OSP算法及现有的生成模型放置策略，特别是在低传感器预算场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: State estimation is a critical task in scientific, engineering and control applications. Since the reliability of reconstructions depends on the number and position of sensors, optimal sensor placement (OSP) is essential in scenarios where measurements are sparse and expensive. Classical OSP approaches rely on Gaussian assumptions and are consequently unable to account for the complex distributions encountered in many real-world systems. Generative-model-based reconstruction using sensor guided diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) has emerged as a promising technique for reconstructing states from highly complex distributions. However, existing sensor-selection methods either require unrealistically many sensors or emulate classical OSP, creating a mismatch between modern recovery models with classical OSP tools motivating the need for fundamentally new ideas towards OSP that match the recent advances made in powerful recovery models. We introduce a distribution-free sensor placement framework based on the Christoffel function: a mathematical formulation of optimal sampling and recovery guarantees for posterior sampling with arbitrary sensors and signal distributions, from which we derive a new OSP strategy with non-asymptotic bounds on the number of sensors needed for recovery. We develop Christoffel-DPS, with offline and online variants, instantiating Christoffel sampling for generative models. Christoffel-DPS outperforms Gaussian OSP baselines and existing generative-model placement methods, validating that distribution-free sensing is both theoretically principled and practically superior. The framework is model-agnostic; we demonstrate its application to a range of unconditional DPS and flow-matching models on structurally non-Gaussian benchmarks, showing the efficacy of Christoffel-DPS in low sensor budget regimes.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Bandits: From Learning Efficiency to Fairness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06864)
*John Wang,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体多目标多臂老虎机问题，提出了兼顾效率与公平的分布式学习算法及其理论边界分析，证实了公平性约束在提升决策公平的同时会降低整体学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体多目标多臂老虎机（MO-MA-MAB）问题中，现有的研究需要同时平衡学习效率（Pareto regret）和学习公平性（Social Welfare），且智能体需要在时变图上通信，具有异构奖励特征。

Method: 提出了两种算法：1. Pareto UCB1 Gossip：通过创新的探索半径设计，实现了Pareto regret的O(log T)和O(√T)界；2. Simulated NSW UCB Gossip：结合了基于偏好的奖励模拟、基于gossip的效用估计和UCB探索，实现了O(T^3/4)的遗憾界。

Result: Pareto UCB1 Gossip在效率维度上达到了理论最优界，Simulated NSW UCB Gossip在公平性维度上取得了次线性遗憾；实验证明两种方法相对于基线分别提升了约100%和50%的性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了公平性约束与学习效率之间的权衡：引入公平性目标（Nash Social Welfare）会限制信息的聚合，从而导致收敛速度变慢（REGRET界从O(√T)增加到O(T^3/4)）。

Abstract: We study multi-objective multi-agent multi-armed bandits (MO-MA-MAB) under stochastic rewards, where agents observe heterogeneous reward vectors and communicate over time-varying graphs. We formulate this emerging problem setting to address \emph{efficient learning}, measured by Pareto regret, and incorporate \emph{fair learning} as an additional goal, captured via social welfare. To measure efficiency, we formulate Pareto regret and develop \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip}, whose novel exploration radius explicitly separates statistical uncertainty in Pareto-based inference from consensus error. To express the fairness constraint, we formulate a Nash Social Welfare objective over preference-scalarized rewards and propose \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip}, which integrates preference-based reward simulation, gossip-based utility estimation, and UCB-style exploration. We prove that \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip} achieves \(\mathcal{O}(\log T)\) regret and an instance-independent rate of \(\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})\), while \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip} achieves an instance-independent regret bound of \(\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})\). This separation reveals the cost of imposing the fairness constraint to our efficiency objective: fairness limits information aggregation and slows convergence. Experiments show that our methods consistently outperform baselines, improving performance by approximately \(100\%\) and \(50\%\) in the efficiency and fairness settings, respectively.

</details>


### [111] [Dataset Watermarking for Closed LLMs with Provable Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06865)
*Pengrun Huang,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对闭源LLM的训练集水印技术，通过增加词对共现频率植入签名，即便在仅使用1%比例的数据训练时，也能通过统计学测试有效溯源，同时保持原始数据的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM预训练和后训练数据缺乏严格监管，可能包含受版权保护的数据或评估基准集，因此需要一种方法在模型中植入“可检测签名”以确认其训练数据来源。

Method: 通过重写文本增加随机选定词对的共现频率作为水印信号，利用统计学方法在模型生成输出中检测该共现模式，从而判断模型是否受到特定数据集的训练。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个基准模型和数据集上均能实现可靠检测（p < 0.01），即使在水印数据仅占训练集1%的情况下依然有效，且不影响模型的性能和语义质量。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种针对闭源LLM的具有可证明检测能力的训练集水印方法，不仅在低污染比例（1%）下依然有效，且能在不破坏数据集实用性和语义完整性的前提下实现可靠的溯源验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are pre-trained and post-trained on vast amounts of loosely curated data, raising the possibility that these models may have been trained on proprietary datasets or the same benchmarks used for evaluation. This motivates the need for dataset watermarking: designing datasets such that training on them leaves detectable signatures in the resulting model. Prior work has explored this problem for open models. We introduce the first dataset watermarking method for closed LLMs with provable detection. In particular, we embed a dataset-level watermark signal by increasing the co-occurrence frequency of randomly selected word pairs through rephrasing, and detect it using a statistical test on co-occurrence patterns in model-generated outputs. We evaluate our method with multiple base models and benchmark datasets and show that it reliably detects the watermark ($p <0.01$) in the fine-tuning stage. Notably, our method remains effective in a data mixture setting where the watermarked dataset constitutes only approximately $1\%$ of the total fine-tuning tokens. Furthermore, we show that our method preserves the utility and semantic integrity of the benchmark.

</details>


### [112] [Revisiting Transformer Layer Parameterization Through Causal Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07588)
*Jin Xu,Camille Couturier,Victor Rühle,Saravan Rajmohan,James Hensman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为 CEM 的框架，将 Transformer 层重新解释为对特定能量函数的优化过程，通过这一理论视角推导出了多种参数高效的层设计，并在实验中证明了这些设计在保持性能的同时显著约束了模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformers 中 MHA 和门控 MLP 的参数化选择大多基于经验，缺乏理论指导，旨在通过能量最小化视角对这些设计进行重新审视和形式化。

Method: 提出因果能量最小化（Causal Energy Minimization, CEM）框架。将 Transformer 层视为对条件能量函数的优化步骤，推导得出权重共享的 MHA 可视为交互能量的梯度更新，而带共享投影的门控 MLP 可通过逐元素能量进行解释。由此推导出一系列受限设计，包括层内权重共享、对角加低秩交互、轻量级预处理器以及递归更新。

Result: 在 1 亿参数规模的语言模型实验中，采用 CEM 推导出的受限层在训练稳定性上表现良好，且性能足以与标准的 Transformer 基准模型相媲美。

Conclusion: CEM 提供了一个理解 Transformer 层参数化的新颖框架，将 Transformer 架构与基于能量的模型（EBM）联系起来，并证明了受限的参数化模型（如权重共享）在保持稳定性的同时，性能可达到标准 Transformer 的基准水平。

Abstract: Transformer blocks typically combine multi-head attention (MHA) for token mixing with gated MLPs for token-wise feature transformation, yet many choices in their parameterization remain largely empirical. We introduce Causal Energy Minimization (CEM), a framework that recasts Transformer layers as optimization steps on conditional energy functions while explicitly accounting for layer parameterization. Extending prior energy-based interpretations of attention, CEM shows that weight-tied MHA can be derived as a gradient update on an interaction energy, and that a gated MLP with shared up/down projections can be viewed through an element-wise energy. This perspective identifies a design space for Transformer layers that includes within-layer weight sharing, diagonal-plus-low-rank interactions, lightweight preconditioners, and recursive updates. We evaluate CEM-derived layers in language-modeling experiments at the moderate hundred-million-parameter scale. Despite their constrained parameterizations, these layers train stably and can match corresponding Transformer baselines. Overall, our results suggest that CEM provides a useful lens for understanding Transformer layer parameterization, connecting Transformer architectures to energy-based models and motivating further exploration of energy-guided layer designs.

</details>


### [113] [When Descent Is Too Stable: Event-Triggered Hamiltonian Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06868)
*Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHAPE 利用哈密顿动力学和事件触发控制，优化了非凸环境下的预算分配，解决了优化器过早陷入局部极小值的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算的非凸优化往往因陷入局部极小值而失败，即优化器在到达稳定点后浪费剩余评估次数进行无效的优化，缺乏全局探索与局部优化的平衡机制。

Method: 提出 SHAPE (Structured Adaptive Port-Hamiltonian Optimizer)，将优化转化为扩充相空间 $(q, p)$ 中的控制问题。利用哈密顿向量场引导局部下降，通过事件触发机制（event-triggered）根据梯度信息和局部平衡检测来动态调整优化策略（何时下降、何时探索）。

Result: 在固定预算的非凸优化任务中，SHAPE 相比传统的固定策略优化器表现出更佳的性能，且具备良好的鲁棒性，支持多种梯度输入形式。

Conclusion: 自适应哈密顿能量塑形（SHAPE）提供了一种原则性的机制，通过优化决策过程来平衡局部下降、探索和预算分配，有效克服了非凸优化中由于陷入局部极小值造成的预算浪费。

Abstract: Fixed-budget nonconvex optimization can fail not because local descent is unstable, but because it is too stable: after reaching a nearby stationary point, an optimizer may spend the remaining evaluations refining an uninformative local minimum. We formulate this failure mode as a control problem over optimizer dynamics, where the learner must decide when to descend, when to exploit a promising basin, and when stagnation should trigger movement elsewhere. We introduce SHAPE, a structured adaptive port-Hamiltonian task-family optimizer for event-triggered minima hunting under local information. Starting from gradient-descent dynamics, SHAPE lifts optimization to an augmented phase space $(q, p)$, where the primal state $q$ represents the candidate solution, the cotangent variable $p$ carries directional sensitivity, and a controller $u$ provides processed information from current gradient oracle. Within each stage, a learned Hamiltonian vector field induces structured local descent; across stages, a fixed event clock in the implementation updates ports and memory when local equilibria are detected, with stage-dependent horizons treated in the analysis as a direct generalization. This design preserves a passivity-compatible structure while allowing the same trained policy to use clean, stochastic, or estimated gradient inputs. Experiments on fixed-budget nonconvex optimization tasks show that SHAPE improves best-so-far performance compared with fixed-policy optimizers. These results suggest that adaptive Hamiltonian energy shaping provides a principled mechanism for balancing descent, exploration, and budget allocation in difficult optimization landscapes.

</details>


### [114] [Black-box model classification under the discriminative factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07878)
*Hayden Helm,Merrick Ohata,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对黑盒生成模型审计中的查询集选择依赖性问题，本文提出了一种判别式因子分解框架，能够量化查询集质量并优化查询集选择，从而提升模型属性推断的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式系统多以黑盒形式存在，用户难以获知其内部属性。现有基于嵌入响应关系的低维表示方法极其依赖查询集的选择，缺乏一种量化查询集质量的有效机制。

Method: 提出了“判别式因子分解”（Discriminative Factorization）框架，用于在黑盒设置下评估和筛选模型审计任务中的查询集质量，并利用判别场（Discriminative Field）来优化查询集的选择。

Result: 实验证明，在该框架下，分类任务的随机猜测概率随查询预算的增加呈指数级衰减；所提出的参数化方法能有效预测模型审计的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过估计的判别因子参数，可以准确预测模型性能的衰减率。使用判别场选择出的查询集能够复现最优（Oracle）查询集的性能排序，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Access to modern generative systems is often restricted to querying an API (the ``black-box" setting) and many properties of the system are unknown to the user at inference time. While recent work has shown that low-dimensional representations of models based on the relationship between their embedded responses to a set of queries are useful for inferring model-level properties, the quality of these representations is highly sensitive to the query set. We introduce the \emph{discriminative factorization} to distinguish between high- and low-quality query sets in the context of black-box model-level classification. Under this framework, the probability of chance-level classification decays exponentially in the query budget. On three auditing tasks, estimated factorization parameters predict the empirical performance decay rate. We conclude by showing that query sets selected using the estimated discriminative field reproduce the empirical ordering of oracle query sets.

</details>


### [115] [Continuous First, Discrete Later: VQ-VAEs Without Dimensional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06870)
*Xinyu Zhao,Nikita Karagodin,Hamed Hassani,Sinan Hersek,Paul Pu Liang,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了 VQ-VAE 中导致性能受限的“维度坍缩”现象，并提出了一种通过无量化自编码器“预热（Warm-up）”来恢复表征维度的方法，显著提升了大型图像和音频模型的重建与感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ-VAE 存在严重的“维度坍缩”现象（表征仅占全秩的 1-2%），导致重建表现存在硬性的损失下界。现有的代码本优化方法无法解决这一本质性的低秩问题，因此需要理论支撑来理解并修复该缺陷。

Method: 1. 建立分析框架：结合 Saxe 等人 (2014) 的序贯学习效应和率失真理论，解释了 VQ 的抑制作用导致维度坍缩的机制；2. 提出 AE Warm-up：在引入离散化之前，先将模型作为无量化自编码器（AE）进行预训练；3. 自适应准则：根据理论分析预测下游性能与预热长度的关系，确立切换至 VQ 训练的自适应点。

Result: 在 VQGAN 和 WavTokenizer 上实验表明：AE Warm-up 将代码本有效维度显著提升（如从 3-5 提升至 17-19），VQGAN 的 rFID 指标降低 17-35%，WavTokenizer 的 PESQ 指标提升 11-14%，且在不同代码本规模下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: AE Warm-up 是一种简单且有效的机制，通过在引入离散化前先进行预训练，从理论和实践上解决了 VQ-VAE 中的维度坍缩问题，从而大幅提升了模型重建质量和感知性能。

Abstract: While many approaches to improve VQ-VAE performance focus on codebook size and utilization, the effect of dimensional collapse, where trained VQ-VAE representations live in an extremely low-dimensional subspace (1-2% of full rank), remains unaddressed. We show theoretically and empirically that dimension collapse causes a hard loss lower bound that various codebook improvement techniques fail to surpass. Our analytic framework extends the sequential learning effect of Saxe et al. [2014] by introducing ideas from rate-distortion theory and explains how the latent collapse is caused by the VQ suppressing lower-variance directions. Our theory justifies a simple solution: a "warm-up phase" that trains the model as an (unquantized) autoencoder before introducing VQ. On both synthetic experiments and large-scale image (VQGAN) and audio (WavTokenizer) VQ-VAEs, we show that AE Warm-Up successfully restores representation dimension, leading to lower reconstruction and perceptual loss at the same training budget. Across codebook sizes $K \in$ {$2^{10}, 2^{14}, 2^{16}$}, AE warm-up raises VQGAN codebook effective dimension from 3-5 to 17-19 and reduces rFID by 17-35%; on WavTokenizer at $K \in$ {$2^{13}, 2^{14}$}, it raises codebook dimension from 4 to 17-19 and improves PESQ by 11-14%. We empirically characterize how warm-up duration governs the achievable final loss. In agreement with experiment, our theoretical analysis predicts downstream performance as a function of warm-up length, enabling an adaptive criterion for switching from AE Warm-up to VQ-VAE training.

</details>


### [116] [It Just Takes Two: Scaling Amortized Inference to Large Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07972)
*Antoine Wehenkel,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich,Chris Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种高效的神经后验估计策略，通过将Deep Set表征学习与推断头解耦，实现了计算代价与集合规模N的独立，从而解决了大规模集合数据推断的内存与计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在处理包含共享未知因素的集合观测数据时，传统的神经后验估计方法随着集合规模N的增大，计算与存储开销呈线性增长，难以扩展至大规模数据集。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段方法：首先通过训练一个在集合大小至多为2上进行均值池化的Deep Set来学习通用的编码器；随后在预聚合的嵌入上微调推断头。

Result: 在标量、图像、多视图3D、分子及高维条件生成等基准测试中，该方法在N达到数千的情况下，以极低的计算成本匹配或超越了标准基准。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过将表征学习与后验建模解耦，可以在不牺牲精度的前提下，实现对任意大小集合的摊销推断，且具备极高的计算效率。

Abstract: Neural posterior estimation has emerged as a powerful tool for amortized inference, with growing adoption across scientific and applied domains. In many of these applications, the conditioning variable is a set of observations whose elements depend not only on the target but also on unknown factors shared across the set. Optimal inference therefore requires treating the set jointly, which in turn requires training the estimator at the deployment set size -- a regime where memory and compute quickly become prohibitive. We introduce a simple, theoretically grounded strategy that decouples representation learning from posterior modeling. Our method trains a mean-pool Deep Set on sets of size at most two, producing an encoder that generalizes to arbitrary set sizes. The inference head is then finetuned on pre-aggregated embeddings, making training cost essentially independent of the deployment set size N. Across scalar, image, multi-view 3D, molecular, and high-dimensional conditional generation benchmarks with N in the thousands, our approach matches or outperforms standard baselines at a fraction of the compute.

</details>


### [117] [On the Divergence of Differential Temporal Difference Learning without Local Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06874)
*David Antrobius,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过反例证明，在平均奖励强化学习中，局部时钟与全局时钟的学习率并不等价，差异时间差分学习算法在全局时钟下可能发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨强化学习中全局时钟（step $t$）与局部时钟（状态访问次数 $\nu(s, t)$）学习率对算法收敛性的影响，并验证在平均奖励强化学习框架下两者是否依然具有等价性。

Method: 通过构建反例（counterexample），对比分析差异时间差分学习算法（Differential TD）分别采用局部时钟（计数访问次数）和全局时钟（随时间步衰减）时的收敛行为。

Result: 证明了折扣强化学习中局部时钟与全局时钟的一致性在平均奖励强化学习中不再成立，解决了Wan et al. [2021]和Blaser et al. [2026]提出的公开问题。

Conclusion: 在平均奖励强化学习中，基于频率的局部时钟（local clock）与基于时间的全局时钟（global clock）在收敛性上并不等价；差异时间差分学习（Differential TD）虽然在局部时钟下收敛，但在全局时钟下可能发散。

Abstract: Learning rate is a critical component of reinforcement learning (RL). This work uses global and local clocks to distinguish two types of learning rates. The former is of the standard form $α_t$ that depends only on the time step $t$ (i.e., a global clock). The latter is of the form $α_{ν(S_t, t)}$, where $ν(s, t)$ counts the number of visits to state $s$ until time $t$ (i.e., a local clock). In discounted RL, an RL algorithm that is convergent with a local clock is always also convergent with a global clock, and vice versa. We are not aware of any counterexample. The key contribution of this work is to show that this nice correspondence breaks down in average-reward RL. Specifically, we construct a counterexample showing that although differential temporal difference learning is convergent with a local clock, it can diverge with a global clock. This counterexample closes the open problem in Wan et al. [2021], Blaser et al. [2026].

</details>


### [118] [Better Protein Function Prediction by Modeling Survivorship Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06879)
*Zhongmou Chao,Poompol Buathong,Ekaterina Selivanovitch,Susan Daniel,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG
