TL;DR: 针对由于自然选择导致的蛋白质序列生存者偏差，Evo-PU 框架通过模拟进化过程来优化 PU 学习，在病毒突变预测任务中显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质序列数据存在严重的生存者偏差，即只能观察到自然选择保留下来的功能性序列。传统的 PU 学习忽略了进化过程对序列可观测性的影响，导致模型在预测蛋白质功能时准确性受限。

Method: 提出 Evo-PU 框架，利用对核苷酸突变过程的科学理解，显式建模序列在进化中的“可观测性”。该方法区分了因“非功能性”而未被观察到的序列和因“未产生/突变率低”而未被观察到的序列，从而改进了对未标记样本的加权处理。

Result: 在针对流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 的三个单生物体任务中，Evo-PU 的预测精度显著优于标准的 PU 学习方法、单类分类器（OCC）和大型蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）。同时在多生物体数据集上验证了其泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Evo-PU 通过将进化论（核苷酸突变过程）纳入正例-未标记学习（PU Learning），成功克服了蛋白质序列分析中的生存者偏差问题，在病毒变异预测任务中表现优于现有的 PU、单类分类（OCC）和蛋白质语言模型方法。

Abstract: Protein sequence data from nature exhibits survivorship bias: we only observe data from those organisms that survive and reproduce, while non-functional protein mutations are eliminated by natural selection. Thus, predicting whether a protein sequence is functional often requires learning from positive examples alone. While positive-unlabeled (PU) learning frameworks offer a generic solution to this problem, existing PU methods ignore the evolutionary processes that shape sequence observability and cause survivorship bias. Consider a sequence that is one mutation away from a commonly-observed protein variant in a well-surveilled organism. If the sequence were functional, it would likely be observed. If it is not observed, this suggests non-functionality. In contrast, sequences that are unlikely to arise through mutation may be missing simply because they never arose. Thus, these two kinds of missing sequences should be treated differently when training models. In this work, we propose Evo-PU, a PU learning framework that uses a scientific understanding of nucleotide mutation to model survivorship bias for well-surveilled single-organism sequence data. On three prediction tasks using single-organism uniform-coverage surveillance data -- predicting results from held-out influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) mutagenesis studies, and predicting future SARS-CoV-2 variants -- Evo-PU outperforms standard PU learning, one-class classification (OCC), and protein language models (PLMs). On prediction tasks from multi-organism ProteinGym datasets with more heterogeneous surveillance coverage, we identify opportunities to generalize our approach.

</details>


### [119] [Don't Retrain, Align: Adapting Autoregressive LMs to Diffusion LMs via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06885)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Alexis Fox,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 REPR-ALIGN 方法，通过将扩散语言模型的隐藏状态与冻结的自回归模型的表示进行对齐，实现了从 AR 到 DLM 的高效转换，大幅提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将自回归（AR）模型转化为扩散模型（DLM）的方法通常涉及大量的继续去噪训练。研究者质疑：AR 预训练中学到的语义表示结构是否可以在不改变架构的前提下，通过 explicit（显式）保存表示几何结构而非单纯的去噪训练来更高效地完成转换。

Method: 提出 REPR-ALIGN 方法：在保持预训练自回归（AR）模型参数冻结的同时，通过余弦相似度将扩散语言模型（DLM）的隐藏状态与 AR 模型的对应层进行对齐，并结合标准的掩码去噪任务进行联合优化。不需要额外的适配器或架构修改。

Result: 与传统方法相比，该方法实现了高达 4 倍的训练加速，并在低数据量场景下表现尤为有效，证明了 AR 模型的内部表示几何结构在转换过程中得到了有效保留。

Conclusion: 语言模型的语义表示可以跨生成顺序迁移，证明了“表示对齐”是一种训练扩散语言模型的高效技术：即DLM训练的本质是重新学习解码路径，而非重新学习语言表示。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently demonstrated capabilities that complement standard autoregressive (AR) models, particularly in non-sequential generation and bidirectional editing. Although recent work has shown that pretrained autoregressive checkpoints can be converted into diffusion language models, existing recipes primarily transfer parameters through continued denoising training with objective- and attention-level modifications. We instead ask whether the internal representation geometry learned by next-token prediction can be explicitly preserved during AR-to-DLM conversion. We hypothesize that much of the semantic structure learned by AR pretraining can transfer across generation orders, and thus DLM training should be viewed as relearning the decoding path rather than relearning language representations. To investigate this, we introduce REPR-ALIGN, a representation alignment objective that adapts a bidirectional masked diffusion model to reuse representations from a pretrained AR model of identical architecture. Concretely, we align the hidden states of the DLM to the frozen AR model at every layer using cosine similarity, while optimizing the standard masked denoising objective. This simple alignment, with no adapters and no architectural changes beyond the attention mask, yields up to 4x training acceleration in our setting and is particularly effective in low-data regimes. Our results suggest that linguistic representations can transfer across generation order, and that representation alignment provides a simple and effective technique for training diffusion language models. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Open-dLLM.

</details>


### [120] [Conservative Flows: A New Paradigm of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06905)
*Eshed Gal,Md Shahriar Rahim Siddiqui,Moshe Eliasof,Eldad Haber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练流模型的替代性生成范式，通过从数据而非噪声出发的随机动力学，辅以修正采样机制，有效提升了现有模型的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型多依赖从噪声先验到数据的传输，作者试图探索一种基于从数据分布出发的离散随机动力学，以改善生成质量。

Method: 提出了一种新的生成范式，从数据支持的状态出发而非噪声，保持数据分布不变。开发了两种概率不变采样机制：带有Metropolis修正的校正朗之万动力学（Corrected Langevin dynamics）和预测器-校正器流（Predictor-corrector flow）。

Result: 在合成数据集（Swiss-roll）和真实数据集（ImageNet-256, Oxford Flowers-102）上的实验表明，该方法能持续优于原始生成过程。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了通过基于数据支持状态的离散随机动力学进行生成不仅可行，而且能够显著提升现有预训练生成模型（如流模型）的采样质量。

Abstract: Modern generative modeling is dominated by transport from a noise prior to data. We propose an alternative paradigm in which generation is performed by a discrete stochastic dynamics that leaves the data distribution invariant, initialized from data-supported states rather than from noise. The framework can utilize any pretrained flow model. We develop two probability-preserving sampling mechanisms, a corrected Langevin dynamics with a Metropolis adjustment and a predictor-corrector flow, that operate directly on existing checkpoints. We validate the framework on a synthetic Swiss-roll target, ImageNet-256 and Oxford Flowers-102, where our samplers consistently improve over the original generation procedures.

</details>


### [121] [Dual-Scale Temporal Fusion Reveals Structured Predictability in Subseasonal-to-Seasonal Temperature Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06911)
*Elnaz Bashir,Jiali Wang,Lin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种双尺度学习框架，通过动态融合历史气候背景与近期天气特征，重构了对S2S温度预测的理解，证实了空间结构和时间多尺度交互作用是提升预测技能的核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的S2S温度预测主要依赖于超前时间（lead time），无法充分解释预测性在不同季节和地区的时空差异；亟需一种能够利用时空异质性和大尺度模式连贯性的新方法。

Method: 开发了一种双尺度学习架构（Dual-scale learning framework），将历法对齐的历史气候背景与超前时间匹配的近期天气演变分离，并通过空间自适应融合（Spatially adaptive fusion）与拓扑感知约束（Topology-aware structural constraints）进行整合。

Result: 该框架在30至90天的窗口期内实现了稳定的温度预测；研究揭示了季节与地理位置决定了不同时间尺度（历史背景 vs. 近期演变）的贡献权重，并证明空间组织的重组是决定预测技能的关键因子。

Conclusion: S2S可预测性本质上是一种结构化的多尺度现象，而非简单的超前时间衰减。该研究通过显式建模时空尺度，不仅提升了预测精度，还为理解气候系统的可预测性机制提供了更具可解释性的基础。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) temperature forecasts, spanning several weeks to a few months, are critically needed in agriculture practice, energy planning, and extreme-weather induced risk management, yet their reliability varies substantially across seasons and regions. Forecast skill is often attributed primarily to lead time, but this perspective does not fully explain the spatiotemporal patterns of predictability. Here we show that S2S predictability is organized across interacting temporal components, spatial heterogeneity, and large-scale pattern coherence, and that this structure can be explicitly characterized and exploited. We develop a dual-scale learning framework that separates calendar-aligned historical climate context from lead-time matched recent weather evolution, combining them through spatially adaptive fusion to enable stable temperature forecasts across the 30 to 90-day window. The learned fusion weights reveal that the balance between these two temporal scales shifts systematically with season and geography: during winter, interannual context dominates over high latitudes and complex terrain where forecast is the most difficult, while summer predictions reflect a more balanced temporal contribution across the domain. This spatially explicit reorganization of predictability, rather than simple lead-time decay, emerges as the primary determinant of forecast skill within the subseasonal window. Topology-aware structural constraints further improve spatial coherence of predicted temperature fields, stabilizing large-scale pattern organization particularly over complex terrain. These results reframe S2S predictability as a structured, multi-scale phenomenon, providing a more interpretable foundation for improving forecast systems and informing their use in practice.

</details>


### [122] [LLMs are not (consistently) Bayesian: Quantifying internal (in)consistencies of LLMs' probabilistic beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06915)
*Chacha Chen,Matthew Jörke,Adam Goliński,Masha Fedzechkina,Guillermo Sapiro,Sinead Williamson,Nicholas Foti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将LLM视为信息处理规则的方法，通过研究其处理新证据时的概率信念更新机制，发现LLM倾向于使用优于精确贝叶斯计算的启发式更新，并提供了一套评估和诊断LLM推理能力的方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在医疗、科学等高风险领域部署时，不仅需要正确的输出，还需具备随着新证据不断更新不确定性信念的能力，但目前对其内部信念更新的机制了解不足。

Method: 将大语言模型（LLM）建模为信息处理规则，利用“信息处理差距”（information processing gap）量化并研究LLM基于新证据更新概率信念时的内部一致性与更新方式，并对比了贝叶斯更新与启发式更新的表现。

Result: 实验发现LLM采用的更新策略各异，部分接近贝叶斯更新，而另一些则倾向于使用启发式策略；启发式策略在下游任务中表现更佳，且本文的方法能够准确诊断LLM推理系统的潜在问题。

Conclusion: LLMs的概率模型存在错误设定（misspecified）。非贝叶斯启发式更新在下游任务中表现优于精确贝叶斯计算，且所提出的测量方法可作为诊断LLM推理系统不一致性的有效工具。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are being deployed in complex domains such as medicine, science, and law, where it is important that they not only produce correct answers, but also represent and update uncertain beliefs about the world as new evidence arrives. We introduce the novel technique of studying LLMs as information processing rules and utilize the information processing gap to study the internal (in)consistencies of how LLMs update their probabilistic beliefs from evidence. Our extensive experiments evaluate multiple approaches in which LLMs can incorporate evidence into their beliefs. Some of these approaches produce (nearly) Bayesian updates; others seem to use a learned heuristic. Surprisingly, the non-Bayesian heuristic updates often outperform exact Bayesian computation in terms of downstream task performance -- indicating the LLMs' probabilistic models of the world are misspecified. Lastly, we show how our measure can provide diagnostics to identify issues with LLM-powered inferential systems.

</details>


### [123] [Tyche: One Step Flow for Efficient Probabilistic Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06916)
*Fan Xu,Yuan Gao,Kun Wang,Rui Su,Fenghua Ling,Hao Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有概率天气预报模型推理成本高的问题，本文提出了Tyche，一种基于条件流的单步概率预报架构，通过创新的训练目标和Transformer结构，在保持高性能和高质量不确定性量化的同时，极大提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型虽能实现很好的不确定性量化，但由于需要多次去噪推理，计算开销巨大。因此，亟需一种高效的、支持单步推理且具备高质量概率预报能力的模型来替代多步生成基线。

Method: 1. 提出了基于“目的地感知平均速度流”（destination-aware average-velocity flow）的单步条件流模型；2. 引入了JVP正则化的修正目标（JVP-regularized rectification objective），确保高维气候场的时序自洽性；3. 使用各向同性的Swin-style Transformer参数化传输场；4. 采用基于滚动（rollout-based）的微调策略，结合CRPS课程学习进行校准。

Result: 在ERA5数据集（1.5°分辨率）上，Tyche仅需单次函数评估（1-NFE）即可实现与现有最先进多步生成模型及ECMWF IFS集合预报相当或更优的预报精度与校准效果。

Conclusion: Tyche模型仅需单步推理（1-NFE）即可实现高性能的概率天气预报，在预测技能和校准度上比肩甚至超越了复杂的迭代式生成模型及ECMWF官方集合预报系统，显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Probabilistic weather forecasting requires not only accurate trajectories, but calibrated distributions over plausible atmospheric futures. Recent data-driven systems have achieved remarkable deterministic skill, and diffusion-based ensemble forecasters have substantially improved sample realism and uncertainty quantification. However, their inference cost scales with forecast horizon, ensemble size, and the number of denoising steps required for each transition, making large operational ensembles expensive. To address this, we present Tyche, a one-step conditional flow model for efficient probabilistic weather forecasting. Tyche models the conditional forecast distribution with a destination-aware average-velocity flow that maps Gaussian noise directly to future weather states in a single function evaluation (1-NFE). To make this one-step transport learnable in high-dimensional geophysical fields, we derive a JVP-regularized rectification objective that enforces temporal self-consistency across source and destination flow timesteps without explicitly forming Jacobians. The transport field is parameterized by an isotropic Swin-style transformer that preserves fine-scale spatial structure while remaining scalable on global grids. To improve ensemble reliability under autoregressive forecasting, we further introduce a rollout-based finetuning stage with curriculum CRPS calibration supervision. Experiments on ERA5 at 1.5$^\circ$ and 6-hour resolution show that our Tyche, using merely a single NFE, matches or exceeds the forecast skill and calibration of state-of-the-art multi-step generative baselines and the operational ECMWF IFS ensemble.

</details>


### [124] [Rollback-Free Stable Brick Structures Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06947)
*Chenhui Xu,Ziyue Bai,Fuxun Yu,Heng Huang,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习训练的积木生成模型，通过将物理约束转化为训练奖励，实现了无需回溯的高效、稳定3D结构生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归3D生成模型在构建物理稳定的积木结构时，依赖推理时的重复采样和回退，导致生成效率极低。

Method: 提出了一种强化学习范式，将物理约束（碰撞规避、全局连通性、结构互锁、形状一致性）从测试时的修正阶段前置到训练阶段，通过组装级别的奖励（assembly-level rewards）优化生成策略。

Result: 实验表明，该方法达到了行业领先的生成质量，同时将推理速度提升了数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的强化学习范式成功让生成模型内化了物理先验，实现了首个无需回溯（rollback-free）的稳定积木结构生成，在保证生成质量的同时显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: While autoregressive models have advanced 3D generation, creating physically stable brick structures remains a challenge due to the strict requirements of gravity and interconnectivity. Existing approaches rely on external physical simulators during inference to perform rejection sampling and brick-by-brick rollbacks, which severely bottlenecks efficiency. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning paradigm that shifts physical validity enforcement from test-time correction to training-time policy optimization. By utilizing assembly-level rewards, the model optimizes for collision avoidance, global connectivity, structural interlocking, and shape conformity. This paradigm allows the model to internalize physical priors, enabling the first rollback-free generation of stable brick structures. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art generation quality while accelerating inference speed by orders of magnitude. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/miniHuiHui/STABLE. Our models are available at https://huggingface.co/miniHui/STABLE.

</details>


### [125] [FastOmniTMAE: Parallel Clause Learning for Scalable and Hardware-Efficient Tsetlin Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06982)
*Ahmed K. Kadhim,Lei Jiao,Rishad Shafik,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Mayur Kishor Shende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastOmniTMAE 通过并行计算优化和 FPGA 加速，显著提升了 Tsetlin Machine 在 NLP 嵌入任务中的训练速度与硬件效能，在保持高质量嵌入的同时适应了资源受限环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深层嵌入模型（如 BERT）计算昂贵，而传统的 Word2Vec 模型可解释性较差；Tsetlin Machine (TM) 虽然提供了新的逻辑学习范式，但其现有的 Omni TM-AE 实现训练效率低下，不适合实时或受限环境。

Method: 1. 算法重构：将 Omni TM-AE 的序列化训练改为“评估”与“更新”两阶段并行处理；2. 硬件加速：针对传统 GPU 对 TM 支持不足的问题，在 SoC-FPGA 平台上实现了可重用的硬件加速方案。

Result: 与 Omni TM-AE 相比，FastOmniTMAE 在分类任务中训练速度提升至 5 倍，同时保持了相当的嵌入质量（Spearman 和 Kendall 度量）；在 FPGA 硬件上实现了高得分的嵌入性能，且占用资源极小。

Conclusion: FastOmniTMAE 通过并行化训练与 FPGA 硬件加速，成功解决了 Tsetlin Machine 在静态嵌入任务中训练缓慢的问题，证明了基于逻辑的学习范式在资源受限设备上实现高效 NLP 嵌入的可行性。

Abstract: Embedding models in natural language processing (NLP) increasingly rely on deep architectures such as BERT, while simpler models such as Word2Vec provide efficient representations but limited interpretability. The Tsetlin Machine (TM) offers an alternative logic-based learning paradigm. Omni TM Autoencoder (Omni TM-AE) applies this paradigm to static embedding by exploiting automaton state distributions within a single clause layer, but its training process remains slow. In this work, we propose FastOmniTMAE, a reformulation of Omni TM-AE that replaces sequential training dependencies with a two-stage parallel process: evaluation and update. Using a Single-Run Multi-Environment Benchmark covering classification, similarity, and clustering, FastOmniTMAE achieves up to 5$\times$ faster training in classification while maintaining comparable embedding quality under both Spearman and Kendall similarity measures. To address the limited efficiency of TM training on conventional GPUs, we further implement FastOmniTMAE as a reusable accelerator on SoC-FPGA platforms. The Multi-Hardware Benchmark shows that FastOmniTMAE achieves similarity scores of 0.669 on a resource-constrained FPGA and 0.696 on an UltraScale+ SoC, demonstrating efficient logic-based embedding training with a small hardware footprint.

</details>


### [126] [FlashMol: High-Quality Molecule Generation in as Few as Four Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07020)
*Xinyuan Wei,Zian Li,Shaoheng Yan,Cai Zhou,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashMol 是一种通过结合分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）、时间步重规划和 Jensen-Shannon 正则化技术实现的超快分子构象生成模型，在保持高质量的同时，实现了 4 步生成和 250 倍采样加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子构象生成模型（如 GeoLDM）采样速度慢，难以满足大规模虚拟筛选需求；近期旨在减少步数的方法虽然加速了采样，但往往严重牺牲了生成分子的化学稳定性。

Method: 1. 采用分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）进行逆向 KL 散度最小化；2. 对分子生成的扩散时间步进行重新规划（Respace），以优化生成器的初始化；3. 引入 Jensen-Shannon 散度正则化项，以缓解 DMD 的模式崩塌问题并提升生成多样性。

Result: 在 QM9 和 GEOM-DRUG 数据集上的实验结果表明，FlashMol 在 4 步采样下取得了超越传统 1000 步扩散模型的性能，并显著提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: FlashMol 实现了在保持高质量分子构象的前提下，将生成步数压缩至最低 4 步，实现了高达 250 倍的采样加速，性能可与 1000 步的教师模型媲美甚至超越。

Abstract: Generating chemically valid 3D molecular conformations is critical for computational drug discovery. Classical diffusion-based models like GeoLDM perform well but require hundreds of steps, making large-scale in silico screening impractical. Recent efforts on few-step molecular generation have accelerated this process to 12-50 steps, but they often largely sacrifice sample stability. In this work, we present FlashMol, an ultra-fast molecule generative model producing high-quality molecular conformations in as few as 4 steps. To achieve this, we adapt distribution matching distillation (DMD) - a reverse KL-divergence minimization objective - to the molecular domain for effective distillation. Considering the local minimization behavior of DMD, we respace the molecule generation timesteps, providing the generator with much better initialization and enables effective distillation. Additionally, to mitigate the mode-seeking behavior of DMD and improve diversity, we further regularize it with a Jensen-Shannon divergence term, which incorporates the mean-seeking behavior of the forward KL divergence. Extensive experiments on QM9 and GEOM-DRUG datasets demonstrate that FlashMol matches and even surpasses the original 1000-step teacher, achieving up to 250$\times$ acceleration in sampling speed while maintaining high molecular quality.

</details>


### [127] [A Systematic Investigation of The RL-Jailbreaker in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07032)
*Montaser Mohammedalamen,Kevin Roice,Reginald McLean,Alyssa Lefaivre Škopac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次对基于强化学习的生成模型越狱进行了系统性分解，揭示了密集奖励和长情境长度是导致越狱成功的核心机制，旨在通过理论指导强化模型防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型在向复杂系统自主引擎演进的过程中面临严峻的安全挑战（越狱攻击），现有的强化学习（RL）越狱方法虽已得到应用，但其成功的深层机制尚不明确，缺乏对RL越狱框架的系统性解析。

Method: 通过系统性分解的方法论，将RL越狱框架拆解为：1. 问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、情境长度）；2. 算法度量（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑造）。通过控制变量对这些因素进行结构性分析，以识别对抗攻击成功的决定性因素。

Result: 实验结果表明，RL越狱器成功攻破了所有目标模型及其防护机制。分析证实了环境的形式化要素（而非单纯的模型参数）是决定越狱成功的关键结构性因素。

Conclusion: 强化学习越狱攻击的核心驱动力是环境的形式化设计，特别是密集奖励（dense rewards）和较长的情境长度（extended episode lengths）。该研究为提升RL攻击效率提供了工具，并为构建防御RL攻击的鲁棒模型奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The evolution of generative models from next-token predictors to autonomous engines of complex systems necessitates rigorous safety hardening. Adversarial jailbreaking, the strategic manipulation of models to elicit harmful output, remains a primary threat to safe deployment. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) frames jailbreaking as a multi-step attack through sequential optimization, a mechanistic understanding of why the framework succeeds remains incomplete. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic decomposition of RL jailbreaking. We deconstruct the framework into problem formalization (reward function, action space, episode length), and algorithmic measures (RL algorithm, training data, reward-shaping) to identify the structural determinants of adversarial success. Our results reveal that the RL-jailbreaker successfully compromised all targeted models and safeguards. Through this first-of-its-kind analysis, we demonstrate that environment formalization, specifically dense rewards and extended episode lengths, is the primary driver of jailbreaking success. This work provides a tool for improving RL-jailbreaker efficiency and, ultimately, harden generative models resistant to RL-based attacks.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Material-Aware Hamiltonian Risk Fields for Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07038)
*Aditya Sai Ellendula,Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于端口哈密顿系统的选择性风险导航策略，通过上下文能量项实现智能的避险力门控，有效解决了传统导航在无安全路径时误触发避险动作的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的风险感知导航策略往往缺乏“选择性”，即在面临不可避免的危险时，容易产生错误的避险动作（hallucinating unsafe maneuvers）。本文旨在解决“如何在存在可行避险方案时充分利用自由度，而在无避险方案时有效抑制错误操作”这一矛盾。

Method: 提出了一种基于端口哈密顿系统（Port-Hamiltonian）的导航框架，通过添加一个“上下文能量项”（context-energy term）来调制避险力。同时，引入CVaR（条件风险价值）目标函数，重点优化罕见但高后果的风险转换，并利用该系统的梯度结构实现隐式的动力学门控（gating）。

Result: 在延迟逃逸、越野地形、静态语义地图及高速公路交通四项基准测试中表现显著：成功率最高提升至81%，虚假触发率大幅降低（从0.752降至0.114），灾难性故障减少了83%，且在不可逃逸场景下成功实现了避险动作的抑制。

Conclusion: 通过在端口哈密顿导航策略中引入上下文能量项，实现了在风险感知导航中的选择性避让机制。该方法通过梯度结构的内在约束，确保了仅在确实存在更安全路径时才执行规避操作，从而大幅提升了导航的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Risk-aware navigation should be selective: a policy should expose evasive degrees of freedom only when the local scene admits a lower-risk feasible maneuver, and suppress them when no safer alternative exists. We show that adding one context-energy term to a port-Hamiltonian navigation policy produces a learned force channel with exactly this falsifiable signature. When the local risk field contains a feasible lower-risk direction, the induced context force activates toward it; when the apparent escape is blocked or not yet available, a route-aware gate suppresses lateral force rather than hallucinating an unsafe maneuver. A CVaR tail-risk objective focuses gradient updates on rare but consequential risk transitions. We validate the selectivity signature across four settings. In the primary delayed-required-escape benchmark, route-aware CVaR reduces premature force activation from 0.950 to 0.180 versus DWA while raising success from 0.480 to 0.810 with zero replans. On real off-road terrain (RELLIS-3D), route-aware enrichment achieves correct activation rate 0.837 and false activation rate 0.114, compared to 0.378/0.752 for scalar risk gradients. On static semantic maps (DFC2018), enrichment reduces catastrophic failure from 0.60 to 0.10 and oscillation by 90.7% while preserving path efficiency. In highway traffic, collisions drop from 100% to 0% when a lane escape is feasible; when no escape exists, the policy suppresses the lateral maneuver. The selectivity property follows from the gradient structure of the context energy rather than from training-time tuning.

</details>


### [129] [CommFuse: Hiding Tail Latency via Communication Decomposition and Fusion for Distributed LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24013)
*Rezaul Karim,Austin Wen,Wang Zongzuo,Weiwei Zhang,Yang Liu,Walid Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommFuse 是一种通过将聚合通信分解为 P2P 调度来消除分布式 LLM 训练中“拖尾延迟”的新技术，有效提升了计算与通信重叠效率，在大规模训练任务中展现出卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练中分布式并行策略带来的高通信开销是瓶颈；现有的基于数据切片（data-slicing）的重叠优化方案存在严重的“拖尾延迟（tail latency）”问题，限制了训练效率。

Method: 提出 CommFuse 方法：将传统的规约-分发（reduce-scatter）和全收集（all-gather）操作分解为点对点（P2P）通信，并对计算任务进行精细化调度，从而实现计算与通信的深度细粒度重叠。

Result: 实验表明，与现有算法相比，CommFuse 能够消除拖尾延迟，在数据并行及多种张量并行策略（如 TPSP、UP）下，均实现了更低的通信开销、更高的 MFU 和系统吞吐量。

Conclusion: CommFuse 成功解决了分布式 LLM 训练中因数据分片导致的通信拖尾延迟问题，通过细粒度 P2P 通信与计算调度，显著提升了模型算子利用率（MFU）和系统吞吐量，且具有极高的通用性。

Abstract: The rapid growth in the size of large language models has necessitated the partitioning of computational workloads across accelerators such as GPUs, TPUs, and NPUs. However, these parallelization strategies incur substantial data communication overhead significantly hindering computational efficiency. While communication-computation overlap presents a promising direction, existing data slicing based solutions suffer from tail latency. To overcome this limitation, this research introduces a novel communication-computation overlap technique to eliminate this tail latency in state of the art overlap methods for distributed LLM training. The aim of this technique is to effectively mitigate communication bottleneck of tensor parallelism and data parallelism for distributed training and inference. In particular, we propose a novel method termed CommFuse that replaces conventional collective operations of reduce-scatter and all-gather with decomposed peer-to-peer (P2P) communication and schedules partitioned computations to enable fine-grained overlap. Our method provides an exact algorithm for reducing communication overhead that eliminates tail latency. Moreover, it presents a versatile solution compatible with data-parallel training and various tensor-level parallelism strategies, including TPSP and UP. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our technique consistently achieves lower latency, superior Model FLOPS Utilization (MFU), and high throughput.

</details>


### [130] [PACEvolve++: Improving Test-time Learning for Evolutionary Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07039)
*Minghao Yan,Bo Peng,Benjamin Coleman,Ziqi Chen,Zhouhang Xie,Shuo Chen,Zhankui He,Noveen Sachdeva,Weili Wang,Ed H. Chi,Shivaram Venkataraman,Wang-Cheng Kang,Derek Zhiyuan Cheng,Beidou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACEvolve++ 通过引入可学习的顾问模型（Advisor Model）对演化搜索进行阶段性自适应管控，克服了传统固定提示策略的局限，提升了复杂搜索任务的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型驱动的演化搜索通常依赖固定的提示（prompt）策略，难以适应评估成本高且需要学习任务特定动态的复杂工程与科研任务。

Method: 提出了一种名为 PACEvolve++ 的“顾问-模型”强化学习框架。核心机制包括：（1）策略解耦：由可训练的顾问模型生成/评估假设，由更强的先锋模型生成具体候选项；（2）阶段自适应学习：根据演化进程动态调整反馈机制，初期采用组内相对反馈以涵盖广泛搜索，后期采用 best-of-k 贡献反馈以实现稳定优化。

Result: 在任务负载均衡、序列推荐及蛋白质适应度推断等任务中，PACEvolve++ 表现优于现有的前沿演化搜索框架，实现了更快的收敛速度并稳定了测试时的演化搜索训练。

Conclusion: PACEvolve++ 框架通过将搜索策略决策与模型执行解耦，并在测试时进行动态调整，显著提升了演化搜索的效率、收敛速度及稳定性，展现了在工程与科学任务中的通用性和优越性。

Abstract: Large language models have become drivers of evolutionary search, but most systems rely on a fixed, prompt-elicited policy to sample next candidates. This limits adaptation in practical engineering and research tasks, where evaluations are expensive, and progress depends on learning task-specific search dynamics. We introduce PACEvolve++, an advisor-model reinforcement learning framework for test-time policy adaptation in evolutionary search agents. PACEvolve++ decouples strategic search decisions from implementation: a trainable advisor generates, assesses, and selects hypotheses, while a stronger frontier model translates selected hypotheses into executable candidates. To train the advisor under non-stationary feedback, we propose a phase-adaptive approach that adapts its optimization strategy to different phases of the evolutionary process. Early in evolution, it uses group-relative feedback to learn broad search preferences; later, as reward gaps compress, it emphasizes best-of-$k$ frontier contribution to support stable refinement. Across expert-parallel load balancing, sequential recommendation, and protein fitness extrapolation, PACEvolve++ outperforms the state-of-the-art evolutionary search framework with frontier models, achieving faster convergence and stabilizing test-time training during evolutionary search.

</details>


### [131] [Unlocking High-Fidelity Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra via Dual-Stream Line Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07048)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对质谱分子生成中的原子-键循环依赖难题，DualLGD 模型通过创新的双流（原子/线图空间）耦合架构与关联约束注意力，将生成准确度提升至 SOTA 水平的 3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图扩散模型在处理分子生成时采用单流架构，难以有效捕捉原子环境与键类型之间存在的复杂循环依赖关系，构成了性能提升的架构瓶颈。

Method: 提出 DualLGD (Dual-stream Line Graph Diffusion) 模型，将分子图去噪重构为原子级推理和键级推理（基于线图）两个耦合的子问题，并通过“关联约束双向交叉注意力机制”实现两者的同步。

Result: 在 NPLIB1 和 MassSpecGym 基准测试中，DualLGD 的 Top-1 准确率分别达到 34.37% 和 23.89%，约为现有最先进模型（SOTA）的 3 倍，且无需预训练即可超越此前表现最好的预训练模型。

Conclusion: DualLGD 提出的双流架构有效解决了分子图生成中原子与化学键之间的循环依赖问题，通过显式的双流耦合替代了传统的单流隐式同步，大幅提升了从质谱生成分子的准确性。

Abstract: De novo molecular generation from tandem mass spectra is a challenging inverse problem whose core difficulty lies in the circular dependency between atom-level and bond-level reasoning: determining a bond's type requires knowing its endpoint atoms' chemical environment, yet an atom's environment is in turn defined by its incident bonds. Existing graph diffusion methods process atoms and bonds within a single computation stream, where atom-bond information synchronization can only occur implicitly across layers. We argue that this single-stream paradigm, rather than the choice of any particular aggregation kernel, is a key architectural bottleneck. We propose DualLGD (Dual-stream Line Graph Diffusion), which reformulates molecular graph denoising as the alternating solution of two coupled subproblems: atom-level reasoning and bond-level reasoning, each operating in its own dedicated representation space. The line graph provides a natural mathematical construction for the bond space, in which bond angles, dihedrals, conjugation chains, and rings correspond to local topological motifs between bonds. Incidence-constrained bidirectional cross-attention synchronizes the two streams at every layer, ensuring that each atom attends only to its incident bonds and vice versa, respecting the fundamental chemical principle that an atom's environment is determined by its bonding context. On the NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym benchmarks, DualLGD achieves top-1 accuracy of 34.37\% and 23.89\%, approximately $3\times$ the previous state of the art. Ablation studies confirm the architecture as the primary source of improvement: DualLGD without any pre-training already surpasses the previous best fully pretrained model.

</details>


### [132] [Toeplitz MLP Mixers are Low Complexity, Information-Rich Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06683)
*Benjamin L. Badger,Ethan Roland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TMM的新型Transformer架构，用Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代Attention，实现了计算效率的提升并优化了长序列信息保留能力，在各类基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的注意力机制具有平方级的计算复杂度和内存占用，限制了模型处理长序列的能力。现有的亚线性架构在处理输入信息保留能力上存在不足。

Method: 提出Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM) 架构，利用序列维度的三角掩码Toeplitz矩阵乘法（代替Attention）实现亚线性复杂度，训练时复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(dn \log n)$，推理时为 $\mathcal{O}(dn)$。

Result: TMM在单位计算和内存资源下实现了更低的训练损失，且在信息检索和上下文学习（In-Context Learning）任务中表现优于同类架构。

Conclusion: TMM通过三角掩码Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制，在保证高效训练与推理的同时，能够更好地保留输入信息。此外，从算子索引理论分析得出，即使是因果非可逆模型，其训练后的Toeplitz层往往表现出接近可逆的特性。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models are in some respects limited by the quadratic time and space computational complexity of attention. We introduce the Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM), a transformer-like architecture that swaps attention for triangular-masked Toeplitz matrix multiplication over the sequence dimension resulting in $\mathcal{O} (dn \log n)$ time and $\mathcal O(dn)$ space complexity during training and $\mathcal O(dn)$ time and space at inference prefill. Despite the lack of sophisticated input modulation or state maintenance present in other sub-quadratic architectures, TMMs yield greater training efficiency in terms of loss achieved per compute and device memory. We demonstrate that TMMs are capable of retaining more input information resulting in improved copying ability, which we argue results from a lack of architectural biases. Consistent with higher input information retention, TMMs exhibit superior information retrieval and in-context learning benchmark accuracy compared to comparable architectures. We conclude with an analysis from the perspective of operator index theory and show that, counterintuitively, trained Toeplitz layers of causal non-invertible models are more likely to be invertible or nearly so than models that are actually invertible over their inputs.

</details>


### [133] [Towards Differentially Private Reinforcement Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07049)
*Yi He,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为通用函数逼近下的差分隐私在线强化学习提供了理论保障，实现了 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$ 的最优遗憾界，并修正了既有线性函数逼近研究中的结论差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私在线强化学习研究大多局限于表格设置或特定的线性函数逼近设置，缺乏在更通用的函数逼近设置下的理论保障。

Method: 结合了分批策略更新方案（batched policy update）、指数机制（exponential mechanism）以及一种新颖的遗憾（regret）分析方法。

Result: 在通用函数逼近的无模型设置下，证明了差分隐私强化学习能够达到与线性模型相同的遗憾界，即 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$；同时提出了基于“可覆盖性”（coverability）复杂度度量的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了通用函数逼近下差分隐私在线强化学习的理论空白，不仅给出了性能保证，还澄清并修正了线性函数逼近领域既有研究的不足，完善了该领域的理论框架。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical guarantees for differentially private online reinforcement learning (RL) with general function approximation, extending beyond prior work restricted to tabular and linear settings. Our approach combines a batched policy update scheme with the exponential mechanism, together with a novel regret analysis. We show that, even under general function approximation, the regret in the model-free setting under differential privacy matches the state of the art for the linear case, scaling as $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. As an important by-product, we also establish the first regret bound for online RL with batch update that depends on the standard complexity measure of coverability, complementing existing results based on a newly introduced Eluder-Condition class. In addition, we uncover fundamental gaps in recent results for private RL with linear function approximation, thereby clarifying its landscape.

</details>


### [134] [Dr. Post-Training: A Data Regularization Perspective on LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07063)
*Pingbang Hu,Xueshen Liu,Z. Morley Mao,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM后训练中高质量数据稀缺问题，本文引入Dr. Post-Training框架，将通用数据转化为正则化机制而非单纯筛选对象，通过梯度投影技术有效平衡目标任务性能与过拟合风险，实现了性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLM）后训练通常面临高质量目标数据稀缺与大量非对齐通用数据并存的问题，传统的数据筛选方法局限于从通用数据中挑选题材，未能充分利用其作为正则化手段来防止模型向目标任务过拟合的能力。

Method: 提出Dr. Post-Training框架，核心在于在训练的每一步，利用通用训练数据构建可行模型更新方向集，并将目标数据引导的更新方向投影到该集合中，从而实现对更新过程的正则化。提供了一系列方法来调整偏差-方差权衡，并进行了系统级的性能优化以降低计算开销。

Result: 在多种后训练场景（SFT、RLHF、RLVR）下，该方法在性能上 consistently 优于当前最先进的数据筛选基准，同时经过系统优化，实现了极低的训练额外开销。

Conclusion: 该文提出的Dr. Post-Training框架通过将通用训练数据视为数据引导的正则化项，而非单纯的筛选池，能够更有效地防止模型在稀缺的高质量目标数据上过拟合，在SFT、RLHF和RLVR任务中均优于现有的数据筛选方法，且具有高计算效率。

Abstract: Data selection methods address a critical challenge in LLM post-training: effectively leveraging scarce, high-fidelity target data alongside abundant but imperfectly aligned general training data. In this work, we move beyond the data-selection framing and introduce Dr. Post-Training (Data-Regularized Post-Training), a novel framework that reconceptualizes general training data as a data-induced regularizer that prevents overfitting to the scarce target objective, rather than serving as a pool for selection. Specifically, our framework proposes that at each training step, construct a feasible set of model update directions using the general training data, and project the model update direction specified by the scarce target data onto that feasible set. Standard training and existing data selection methods arise as special cases with different choices of the data-induced regularizer, and these methods correspond to different points on a bias--variance spectrum with different regularization strength. Building on this view, we propose a family of methods offering a richer design space and more flexible bias--variance tradeoffs. For practical LLM-scale use, we introduce careful system optimizations that realize these methods with minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across SFT, RLHF, and RLVR show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art data selection baselines, and system benchmarks confirm their efficiency.

</details>


### [135] [Less Random, More Private: What is the Optimal Subsampling Scheme for DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07072)
*Andy Dong,Ayfer Özgür*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了平衡迭代子采样 (BIS) 取代泊松子采样。通过减少训练中样本的参与方差，BIS 实现了更优的隐私放大效果，并配合新提出的蒙特卡洛会计方案，在实际 DP-SGD 任务中显著提升了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的默认方案“泊松子采样”存在较大的参与方差（即样本在不同迭代中出现的频次波动大），导致隐私放大效果次优，且现有分析方法存在一定的宽松度（slack）。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明了平衡迭代子采样 (BIS) 在噪声极端情况下（σ→0 和 σ→∞）的最优性；2. 隐私核算：引入了一种高精度的近乎精确的蒙特卡洛会计方案，消除了现有 RDP 和 PLD 分析中的理论宽松度；3. 实验验证：在 60 多种 DP-SGD 配置下进行了大规模对比评估。

Result: BIS 通过消除参与方差并保持均匀的边际参与，在低噪声区间（高实用性场景）表现优于泊松子采样，最高可将所需的噪声乘数降低 9.6%。

Conclusion: 该研究推翻了“更多采样随机性总是带来更强隐私放大”的传统直觉，证明了结构化参与（如 BIS）在差分隐私 SGD 中不仅更具实用性，且能提供更优的隐私保护能力。

Abstract: Poisson subsampling is the default sampling scheme in differentially private machine learning, largely because its unstructured randomness yields tractable privacy amplification analyses. Yet this same randomness introduces substantial participation variance: each sample appears in very different numbers of training iterations. In this work, we show that this variance is not merely a practical artifact to be tolerated, but a fundamental source of suboptimal privacy amplification. We prove that Balanced Iteration Subsampling (BIS), a structured scheme in which each sample participates in exactly a fixed number of iterations, achieves stronger privacy amplification than Poisson subsampling and is optimal at both extremes of the noise spectrum ($σ\to 0$ and $σ\to \infty$). Our analysis reveals that the privacy-noise tradeoff is governed not by maximizing randomness, but by eliminating participation variance while preserving uniform marginal participation across iterations. To translate this asymptotic theory into finite-noise guarantees, we introduce a practical near-exact Monte Carlo accountant for BIS, which removes the analytical slack of existing RDP and composition-based PLD analyses. Evaluations across more than 60 practical DP-SGD configurations show that BIS consistently outperforms Poisson subsampling in the low-noise regimes most relevant for high-utility private training, reducing the required noise multiplier by up to $9.6\%$. These results overturn the common intuition that more sampling randomness necessarily yields stronger privacy amplification: in DP-SGD, structured participation can be both more practical and more private. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/dong-xin-ao-andy/bis-mc-accountant.

</details>


### [136] [ModelLens: Finding the Best for Your Task from Myriads of Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07075)
*Rui Cai,Weijie Jacky Mo,Xiaofei Wen,Qiyao Ma,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Muhao Chen,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModelLens 是一款无需在目标数据集上进行昂贵的推理计算，即可通过学习海量公开性能数据实现“野外”模型推荐的统一框架，显著提升了模型选择的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源生态中模型数量激增，但在面对新数据集时，现有的自动机器学习（AutoML）和可迁移性评估方法存在候选池受限、计算成本过高或无法处理“野外（in-the-wild）”场景等局限性，缺乏一种通用且高效的模型推荐机制。

Method: 提出了 ModelLens 框架，其核心在于构建了一个关于“模型-数据集-指标”元组的性能感知潜在空间（Performance-aware Latent Space）；通过利用公开排行榜中分散且嘈杂的性能数据，直接学习模型的能力图谱，从而实现对未知模型在未知数据集上的性能预测与排序。

Result: 在包含 162 万条评估记录（涵盖 4.7 万个模型和 9600 个数据集）的新基准测试中，表现优于仅依赖元数据或需要进行目标端推理的基准模型；在 QA 基准任务中，其推荐的候选池将现有路由方法的性能提升了高达 81%。

Conclusion: ModelLens 通过利用异构的公开性能数据，在无需对目标数据集进行推理的情况下，实现了大规模、高效率的准确模型推荐，并能有效协同各类路由方法以提升性能。

Abstract: The open-source model ecosystem now contains hundreds of thousands of pretrained models, yet picking the best model for a new dataset is increasingly infeasible: new models and unbenchmarked datasets emerge continuously, leaving practitioners with no prior records on either side. Existing approaches handle only fragments of this in-the-wild setting: AutoML and transferability estimation select models from small predefined pools or require expensive per-model forward passes on the target dataset, while model routing presupposes a given candidate pool. We introduce ModelLens, a unified framework for model recommendation in the wild. Our key insight is that public leaderboard interactions, though scattered and noisy, collectively trace out an implicit atlas of model capabilities across heterogeneous evaluation settings, a signal rich enough to learn from directly. By learning a performance-aware latent space over model--dataset--metric tuples, ModelLens ranks unseen models on unseen datasets without running candidates on the target dataset. On a new benchmark of 1.62M evaluation records spanning 47K models and 9.6K datasets, ModelLens surpasses baselines that either rely on metadata alone or require running each candidate on the target dataset. Its recommended Top-K pools further improve multiple representative routing methods by up to 81% across diverse QA benchmarks. Case studies on recently released benchmarks further confirm generalization to both text and vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [137] [Test-Time Compositional Generalization in Diffusion Models via Concept Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07078)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Tianyi Zhu,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无需预定义概念库的测试时组合生成方法，利用预训练扩散模型的时序得分几何特性，通过梯度上升自动发现局部密度模式并重组，在多个组合生成任务中取得了优于现有基准的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型组合生成方法通常假设相关的概念或条件信号是预先给定的，无法处理未知的、查询特定的概念组合。本研究旨在探索预训练扩散模型是否能从其学习的含噪边际分布的时序得分中自动发现并组合查询特定的概念。

Method: 提出了一种无需预定义概念库的测试时组合方法：首先通过在多个噪声步长上对得分函数进行梯度上升恢复局部密度模式；随后将这些模式映射为干净空间的高斯分布；利用子模似然目标函数贪婪选择相关原型；最后将其组合为乘积专家（PoE）模型，通过分类器无引导采样或微调LoRA适配器进行生成。

Result: 在ColorMNIST和CelebA的组合基准测试中，所提出的解析PoE采样器和微调后的低秩适配器模型在性能上均显著优于基于“查询仅限”和“最近训练类别”的基准方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的时序得分几何结构中蕴含了可复用的概念模式，证实了在无需预定义概念库的情况下，模型能够支持测试时的组合生成。

Abstract: Compositional generalization requires models to produce novel configurations from familiar parts. In diffusion models, prior compositional generation methods typically assume that the relevant concepts or conditioning signals are already available. We instead ask whether a pretrained diffusion model can discover query-specific concepts from the time-indexed scores it learns for the noisy marginals $p_t(x_t)$ and compose them at test time. Given a single out-of-distribution query, our method performs gradient ascent on $s_θ(x_t,t) \approx \nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)$ at multiple noising timesteps to recover local density modes, maps these modes into clean-space Gaussians, greedily selects relevant prototypes with a submodular likelihood objective, and combines them into a product-of-experts (PoE) teacher model with an analytic score. This teacher model can be sampled directly through classifier-free guidance or used to generate a sample pool for training a new class embedding and low-rank adapter. On held-out composition benchmarks built from ColorMNIST and CelebA, both the analytic PoE sampler and the low-rank adapted model outperform query-only and nearest trained-class baselines. These results suggest that the time-indexed score geometry of the diffusion model contains reusable density-mode concepts that support test-time compositional generation without a predefined concept library.

</details>


### [138] [Actor-Critic with Active Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07094)
*Majid Molaei,Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AISAC 是一种新型 Actor-Critic 算法，通过自适应构建更优的行为策略来降低梯度估计方差，从而显著提升了强化学习算法的样本效率与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Actor-Critic 方法在策略梯度估计中存在高方差问题，导致学习过程不稳定、样本效率低下。传统方法缺乏对数据收集分布的有效优化。

Method: 提出了一种结合重要性采样（Importance Sampling）的 Actor-Critic 扩展算法。通过最小化梯度方差来优化行为策略（Behavior Policy），在确保梯度估计无偏的前提下，使数据收集分布更接近目标策略梯度。针对连续动作空间，利用交叉熵最小化技术优化高斯行为策略。

Result: 在 Inverted Pendulum 和 Half Cheetah 任务上的实验证明，该方法相比标准 Actor-Critic 方法表现更优，不仅改善了目标策略的更新效率，还提高了评价器（Critic）的估计精度。

Conclusion: AISAC 通过优化行为策略成功降低了策略梯度估计的方差，提升了强化学习在连续动作空间中的收敛速度、样本效率及训练稳定性，为复杂环境下的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Active-Importance-Sampling Actor-Critic (AISAC) algorithm, an extension of the Actor-Critic framework for reducing variance in policy gradient estimation. AISAC optimizes the behavior policy to minimize gradient variance while preserving unbiased gradient estimates. Using importance sampling principles, the algorithm adapts the behavior policy toward efficient data collection distributions aligned with target policy gradients.
  For continuous action spaces, AISAC employs Gaussian behavior policies optimized through cross-entropy minimization. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating variance reduction and unbiasedness. Experiments on Inverted Pendulum and Half Cheetah tasks show improved learning speed, sample efficiency, and training stability compared to standard Actor-Critic methods.
  Results indicate that optimizing the behavior policy improves both target policy updates and critic estimation accuracy across different hyperparameter settings. AISAC accelerates convergence and stabilizes reinforcement learning training, making it promising for real-world applications. Future work includes integration with advanced algorithms such as Soft Actor-Critic and TD3 for more complex environments.

</details>


### [139] [Query-efficient model evaluation using cached responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07096)
*Hayden Helm,Ben Johnson,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DKPS的方法，通过利用已缓存的其他模型响应来高效预测新模型在基准测试上的表现，减少了大规模评估所需的查询开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模基准评估中，对所有查询进行生成和测评的计算成本过高。利用已评估模型的缓存响应信息来减少新模型评估所需的查询量，具有重要的实际意义。

Method: 引入“数据核透视空间”(Data Kernel Perspective Space, DKPS) 方法，在黑盒环境下量化模型间的关系，并结合已缓存的响应数据来预测新模型的性能；同时提出一种离线方法来选择最优查询集，以最大化参考模型的拟合优度。

Result: 理论上证明了基于DKPS的方法在特定条件下具备查询高效性；实证结果表明，该方法能在显著降低查询预算的情况下，达到与基准方法相同的平均绝对误差(MAE)，且优于随机查询选择。

Conclusion: 提出了基于DKPS的能够有效利用缓存模型响应来预测基准测试性能的方法，并设计了一种离线查询选择策略，在降低查询成本的同时保持了较高的预测精度。

Abstract: Evaluating a new model on an existing benchmark is often necessary to understand its behavior before deployment. For modern evaluation frameworks, generating and evaluating a response for all queries can be prohibitively expensive. In practice, responses from previously-evaluated models are often cached -- creating a potential opportunity to use this additional information to decrease the number of queries required to accurately evaluate a new model. In this paper, we introduce an approach for predicting benchmark performance that leverages cached model responses based on the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a method for quantifying the relationship between models in the black-box setting. Theoretically, we show that DKPS-based methods are query-efficient under certain conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate that DKPS-based methods achieve the same mean absolute error as baselines with a substantially decreased query budget. We conclude by proposing an offline method for selecting a set of queries that maximizes the goodness-of-fit on reference models, improving prediction accuracy over random query selection.

</details>


### [140] [CarCrashNet: A Large-Scale Dataset and Hierarchical Neural Solver for Data-Driven Structural Crash Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07098)
*Mohamed Elrefaie,Dule Shu,Matthew Klenk,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CarCrashNet，这是一个包含大规模、高保真组件级和全车碰撞仿真的开源基准数据集，并引入了基于AI的碰撞预测求解器CrashSolver，旨在解决汽车工业中结构碰撞模拟的计算复杂性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆开发高度依赖碰撞仿真以减少物理原型成本并加速设计优化，但现有的结构碰撞模拟面临高度非线性（接触、变形、塑性、失效等）的计算难题，且缺乏高质量的公开基准数据集。

Method: 1. 构建开源数据集CarCrashNet，包含超过14,000个组件级碰撞仿真和825个全车碰撞仿真（基于OpenRadioss工作流）；2. 建立针对全车碰撞预测的深度学习模型CrashSolver；3. 将该数据集与商业仿真软件（Ansys LS-DYNA）及实验数据进行验证对比，并利用几何深度学习和Transformer架构对CrashSolver进行评估。

Result: 成功开发了CarCrashNet基准数据集，验证了其与商业软件的一致性；CrashSolver模型在碰撞预测任务中表现优异，优于多种现有的SOTA神经求解器，为机器学习解决高分辨率有限元碰撞模拟提供了范例。

Conclusion: CarCrashNet为结构动力学仿真、耐撞性建模及AI辅助虚拟碰撞测试提供了重要的研究基础，通过开源基准推动了该领域的复现性与数据驱动研究。

Abstract: Crash simulation is a cornerstone of modern vehicle development because it reduces the need for costly physical prototypes, accelerates safety-driven design iteration, and increasingly supports virtual testing workflows. At the same time, modeling structural crash mechanics remains exceptionally challenging: the response is governed by nonlinear contact, large deformation, material plasticity, failure, and complex multi-body interactions evolving over space and time on high-resolution finite-element meshes. In this work, we introduce \textsc{CarCrashNet}, a public high-fidelity open-source benchmark for data-driven structural crash simulation. \textsc{CarCrashNet} combines component-scale and full-vehicle simulations in a multi-modal format, including more than 14{,}000 bumper-beam pole-impact simulations with varying geometry, materials, and boundary conditions, together with 825 full-vehicle crash simulations built from three industry-standard vehicle models of increasing structural complexity: Dodge Neon, Toyota Yaris, and Chevrolet Silverado. To establish the reliability of the benchmark, we validate our open-source finite-element workflow based on OpenRadioss against both experimental crash data and the commercial solver Ansys LS-DYNA. We also introduce \textsc{CrashSolver}, a machine-learning model designed for full-vehicle crash prediction from high-resolution finite-element crash data. We further perform extensive benchmarking across the released datasets and evaluate \textsc{CrashSolver} against state-of-the-art geometric deep learning and transformer-based neural solvers. Our results position \textsc{CarCrashNet} as a foundation for reproducible research in structural simulation, crashworthiness modeling, and AI-driven virtual crash testing. The dataset is available at https://github.com/Mohamedelrefaie/CarCrashNet.

</details>


### [141] [Almost Sure Convergence Rates of Stochastic Approximation and Reinforcement Learning via a Poisson-Moreau Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07104)
*Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究为马尔可夫噪声环境下的强化学习算法（如Q学习）提供了几乎处处收敛速率的界，通过创新的李雅普诺夫分析技术，将收敛速率提升至接近最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫噪声干扰下，为随机逼近及强化学习算法建立几乎处处的收敛速率是核心理论挑战，现有研究在这一方向上尚显不足。

Method: 使用基于泊松方程（Poisson-equation）的马尔可夫噪声修正，结合莫罗包络（Moreau-envelope）平滑技术，构造了一种新的李雅普诺夫漂移（Lyapunov drift）分析方法。

Result: 对于步长为 $O(n^{-\eta})$ ($\eta \in (1/2, 1)$) 的幂律学习率，获得了接近 $o(n^{1-2\eta})$ 的几乎处处收敛速率；对于 $O(n^{-1})$ 的调和学习率，获得了接近 $o(n^{-1})$ 的收敛速率，这已非常接近最优的迭代对数律速率。

Conclusion: 针对具有收缩性质的随机逼近算法（如Q学习和线性TD学习），在马尔可夫噪声环境下，该研究成功建立了近乎最优的几乎处处（almost sure）收敛速率，弥补了该领域理论分析的空白。

Abstract: Establishing almost sure convergence rates for stochastic approximation and reinforcement learning under Markovian noise is a fundamental theoretical challenge. We make progress towards this challenge for a class of stochastic approximation algorithms whose expected updates are contractive, a setting that arises in many reinforcement learning algorithms such as $Q$-learning and linear temporal difference learning. Specifically, for a power-law learning rate $O(n^{-η})$ with $η\in (1/2, 1)$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{1 - 2η})$. For a harmonic learning rate $O(n^{-1})$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{-1})$, which we argue is a strong result because it is close to the optimal rate $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$ given by the law of the iterated logarithm (for a special case of i.i.d. noise). Key to our analysis is a novel Lyapunov drift construction that applies a Poisson-equation based correction for Markovian noise to the well-established Moreau-envelope smoothing for the contractive mapping.

</details>


### [142] [Why DDIM Hallucinates More than DDPM: A Theoretical Analysis of Reverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06831)
*Muhammad H. Ashiq,Samanyu Arora,Abhinav N. Harish,Ishaan Kharbanda,Hung Yun Tseng,Grigorios G. Chrysos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过理论与实验证明，扩散模型中的随机性（如DDPM）比确定性采样（如DDIM）更能有效避免生成过程中的幻觉现象，并提出在DDIM中加入随机步骤可缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探究扩散模型（DDPM与DDIM）中产生的“幻觉”（hallucination）现象，特别是分析确定性采样与随机性采样在处理多模态分布时表现差异的理论根源。

Method: 通过理论分析高斯混合分布下的反向ODE（针对DDIM）和SDE（针对DDPM）动力学，并结合实验验证。

Result: 理论证明了DDIM在临界时间点后容易陷入模态间的伪影区域，而DDPM凭借随机性能够克服这一问题；实验数据证实DDPM的幻觉率显著低于DDIM。

Conclusion: DDPM的随机性有助于模型跳出伪影区域，避开幻觉；通过在DDIM采样中引入额外的随机步骤，可以显著降低其产生的幻觉现象。

Abstract: We theoretically study the hallucination phenomena in two canonical diffusion samplers: the stochastic Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) and the deterministic Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM). We analyze the reverse ODE (DDIM) and SDE (DDPM) for a Gaussian mixture target, proving that after a critical time $τ$, (a) DDIM can become stuck on the segment connecting the two nearest modes and (b) DDPM *stochasticity* helps it become unstuck from this region, thus avoiding hallucination. Our empirical validation verifies that DDPM has a significantly lower hallucination rate than DDIM when this region is entered. Building on our observations, we exhibit how using additional stochastic steps can help DDIM avoid hallucinations and offer new insights on how to design improved samplers.

</details>


### [143] [Where to Spend Rollouts: Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07114)
*Tao Wang,Shuo Li,Yan Sun,Dongsheng Ding,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型强化学习中固定 rollout 分配效率低的问题，作者提出了一种名为 HORA 的自适应采样分配算法，通过动态分配计算量提升了模型在推理任务中的 Pass@K 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组策略优化（如 GRPO）对所有提示词（prompt）采用固定的采样配额，导致计算资源分配效率低下：容易给已经饱和（已找到正确解）的任务分配过多计算量，同时对困难任务分配不足。

Method: 提出了“命中效用”（Hit Utility）度量，即增加采样次数后至少获得一个正确轨迹的后验概率；开发了 HORA（Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation）策略，在每一批次内根据命中效用动态调整采样预算，且无需更改下游奖励评估和优势估计器。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准测试和三种模型规模上，与计算量相当的 GRPO 相比，HORA 在 12 种配置中的 10 种提升了 Pass@K，且在 Pass@1 上保持了相当的性能；该方法同时具备“即插即用”特性，兼容 RLOO 等其他基于组的估计器。

Conclusion: HORA 是一种高效的、无需学习的采样分配策略。通过引入“命中效用”概念，它实现了比标准固定分配（如 GRPO）更优的计算资源分配，在保持推理精度的同时提升了 Pass@K 指标。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Group-based policy optimization methods, such as GRPO, typically allocate a fixed number of rollouts to every prompt. This uniform allocation can be inefficient: it over-allocates compute to prompts whose sampled groups are already saturated while under-exploring prompts for which additional samples may reveal useful correct trajectories. To address this limitation, we introduce hit utility, the posterior probability that at least one rollout in a proposed additional allocation for a prompt will be correct. Building on this notion, we propose Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation (HORA), a learning-free rollout allocation policy that maximizes total posterior hit utility within each allocation batch. HORA adaptively reallocates rollout budgets while leaving the downstream reward evaluation and group-based advantage estimator unchanged. Across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model scales, HORA preserves comparable Pass@1 and improves Pass@K over compute-matched GRPO in ten of twelve model--benchmark configurations, with one tie and one saturated exception. It is also drop-in compatible with other group-based estimators such as RLOO. Ablation studies indicate that the uniform prior used by HORA is competitive with five prompt-conditioned learned-prior alternatives.

</details>


### [144] [RateQuant: Optimal Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization via Rate-Distortion Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06675)
*Fei Zuo,Zikang Zhou,Hao Cong,Xiaoyan Xi,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型 KV Cache 量化，RateQuant 通过校准失真模型并运用反向注水算法实现最优比特分配，解决了混合精度量化中的性能衰退问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 KV Cache 内存瓶颈。现有量化方法忽略了注意力头之间重要性的差异，单纯采用混合精度分配却因忽略不同量化器间失真曲线差异而导致“失真模型失配（distortion model mismatch）”，反而降低模型性能。

Method: 提出 RateQuant 方法：首先通过小型校准集拟合特定量化器的失真模型（D(b)=α*β^{-b}），随后利用率失真理论中的“反向注水算法（reverse waterfilling）”以闭式解形式计算各注意力头的最优比特分配。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 模型（平均 2.5 bits）上，相较于 KIVI，困惑度从 49.3 显著降至 14.9，相较于 QuaRot 提升了 6.6 PPL；校准仅需 1.6 秒，推理阶段零额外开销。

Conclusion: RateQuant 成功解决了混合精度量化中常见的“失真模型失配”问题，通过基于少量校准集的闭式解分配策略，在不增加推理开销的前提下，显著提升了 KV Cache 量化的性能。

Abstract: Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving. Quantizing the KV cache to fewer bits reduces this cost, yet all current quantizers assign the same bit-width to every attention head, ignoring the large variation in head importance. A natural idea is to allocate more bits to important heads and fewer to the rest. We show, however, that such mixed-precision allocation has a hidden pitfall: each quantizer follows a different distortion curve D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}, and the decay rate beta varies from 3.6 to 5.3 across quantizer designs. Applying one quantizer's distortion model to another inverts the allocation order and makes performance worse than uniform quantization. We call this failure mode distortion model mismatch and propose RateQuant to resolve it. RateQuant fits a per-quantizer distortion model from a small calibration set, then solves the resulting bit-allocation problem in closed form via reverse waterfilling from rate-distortion theory. On Qwen3-8B at 2.5 average bits, calibrated RateQuant reduces KIVI's perplexity from 49.3 to 14.9 (70% reduction) and improves QuaRot by 6.6 PPL. The entire calibration takes 1.6 s on a single GPU and adds zero overhead at inference time.

</details>


### [145] [LKV: End-to-End Learning of Head-wise Budgets and Token Selection for LLM KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06676)
*Enshuai Zhou,Yifan Hao,Chao Wang,Rui Zhang,Di Huang,Jiaming Guo,Xing Hu,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LKV 框架，通过可微优化学习 KV 缓存的压缩预算和重要性评估，取代了传统的启发式方法，在极高压缩比下实现了长上下文推理的高保真表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 长上下文推理受到 KV 缓存线性增长的限制；现有的启发式缓存压缩方法（如依赖统计先验或注意力汇聚）存在资源分配不均和启发式偏差问题。

Method: 提出 LKV (Learned KV Eviction) 框架：1) LKV-H：通过端到端可微优化学习任务特定的全局分配预算；2) LKV-T：无需计算注意力矩阵即可评估 KV 的内在重要性。该方法替代了启发式压缩策略，直接对齐任务目标。

Result: 在 LongBench 和 RULER 基准测试中达到最先进水平；在 LongBench 上，仅保留 15% 的 KV 缓存即可实现近乎无损的性能。

Conclusion: 学习型预算分配比启发式方法更能有效提升 KV 缓存压缩后的模型性能，证明了数据驱动的分配策略是突破当前长上下文推理瓶颈的关键。

Abstract: Long-context inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is bottlenecked by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory. Existing KV cache compression paradigms are fundamentally limited by heuristics: heuristic budgeting relies on statistical priors rather than task objectives, causing resource misallocation, while heuristic selection relies on coupled query-key interactions or static inductive biases (e.g., attention sinks). To address this limitation, we introduce LKV (Learned KV Eviction), which formulates KV compression as an end-to-end differentiable optimization problem. LKV integrates LKV-H to learn task-optimized global budgets, and LKV-T to derive intrinsic KV importance without materializing attention matrices. This design bypasses heuristic proxies, strictly aligning compression with task objectives. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that LKV achieves state-of-the-art performance on both LongBench and RULER benchmarks at high compression rates. In particular, on LongBench, LKV achieves near-lossless performance with only 15\% KV cache retention. Crucially, our analysis identifies learned budgeting as the dominant driver of fidelity, demonstrating that data-driven allocation is essential to overcome the limitations of hand-crafted heuristics.

</details>


### [146] [Convergence and Emergence of In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07123)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上揭示了思维链（CoT）如何增强上下文强化学习（ICRL），证明了CoT生成本质上是梯度更新过程（TD学习），并解释了模型为何能在预训练中自动习得相关能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经验研究表明思维链（CoT）能增强上下文强化学习（ICRL）能力，但目前缺乏对其背后机制的理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，探讨CoT如何促进ICRL。

Method: 在线性Transformer的策略评估框架下进行理论分析；通过数学证明展示CoT生成与TD学习的等价性；提供有限样本的收敛性分析，阐述误差随CoT长度的几何级下降趋势。

Result: 证明了随着CoT长度增加，策略评估误差呈现几何级数下降，最终收敛至受上下文长度限制的统计底限；同时验证了模型在预训练阶段能够自发学习到实现上述功能的特定Transformer参数。

Conclusion: 针对线性Transformer，本文证明了CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时间差分（TD）学习更新，并证明了相关参数是预训练损失的全局最小值，从而在理论上解释了利用CoT增强上下文强化学习（ICRL）现象的产生机制。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) refers to the ability of RL agents to adapt to new tasks at inference time without parameter updates by conditioning on additional context. Recent empirical studies further demonstrate that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation can amplify this ICRL capability. This paper is the first to provide a theoretical understanding on how CoT interacts with ICRL. We conduct our analysis in a policy evaluation setup with linear Transformer. We prove that with specific Transformer parameters, the CoT generation process is equivalent to repeatedly executing temporal difference learning updates. Additionally, we provide finite sample convergence analysis showing that the policy evaluation error decreases geometrically with CoT length and eventually saturates at a statistical floor determined by the context length. We also prove that the desired Transformer parameters are a global minimizer of the pretraining loss, providing a theoretical understanding on the empirical emergence of those parameters.

</details>


### [147] [The Position Curse: LLMs Struggle to Locate the Last Few Items in a List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07127)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Hua-Dong Xiong,Robert C. Wilson,Mikio Aoi,Marcelo G. Mattar,Li Ji-An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大模型存在“位置诅咒”现象，即在短列表中检索靠近末尾的信息时准确率极低（尤其是反向检索）。研究通过构造PosBench数据集进行微调，证实了该能力可提升但难以完全解决，强调了提高精确索引能力对AI编码任务的关键性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理长上下文（“大海捞针”）时表现良好，但在处理较短列表的末尾项检索时却表现异常，作者旨在揭示并量化这一普遍存在的“位置诅咒”现象，以解决大模型在代码理解与编辑中位置检索精度不足的问题。

Method: 1. 提出“位置诅咒”（Position Curse）的概念，指出LLM在检索列表最后位置信息时的失效现象。2. 构建了两类评估查询：给定位置找内容，以及给定内容找位置（涉及正向/反向偏移）。3. 开发了PosBench数据集，并利用LoRA对模型进行微调，随后在PyIndex基准测试中验证泛化能力。

Result: 1. 证实了“位置诅咒”广泛存在于开源及闭源模型中，且反向检索能力显著弱于正向检索。2. LoRA微调显著提升了模型的正向和反向检索能力，并在PyIndex基准上表现出良好的泛化性，但绝对性能仍未达到理想水平。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在位置检索（尤其是反向检索）能力上存在显著缺陷，即“位置诅咒”（Position Curse）。尽管微调能有所改善，但仍未达到饱和，且在长代码库分析等实际应用中，这种精确索引能力至关重要，未来需在预训练目标和模型设计上予以针对性改进。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can find a needle in a haystack (locating a single relevant fact buried among hundreds of thousands of irrelevant tokens) with near-saturated accuracy, yet fail to retrieve the last few items in a short list. We call this failure the Position Curse. For instance, even in a two-line code snippet, Claude Opus 4.6 misidentifies the second-to-last line most of the time. To characterize this failure, we evaluated two complementary queries: given a position in a sequence (of letters or words), retrieve the corresponding item; and given an item, return its position. Each position is specified as a forward or backward offset from an anchor, either an endpoint of the list (its start or end) or another item in the list. Across both open-source and frontier closed-source models, backward retrieval substantially lags forward retrieval. To test whether this capability can be rescued by post-training, we constructed PosBench, a position-focused training dataset. LoRA fine-tuning improves both forward and backward retrieval and generalizes to a held-out code-understanding benchmark (PyIndex), yet absolute performance remains far from saturated. As LLM coding agents increasingly operate over large codebases where precise indexing becomes essential for code understanding and editing, position-based retrieval emerges as a key capability for future pretraining objectives and model design.

</details>


### [148] [GAD in the Wild: Benchmarking Graph Anomaly Detection under Realistic Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07133)
*Jingjing Zhou,Shiyu Huang,Qing Qing,Zuquan Yuan,Huafei Huang,Ziqi Xu,Mingliang Hou,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有图异常检测（GAD）基准测试与实际部署脱节的问题，提出了一套全新的多维度基准测试，揭示了主流模型在处理百万级规模数据、高稀疏性异常及缺失属性时的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAD基准测试多局限于小规模、数据清洗完善且异常比例平衡的图，与实际工业应用场景（如欺诈检测）存在巨大脱节。

Method: 构建了一个多维度的GAD基准测试，包含5个不同特性的数据集（含2个百万级规模数据集），并针对百万级规模、极端异常稀疏（0.1%）以及节点属性缺失三种挑战，对9种代表性GAD模型进行了广泛评估。

Result: 发现现有模型存在三大局限：GNN模型扩展性差（显存限制）；在极端异常比例下性能骤降（召回率为零）；重构型模型高度依赖属性补全策略。

Conclusion: 现有学术界GAD模型在处理大规模、稀疏异常及不完整图结构时表现不佳，实验室性能不能代表生产环境的鲁棒性，该基准测试为开发更强健的GAD系统提供了诊断框架。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is a critical task in graph machine learning with vital applications in financial fraud detection and social platform governance. However, existing GAD benchmarks are often restricted to small-scale, curated graphs with relatively balanced anomaly ratios, leaving a substantial gap between academic evaluation and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-dimensional benchmark that systematically evaluates GAD models under three deployment-relevant challenges: million-scale graphs, extreme anomaly scarcity, and missing node attributes. We derive a family of controlled benchmark variants from five diverse graphs, including two native industrial-scale datasets with over 3.7 million nodes. Our extensive evaluation of nine representative GAD models reveals three major limitations: (1) most GNN-based methods fail to scale to million-node graphs due to prohibitive memory requirements; (2) detection performance drops sharply under realistic anomaly ratios (e.g., 0.1\%), often resulting in zero recall; and (3) reconstruction-based models are highly sensitive to attribute imputation strategies. Our findings suggest that strong performance in laboratory settings does not guarantee robustness in production environments. We release this benchmark and empirical evaluation as a diagnostic testbed to promote the development of robust and scalable GAD systems for large-scale, imperfect graphs encountered in practice. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3.

</details>


### [149] [Adaptive Negative Reinforcement for LLM Reasoning:Dynamically Balancing Correction and Diversity in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07137)
*Yash Ingle,Jaival Chauhan,Ankit Yadav,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有NSR方法在大语言模型推理训练中惩罚力度固定、单一的问题，本文提出了自适应调度（A-NSR）和置信度加权（CW-NSR）两种优化策略，提升了训练的稳定性、效率及针对不同类型错误的纠偏能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负样本强化学习（NSR）方法通常采用固定的惩罚机制，且对比所有错误一视同仁，忽略了不同错误阶段和不同置信度错误的差异性。

Method: 1. 提出 A-NSR (Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement)：引入随训练过程动态变化的时间调度函数，初期侧重错误纠正，后期平滑过渡。
2. 提出 CW-NSR (Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement)：根据序列似然度分配惩罚权重，对高置信度的错误施以重罚，对不确定性探索带来的错误则减小惩罚。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构上，于MATH、AIME 2025及AMC23等困难推理数据集上进行了评估，验证了该方法能实现有效的概率重分布并提升推理性能。

Conclusion: A-NSR和CW-NSR通过动态惩罚机制和置信度加权，有效优化了模型在推理任务中的学习过程，在MATH、AIME 2025等数据集上表现出色，并提高了训练稳定性和防止过拟合的能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a highly effective method for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recent research shows that Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) -- which focuses on penalizing incorrect steps rather than simply rewarding correct ones -- can match or even exceed the performance of more complex frameworks like PPO and GRPO across the entire Pass@k spectrum. However, current NSR techniques usually apply a fixed penalty throughout the training process and treat every incorrect response with the same weight.
  To address these limitations, we propose two extensions to the NSR framework: Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement. Rather than using a fixed update rule, A-NSR uses time-dependent scheduling functions. In the initial training phases, the system focuses heavily on correcting errors to stabilize the model. As training continues, it shifts toward more subtle and controlled updates. We also introduce Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, which operates on the principle that different mistakes carry different levels of importance. CW-NSR assigns specific penalty weights based on the model's normalized sequence likelihood. If the model is highly confident in a wrong path, it receives a larger penalty and for uncertain errors -- where the model is effectively exploring -- are penalized less strictly. Our formal analysis shows how these mechanisms govern token-level updates, allowing the model to leverage prior-guided probability redistribution while providing a natural defense against overfitting. We evaluated these methods on difficult reasoning datasets, including MATH, AIME 2025, and AMC23, using the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B architecture.

</details>


### [150] [Regret-Oracle Complexity Tradeoffs in Agnostic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07155)
*Idan Attias,Steve Hanneke,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的不可知在线学习算法，仅利用弱一致性预言机，将查询复杂度从双指数级降低为多项式级，并揭示了查询预算与学习后悔之间的基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的不可知在线学习算法（如SOA）计算上不可行。现有的基于ERM预言机的在线学习算法虽然在后悔上近乎最优，但在预言机查询复杂度上存在双指数级的爆炸（与Littlestone维数深度相关），限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种自适应且动态的“不一致到一致”约简算法。该算法利用VC维（$d_\mathrm{VC}$）来限制动态维护的活动路径，并利用弱一致性预言机（判断数据集是否可实现）作为基础接口，对不可实现的标签序列进行在线裁剪。

Result: 将查询复杂度优化至 $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$；分析了后悔与查询复杂度之间的权衡，证明了若查询次数限制为 $o(\sqrt{T})$，则期望后悔至少为 $\Omega(T/Q)$。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入一种基于弱一致性预言机的自适应、动态约简方法，显著降低了不可知在线学习的预言机查询复杂度（从双指数级降至多项式级 $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$），同时保持了近最优的期望后悔，并确立了后悔与查询复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Agnostic online learning is classically solved via a reduction to the realizable setting, utilizing Littlestone's Standard Optimal Algorithm (SOA) as a base learner. However, the SOA is computationally intractable to execute even for a single round. To overcome this barrier, recent work in oracle-efficient online learning replaces the SOA with a realizable base learner that accesses the concept class exclusively through an offline empirical risk minimization (ERM) oracle. While such agnostic learners achieve near-optimal expected regret, they suffer from a doubly-exponential oracle complexity of $O\big(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}}\big)$, where $d_\mathrm{LD}$ is the Littlestone dimension and $T$ is the number of rounds. In this work, we significantly improve this oracle complexity while relying on an even weaker primitive: a weak-consistency oracle, which merely decides whether a given labeled dataset is realizable. At the core of our approach is an adaptive and dynamic agnostic-to-realizable reduction that actively prunes non-realizable label sequences on the fly. By using the VC dimension ($d_\mathrm{VC}$) to bound the number of dynamically maintained active paths, our algorithm reduces the total query complexity down to $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$ while perfectly preserving near-optimal expected regret. Crucially, this dynamic pruning also yields a memory reduction over the standard reduction. Furthermore, we formally quantify the regret--oracle complexity tradeoff, providing upper bounds that smoothly interpolate between restricted query budgets and attainable expected regret. We complement these with lower bounds proving that any learner restricted to $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ queries must suffer an expected regret of $Ω(T/Q)$.

</details>


### [151] [Learned Lagrangian Models of PDEs via Euler-Lagrange Residual Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07157)
*Lyra Zhornyak,Eric Forgoston,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用学习到的拉格朗日量预测PDE动力学的新方法，通过局部时空优化代替传统时间步进，实现了长期稳定预测、线性扩展性及强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏微分方程的学习模型在进行长期动力学预测时面临稳定性差、误差积累的问题，且传统的时间步进积分方法存在全局耦合带来的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种基于优化的积分器，通过在局部时空块上进行无网格、近辛构造，最小化平方欧拉-拉格朗日残差，并利用雅可比迭代实现线性扩展。

Result: 在双摆、一维和二维波动方程的实验中，该方法实现了与经典辛积分器相当的误差水平，且能够推广到空间变化的动力学和任意边界条件，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了利用学习到的连续拉格朗日量对偏微分方程系统进行长期稳定的动力学预测，在保持与经典辛积分器相当精度的同时，具备更好的泛化能力，且无需结构约束。

Abstract: We present the first method to directly use a learned continuous Lagrangian to forecast the dynamics of systems governed by partial differential equations, exploiting the inherent conservative structure to achieve stable long-range predictions. We develop an optimization-based integrator that minimizes the squared Euler--Lagrange residual via a mesh-free near-symplectic construction on local space-time patches. Different from integrators for analytical models, integrators for learned models should decouple model error (phase error) from integration error (conservation error). By relying on optimization rather than time-stepping, we bypass the global coupling inherent to fixed discretizations, which slows time- and space-stepping and complicates learning. Our method scales linearly with domain size via Jacobi iteration, and places no structural requirements on the learned network, allowing it to be coupled with existing physics-guided machine learning (ML) methods. We validate our approach on a learned representation of a double pendulum, a one-dimensional wave equation, and a two-dimensional wave equation. Our method achieves error comparable to classical symplectic methods while generalizing to spatially varying dynamics and arbitrary boundary conditions without retraining.

</details>


### [152] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有成本约束的多臂赌博机问题（MAB-CS），推导了新的遗憾下界，并提出了COF算法，在理论上实现了更紧的上界，且在实证中展现了更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MAB侧重于最大化奖励，但在实际应用中更强调在满足最低奖励约束的前提下最小化成本，现有研究对这种约束条件下的遗憾界分析仍有提升空间。

Method: 针对带有成本补贴的多臂赌博机问题（MAB-CS），推导了基于实例的遗憾下界，并提出了“成本排序可行性”（COF）算法，该算法通过整合来自所有臂的样本来评估低成本臂的可行性。

Result: 理论上证明了COF算法关于累积成本和质量遗憾的实例依赖上界，在MovieLens、Goodreads及合成数据集上的实验结果表明，COF在性能上优于现有基准算法。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析与实证验证，COF算法在处理基于成本约束的多臂赌博机问题时，在理论上达到了更优的遗憾上界，在实践中表现出比现有基准更好的性能。

Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

</details>


### [153] [Coupling Models for One-Step Discrete Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07193)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Avishek Joey Bose,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 耦合模型（Coupling Models）通过学习离散数据与高斯潜变量间的直接映射，实现了离散结构的高效单步生成，在文本和生物序列等多任务中显著超越了现有的强基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散结构生成（如生物序列、代码、语言模型）主要依赖于低效的自回归解码或多步迭代优化，缺乏高效的单步生成方案。

Method: 引入“耦合模型”（Coupling Models），该模型通过学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的直接耦合关系，训练一个专门的解码器，从而摆脱了传统的自回归解码或迭代优化，实现了单步生成。

Result: 在LM1B文本生成任务中困惑度降低了33%，Fly Brain增强子设计性能提升18%，MNIST-Binary的FID指标提升46%，各项指标均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 离散数据与噪声之间的耦合方式（coupling）是实现高效一步式离散生成的关键核心，耦合模型提供了一种比现有蒸馏方法更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Generative modeling over discrete structures underpins applications across deep learning, from biological sequence design and code generation to large language models, yet generation often remains sequential, relying on autoregressive decoding or iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce Coupling Models(Coupling Models), a one-step discrete generative model that learns a direct coupling between discrete sequences and Gaussian latents. Unlike recent distillation methods that compress a pretrained multi-step sampler into a few steps, Coupling Model trains a purpose-built decoder to invert this coupling and generate samples in a single step. The model also avoids complex continuous flows over the simplex and hand-specified data-to-noise couplings. Empirically,Coupling Model improves the strongest one-step baselines in each domain: it reduces LM1B text-generation perplexity by 33% at its lowest-perplexity operating point, Fly Brain enhancer-design FBD by 18%, and MNIST-Binary FID by 46%. These results suggest that effective one-step discrete generation depends strongly on how data and noise are coupled before decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Coupling-Models.

</details>


### [154] [Arrow: A Foundation Model for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07204)
*Ryan Thompson,He Zhao,Daniel M. Steinberg,Edwin V. Bonilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Arrow 是一个用于因果发现的 Transformer 基础模型，通过将 DAG 分解为骨架和拓扑排序来实现零样本学习，在保持高精度和低推理成本的同时，解决了大规模因果发现的高效性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统因果发现方法在处理新数据集时计算成本高、通用性差的问题，旨在通过预训练模型实现零样本因果推断。

Method: 提出 Arrow 模型，采用 Transformer 架构。通过将 DAG 分解为无向骨架（skeleton）和拓扑排序（topological order）来保证无环性。模型通过监督学习在由多种图结构、函数形式和噪声模型组成的合成数据集上进行训练，并使用端到端可微的有向边复合似然函数进行优化。

Result: 在多种分布内（ID）和分布外（OOD）的合成、半合成及真实数据集上，Arrow 达到或超过了现有因果发现方法的性能，且推理成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 大规模的合成数据预训练可以产生零样本因果发现模型，这些模型在各种数据集上表现出高效、准确且具有可重用性的特点。

Abstract: We introduce Arrow, a foundation model for zero-shot causal discovery on observational tabular data. Arrow factorizes a directed acyclic graph into an undirected skeleton and a topological order, guaranteeing acyclicity by construction. Given a new dataset, it uses a transformer-based architecture to contextualize variables within and across observations, then predicts skeleton edge probabilities and node order scores that together define a graph. Arrow is trained in a supervised fashion on synthetic datasets with ground-truth graphs, using an end-to-end differentiable directed edge composite likelihood induced by the skeleton-order factorization. The training distribution spans diverse graph families, functional forms, noise models, and dataset shapes. Across in- and out-of-distribution synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real datasets, Arrow matches or outperforms existing causal discovery methods at substantially lower inference cost than competitive alternatives.
