This work targets the simultaneous estimation of multiple subject-specific motor unit parameters from electromyography (EMG) recordings measured non-invasively at the skin surface. This results in an inverse problem with a nonlinear loss function. To address this problem, an informed autoencoder is developed. This autoencoder reconstructs the surface EMG recordings while learning the parameters in its latent space and adhering to physical laws that relate the parameters to the EMG signals. In experiments on synthetic data, innervation zone centres are estimated with a mean absolute error of 2.5989 $\mathrm{mm}$, and conduction velocities of the electric potential are estimated with a mean absolute error of 0.1697 $\mathrm{m}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate the plausibility of this novel approach, which enables the simultaneous estimation of several motor unit parameters while reducing manual modelling effort through the integration of data-driven machine learning.

</details>


### [192] [Physical Simulators as Do-Operators: Causal Discovery under Latent Confounders for AI-for-Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07467)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CFM-SD 是一种结合物理模拟器的因果发现框架，解决了科学领域中潜在混淆因素带来的识别难题，显著提升了因果结构发现的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如 IGSP, DCDI, ENCO）假设因果充分性且依赖虚拟干预，无法应对 AI-for-Science 场景中普遍存在的潜在混淆因素及高昂的真实实验/模拟成本。

Method: 提出 CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data)，利用第一性原理物理模拟器作为 Pearl 因果演算中的 do-算子，能够同步处理潜在混淆因素和真实干预数据。

Result: 在合成数据基准上取得最优表现（F1=0.800，远超现有基准）；在分子毒性预测和电池电解液优化等真实科学任务中，实现了 57-58% 的偏差降低。

Conclusion: CFM-SD 能够有效处理具有潜在混淆因素的科学场景，在理论上达到了 $O(d)$ 最小干预识别复杂度，并在实际科学应用（分子设计、电池电解液优化）中展现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Existing interventional causal discovery methods -- IGSP, DCDI, ENCO -- assume causal sufficiency (no latent confounders) and rely on virtual interventions in synthetic simulators. In AI-for-Science settings such as molecular design and materials science, latent confounders are ubiquitous and real interventions (e.g., physics-based simulations) require hours to days per data point. We propose CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data), which uses first-principles physical simulators as do-operators in Pearl's interventional calculus to simultaneously handle latent confounders and real interventional data. Theoretically, $d$-variable causal structure is identifiable with $O(d)$ single-variable interventions -- the minimum under physical realizability constraints. In Intrinsic Evaluation on synthetic data ($γ=0.2$--$0.8$), CFM-SD achieves average F1$=0.800$ vs. F1$=0.127$--$0.562$ for all baselines. In Extrinsic Evaluation on real scientific data, CFM-SD achieves 57--58\% bias reduction in molecular toxicity prediction and battery electrolyte optimization, demonstrating practical value beyond synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [193] [NPMixer: Hierarchical Neighboring Patch Mixing for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07476)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NPMixer 是一款通过自适应小波分解与层次化 MLP 混合建模实现多尺度时序依赖捕捉的多元时间序列预测架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多元时间序列预测难以同时兼顾复杂的局部时序动态特性与跨变量的全局依赖关系。

Method: 提出了 NPMixer 模型，主要包含三个核心组件：(1) 可学习平稳小波变换 (Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform)，自适应分解趋势与细节成分；(2) 相邻混合块 (Neighboring Mixer Block)，通过分层 MLP 对时间序列分块并捕捉多尺度时序模式；(3) 通道混合编码器 (Channel-Mixing Encoder)，专门用于处理高频成分以学习通道间相关性。

Result: 在七个数据集的 28 项实验设置中，有 20 项（占比 71.4%）在 MSE 指标上优于当前最先进的模型。

Conclusion: NPMixer 通过整合可学习的小波变换和层次化 MLP 结构，有效解决了多元时间序列中局部与全局依赖关系的建模难题，在多个基准测试中展现了优于主流 SOTA 模型的性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting remains a challenge due to the complexity of local temporal dynamics and global dependencies across multiple variables.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{N}eighboring \textbf{P}atching \textbf{Mixer} (\textbf{NPMixer}), a hierarchical architecture featuring a Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform that adaptively learns filter coefficients to decompose signals into trend and detail components in a data-dependent manner.
  Our framework introduces a Neighboring Mixer Block that captures local temporal dynamics through a series of hierarchical MLP layers operating on non-overlapping patches.
  Specifically, the mixer block utilizes MLPs to learn temporal patterns within and across these patches, expanding the receptive field to capture multi-scale dependencies.
  A Channel-Mixing Encoder is applied to high-frequency components to learn channel correlations while preserving the stability of the underlying global trend.
  Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that NPMixer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, achieving better performance in 20 out of 28 ($71.4\%$) evaluated experimental setups for MSE.

</details>


### [194] [SHRED: Retain-Set-Free Unlearning via Self-Distillation with Logit Demotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07482)
*Zizhao Hu,Ameya Godbole,Johnny Tian-Zheng Wei,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHRED 是一种无需依赖额外数据（保留集）的 LLM 机器遗忘框架，通过选择性地抑制遗忘数据中的“高信息量”词项并利用正常词项作为锚点，实现了高效的遗忘效果与模型效能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 机器遗忘方法通常依赖“保留集”（retain set）来防止灾难性的效能退化，这增加了部署的复杂性和数据依赖性。本文旨在解决无需保留集且不损耗模型通用语言能力的问题。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段的无保留集遗忘方法 SHRED：1. 选择阶段：通过计算遗忘数据集的每词项香农信息（Shannon information），识别并重点处理高信息量（低概率）词项；2. 训练阶段：构建改进的 KL 散度目标，抑制遗忘位置的逻辑值（logit），同时通过作为“良性锚点”的保留位置维持非敏感信息的分布，实现单目标下的自蒸馏遗忘与效能保持。

Result: 在四个标准遗忘基准测试中，SHRED 展现了最优的遗忘效果与模型效能权衡（Pareto-optimal trade-off），在抵御重学攻击（relearning attacks）和成员推理攻击（membership-inference attacks）方面表现稳健，并支持多次连续遗忘而不影响稳定性。

Conclusion: SHRED 是一种无需保留集（Retain-set-free）的大语言模型机器遗忘方法，通过基于信息论的选择机制和自蒸馏训练目标，在保证遗忘效果的同时有效地保护了模型效能，在多个基准测试中优于现有的依赖保留集的方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to selectively remove memorized content such as private data, copyrighted text, or hazardous knowledge, without costly full retraining. Most existing methods require a retain set of curated examples to prevent catastrophic degradation of general model utility, creating an extra data dependency that complicates deployment. We propose SHRED (Self-distillation via High-surprisal-only Retain-set-free Entropy Demotion), a retain-set-free unlearning method built on a key insight: not all tokens within a forget set instance carry memorized information equally. High-information tokens concentrate the model's memorized knowledge, while low-information tokens reflect general language competence. SHRED operates in two stages. (1) Selection: We perform a forward pass on a forget set instance, collect per-token autoregressive probabilities, and select the bottom (lowest probability, highest Shannon information) as forget positions; the remaining positions are retained as benign anchors. (2) Training: We construct modified KL targets that demote the memorized token's logit at forget positions while preserving the original distribution at benign positions. The model is then trained via a single top KL self-distillation objective that simultaneously drives forgetting and utility preservation. We evaluate SHRED across four standard unlearning benchmarks and demonstrate that it establishes a new Pareto-optimal trade-off between forget efficacy and model utility, outperforming retain-set-dependent methods. Our analysis shows that SHRED is robust against relearning attacks and membership-inference attacks, and it maintains stable utility even after many sequential unlearning runs.

</details>


### [195] [Excluding the Target Domain Improves Extrapolation: Deconfounded Hierarchical Physics Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07485)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DHG 框架，通过反事实推断消除温度混杂，结合分层物理约束，显著优化了生成模型在未知分布下的物理规律外推性能，并揭示了避免预训练过拟合目标域的有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理约束生成模型难以处理分布外场景，缺乏对物理定律层次结构的建模，且无法有效解决温度等混杂变量导致的偏差问题。

Method: 提出了去混杂层次门（DHG）机制，利用 do-operator 进行反事实估计，结合后门调整（backdoor adjustment）消除温度混杂，并采用从粗到细（Coarse-to-Fine）的物理约束策略。

Result: 在锂离子电池温度外推任务中，RMSE 降至 0.215（相比基线的 0.397 提升 46%）；研究发现排除目标域数据进行预训练反而能提升 39% 的外推表现。

Conclusion: DHG 通过消除温度混杂因素和采用从粗到细的物理约束，显著提升了物理约束深度生成模型在分布外（OOD）环境下的外推能力，并提出了排除目标域的预训练策略能带来更好的迁移效果。

Abstract: Extrapolation to out-of-distribution conditions is a fundamental challenge for physics-constrained deep generative models. Existing methods apply physical constraints as a single static regularization term uniformly across the generation process, and address neither the hierarchical structure of physical laws and the confounding variable problem. We propose the Deconfounded Hierarchical Gate (DHG), which serves as a diagnostic and control mechanism: it identifies when and how strongly temperature confounding contaminates each constraint level, so that hierarchical gates reflect intrinsic physical inconsistency rather than spurious temperature effects. DHG combines counterfactual estimation via the do-operator with backdoor adjustment to remove confounding, then applies Coarse-to-Fine physical constraints progressively. We report a counter-intuitive finding in pretraining: excluding the target-domain data from pretraining outperforms including it by 39% in extrapolation performance (RMSE 0.224 vs. 0.324). This occurs because FNO learns domain-agnostic physical patterns that transfer more effectively when the target domain is withheld. On a lithium-ion battery temperature extrapolation benchmark (trained at 24 degrees Celsius, evaluated at 4.0--43.0 degrees Celsius), our method achieves RMSE = 0.215, a 46% improvement over the unconstrained baseline (Pure CFM: 0.397).

</details>


### [196] [SGD for Variational Inference: Tackling Unbounded Variance via Preconditioning and Dynamic Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07531)
*Hippolyte Labarrière,Cesare Molinari,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对BBVI中随机梯度无界方差带来的优化挑战，在BG条件下为使用动态批处理和预处理的投影SGD提供了严格的收敛性保证，并证明了ELBO最优解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒变分推断（BBVI）中的随机梯度通常具有无界方差，超出了标准SGD的假设条件，现有研究缺乏对该特定优化环境下收敛性和解存在性的严格证明。

Method: 针对椭圆位置-尺度族分布，利用BG条件（Blum-Gladyshev condition）下的投影随机梯度下降法（PSGD），结合动态批量（dynamic batching）和预处理（preconditioning）技术进行优化。

Result: 证明了ELBO解的存在性；建立了PSGD在BG条件下有限时间和渐近域内的完整收敛保证；通过数值实验验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究在理论上填补了BBVI随机梯度优化在BG条件下可行性的空白，证明了动态批量和预处理策略在实现收敛保证方面的有效性。

Abstract: Black-Box Variational Inference (BBVI) typically relies on Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) to optimize the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). However, the stochastic gradients in BBVI inherently exhibit unbounded variance, violating standard assumptions and instead satisfying the weaker Blum-Gladyshev (BG) condition, where variance grows quadratically with distance from the optimum. In this paper, we bridge the gap between stochastic optimization theory and the practical instances of BBVI. Focusing on the broad elliptic location-scale family of parameterized distributions, we offer two main contributions. First, we prove the existence of an ELBO solution, a foundational property usually assumed a priori in the literature. Second, we establish comprehensive convergence guarantees spanning finite-time and asymptotic regimes for Minibatch Projected SGD (PSGD) equipped with dynamic batching and preconditioning under the BG condition. Our theoretical framework demonstrates that dynamic batching combined with preconditioning systematically enables rigorous guarantees even in complex settings. We illustrate our theoretical findings with numerical results, highlighting the efficacy of our approach for modern inference tasks.

</details>


### [197] [On the Invariance and Generality of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07546)
*Xing Han,Ziyin Liu,Suchi Saria,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨领域的通用神经缩放律框架，通过量化数据的信息分辨率变化，实现了无需重做大规模实验即可预测新任务的性能趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经缩放律（Neural Scaling Laws）在应用前需要进行昂贵的资源消耗来进行拟合，这违背了缩放律旨在优化资源配置的初衷。本研究旨在解决如何实现可泛化的缩放律。

Method: 1. 提出神经缩放律在双射变换下是不变的，在非双射变换（导致信息分辨率下降）下可以通过信息论方法进行预测性转换。
2. 引入信息分辨率 $\rho$ 作为连接不同领域的关键轴。
3. 通过跨语言、视觉和语音任务进行实证验证。

Result: 成功在语言模型（EHR数据 vs 通用文本）和时间序列分类（噪声注入）中实现了缩放律的跨领域预测，数据缩放指数的预测误差控制在 3% 以内。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过识别信息论中的“信息分辨率（Information Resolution, $\rho$）”作为不变性特征，可以将一个领域的神经缩放律可靠地迁移到其他领域，无需在目标域进行昂贵的重新实验。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws establish a predictable relationship between model performance and data or compute, offering crucial guidance for resource allocation in new domains and tasks. Yet such laws are most needed precisely where they are hardest to obtain: fitting one for a new model task pair demands expensive sweeps that typically exhaust the very compute budget the law is meant to economize. This paper poses the research question of how to develop generalizable scaling laws: laws fit once on a well-resourced source domain and reliably transported to new domains where running a full sweep is infeasible, which requires a fundamental understanding of when and why scaling properties change. We address this by identifying the right invariants: scaling laws are preserved under bijective (information-preserving) transformations of the data and modified in predictable, information-theoretically grounded ways under non-bijective transformations that lower its information resolution $ρ$: a single axis along which a law fit in one domain can be transported to another. We validate this across language, vision, and speech, and demonstrate two cross-domain applications: predicting scaling for language models trained on electronic health records from laws fit on general text, and predicting time-series classification scaling under varying levels of noise injection, recovering the data-scaling exponents to within $3\%$ error.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Distribution Estimation: Simplex Anchored Structural Inference Towards Universal Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07557)
*Yaxin Hou,Jun Ma,Hanyang Li,Bo Han,Jie Yu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对通用半监督学习 (UniSSL) 中伪标签易错的问题，提出 SAGE 方法，通过挖掘样本间结构关系替代分布估计，显著提升了模型表征学习的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法在处理无标签数据分布未知且带标签数据稀缺（UniSSL）的场景时，过度依赖伪标签导致的分布假设偏差会引发表征混乱。

Method: 提出 SAGE (Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition) 方法：1. 利用高阶样本间依赖构建结构共识；2. 使用单纯形等角紧框架向量指导类间表征分离；3. 采用分布无关的加权策略与辅助分支处理不可靠的伪标签。

Result: 在五个标准数据集上进行评估，SAGE 相比现有最先进方法平均准确率提升了 8.52%。

Conclusion: SAGE 通过引入基于单纯形锚点的图状态等分机制，成功在未知分布的无标签数据场景下提升了半监督学习性能，验证了从样本间关系入手优于依赖伪标签的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning faces significant challenges in realistic scenarios where labeled data is scarce and unlabeled data follows unknown, arbitrary distributions. We formalize this critical yet under-explored paradigm as Universal Semi-supervised Learning (UniSSL). Existing methods typically leverage unlabeled data via pseudo-labeling. However, they often rely on the idealized assumption of a uniform unlabeled data distribution or require sufficient labeled data to estimate it. In the UniSSL setting, such dependencies lead to numerous erroneous pseudo-labels, thereby triggering representation confusion. Fortunately, we observe that inter-sample relations captured by representations are more reliable than pseudo-labels. Leveraging this insight, we shift our focus to representation-level structural inference to bypass distribution estimation. Accordingly, we propose Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE), which captures high-order inter-sample dependencies to establish structural consensus for guiding representation learning. Meanwhile, to mitigate representation confusion, we employ vectors that satisfy a simplex equiangular tight frame to serve as a coordinate frame for guiding inter-class representation separation. Finally, we introduce a weighting strategy based on distribution-agnostic metrics to prioritize reliable pseudo-labels and an auxiliary branch to isolate potentially erroneous pseudo-labels. Evaluations on five standard benchmarks show that SAGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with an average accuracy gain of $\textbf{8.52\%}$.

</details>


### [199] [Bilevel Graph Structure Learning, Revisited: Inner-Channel Origins of the Reported Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07577)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了双层图结构学习的性能提升主要源于内部训练动态带来的隐式正则化，而非显式的图重连。作者提出了 frozen-φ 诊断方法以客观评估不同结构学习组件的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为双层图结构学习的性能提升主要归功于图重连，但该归因可能被夸大，训练动态效应的作用被忽视。

Method: 提出 frozen-φ 控制变量法，通过冻结图结构但保留内部循环的训练过程，将双层增益分解为“内部训练动态信道”和“图重连信道”；同时引入三前提框架来预测双层增益方向。

Result: 在时空流预测任务中，内部训练动态贡献了 78-101% 的增益；在节点分类任务中贡献了 37-44%。传统的谱诊断指标在反映任务性能增益时可能失效。

Conclusion: 双层图结构学习中的性能增益很大程度上源于内部循环的训练动态效应（隐式梯度正则化），而非单纯的图重连（Rewiring）。作者建议将 frozen-φ 作为评估双层图学习的标准诊断工具。

Abstract: Bilevel graph structure learning is widely understood to improve graph neural networks by jointly optimizing model parameters and a learned graph structure, with the resulting performance gain attributed to the rewired adjacency. We find that this attribution may be overstated: training-dynamics effects in the inner loop, rather than the rewiring itself, capture a substantial share of the gain. To establish this, we introduce frozen-$φ$, a control that freezes the graph while retaining the inner-loop training schedule. This decomposes the bilevel gain into an inner channel of $T$-step training dynamics with implicit gradient regularization and a graph channel of the graph rewiring itself. On spatio-temporal flow forecasting the inner channel matches or exceeds the full bilevel pipeline, accounting for 78-101% of the gain; on node classification it accounts for 37-44% under a Bernoulli edge-level parameterization. We also verify that classical spectral diagnostics can dissociate from task gain. We propose frozen-$φ$ as a standardized diagnostic for bilevel graph structure learning, with graph distillation as a method-agnostic complement. A three-precondition framework further predicts the sign of the bilevel gain on all six benchmarks.

</details>


### [200] [Optimal Recourse Summaries via Bi-Objective Decision Tree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07598)
*Ioannis Chatzis,Jason Liartis,Athanasios Voulodimos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOGAR 是一种新的归因汇总框架，通过最优决策树学习平衡归因有效性与成本，并提供帕累托前沿以实现灵活的全局策略审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的局部归因方法（Actionable Recourse）在全局审计和偏差检测中存在计算开销大且不一致的问题；现有的归因汇总方法未能有效解决归因有效性与成本之间的权衡。

Method: 将归因汇总（Recourse Summaries）表述为最优决策树学习问题；利用浅层轴平行决策树和稀疏叶节点动作；计算并提取归因有效性与成本之间的帕累托前沿（Pareto front）。

Result: SOGAR 在归因有效性和成本指标上均优于现有的汇总方法，生成的归因更稳定且成本更低，同时允许用户在部署后灵活选择所需的平衡点。

Conclusion: SOGAR 将归因汇总建模为最优决策树学习问题，通过生成帕累托前沿（Pareto front），有效平衡了归因有效性与成本之间的权衡，在不需重新训练的情况下提供了更优、更稳定且可解释的全局归因分析方案。

Abstract: Actionable Recourse provides individuals with actions they can take to change an unfavorable classifier outcome. While useful at the instance level, it is ill-suited for global auditing and bias detection, since aggregating local actions is costly and often inconsistent. Recourse Summaries address this limitation by partitioning the population and assigning one shared action per subgroup, enabling comparison across subgroups. Designing summaries involves a fundamental trade-off between recourse effectiveness and recourse cost, which existing methods do not adequately address. We introduce Summaries of Optimal and Global Actionable Recourse (SOGAR), which formulates recourse summary learning as an optimal decision tree learning problem and finds the Pareto front -- the complete set of solutions where improving one objective necessarily worsens the other. SOGAR enables post-hoc selection of the desired trade-off without retraining. Using shallow axis-parallel decision trees and sparse leaf actions, SOGAR produces stable, low-cost, and effective recourse summaries that outperform existing approaches across effectiveness and cost metrics.

</details>


### [201] [Learning Large-Scale Modular Addition with an Auxiliary Modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07648)
*Hanato Kikuchi,Ryosuke Masuya,Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对模加法学习中因降低难度而引入协变量偏移的问题，本文提出了一种基于辅助模数（Kq）的新方法，在保持输入分布一致性的同时，大幅提升了模型在长序列与大模数任务上的学习效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模加法学习方法（如稀疏化方法）通过在训练序列中增加零值来降低训练难度，但这会导致训练与测试数据分布的不一致（协变量偏移），限制了模型在复杂场景下的表现。本研究旨在解决这一分布偏移问题，同时提升模加法任务的可扩展性。

Method: 提出了一种利用辅助模数（Auxiliary Modulus, Kq）的方法。该方法通过引入辅助模数来降低求和环绕频率（wrap-around frequency）以简化训练难度，同时确保训练过程与测试过程遵循相同的输入分布，从而消除了前人研究中存在的协变量偏移（covariate shift）问题。

Result: 在大模数（如q=974269）和小数据集规模下，新方法表现出卓越的性能。在N=64、q=974269的实验中，仅使用10万样本即可达到97.0%的τ-accuracy，显著优于现有稀疏方法（即使在使用10倍于该数量的样本时表现也更差）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无协变量偏移的模加法学习方法，通过引入辅助模数Kq，在保持训练和测试分布一致的前提下，显著提升了模型在处理大规模模数和长序列时的可扩展性与样本效率。

Abstract: Learning parity functions, more general modular addition, is a challenging machine learning task due to its input sensitivity. A recent study substantially scaled modular addition learning in both the number of summands and the modulus. Its key idea is to increase zeros in training sequences, reducing the effective number of summands and thus controlling training difficulty; however, this induces covariate shift between training and test input distributions. This study theoretically and empirically analyzes this side effect and proposes a covariate-shift-free method for modular addition. Specifically, we introduce an auxiliary modulus $Kq$ during training, which reduces wrap-around frequency and problem difficulty while preserving the same input distribution across training and testing. Experiments show strong scalability and sample efficiency: even for large input length $N$, large modulus $q$, and small datasets -- where the sparse method fails to learn -- our method achieves equal or better match accuracy and relaxed $τ$-accuracy. For example, at $N=64$ and $q=974269$, our method trained on 100K samples achieves $97.0\%$ $τ$-accuracy at $τ=0.05$, while the sparse method achieves only $9.5\%$ with the same data size and $93.9\%$ even when extended to 1M samples.

</details>


### [202] [Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation for Fast Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07661)
*George Rapakoulias,Peter Garud,Lingjiong Zhu,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STMD是一种无需教师模型的扩散模型加速新框架，它通过学习随机微分方程的完整转移映射，实现了高效的少步推理，同时保留了重要的随机生成特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型虽然生成质量高但推理代价昂贵；现有的蒸馏方法（如知识蒸馏）通常依赖复杂的教师模型、双层优化或轨迹缓存，训练成本高且难以直接保留原有的随机采样特性。

Method: 提出随机转移映射蒸馏（STMD），采用条件流模型（Mean Flow model）参数化SDE的转移过程；摒弃了传统的评分匹配或教师指导，通过直接训练实现高效的单步/少步采样。

Result: STMD在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CelebA数据集上表现优异，能够在极少步骤下完成高质量的随机图像生成，并适用于需要随机性的下游任务（如反问题求解和能量引导微调）。

Conclusion: STMD通过直接拟合SDE的转移映射，实现了无需教师模型的少步采样，在保持扩散模型随机生成特性的前提下显著提升了推理效率，且具备完善的理论收敛性支持。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong generation quality, diversity, and distribution coverage, but their performance often comes with expensive inference. In this work, we propose Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation (STMD), a teacher-free framework for accelerating diffusion model inference while preserving probabilistic sample generation. In contrast to score-based diffusion models, whose denoising parametrization models the mean of the posterior distribution, STMD distills the full transition map associated with the sampling stochastic differential equation (SDE). We parameterize these SDE transitions with a conditional Mean Flow model, yielding a one- or few-step stochastic sampler that retains the transition structure of the underlying diffusion process. This perspective is especially useful for downstream tasks that require stochastic inference, such as diffusion posterior sampling, inverse problems, and energy-based fine-tuning. Compared to recent distillation methods, STMD requires no pretrained teacher, bi-level optimization, or trajectory simulation and caching, enabling efficient and scalable training. We derive convergence bounds for our method in the Wasserstein distance, providing a strong theoretical foundation for our approach, and validate STMD on various image generation examples on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets.

</details>


### [203] [Direction-Preserving Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07662)
*Bardia Zadeh,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了低精度数值格式在矢量方向表示上的几何极限，提出了一个量化评估框架，证明了现有浮点/定点格式在方向覆盖上的次优性，并基于几何优化发现了更优的标量编码方案，同时验证了NVIDIA NVFP4格式的理论合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数值格式对机器学习至关重要，而矢量运算的准确性依赖于方向表示。现有广泛使用的格式（浮点、定点）往往源于算术便捷性而非几何方向的最优覆盖，本文旨在探索如何通过优化标量元素的选择来最大化矢量方向的表示覆盖。

Method: 构建了一个用于分析乘积结构编码（product-structured codes）方向覆盖能力的几何框架；利用该框架量化了此类编码与球形编码（spherical codes）之间的次优差距；通过数值优化方法针对不同块维度搜索最优标量字母表，并使用Lean定理证明器对核心结论进行形式化验证。

Result: 证明了标准格式（补码、定点、浮点）在方向表示上存在可量化的次优缺口；实验结果显示，NVIDIA的E2M1格式在4位精度下非常接近数学最优解，为该格式在低精度AI workloads中的性能提供了理论解释。

Conclusion: 现有的标准低精度数值格式（如定点数、浮点数）在矢量方向覆盖上并非最优，通过针对性优化标量字母表可提升性能。NVIDIA的NVFP4（E2M1）在4位精度下接近方向覆盖最优解，验证了其在机器学习任务中卓越表现的几何原理。

Abstract: Low-precision number formats are widely used in modern machine learning systems due to their efficiency. Accurate direction representation is key to the accuracy of vector operations. This work precisely explores the extent to which the direction of a vector can be represented by selecting its scalar elements from a common finite alphabet of a given size. This is standard practice in machine learning, where low-precision significands may be narrow-width floating-point or integer values. A geometric framework is introduced for analyzing the directional coverage of such product-structured codes.
  This work analytically quantifies the suboptimality gap between such product-structured codes and spherical codes for the vector as a whole, in both low and asymptotically high dimensions. Furthermore, within the product code class, it is proven that the standard formats of two's complement, fixed-point, and floating-point are suboptimal, again with quantified gap, pointing to the potential to develop new scalar number formats.
  Such scalar alphabets are numerically optimized across multiple block dimensions for directional coverage, including the dimension used in NVIDIA's NVFP4 format. Experimental results are presented comparing the performance of standard formats and the optimized alphabet. We find that for four bits, NVIDIA's choice of E2M1 closely approximates the optimized alphabet, providing a geometric explanation for its strong performance in low-precision machine learning workloads and an analytical understanding of the link between that superiority and block size.
  We provide open-source formal proofs in Lean for the theorems in this work, along with the experimental code and the optimized alphabets obtained.

</details>


### [204] [Curated Synthetic Data Doesn't Have to Collapse: A Theoretical Study of Generative Retraining with Pluralistic Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07724)
*Ali Falahati,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Kate Larson,Lukasz Golab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过多奖励函数策划（Curation）来解决生成模型递归训练中的模型崩塌问题，证明了异构偏好能够引导模型收敛至保留多样性的稳定分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归训练生成模型面临“模型崩塌”挑战，即在固定奖励信号下，模型倾向于过度优化少数输出，导致多样性丧失。传统观点认为必须掺杂真实数据才能避免，本文旨在探讨是否可以通过改进奖励机制来解决此问题。

Method: 从对齐视角重新审视递归训练，将单一奖励模型扩展为异构偏好模型。通过数学形式化异构偏好下的递归训练动力学，并推导出模型在满足特定条件下收敛的稳定分布性质。

Result: 理论证明了在 heterogeneous preferences（异构偏好）下，递归训练不仅不会导致模型崩塌，反而能收敛到一个能够平衡多个高奖励区域的稳定分布，且该分布具有良好的理论特性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过使用多个奖励函数进行筛选，可以有效缓解生成模型在递归训练中的崩塌现象。模型最终会收敛到一个稳定的分布，在多个高奖励区域之间分配概率质量，并满足加权纳什谈判解，从而在保留多样性的同时实现价值聚合。

Abstract: Recursive retraining of generative models poses a critical representation challenge: when synthetic outputs are curated based on a fixed reward signal, the model tends to collapse onto a narrow set of outputs that over-optimize that objective. Prior work suggests that such collapse is unavoidable without adding real data into the mix. We revisit this conclusion from an alignment perspective and show that collapse can be mitigated through curation based on multiple reward functions. We formalize the dynamics of recursive training under heterogeneous preferences and prove that, under certain conditions, the model converges to a stable distribution that allocates probability mass across competing high-reward regions. The limiting distribution preserves diversity and provably satisfies a weighted Nash bargaining solution, offering a formal interpretation of value aggregation in synthetic retraining loops.

</details>


### [205] [Fortifying Time Series: DTW-Certified Robust Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07690)
*Shijie Liu,Tansu Alpcan,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有时间序列认证防御在应对DTW度量下的时序扰动时失效的问题，本文引入了首个DTW认证防御方案，通过随机平滑技术建立了DTW下的鲁棒性保证，显著提升了模型在对抗环境下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Lp范数的时间序列异常检测认证防御无法有效刻画时间序列的内在时序结构（时间偏移会导致Lp范数剧增），因此需要一种基于更符合时序特性度量（如DTW）的认证防御方法来提升系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种基于随机平滑（Randomized Smoothing）的认证机制，通过建立Lp范数与DTW距离之间的下界转换关系，实现了在DTW度量下的鲁棒性认证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种数据集和模型下表现优异，在面对基于DTW的对抗攻击时，F1得分相比传统认证模型最高提升了18.7%。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了时间序列异常检测在DTW度量下缺乏认证鲁棒性的空白，提出了首个基于随机平滑的DTW认证防御框架，在理论和实验上均证明了该方法对于应对具有时序扰动的对抗样本具有显著优势。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is critical for ensuring safety in high-stakes applications, where robustness is a fundamental requirement rather than a mere performance metric. Addressing the vulnerability of these systems to adversarial manipulation is therefore essential. Existing defenses are largely heuristic or provide certified robustness only under $\ell_p$-norm constraints, which are incompatible with time-series data. In particular, $\ell_p$-norm fails to capture the intrinsic temporal structure in time series, causing small temporal distortions to significantly alter the $\ell_p$-norm measures. Instead, the similarity metric \emph{Dynamic Time Warping} (DTW) is more suitable and widely adopted in the time-series domain, as DTW accounts for temporal alignment and remains robust to temporal variations. To date, however, there has been no certifiable robustness result in this metric that provides guarantees. In this work, we introduce the first \emph{DTW-certified robust defense} in time-series anomaly detection by adapting the randomized smoothing paradigm. We develop this certificate by bridging the $\ell_p$-norm to DTW distance through a lower-bound transformation. Extensive experiments across various datasets and models validate the effectiveness and practicality of our theoretical approach. Results demonstrate significantly improved performance, e.g., up to 18.7\% in F1-score under DTW-based adversarial attacks compared to traditional certified models.

</details>


### [206] [Toward Better Geometric Representations for Molecule Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07693)
*Shaoheng Yan,Zian Li,Cai Zhou,Qiaojing Huang,Kai Liu,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了LENSEs框架，通过引入多级表征头、语义感知损失和节点级对齐损失，优化了基于几何表征的分子生成过程，显著提升了生成质量与模型表征的平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于几何表征条件的分子生成方法受到预训练分子编码器（如UniMol）表征空间不平滑且未被充分利用的限制。

Method: 提出了LENSEs框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) 训练过程中同步更新的多级表征头；(2) 基于语义信息空间的分子感知损失；(3) 显式实现生成器隐状态与编码器表征对齐的节点级表征对齐（REPA）损失。

Result: 在GEOM-DRUG数据集上实现了97.28%的有效性和98.51%的稳定性，优于现有先进方法；Lipschitz常数降低了4.6倍，表明表征更平滑。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过对齐目标进行生成训练，可以作为分子编码器的一种有效的预训练范式，显著提升了表征的平滑性和信息量，并优于现有的生成方法。

Abstract: Geometric representation-conditioned molecule generation provides an effective paradigm that decouples molecule representation modeling from structure generation. By decoupling molecule generation into two stages-first generating a meaningful molecule representation, and then generating a 3D molecule conditioned on this representation-the efficiency and quality of the generation process can be significantly enhanced. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by the quality of the representation space: pretrained molecular encoders, such as UniMol, produce representations that are non-smooth and not fully exploited during the generative training process. In this work, we propose LENSEs, a framework that better exploits the potential of molecule representations in representation-conditioned generation methods. In particular, LENSEs introduces three complementary mechanisms: (1) a representation head, simultaneously trained during generative tasks, that extracts multi-level representations from the pretrained encoder; (2) a molecule perceptual loss that optimizes the generator in a semantic-informative representation space; and (3) a node-level representation alignment (REPA) loss that explicitly aligns the generator's hidden states with encoder representations, reducing the semantic gap between pretraining and generation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of these improvements through extensive molecule generation tasks. Specifically, on the challenging molecule generation dataset GEOM-DRUG, LENSEs achieves 97.28% validity and 98.51% molecule stability, surpassing existing advanced methods. Further analyses through Lipschitz constant reduction (4.6x) and QM9 probing tasks also demonstrate the smoother, more informative refined representations, establishing generative training with alignment objectives as a potential pretraining paradigm for molecular encoders.

</details>


### [207] [Intelligent Truck Matching in Full Truckload Shipments using Ping2Hex approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07733)
*Srinivas Kumar R,Jose Mathew,Ankit Singh Chauhan,Dinesh Rajkumar,Aravind Manoj,Mohit Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITM 2.0 是一款基于机器学习的卡车与货物匹配系统，通过 H3 空间索引和 LightGBM 模型解决了因车辆标识缺失导致的物流可视化缺失问题，显著提升了匹配精度与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整车物流供应链中，由于车辆识别码缺失或损坏，传统的匹配方法难以实现实时跟踪和 ETA 预测，导致大量货物失去可视化能力。

Method: 将匹配任务转化为概率排名问题：1. 利用 Uber H3 六边形空间索引离散化 GPS 点以提取路线相似度特征；2. 结合时间信息；3. 使用 LightGBM 梯度提升决策树进行建模；4. 采用基于阈值的后处理优化最终结果。

Result: 在北美和欧洲市场均表现优异，相比规则基准，精度分别提升了 26 和 14 个百分点，同时覆盖范围翻倍；验证了对高达 1km 的地理编码误差及稀疏数据的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ITM 2.0 证明了通过结合空间网格化索引、时间特征与梯度提升树，可以解决卡车识别码缺失导致的可视化难题，显著提升了匹配的精度和覆盖范围，并具备应对真实生产环境（如定位误差、稀疏数据）的高鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate truck-to-shipment matching using GPS data is foundational for full truckload supply chain visibility, enabling real-time tracking and accurate estimated time of arrival (ETA) predictions. However, missing or corrupted vehicle identifiers prevent traditional matching approaches, leaving shipments without visibility. This paper presents Intelligent Truck Matching (ITM) 2.0, a machine learning system that addresses this critical gap by formulating matching as a probabilistic ranking problem. Our approach leverages Uber H3 hexagonal spatial indexing to discretize GPS pings into route similarity features, combined with temporal information, then applies LightGBM gradient boosting with threshold-based post-processing. Through rigorous evaluation including offline model selection (SVM, XGBoost, LightGBM), comprehensive ablation studies, and production shadow testing, we demonstrate substantial gains over rule-based baselines. ITM 2.0 achieves 26 percentage point precision improvement in North America and 14 points in Europe, while doubling coverage. Deployed in production at Project44 handling full truckload shipments, the system demonstrates robustness to geocoding errors up to 1 km, multiple candidate trucks, and sparse pings.

</details>


### [208] [When Losses Align: Gradient-Based Composite Loss Weighting for Efficient Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07756)
*Ivan Karpukhin,Andrey Savchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种高效的梯度双层优化方法，通过在线学习预训练损失权重，以极小的额外代价（约30%）取代昂贵的超参数调优搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习模型常使用复合目标进行预训练，调整不同损失项的权重（超参数）需通过随机搜索或贝叶斯优化，这需要多次独立训练，计算极其昂贵。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度的双层优化方法（gradient-based bilevel method），通过将复合预训练梯度与下游目标对齐来在线学习预训练权重，并利用损失函数特定的结构避免了全模型截断反向传播的多重计算，仅增加约30%的单次训练成本。

Result: 在事件序列建模和自监督计算机视觉实验中，该方法显著降低了调优成本，同时达到了与精心调优的基准相当甚至更好的效果。

Conclusion: 该方法通过在线学习加权，大幅降低了复合损失函数中超参数调优的计算开销，同时在事件序列建模和自监督视觉任务中展现了优于或等同于手工调优基准的性能。

Abstract: Modern deep models are often pretrained on large-scale data with missing labels using composite objectives, where the relative weights of multiple loss terms act as hyperparameters. Tuning these weights with random search or Bayesian optimization is computationally expensive, as it requires many independent training runs. To address this, we propose a gradient-based bilevel method that learns pretraining loss weights online by aligning the composite pretraining gradient with a downstream objective. By exploiting the structure of the loss, the method avoids the multiple backward passes typically required by truncated backpropagation through the full model, reducing the overhead of hyperparameter tuning to approximately 30% above a single training run. We evaluate the approach on event-sequence modeling and self-supervised computer vision, where it matches or improves upon carefully tuned baselines while substantially reducing the cost of hyperparameter tuning compared to random or Bayesian search.

</details>


### [209] [Bayesian Fine-tuning in Projected Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07706)
*Viktar Dubovik,Patryk Marszałek,Jacek Tabor,Tomasz Kuśmierczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数高效的贝叶斯微调框架，通过利用低秩协方差特性在极低维空间中进行不确定性量化，解决了传统贝叶斯LoRA参数量大且训练不稳定的问题，实现了性能与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的LoRA缺乏不确定性量化能力，而现有的贝叶斯LoRA变体虽然解决了这一问题，却增加了大量可训练参数，削弱了LoRA的效率优势，且训练难度较高、收敛不稳定。

Method: 提出一种参数高效的贝叶斯微调框架，通过在极低维参数空间中建模权重不确定性，克服了传统贝叶斯LoRA参数量过大及训练不稳定的问题。

Result: 在实现高效不确定性量化的同时，保持了计算效率，并在模型校准和泛化能力上表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过适当的权重空间投影，可以在极低维空间中实现不确定性量化，且模型权重协方差矩阵具有低秩特性。该方法在保持计算效率的同时，提升了模型的校准能力和泛化性能。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large models by decomposing weight updates into low-rank matrices, significantly reducing storage and computational overhead. While effective, standard LoRA lacks mechanisms for uncertainty quantification, leading to overconfident and poorly calibrated models. Bayesian variants of LoRA address this limitation, but at the cost of a significantly increased number of trainable parameters, partially offsetting the original efficiency gains. Additionally, these models are harder to train and may suffer from unstable convergence. In this work, we propose a novel framework for parameter-efficient Bayesian fine-tuning, demonstrating that effective uncertainty quantification can be achieved in very low-dimensional parameter spaces. The proposed method achieves strong performance with improved calibration and generalization while maintaining computational efficiency. Our empirical findings show that, with the appropriate projection of the weight space uncertainty can be effectively modeled in a low-dimensional space, and weight covariances exhibit low ranks.

</details>


### [210] [Toward Privileged Foundation Models:LUPI for Accelerated and Improved Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07799)
*Xueying Ding,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIQL 是一种利用特权信息（PI）加速表格基础模型训练并提升泛化性能的新框架，通过在训练时引入统计信息和程序逻辑，并在推理时重构这些信息，有效降低了计算与数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型训练过程计算密集且收敛缓慢，旨在寻求一种能同时加速学习并提升泛化能力的方法，以降低资源需求。

Method: 1. 构建两种特权信息（PI）：数据集级聚合统计量（辅助上下文学习）和数据生成程序的编码（提供超观测数据的信息）。
2. 设计架构：通过训练时引入PI，并学习在推理时从观测到的上下文中重构该信息，以实现PI的有效迁移。
3. 理论分析：表征PI如何缩减总体近似差距并加速有限数据下的收敛。

Result: 实验表明，PIQL 在表格基础模型（TFMs）上实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的最终损失以及更好的泛化效果，显著降低了数据和计算开销。

Conclusion: PIQL 确立了以特权信息（PI）为导向的预训练范式，证明了其在提升基础模型预训练效率与性能方面的有效性与理论基础，为优化计算和数据资源提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Training foundation models is computationally intensive and often slow to converge.We introduce PIQL,Privileged Information for Quick and Quality Learning, the first framework to systematically integrate privileged information (PI) to simultaneously accelerate learning and improve generalization in tabular foundation models (TFMs). We construct two complementary forms of PI: (i) aggregate dataset-level statistics that reduce the burden on in-context learning, and (ii) encodings of the underlying data-generating program, providing knowledge beyond observable data. We further design an architecture that effectively transfers the train-time-only PI by learning to reconstruct it from observed context at inference. We provide a theoretical analysis characterizing conditions under which PI reduces the population-level approximation gap and accelerates convergence in finite-data regimes. Empirical evidence shows that PIQL enables TFMs to achieve faster convergence, lower final loss, and better generalization, in effect, reducing data and compute requirements. Our work establishes PI-guided pretraining as a principled and practical paradigm for improving the efficiency and performance of foundation models.

</details>


### [211] [Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07804)
*Zhicheng Yang,Zhijiang Guo,Yifan Song,Minrui Xu,Yongxin Wang,Yiwei Wang,Xiaodan Liang,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prune-OPD 是一种通过实时检测学生-教师前缀偏移来动态截断无效生成并重新分配训练预算的框架，有效解决了长视距推理任务中策略蒸馏的效率与可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长视距任务中，策略蒸馏（OPD）常因学生生成的前缀偏离教师的思维过程而导致密集奖励失效，进而引发奖励质量下降与计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出 Prune-OPD 框架：1. 通过实时监测学生与教师预测的局部兼容性（如 Top-k 重叠度）检测前缀偏移；2. 当检测到偏移时，动态降低不可靠后续奖励的权重；3. 触发动态轨迹截断，终止无效生成并重新分配计算资源。

Result: 在复杂的推理基准测试（AMC, AIME, HMMT）中，Prune-OPD 在保持或提升性能的同时，成功减少了 37.6% 至 68.0% 的训练时间。在兼容性高时，它能自动扩展训练窗口以保持长上下文监督。

Conclusion: Prune-OPD 通过动态对齐计算资源与监督质量，能够高效识别并处理前缀偏移，在提升推理任务性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。该方法证明了优化推理模型训练的关键在于将计算资源重新分配给局部可利用的教师奖励，而非盲目截断轨迹。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.

</details>


### [212] [Robust and Reliable AI for Predictive Quality in Semiconductor Materials Manufacturing with MLOps and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07752)
*Min Gao,Julia Maria Perathoner,Anton Ludwig Bonin,Steven Eulig,Gianni Klesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对半导体制造中的模型性能漂移问题，提出了一种轻量级、高稳健性的MLOps方案，通过固定频率重训与共形预测技术，在降低算力需求的同时实现了高质量的预测与质量保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造工艺中存在设备退化、原料变异等动态漂移现象导致模型性能衰减，且制造环境对计算开销敏感，亟需一套兼顾模型稳健性、计算效率与决策可信度（不确定性量化）的MLOps部署策略。

Method: 利用5年实际制造数据，基准测试多种MLOps重训练频率与超参数优化策略；使用控制限归一化残差（CLNR）作为评估指标；引入共形预测（Conformal Prediction）技术量化预测不确定性。

Result: 每5批次更新一次、不进行超参数重调的“固定频率策略”表现最优，不仅维持了模型在高强度漂移下的准确性，还显著降低了计算开销；共形预测生成的置信区间提供了强大的统计保障，使制造决策从被动管理转变为预测性控制。

Conclusion: 在半导体制造环境下，固定频率（每5批次）的重训练策略优于包含超参数优化的复杂策略，能在保证计算效率的同时有效应对各类漂移，且通过共形预测实现的置信区间能有效赋能主动式质量控制。

Abstract: Semiconductor materials manufacturing presents unique challenges for machine learning deployment due to evolving process conditions, equipment degradation, and raw material variability that can cause model performance deterioration over time. This study benchmarks machine learning operations (MLOps) retraining strategies using five years of real manufacturing data to identify optimal retraining approaches for quality prediction. We evaluate various retraining frequencies and hyperparameter optimization strategies using control limit normalized residuals as key performance metric. Results demonstrate that a fixed retraining cadence every five production batches without hyperparameter retuning achieves superior performance across all drift conditions while significantly reducing computational overhead compared to strategies incorporating hyperparameter optimization. This approach effectively maintains model accuracy during both abrupt process changes and gradual equipment degradation patterns. To address the critical need for uncertainty quantification in manufacturing decision-making, we implement conformal prediction to generate prediction confidence intervals with strong statistical guarantees. This enables proactive quality control by identifying when prediction intervals fall within acceptable control limits, transforming traditional reactive quality management into a predictive framework. The findings provide practical guidelines for implementing robust MLOps strategies in manufacturing environments where computational efficiency and reliable uncertainty quantification are paramount for operational success.

</details>


### [213] [On the Tradeoffs of On-Device Generative Models in Federated Predictive Maintenance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07860)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Piero Baraldi,Enrico Zio,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了VAE、GAN和扩散模型在联邦工业预测性维护中的表现，并提出了一种通过部分组件共享实现个性化的分类法，证明了在带宽受限和数据异构场景下，部分联邦策略（特别是针对扩散模型）具有更好的性能与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然判别式模型在联邦学习（FL）中占主导地位，但在复杂的工业物联网（IoT）预测性维护中，生成式模型（VAE、GAN、DM）在无监督异常检测方面具有显著潜力，迫切需要对这些模型在联邦环境下的适用性、通信开销及个性化策略进行系统性评估。

Method: 针对联邦预测性维护（PdM）场景，对VAE、GAN和扩散模型（DM）进行了全面分析；提出了一种新的联邦生成模型分类法，将“部分组件共享”机制正式化，以实现模型个性化；通过真实工业时间序列数据集进行实验对比，评估了全联邦与部分联邦设置下的模型效能、稳定性及通信开销。

Result: 实验表明：在通信受限与Non-IID环境下，GAN的全联邦训练虽比本地训练稳定，但整体鲁棒性弱于VAE和扩散模型；对于扩散模型（DM），采用部分联邦（特别是仅共享解码器）的方案能显著优于全联邦架构。

Conclusion: 在工业物联网联邦预测性维护（PdM）场景下，生成式模型（VAE、GAN、DM）各具优劣。部分联邦架构（即仅共享模型的部分组件）在处理数据异构性（Non-IID）及带宽受限的工业环境下，往往能比全联邦提供更优的模型性能与个性化效果。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for preserving client data ownership and control over distributed Internet of Things (IoT) environments. While discriminative models dominate most FL use cases, recent advances in generative models -- such as Variational Autoencoders (VAE), Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN), and Diffusion Models (DM) -- offer new opportunities for unsupervised anomaly detection in time series analysis, with relevant applications in predictive maintenance (PdM) in critical industrial infrastructures. In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of VAEs, GANs, and DMs in the context of federated PdM. We analyze their performance and communication overhead under both full and partial federation setups, where only subsets of model components are shared. Building on this analysis, the paper proposes a novel taxonomy for federated generative models that formalizes partial component sharing as a principled mechanism for model personalization. Our experiments over a real-world time series dataset reveal distinct trade-offs in model utility, stability, and scalability, especially in heterogeneous and bandwidth-constrained FL settings. For the evaluated GAN-based configurations, full federation improves training stability relative to independent local training, although the model remains less robust than the VAE- and DDPM-based alternatives. For DMs, however, partial federation -- especially decoder sharing -- can outperform full federation in bandwidth-constrained, non-IID settings.

</details>


### [214] [INO-SGD: Addressing Utility Imbalance under Individualized Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07930)
*Xiao Tian,Jue Fan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对个体化差分隐私（IDP）中不同隐私需求导致的数据效用不平衡问题，本文提出了INO-SGD算法，通过动态降权机制在满足IDP的同时提升了敏感数据的模型表征效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个体化差分隐私（IDP）算法会导致严重的“效用不平衡”问题：即对隐私要求高的数据在训练中被过度抑制（欠表示），导致模型在部署时处理此类敏感数据时性能较差。需要一种在满足隐私约束的同时提升受保护类别性能的方法。

Method: 提出了INO-SGD（Individualized Noise-aware SGD）算法，通过在每个训练批次中对数据进行策略性降权（down-weighting），以在训练过程中针对性地提升对强隐私保护需求数据的准确性，并严格满足IDP约束。

Result: 实验证明了INO-SGD的可行性及其优于现有方案的能力，有效平衡了强隐私需求带来的效用损失。

Conclusion: INO-SGD有效解决了在满足个体化差分隐私（IDP）约束下的效用不平衡问题，在保护用户高隐私需求的同时，提升了模型对个体化隐私敏感数据的表现。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is widely employed in machine learning to protect confidential or sensitive training data from being revealed. As data owners gain greater control over their data due to personal data ownership, they are more likely to set their own privacy requirements, necessitating individualized DP (IDP) to fulfil such requests. In particular, owners of data from more sensitive subsets, such as positive cases of stigmatized diseases, likely set stronger privacy requirements, as leakage of such data could incur more serious societal impact. However, existing IDP algorithms induce a critical utility imbalance problem: Data from owners with stronger privacy requirements may be severely underrepresented in the trained model, resulting in poorer performance on similar data from subsequent users during deployment. In this paper, we analyze this problem and propose the INO-SGD algorithm, which strategically down-weights data within each batch to improve performance on the more private data across all iterations. Notably, our algorithm is specially designed to satisfy IDP, while existing techniques addressing utility imbalance neither satisfy IDP nor can be easily adapted to do so. Lastly, we demonstrate the empirical feasibility of our approach.

</details>


### [215] [Where's the Plan? Locating Latent Planning in Language Models with Lightweight Mechanistic Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07984)
*Nicole Ma,Nick Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管大型语言模型普遍能在内部表示中提前编码未来受限信息（如韵脚），但这种表征并不总是因果性地驱动生成；仅在特定大型模型（Gemma-3-27B）中确认了这种提前规划机制的实际运作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在生成过程中是否确实通过“预先规划”（在生成目标词之前先形成该词的内部表示）来指导输出，即探讨模型内部表示是否具备因果驱动力。

Method: 采用线性探测（Linear Probing）验证内部表示，并通过激活修补（Activation Patching）及两阶段路径修补（Path Patching）定位因果依赖关系。选取韵脚对补全任务作为测试基准，在Qwen3、Gemma-3及Llama-3的多个尺度版本上进行实验。

Result: 所有测试模型均表现出强大的韵脚信息表征能力（随参数增加而增强）；然而，只有Gemma-3-27B表现出因果依赖性演变，在生成过程中实现了从依赖原始输入到提前处理规划信息的切换，且该能力由特定的5个注意力头主导。

Conclusion: 模型对于未来受限信息的表征能力（可探测性）并不等同于对其的因果依赖（决策驱动），只有在大参数模型（如Gemma-3-27B）中观察到了从“基于当前词”向“基于提前规划表征”的策略迁移。

Abstract: We study planning site formation in language models -- where internal representations of structurally-constrained future tokens form during the forward pass, and whether they causally drive generation. Using rhyming-couplet completion as a clean test of forward-looking constraint, we apply two lightweight methods (linear probing and activation patching) across Qwen3, Gemma-3, and Llama-3 at more than ten scales. Probing shows that future-rhyme information is linearly decodable at the line boundary, with signal that strengthens with scale in all three families. Activation patching reveals that only Gemma-3-27B causally relies on this encoding, exhibiting a handoff in which the causal driver migrates from the rhyme word to the line boundary around layer 30. Every other model we test conditions on the rhyme word throughout generation, with near-zero causal effect at the line boundary despite strong probe signal. We localize the Gemma-3-27B handoff to five attention heads through two-stage path patching that recover ~90% of the rhyme-routing capacity at the newline.

</details>


### [216] [Flexible Routing via Uncertainty Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07805)
*Charlotte Peale,Siddartha Devic,Parikshit Gopalan,Udi Wieder,Aravind Gollakota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于不确定性分解的模型路由新框架，通过区分可纠正误差（路由至Oracle）与固有模糊性（拒绝回答），实现更智能的成本性能平衡，且无需重训即可适应多变场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习系统中，需要在模型性能与计算成本之间取得平衡。现有的模型路由策略难以有效区分“因模型能力不足导致的可纠正错误（可约不确定性）”与“因数据本身模糊导致无法回答的问题（不可约不确定性）”。

Method: 基于高阶预测器（higher-order predictors）将总体不确定性分解为可约（reducible）和不可约（irreducible）成分。根据不确定性水平采取三种策略：低不确定性由弱模型预测，高可约不确定性路由至Oracle，高不可约不确定性拒绝回答。该方法支持通过简单调整超参数以适应不同的损失函数和成本，无需重训。

Result: 通过理论推导证明了该方法在任务特定最优路由策略下具有强后悔界（regret bounds）。实验表明，当可约不确定性和不可约不确定性相关性较低时，该方法在平衡性能与成本方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种利用不确定性分解（可约与不可约不确定性）进行模型路由的通用框架，在能够区分这两种不确定性的场景下，该方法能有效降低成本并保持高性能，并具备严格的后悔界（regret bound）理论保障。

Abstract: A key strategy for balancing performance and cost in modern machine learning systems is to dynamically route queries to either a low-cost model or a more expensive oracle (such as a large pretrained model or human expert), an approach known as model routing. In this work we present a new uncertainty-aware router that (1) avoids unnecessary oracle calls on inherently ambiguous queries, and (2) adapts dynamically to different loss functions and cost parameters through simple hyperparameter changes, without retraining. Our method, applicable to any classification setting where multiple independent annotations per input are available, is based on decomposing total uncertainty into irreducible and reducible components using higher-order predictors [Ahdritz et al., 2025]. This enables a unified approach to both routing and abstention: predict with the weak model when uncertainty is low, route to the oracle when reducible uncertainty is high, and abstain when irreducible uncertainty is high. Our router comes with strong theoretical guarantees bounding regret relative to optimal task-specific routers. We conduct experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets that demonstrate the benefits of our approach in suitable regimes -- in particular, whenever reducible and irreducible uncertainty are not too correlated.

</details>


### [217] [The Minimax Rate of Second-Order Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07808)
*Kamil Ciosek,Banafsheh Rafiee,Sina Ghiassian,Nicolò Felicioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了二阶校准误差的最优估计方法，通过解析核多项式回归将收敛速度提升至 $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$，并为不确定性校准提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二阶校准误差估计（如分桶或核平滑）收敛速度较慢（$O(n^{-1/4})$），需要更高效的估计方法以准确评估高阶预测器的认知不确定性。

Method: 利用 sech 扰动核（sech perturbation kernel）使校准函数在带状区域解析，并结合多项式回归（polynomial regression）进行误差估计。引入了无需分桶（bucket-free）的二阶校准定义。

Result: 实现了 $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$ 的估计收敛率，相较于传统方法有质的提升，并给出了匹配的 $\Omega(1/\sqrt{n})$ 下界。成功应用于二阶 Platt Scaling，实现了对预测平均值和不确定性的事后重校准。

Conclusion: 本文建立了二阶校准误差估计的极小极大最优性，证明了通过分析核方法达到的 $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$ 收敛率是理论最优的，并为二阶 Platt Scaling 提供了首个有限样本保证。

Abstract: We characterize the minimax rate of estimating the second-order calibration error for binary classification, which quantifies whether a higher-order predictor's epistemic-uncertainty estimate matches the conditional variance of the label probability on its level sets. Our key observation is that the sech perturbation kernel, previously used only to enforce smoothness of calibration functions, in fact makes them analytic in a strip of half-width $hπ/2$. Polynomial regression then estimates the calibration error at rate $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$, with explicit constants, a qualitative improvement over the $O(n^{-1/4})$ rate achievable by bucketing or kernel smoothing. A matching $Ω(1/\sqrt{n})$ lower bound establishes minimax optimality up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, we give the first finite-sample guarantee for second-order Platt scaling, yielding a post-hoc procedure that recalibrates both the mean prediction and the epistemic-variance estimate of any higher-order predictor. Along the way, we provide a bucket-free definition of second-order calibration and relate it quantitatively to the bucketed formulation of Ahdritz et al. [2025]. Our experiments confirm the predicted rate and the quality of the recalibrated uncertainties.

</details>


### [218] [Graph-Structured Hyperdimensional Computing for Data-Efficient and Explainable Process-Structure-Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07999)
*Jingzhan Ge,Ajeeth Vellore,Ajinkya Palwe,Ahsan Khan,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,SeungYeon Kang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PSP-HDC是一种针对复杂微结构制造的图结构超维计算框架，通过将知识编码为有向图结构，在小样本、异构数据环境下实现了高精度的PSP预测及具备内在可解释性的决策归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子光还原技术中，现有的过程-结构-属性（PSP）数据稀疏、异构且交互复杂，传统模型易产生伪相关、泛化能力差且缺乏解释性。

Method: PSP-HDC（图结构超维计算框架）：1. 利用标量到超向量编码器处理异构参数；2. 构建有向PSP依赖图，通过绑定（binding）和绑定（bundling）操作表征样本；3. 使用关联记忆（associative memory）检索进行预测与归因。

Result: 在3D平台片电阻预测任务中，PSP-HDC实现了0.910的预测准确率，在过程折叠（process-fold）泛化测试中达到0.896，优于传统基线模型。

Conclusion: PSP-HDC通过图结构超维计算框架有效解决了稀疏和异构数据下的PSP预测挑战，提供了一种兼具高预测精度和内在可解释性的通用建模方案。

Abstract: Multiphoton photoreduction enables high-fidelity fabrication of complex 3D microstructures, yet reliable process-structure-property (PSP) prediction remains difficult because the available data are sparse, heterogeneous, and interaction-dominated. In this regime, conventional feature-vector models are statistically underdetermined, making them prone to spurious correlations, poor regime transfer, and unstable post hoc explanations, whereas mechanistic pipelines depend on calibrated submodels that are rarely available during early process development. We present PSP-HDC, a graph-structured hyperdimensional computing framework that encodes a directed PSP graph as an internal prior for representation, inference, and explanation. A trainable scalar-to-hypervector encoder learns parameter-specific embeddings on a fixed hyperdimensional basis to accommodate heterogeneous scales and noise. Sample representations are then composed through graph-aligned binding and bundling along directed PSP dependencies, and prediction is performed by associative-memory retrieval against class prototypes. Because the same prototype memories support both decision making and attribution, PSP-HDC provides intrinsic explanations at the parameter, group, and within-group levels, while memory alignment and separation quantify prototype formation during training. On sheet-resistance regime prediction for the 3D platform, PSP-HDC achieves an accuracy of 0.910 +/- 0.077 over 1000 random splits and 0.896 under process-fold generalization, outperforming strong baselines.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Pairs: Your Language Model is Secretly Optimizing a Preference Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08037)
*Ning Liu,Chuanneng Sun,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有 DPO 无法充分利用多样本偏好结构的问题，论文提出了 GraphDPO，利用有向无环图进行偏好建模，实现了更高效、更稳定的模型对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接偏好优化（DPO）仅处理成对数据，无法充分利用多输出（rollouts）中蕴含的丰富结构信息，导致偏好传递性丢失、计算冗余以及训练不稳定。

Method: 提出 GraphDPO 方法：基于有向无环图建模多输出偏好；采用 Plackett-Luce 对数似然目标函数，通过图邻域聚合监督信号；引入等价类构建处理离散信号；配合 ground-truth 锚定和退火调度机制。保持线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在推理和程序合成任务上优于现有的成对对齐方法。该方法成功利用了全图结构信息，同时保持了高效的线性计算开销。

Conclusion: GraphDPO 将偏好建模扩展至图结构，在逻辑推理和程序合成任务中表现优于成对（pairwise）和列表（listwise）方法，是更稳健且高效的对齐优化方案。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aligns language models using pairwise preference comparisons, offering a simple and effective alternative to Reinforcement Learning (RL) from human feedback. However, in many practical settings, training data consists of multiple rollouts per prompt, inducing rich preference structure that pairwise DPO fails to exploit. Collapsing such data into independent pairs discards transitivity, introduces redundant or conflicting supervision, and can lead to unstable optimization. We propose Graph Direct Preference Optimization (GraphDPO), a principled generalization of DPO that operates over directed acyclic preference graphs induced by rollout rankings. GraphDPO encodes dominance relations as edges and optimizes a graph-structured Plackett--Luce-inspired objective that aggregates supervision over graph neighborhoods, enforcing transitivity while recovering standard DPO as a special case. To handle discrete or sparse signals, we introduce an equivalence-class construction where responses with identical preferences form graph layers, and intra-layer edges contribute zero loss, preventing spurious gradients. Despite leveraging full graph structure, GraphDPO maintains linear per-prompt complexity via efficient log-sum-exp aggregation. We further incorporate optional ground-truth anchoring by inserting verified solutions as dominant nodes and applying an annealed schedule that stabilizes early training while gradually relaxing oracle supervision. Experiments on reasoning and program synthesis tasks demonstrate superior performance, suggesting that graph-structured preference modeling is a scalable and robust alternative to pairwise and listwise alignment objectives.

</details>


### [220] [Enhancing Federated Quadruplet Learning: Stochastic Client Selection and Embedding Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07888)
*Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedQuad通过在联邦学习中显式优化特征表征的类内聚拢与类间分离，有效解决了数据异构带来的模型性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在处理非独立同分布（non-IID）数据时，由于数据分布异构、类别不平衡及样本量限制，导致全局模型泛化能力下降，表现为表征学习过程中的错位与坍缩。

Method: 提出FedQuad框架，结合度量学习（Metric Learning）思想，通过最小化类内距离和最大化类间距离，显式优化不同客户端的特征表征，从而解决聚合过程中的表征错位问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet等数据集的多样化non-IID设置下，FedQuad相比现有主流联邦学习基准方法表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: FedQuad通过在联邦学习中引入类内聚合与类间分离的度量学习机制，有效缓解了数据异构带来的表征不一致问题，提升了全局模型的泛化性能。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables decentralised model training across distributed clients without requiring data centralisation. However, the generalisation performance of the global model is usually degraded by data heterogeneity across clients, particularly under limited data availability and class imbalance. To address this challenge, we propose FedQuad, a novel method that explicitly enforces minimising intra-class representations while enabling inter-class splits across clients. By jointly minimising distances between positive pairs and maximising distances between negative pairs, the proposed approach mitigates representation misalignment introduced during model aggregation. We evaluate our method on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under diverse non-IID settings and varying numbers of clients, demonstrating consistent improvements over existing baselines. Additionally, we provide a comprehensive analysis of metric learning-based approaches in both centralised and federated environments, highlighting their effectiveness in alleviating representation collapse under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>


### [221] [Flatness and Gradient Alignment Are Both Necessary: Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration for Multi-Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07914)
*Aristotelis Ballas,Christos Diou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多分布学习，提出了一种同时优化损失函数平坦度和梯度对齐的 SAGE 算法，有效解决了现有方法仅关注单一几何属性导致的泛化缺陷，并在多个基准测试中创下性能新高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的锐度感知（Sharpness-aware）和梯度对齐（Gradient-alignment）方法仅关注损失函数景观的单一几何特性，忽略了在多分布学习场景下两者是独立且必要的风险来源。

Method: 提出 SAGE (Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration) 算法：1. 针对曲率项（Curvature），利用 Newton-Schulz 迭代计算梯度矩阵的极分解，优化扰动方向；2. 针对对齐项（Alignment），在下降步骤中引入与跨分布梯度差异成比例的各向同性噪声。

Result: 理论上推导出了包含曲率项和对齐项的过剩风险分解公式，并通过反例证明了两者无法相互界定。实验验证了 SAGE 在 DomainBed 和多任务学习基准上均达到了 SOTA 或更优水平。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 SAGE 方法通过同时优化平坦度（基于 Hessian）与梯度对齐（基于梯度协方差），突破了现有方法仅关注单一几何特性的局限，在多分布学习任务中实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware and gradient-alignment methods have been shown to improve generalization, however each family of methods targets a single geometric property of the loss landscape, while ignoring the other. In this paper, we show that this omission is structurally unavoidable and that both flatness and gradient alignment should be considered in multi-distribution learning settings. Specifically, we derive an excess-risk decomposition that yields two additive leading-order terms: (i) an alignment term, controlled by the trace of $\bar{H}^{-1}Σ_g$ and (ii) a curvature term, controlled by $\bar{H}$, where $\bar{H}$ is the average Hessian and $Σ_g$ is the covariance of the gradient across distributions. Notably, $\bar{H}$ appears inverted in one and non-inverted in the other. We further show, via a counterexample, that neither quantity bounds the other in general, so no algorithm targeting only one term can guarantee low excess risk. Motivated by this decomposition, we propose SAGE (Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration) that targets both terms. The curvature component replaces SAM's gradient-scaled perturbation with the polar factor of each layer's gradient matrix, computed via Newton-Schulz iteration, so that the ascent step probes all directions with similar magnitude. On the other hand, the alignment component injects isotropic noise at the descent step, the magnitude of which scales with cross-distribution gradient disagreement. Experiments on five domain-generalization and two multi-task learning benchmarks show that the proposed method establishes a new state-of-the-art on DomainBed and acts as a general-purpose improvement to base MTL solvers, remaining competitive with, or even surpassing, state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [On the Complexity of the Matching Problem of Regular Expressions with Backreferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07289)
*Soh Kumabe,Yuya Uezato*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文深入探讨了带回溯（backreferences）的正则表达式匹配问题的计算复杂度，揭示了其在复杂情况下的硬度，并为1-use表达式提供了一套显著更高效的近线性时间匹配算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则表达式拒绝服务攻击（ReDoS）造成的严重实际安全隐患，促使研究者探寻在支持强大功能（如回溯）的情况下，正则表达式匹配引擎能否实现可证明的高效运行。

Method: 1. 复杂度理论分析：基于SETH和参数化复杂性（W-hard等级）证明了REWBs匹配问题的下界。2. 算法设计：针对1-use REWBs，利用后缀树（suffix trees）、正则表达式的转移幺半群（transition monoids）、因子分解森林（factorization forest）及字符串周期性分析，设计了O(n log² n)的算法。

Result: 1. 下界：证明了k-REWBs问题在SETH下无法在O(n^{2k-ε})时间内解决；证明了该问题关于表达式长度是W[2]-hard的；2-use 2-REWBs在特定条件下无法实现线性时间求解。2. 上界：提出了一种针对1-use REWBs的O(n log² n)优化算法，显著优于现有成果。

Conclusion: 带回溯的正则表达式（REWBs）匹配问题的复杂度存在内在壁垒，单次使用（1-use）的REWBs可以实现近线性时间求解，但对于更高复杂度的表达式，该问题在计算上是困难的。

Abstract: ReDoS is a well-known type of algorithmic complexity attack, where an adversary supplies maliciously crafted strings to a regular expression matching engine, aiming to exhaust computational resources of systems. Even quadratic-time behavior in matching engines has been exploited in successful attacks, as exemplified by major outages at Stack Overflow (2016) and Cloudflare (2019). These incidents motivate a fundamental question: Is it possible to construct matching engines that are provably efficient, running in (near-)linear time in the length of the input string?
  For classical regular expressions (REGEX), Thompson's construction yields a linear-time algorithm. However, practical engines support powerful features such as backreferences, which strictly extend the expressive power of REGEX but unfortunately increase the risk of ReDoS attacks.
  This paper investigates the fine-grained complexity of the string matching problem for regular expressions with backreferences (REWBs). Specifically, we consider $r$-use $k$-REWBs.
  On the hardness side, we show that the string matching problem for $k$-REWBs cannot be solved in $O(n^{2k-ε})$ time for any $ε> 0$ under SETH. We also prove that this problem is \textbf{W[2]}-hard when parameterized by the length of the REWB expression, strengthening the previous \textbf{W[1]}-hardness. Moreover, we prove that this problem for $2$-use $2$-REWBs cannot be solved in $n^{1+o(1)}$ time unless the triangle detection problem can be solved in that time.
  On the algorithmic side, we present an $O(n \log^2 n)$-time algorithm for $1$-use REWBs, which significantly improves upon the recent $O(n^2)$-time algorithm by Nogami and Terauchi (MFCS, 2025). Our algorithm employs several techniques including suffix trees, transition monoids of REGEXes, factorization forest data structures, and periodicity of strings.

</details>


### [223] [Towards Settling the Complexity of the Lettericity Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07899)
*Mario Grobler,Nils Morawietz,Silas Cato Sacher*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了图的字面性（lettericity）问题，证明了相关检索任务的复杂性，并发现限制为对称解码器时，字面性等同于邻域多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的字面性问题（lettericity problem）的计算复杂性尚不明确，本文旨在通过分析相关检索问题的复杂度，为理解字面性的计算特性提供基础。

Method: 研究字面性（graph lettericity）的定义及其变体，通过分析“字”、“解码器”和“着色”三元组之间的检索关系来探讨该问题的计算复杂性，并引入对称限制条件。

Result: 字检索和解码器检索为多项式时间可解；着色检索问题与图同构问题等价；对称字面性简化为线性时间可解的邻域多样性问题。

Conclusion: 对称字面性（symmetric lettericity）等同于图的邻域多样性（neighborhood diversity），可在线性时间内计算；字面性（lettericity）问题的复杂度仍未解决，但其相关的字检索和解码器检索问题可在多项式时间内求解，着色检索问题等同于图同构问题。

Abstract: The lettericity of a graph $G=(V,E)$ is defined as the smallest size of an alphabet $Σ$ such that there is a word $w_1 \dots w_{|V|} \in Σ^*$ and a decoder $\mathcal{D} \subseteq Σ^2$ with the property that $G$ is isomorphic to the letter graph $G(\mathcal{D}, w)$, that is, the graph with vertex set $\{1, \dots, n\}$ and edge set $\{ij \mid 1\leq i < j \leq n, w_iw_j \in \mathcal{D}\}$. Note that $G(\mathcal{D}, w)$ can be seen as a graph with inherent coloring $χ\colon V(G) \rightarrow Σ$. It is unknown whether the lettericity of a given graph can be computed in polynomial time. The problem to determine the lettericity of a given graph is called the lettericity problem. As a step towards answering the complexity of this problem, we investigate the following retrieval problems: given a graph $G$ together with two of the three solution-objects (word $w$, decoder $\mathcal{D}$, and coloring $χ$), the goal is to compute the third solution-object. We show that word retrieval and decoder retrieval are solvable in polynomial time, while coloring retrieval is equivalent to the graph isomorphism problem. Beyond this, we introduce symmetric lettericity which is a restricted version of lettericity where each decoder needs to be symmetrical ($ab\in \mathcal{D}$ if and only if $ba\in \mathcal{D}$). As we show, the symmetric lettericity of a graph always equals the neighborhood diversity of the graph, which in fact can be computed in linear time.

</details>


### [224] [Parameterized Local Search for Vertex Cover: When only the Search Radius is Crucial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07941)
*Christian Komusiewicz,Nils Morawietz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对计算复杂的LS Vertex Cover问题，本文提出了利用多种图结构参数设计的参数化算法，实现了在小参数k下保证运行时间的有效控制，并推广至加权版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LS Vertex Cover问题在参数k下是W[1]-hard的。为了在实际应用中控制运行时间与解质量的平衡，需寻找在图结构参数上具有“温和”增长特性（即f(k)poly(n)）的算法，以规避参数k带来的复杂性挑战。

Method: 通过利用图的结构参数（h-index, treewidth, modular-width, 模块化分解中的最大度）设计算法，将问题转化为在这些受限结构上寻找满足条件的k-swap。针对加权情况，通过优化算法逻辑以支持权重改进目标d。

Result: 针对h-index、树宽、模宽以及模块化分解中的最大度等参数，取得了运行时间为$\ell^{f(k)}\cdot n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$的高效算法。同时，这些算法成功扩展到了加权顶点覆盖问题的d-改进k-swap查找中。

Conclusion: 针对LS Vertex Cover问题，在结构参数（如h-index、树宽、模宽及模块化分解中的最大度等）下，实现了运行时间在参数上呈指数增长但在图规模上呈多项式增长的算法，并将其推广至加权及d-改进版本。

Abstract: A vertex set $W$ in a graph $G$ is a valid $k$-swap for a vertex cover $S$ of $G$ if $W$ has size at most $k$ and $S'=(S \setminus W) \cup (W \setminus S)$, the symmetric difference of $S$ and $W$, is a vertex cover of $G$. If $|S'| < |S|$, then $W$ is improving. In LS Vertex Cover, one is given a vertex cover $S$ of a graph $G$ and wants to know if there is a valid improving $k$-swap for $S$ in $G$. In applications of LS Vertex Cover, $k$ is a very small parameter that can be set by a user to determine the trade-off between running time and solution quality. Consequently, $k$ can be considered to be a constant. Motivated by this and the fact that LS Vertex Cover is W[1]-hard with respect to $k$, we aim for algorithms with running time $\ell^{f(k)}\cdot n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ where $\ell$ is a structural graph parameter upper-bounded by $n$. We say that such a running time grows mildly with respect to $\ell$ and strongly with respect to $k$. We obtain algorithms with such a running time for $\ell$ being the $h$-index of $G$, the treewidth of $G$, or the modular-width of $G$. In addition, we consider a novel parameter, the maximum degree over all quotient graphs in a modular decomposition of $G$. Moreover, we adapt these algorithms to the more general problem where each vertex is assigned a weight and where we want to find a valid $d$-improving $k$-swap, that is, a valid $k$-swap which decreases the weight of the vertex cover by at least $d$.

</details>


### [225] [Planarizing Gadgets for (k, l)-tight Graphs Do Not Exist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08016)
*Archit Chauhan,Rohit Gurjar,Kilian Rothmund,Thomas Thierauf*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文否定了利用“平面化小工具”将(k, l)-紧图识别问题从一般图归约为平面图的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (k, l)-紧图的识别在图刚性等领域至关重要，但目前在平面图上有确定性NC算法，而在一般图上尚无此类算法。为了解决此差距，研究者试图通过“平面化小工具”将一般图简化为平面图，而本文旨在探讨这种方法的可行性。

Method: 理论证明，通过证明不存在能够保留特定结构属性（在此处为(k, l)-紧性）的平面化小工具，从而否定了将该问题简化为平面图情况的可能性。

Result: 证明了在一般情况下，不存在用于(k, l)-紧图识别问题的平面化小工具，打破了通过这种归约方式在一般图上获得高效算法的预期。

Conclusion: 作者证明了对于(k, l)-紧图识别问题，不存在通用的平面化小工具（planarizing gadgets）。这意味着试图通过平面化将一般图问题归约为平面图问题的方法在该特定问题上是行不通的。

Abstract: The problem of recognizing (k, l)-tight graphs is a fundamental problem that has close connections to well studied problems like graph rigidity. The problem is better understood for planar graphs as compared to general graphs. For example, deterministic NC-algorithms for the problem are known for planar graphs, but no such algorithm is known for general graphs. A common approach to reduce a graph problem to the planar case is to use planarizing gadgets. Our main contribution is to show that, unconditionally, planarizing gadgets for the problem of recognizing (k, l)-tight graphs do not exist.

</details>


### [226] [Equivalence of Coarse and Fine-Grained Models for Learning with Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07005)
*Adam R. Klivans,Shyamal Patel,Konstantinos Stavropoulos,Arsen Vasilyan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了分布漂移下PQ学习与TDS学习模型的等价性，确立了基础类别的学习硬度，并证明了成员查询可有效提升学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于分布漂移下高效学习算法的研究集中在PQ学习和TDS学习两个模型上，由于两者在拒绝机制上的区别，作者旨在探讨这两个学习模型之间是否存在理论上的联系或区别，并探索特定概念类的计算复杂性。

Method: 1. 建立了一个从PQ学习到TDS学习的黑盒归约（black-box reduction）；2. 引入了一种基于分支程序（branching programs）的提升（boosting）方法，用以增强TDS学习器在拒绝目标域后的区分能力；3. 利用迭代式的Forster变换算法来实现含成员查询的半空间PQ学习。

Result: 1. 证明了在分布无关设置下，PQ学习与TDS学习在计算上是等价的；2. 推导出半空间等基础概念类在TDS学习下的首个硬度结果；3. 证明了在提供成员查询的情况下，半空间可以实现高效的分布无关PQ学习。

Conclusion: PQ学习与TDS学习在分布无关（distribution-free）设置下是等价的。该研究证明了TDS学习在处理基础概念类（如半空间）时存在硬度（hardness），并指出成员查询（membership queries）可以绕过这些限制。

Abstract: Recent work on provably efficient algorithms for learning with distribution shift has focused on two models: PQ learning (Goldwasser et al. (2020)) and TDS learning (Klivans et al. (2024)). Algorithms for TDS learning are allowed to reject a test set entirely if distribution shift is detected. In contrast, PQ learners may only reject points that are deemed out-of-distribution on an individual basis. Our main result is a surprising equivalence between these two models in the distribution-free setting. In particular, we give an efficient black-box reduction from PQ learning to TDS learning for any Boolean concept class. This equivalence implies the first hardness results for distribution-free TDS learning of basic classes such as halfspaces. The main technical contribution underlying our equivalence is a method for boosting, via branching programs, the weak distinguishing power of TDS learners that have rejected the target domain.
  We also show that giving a learner access to membership queries sidesteps these hardness results and allows for efficient, distribution-free PQ learnability of halfspaces. Our algorithm iteratively recovers large-margin separators obtained by applying successive Forster transforms on the training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Bridging Textual Profiles and Latent User Embeddings for Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06981)
*Zhaoxuan Tan,Xiang Zhai,Yan Zhu,Meng Jiang,Mohamed Hammad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BLUE 是一种通过强化学习将文本用户画像与嵌入式推荐目标对齐的框架，旨在实现既具可解释性又具备高下游推荐性能的用户表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用户表征方法中，监督式潜在嵌入（难以解释）与文本用户画像（难以优化下游效用）之间存在脱节。该研究旨在弥合这一鸿沟，兼顾用户表示的可解释性与推荐任务的有效性。

Method: 提出了名为 BLUE 的强化学习框架，利用 Profiler LLM 生成文本配置文件，并结合嵌入模型的奖励信号（根据推荐结果）和基于下一项预测的文本空间监督信号，通过强化学习策略对文本配置文件进行优化对齐。

Result: 在 Amazon Reviews 2023 和 Google Local Reviews 数据集上的零样本顺序推荐任务中，BLUE 优于多个强基线模型；在跨域迁移任务中表现出较强的泛化能力；此外，生成的配置文件在问答任务中提供了更优的个性化上下文。

Conclusion: BLUE 有效地统一了可解释的文本建模与判别式潜在嵌入，为个性化系统中用户表征的学习提供了新的范式，在零样本顺序推荐和跨域迁移任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Personalized systems rely on user representations to connect behavioral history with downstream recommendation applications. Existing methods typically employ either supervised latent user embeddings, which are effective for retrieval but difficult to interpret, or textual user profiles, which are interpretable but challenging to optimize for downstream utility due to lack of direct supervision. To bridge this gap, we present BLUE, a reinforcement learning framework that unifies these two forms of user representation by aligning language-based user profiles with embedding-based recommendation objectives. Given a user interaction history, BLUE leverages a profiler Large Language Model (LLM) to generate textual profiles, while an embedding model provides reward signals. This encourages the resulting textual representations to move closer to positive items and farther from negative ones in the embedding space. We further introduce a text-space supervision signal based on next-item prediction, ensuring the learned profiles remain both semantically meaningful and highly effective for downstream retrieval. Experiments on Amazon Reviews 2023 and Google Local Reviews in zero-shot sequential recommendation settings demonstrate that BLUE consistently outperforms strong baselines under both frozen and trainable embedding conditions. Notably, BLUE achieves clear gains in cross-domain transfer, highlighting the strong generalization ability of the learned user profiles. Furthermore, these generated profiles provide superior personalized context for question answering compared to raw user histories or alternative profile optimization methods. Overall, these results show that BLUE provides an effective way to unify interpretable textual profiling with discriminative latent embeddings for personalization.

</details>


### [228] [An Embarrassingly Simple Graph Heuristic Reveals Shortcut-Solvable Benchmarks for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07125)
*Haoyu Han,Li Ma,Hanbing Wang,Bingheng Li,Daochen Zha,Chun How Tan,Huiji Gao,Xin Liu,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya,Hui Liu,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 深度学习推荐模型在现有基准上表现出的强大能力可能源于数据集本身包含“简单捷径”而非模型的高级建模能力；一个简单的基于图的启发式方法即可击败大多数复杂基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对序列化推荐领域中生成式模型在常用基准测试上表现出色，但其是否真的具备了所声称的高级序列/语义建模能力这一疑问，进行了深入审计。

Method: 设计了一个简单的图启发式算法（仅基于最近的1-2个交互物品，通过少跳物品转移图检索候选集，并按特征相似度排序），并对比了14个数据集上复杂模型与该启发式方法的表现。同时，识别了导致模型出现“捷径”的三种结构特征：低分支局部转换、特征平滑转换以及对长期历史依赖有限。

Result: 即使不使用序列编码器或生成式目标，该简单的启发式方法在14个数据集中的10个上仍能匹配或超越现代基线模型（在Amazon Sports和CDs数据集上NDCG@10指标分别提升了38.10%和44.18%）。

Conclusion: 现有的推荐系统基准测试存在“捷径可解性”问题，导致简单的启发式方法能击败复杂的生成式模型。研究呼吁在评估新模型时应进行更细致的数据集选择和诊断分析，而非仅依赖常用基准。
