Abstract: Sequential recommendation has increasingly shifted toward generative recommenders that combine sequential patterns with semantic item information. Yet these methods are often evaluated on a small set of widely used benchmarks, raising a key question: do these benchmarks actually require the advanced modeling capabilities that modern generative recommenders claim to provide? We conduct a benchmark audit with an intentionally simple graph heuristic. Starting from only the last one or two interacted items, it retrieves candidates from a few-hop item-transition graph and ranks them by item-feature similarity. Despite using no sequence encoder, generative objective, or training, this heuristic matches or outperforms many modern baselines, with relative NDCG@10 improvements of 38.10% and 44.18% over the best competing baseline on Amazon Review Sports and CDs.
  We show that this behavior reflects shortcut solvability rather than an artifact of one heuristic. We identify three shortcut structures that can make next-item prediction easier than expected: low-branching local transitions, feature-smooth transitions, and limited dependence on long user histories. These shortcuts need not appear together; even one or two strong signals can make simple local retrieval highly competitive, while weakening them makes the benefits of more sophisticated models clearer. Across 14 datasets, model rankings vary substantially with dataset properties, yet the heuristic remains competitive on 10 of them. Our findings suggest that strong performance on standard benchmarks does not always demonstrate advanced sequential, semantic, or generative modeling ability. We call for more careful dataset selection and dataset-level diagnostic analysis when using benchmarks to support claims about new recommendation models.

</details>


### [229] [RRCM: Ranking-Driven Retrieval over Collaborative and Meta Memories for LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07129)
*Shijun Li,Wooseong Yang,Yu Wang,Tianxin Wei,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RRCM 是一种由排名驱动的检索与推理框架，允许 LLM 动态获取协作行为和元数据证据，通过策略优化提升推荐准确性，有效解决了传统 LLM 推荐系统上下文构建僵化和效率低下的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 推荐系统存在两点核心缺陷：1. 上下文构建策略僵化（预定义提示词/静态检索），无法按需提取有用信息；2. 异构证据导致上下文窗口拥挤，压缩/过滤机制失效，难以平衡效率与精度。

Method: 提出 RRCM 框架，通过排名驱动的检索与推理策略（RL），在用户历史、协作行为与元数据存储之间动态选择证据，并采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）直接根据推荐质量优化存储读取策略。

Result: 实验表明，RRCM 在各类数据集上均显著优于传统基线模型及现有的 LLM 推荐方法。

Conclusion: RRCM 通过引入基于排名的检索与推理框架，成功解决了 LLM 推荐系统在异构证据处理中的上下文构建僵化与效率瓶颈问题，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for next-generation recommender systems, offering strong semantic understanding and natural-language reasoning abilities. Despite recent progress, current LLM-based recommenders still face key challenges in constructing decision-relevant contexts from heterogeneous evidence. First, existing methods often rely on fixed context construction strategies: collaborative behavioral evidence and item-side metadata are typically incorporated through predefined prompts, static retrieval pipelines, or handcrafted injection mechanisms, making it difficult to determine what information is truly beneficial for each instance. Second, heterogeneous evidence introduces a severe context-efficiency bottleneck. Rich metadata and collaborative interaction records can quickly overwhelm the context window, while aggressive compression or heuristic filtering may discard fine-grained evidence critical for accurate recommendation. To address these challenges, we propose RRCM, a ranking-driven retrieval-and-reasoning framework over collaborative and metadata memories for LLM-based agentic recommendation. RRCM starts from a lightweight user-history context and learns whether to recommend directly, retrieve collaborative evidence, retrieve item metadata, or interleave both through reasoning. Both memories are represented in natural language and accessed through a unified retrieval interface, enabling flexible evidence acquisition without handcrafted CF injection or fixed retrieval rules. We optimize this memory-reading policy with an outcome-only ranking reward, instantiated using group relative policy optimization, so that retrieval decisions are directly driven by final top-k recommendation quality. Extensive experiments show that RRCM significantly outperforms traditional baselines and diverse LLM-based recommendation approaches.

</details>


### [230] [DiffRetriever: Parallel Representative Tokens for Retrieval with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07210)
*Shuai Wang,Yin Yu,Shengyao Zhuang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DiffRetriever 利用扩散模型的双向并行推断能力，有效解决了自回归模型在生成多 Token 表示时的时延和性能瓶颈，实现了高效、高质量的稠密和稀疏检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型作为检索器（如 PromptReps）在推广到多 Token 表示时面临效率瓶颈——即由于必须顺序生成 Token，导致多 Token 变体不仅无法稳定提升性能，反而增加了显著的时延。研究者旨在通过扩散模型特有的并行生成特性来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出 DiffRetriever：一种适用于扩散语言模型的代表性 Token 检索器。其核心是在提示中附加 K 个掩蔽（masked）位置，并通过单次双向传播并行生成 K 个表示，而非依赖自回归的顺序生成。此外，还使用了监督微调方法。

Result: 在所有测试的扩散骨干模型上，多 Token 的 DiffRetriever 均显著优于单 Token 模型。相比之下，自回归模型的多 Token 方案性能提升停滞或下降。DiffRetriever 在 BEIR-7 基准测试中优于 PromptReps、DiffEmbed 和 RepLLaMA，成为同类对比中的最强模型。

Conclusion: DiffRetriever 证明了扩散语言模型（DLM）在处理多 Token 表示检索任务时具有显著优势。通过利用双向并行推理能力，它克服了自回归模型在多 Token 表示上的时延瓶颈，实现了检索性能的稳定提升，并在 BEIR-7 基准测试中展现了卓越的竞争力。

Abstract: PromptReps showed that an autoregressive language model can be used directly as a retriever by prompting it to generate dense and sparse representations of a query or passage. Extending this to multiple representatives is inefficient for autoregressive models, since tokens must be generated sequentially, and prior multi-token variants did not reliably improve over single-token decoding.
  We show that the bottleneck is sequential generation, not the multi-token idea itself. DiffRetriever is a representative-token retriever for diffusion language models: it appends K masked positions to the prompt and reads all K in a single bidirectional forward pass. Across in-domain and out-of-domain evaluation, multi-token DiffRetriever substantially improves over single-token on every diffusion backbone we test, while autoregressive multi-token is flat or negative and pays a latency cost that scales with K where diffusion does not. After supervised fine-tuning, DiffRetriever on Dream is the strongest BEIR-7 retriever in our comparison, ahead of PromptReps, the encoder-style DiffEmbed baseline on the same diffusion backbones, and the contrastively fine-tuned single-vector RepLLaMA. A per-query oracle on the frozen base model exceeds contrastive fine-tuning at the same fixed budget, pointing to adaptive budget selection as future work. Code is available at https://github.com/ielab/diffretriever.

</details>


### [231] [LARAG: Link-Aware Retrieval Strategy for RAG Systems in Hyperlinked Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07517)
*Giorgia Bolognesi,Claudio Estatico,Ulderico Fugacci,Isabella Mastroianni,Claudio Muselli,Luca Oneto*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LARAG通过利用技术文档中原有的超链接结构来优化RAG检索，在无需复杂图建模的情况下，以更低的成本实现了更高质量、更具事实性的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基于嵌入（embedding）的检索器通常将具有结构化的文档（如技术手册）视为扁平的片段集合，忽略了用户在导航这些内容时所依赖的超链接拓扑结构，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 提出LARAG（Link-Aware RAG）：通过将HTML文档中已有的超链接关系作为元数据编码到文本片段（chunk）的表示中，从而在检索过程中实现对局部相关内容进行“图状”获取的策略。

Result: 在Rulex平台技术文档的二十个专家设计查询及四种提示策略的基准测试中，LARAG的表现优于基准RAG架构，在提升回答质量的同时，降低了计算资源消耗（检索更少片段，生成更少Token）。

Conclusion: 直接利用技术文档现有的超链接拓扑结构，无需显式图构建或推理，即可实现隐含的图状检索。这不仅提高了回答的质量（更高的BERTScore F1），还减少了检索片段数量和生成Token数，实现了更高效、更具忠实度的RAG流程。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the factual grounding of Large Language Models by conditioning their outputs on external documents. However, standard embedding-based retrievers treat naturally structured corpora, such as technical manuals, as flat collections of passages, thereby overlooking the hyperlink topology that users rely on when navigating such content.
  We introduce LARAG (Link-Aware RAG): a lightweight, link-aware retrieval strategy that leverages the author-defined hyperlink structure already present in HTML documentation, encoding hyperlink relations as metadata in the chunk representations and exploiting them to perform a form of graph-like retrieval of locally relevant content.
  In a benchmark of twenty expert-designed queries over Rulex Platform technical documentation and four prompting strategies, LARAG consistently improves answer quality, achieving the highest BERTScore F1, while retrieving fewer chunks and generating fewer tokens than a baseline RAG architecture used for comparison. These results show that directly leveraging the existing hyperlink topology of technical documentation, even without explicit graph construction or inference, enables an implicit form of graph-like retrieval that yields a more faithful and efficient RAG pipeline, providing better grounding at lower cost.

</details>


### [232] [TRACE: Tourism Recommendation with Accountable Citation Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07677)
*Zixu Zhao,Sijin Wang,Yu Hou,Yuanyuan Xu,Yufan Sheng,Xike Xie,Wenjie Zhang,Won-Yong Shin,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对旅游推荐系统缺乏真实可信度评估的问题，论文提出了TRACE基准，通过多轮对话、评论引用和拒绝恢复三个维度对系统进行综合评估，揭示了当前LLM与检索模型在准确性、可验证性和自适应性方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅游对话推荐系统（CRS）缺乏对多轮对话、评论证据标注和被拒后的自适应恢复能力的评估，无法应对旅游场景对可信度与可验证性的高要求。

Method: 构建了包含10,000个多轮对话、覆盖2,400个兴趣点（POI）和34,208条评论的数据集，提出了由“准确性、证据标注（Grounding）、恢复能力”三个维度构成的25项评估指标，并对比了14种包含LLM、检索和规划方法的基线模型。

Result: LLM在准确率和恢复能力上表现较好但引用质量不足；纯检索模型可实现文本引用但准确率低；多评论合成方法在对话恢复上表现欠佳。此外，定义的Grounding Score与人工标注高度相关（rho=0.80）。

Conclusion: TRACE通过构建多维评估框架，指出了现有推荐系统在旅游场景下的“三维能力差距”，证明了旅游推荐应被重构为融合任务（正确推荐、可验证证据、自适应修正），而非单一指标的性能竞争。

Abstract: Tourism is a high-stakes setting for conversational recommender systems (CRS): a plausible-sounding suggestion can waste real money and trip time once a traveler acts on it. Existing CRS benchmarks primarily evaluate systems with a single Recall@k score over entity mentions, and tourism-specific resources add spatial or knowledge-graph context, yet none of them couple multi-turn recommendation with verbatim review-span evidence and rejection recovery. This leaves an evaluation gap for tourism recommendation that is simultaneously trustworthy, verifiable, and adaptive: recommend the right point of interest (POI) for multi-aspect preferences (such as cuisine, price, atmosphere, walking distance), justify each suggestion with verifiable evidence from prior visitors so the traveler can act without trial and error, and recover when the first recommendation is rejected mid-dialogue. We introduce TRACE, where each item is a multi-turn tourism recommendation dialogue with review-span citations and explicit rejection turns: 10,000 dialogues over 2,400 Yelp POIs and 34,208 reviews across eight U.S. cities, paired with 14 retrieval, planning, and LLM baselines, along with 25 metrics organized under Accuracy, Grounding, and Recovery. Across these baselines, TRACE reveals the Three-Competency Gap: LLM Zero-Shot leads in closed-set Recall@1 and rejection recovery but cites less densely than retrievers; non-LLM retrievers achieve surface-verbatim grounding but with low accuracy; Multi-Review Synthesis fails at recovery. The Grounding Score agrees with human citation precision (Spearman rho=+0.80, p<10^-20), and paired t-tests reproduce the per-baseline ranking (p<0.01 on the dominant contrasts). TRACE reframes accountable tourism recommendation as a joint target (right POI, verifiable evidence, adaptive repair) rather than a single-axis leaderboard.

</details>


### [233] [FAVOR: Efficient Filter-Agnostic Vector ANNS Based on Selectivity-Aware Exclusion Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07770)
*Junjie Song,Yu Liu,Guoyu Hu,Zhongle Xie,Ming Yang,Beng Chin Ooi,Ke Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FAVOR 是一种高效的、与过滤逻辑无关的向量检索方案，通过动态距离重构和搜索策略路由，解决了混合检索在高选择性场景下的性能瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 HNSW 的行内过滤方法在高选择性（低过滤比例）场景下性能不佳，难以平衡搜索效率、过滤通用性和索引连通性。

Method: 1. 集成架构：统一选择性估计与过滤感知搜索；2. 排除距离机制 (Exclusion Distance)：通过动态调整图内距离分布，优化非目标向量的路径，从而提高检索效率；3. 选择性驱动的搜索选择器：根据查询选择性在暴力预过滤和优化后的 HNSW 搜索之间进行动态路由。

Result: 在真实数据集上，对比现有顶尖方法，FAVOR 在 Recall@10=95% 的条件下实现了 1.3-5 倍的 QPS 提升，并表现出在面对任意过滤条件时的稳健性。

Conclusion: FAVOR 提出了一种高效、通用的混合检索方案，通过集成查询选择器和排除距离机制，在保持对任意过滤条件支持的同时，显著提升了混合查询的吞吐量。

Abstract: Modern retrieval systems increasingly require integrating approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) with complex attribute filtering to handle hybrid queries in applications such as recommendation systems and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). While HNSW-based inline-filtering methods show promise, existing approaches struggle to deliver high throughput under low-selectivity scenarios while balancing search efficiency, filtering generality, and index connectivity. To address these challenges, we propose FAVOR, an efficient filter-agnostic vector ANNS that supports arbitrary filtering conditions while maintaining stable performance across varying selectivity levels. FAVOR introduces three novel features: (1) an integrated architecture that unifies selectivity estimation and filtered ANNS execution, providing a cohesive solution for hybrid vector-attribute queries; (2) a HNSW-based inline-filtering algorithm that introduces an exclusion distance mechanism to dynamically reshape the vector distance distribution, pushing non-target vectors away from the query while promoting valid candidates toward the query, thus improving search efficiency without compromising generality or graph connectivity; and (3) a selectivity-driven search selector that estimates query selectivity and dynamically routes queries between a pre-filtering brute-force algorithm for low-selectivity cases and an optimized HNSW-based search algorithm for other scenarios, ensuring consistent performance. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that FAVOR achieves a 1.3-5$\times$ higher QPS at $Recall@10 = 95\%$ compared to state-of-the-art methods for arbitrary filtering conditions, while maintaining competitive performance even against tailored solutions in some filtering conditions.

</details>


### [234] [Topic Is Not Agenda: A Citation-Community Audit of Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07158)
*Junseon Yoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 现有的向量嵌入模型在学术检索中虽然能区分大致领域，但在精细的“科研议程”匹配上表现极差（8成检索错误）。研究表明，仅靠文本向量无法胜任专业任务，引入引文图谱结构信息能有效弥补这一性能断层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量搜索和RAG依赖于“余弦相似度等于概念相关性”的假设。本文旨在量化该假设在科学文献检索中何时失效，并探索如何利用引文结构信息弥补嵌入模型的缺陷。

Method: 1. 构建包含358万篇论文的增强引文图谱，并通过Leiden CPM算法划分为子领域（L1）和科研议程（L2）两个颗粒度。
2. 在8个科学领域测试4种SOTA嵌入模型（Gemini, Qwen3-8B/0.6B, SPECTER2）的检索能力。
3. 设计对比实验，利用引文计数（Citation-count）重排序作为诊断探针，验证图结构信息对检索的补充作用。

Result: 1. SOTA模型在L1（子领域）尚可，但在L2（科研议程）遭遇“通用性失效”，仅15-21%的检索结果符合研究议程，80%的检索结果处于议程之外。
2. 即使是经过引文对比训练的SPECTER2表现依然最差。
3. 使用简单的引文计数重排序方法，在L2任务上比最佳嵌入模型（Gemini）高出约9个百分点，比纯BM25高出20个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有的向量语义嵌入（Embedding）无法捕捉精细的科研议程（L2颗粒度），导致通用RAG在科学领域表现不佳。引入引用图（Citation Graph）信息能显著提升检索精度，证明了单纯依赖余弦相似度进行知识检索存在根本性的理论与实践局限。

Abstract: Vector search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) rest on the assumption that cosine similarity between text embeddings reflects conceptual relatedness. We measure where this assumption breaks. We build an augmented citation graph over 3.58M scientific papers and partition it via Leiden CPM at two granularities: sub-field (L1) and research-agenda (L2, hierarchical inside each L1). Four state-of-the-art embeddings (Gemini, Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-0.6B, SPECTER2) clear the L1 bar reasonably (45-52% top-10 same-rate) but stop working at L2: only 15-21% of top-10 neighbors share the query's research agenda. In absolute terms, 8 of every 10 retrieved papers are off-agenda. The failure is universal across eight scientific domains and all four models; SPECTER2, despite its citation-based contrastive training, is the weakest. As a diagnostic probe, we test whether the same augmented graph also functions as a retrieval signal: a deliberately simple citation-count rerank reaches 57.7% top-1 L2 on top of LLM-expanded Boolean retrieval and 59.6% on top of plain BM25, on 80 curated agenda queries -- about 9 points above the best cosine retriever (Gemini, 50.6%) and 20 points above BM25 alone (39.3%). The probe isolates a slice of the agenda-matching signal the graph carries but the embeddings miss, connecting recent theoretical limits on single-vector retrieval to a concrete failure mode of scientific RAG.

</details>


### [235] [DCGL: Dual-Channel Graph Learning with Large Language Models for Knowledge-Aware Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07314)
*Xinchi Zou,Tongzhenzhi Su,Jianjun Li,Yuan Fu,Chang Liu,Zhiying Deng,Zhiwei Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对当前KG+LLM推荐系统在语义建模、嵌入耦合及数据分布适应性上的局限，提出DCGL框架实现双通道解耦、跨视图对比对齐及交互频率自适应融合，显著提升了推荐系统的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合知识图谱与大语言模型的推荐系统存在三个痛点：1) 对显式知识图谱链接之外的隐式语义建模不足；2) ID与LLM嵌入融合导致的信号干扰与表示模糊；3) 未充分考虑用户-物品交互频率差异带来的偏差。

Method: 提出了双通道图学习（DCGL）框架：1) 双通道架构实现语义与行为的结构化解耦；2) 多级对比学习机制（视图内对比与视图间对齐）以提升鲁棒性并弥合语义鸿沟；3) 基于交互频率的动态融合机制以自适应调整推荐策略。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验结果表明，DCGL在所有评估指标上均优于现有的先进（SOTA）方法，尤其在稀疏用户场景下表现显著优于基准模型。

Conclusion: 通过双通道架构解耦语义与行为，结合多级对比学习与动态融合机制，DCGL能够有效克服现有模型在语义建模、嵌入干扰及长尾分布方面的问题，在显著提升稀疏场景性能的同时维持了对活跃用户的推荐精度。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) have proven highly effective for recommendation systems by capturing latent item relationships, while recent integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) has further enhanced semantic understanding and addressed knowledge sparsity issues. Nevertheless, current KG-and-LLM-based methods still face three main limitations: 1) inadequate modeling of implicit semantic relationships beyond explicit KG links; 2) suboptimal single-channel fusion of ID and LLM embeddings, which often leads to signal interference and blurred representations; and 3) insufficient consideration of user-item interaction frequency variations in recommendation strategies. To address these challenges, we propose the Dual-Channel Graph Learning (DCGL) framework, featuring three key innovations: 1) a dual-channel architecture that structurally decouples rich semantic information from user behavioral patterns, preventing early interference; 2) a multi-level contrastive learning mechanism that enhances robustness against KG noise through intra-view contrasts and bridges semantic gaps between channels via inter-view alignment; and 3) a dynamic fusion mechanism that adaptively balances semantic generalization and behavioral specificity based on interaction frequency, resolving the cascading limitation. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show that DCGL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding substantial improvements in sparse scenarios while maintaining precision for active users. Our code is available at https://github.com/XinchiZou/DCGL.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [How Does Attention Help? Insights from Random Matrices on Signal Recovery from Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06826)
*Mohamed El Amine Seddik*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文通过高维统计理论分析了带注意力池化的样本协方差矩阵，定义了其谱分布并界定了信号恢复的双重相位转换，明确了最优注意力权重策略，不仅揭示了注意力机制的理论逻辑，还验证了因果自注意力的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从理论层面解释大型语言模型中基于池化序列表示的样本协方差矩阵的谱分布特性，探究注意力权重对信号提取和特征捕捉的具体影响。

Method: 在$d,V,N\to\infty$的高维渐近框架下，利用自由概率论（Free Probability）中的自由乘积卷积（Free Multiplicative Convolution）推导极限特征值分布，并结合BBP（Baik-Ben Arous-Péché）相位转换理论分析信号恢复与特征向量对齐情况。

Result: 1. 导出了受限词汇表结构下非Marchenko--Pastur分布的谱密度；2. 识别出两个连续的BBP型相位转换点；3. 证明了参数无关的因果自注意力机制（通过调和权重）在特定条件下优于传统的均值池化。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了固定注意力加权下的样本协方差矩阵的光谱特性，证明了最优注意力权重即为位置相关矩阵R的主特征向量。分析表明，因果自注意力机制中的调和权重在处理特定信号分布时优于均值池化，为理解Transformer及其注意力机制的信号提取能力提供了严谨的统计理论框架。

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of sample covariance matrices constructed from pooled sequence representations, where token embeddings are drawn from a fixed two-class Gaussian mixture table and pooled via (fixed) attention weights. Working in the high-dimensional regime $d,V,N\to\infty$ with $d/V\toδ$ and $d/N\toγ$, we derive exact characterizations of the limiting eigenvalue distribution, outlier eigenvalues, and eigenvector alignment with the hidden signal. The bulk spectrum follows a non-Marchenko--Pastur law given by the free multiplicative convolution $κ(MP_δ\boxtimes MP_γ)$, reflecting the finite vocabulary structure. Signal recovery undergoes two successive BBP-type phase transitions characterized by the scalars: $δ,γ,α=w^{\top} R w$ and $κ=\|w\|^2$, where $w$ denotes the attention pooling weights and $R$ the positional correlation matrix. An aftermath of our analysis demonstrates that the optimal attention weights maximizing the signal-to-noise ratio $α/κ$ are given by the (normalized) top eigenvector of $R$, and we show (as a particular case of our analysis) that parameter-free causal self-attention with $τ/d$ score scaling yields deterministic harmonic weights that improve signal recovery over mean pooling whenever early tokens carry more signal. Extensive simulations confirm sharp agreement between theory and finite-dimensional experiments.

</details>


### [237] [One Operator for Many Densities: Amortized Approximation of Conditioning by Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06873)
*Panos Tsimpos,Edoardo Calvello,Ayoub Belhadji,Nicholas H. Nelsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经算子的通用概率条件化方法，通过学习一个能够将任意联合密度映射为条件密度的算子，实现了条件化任务的摊销化求解，为贝叶斯推理的基础模型提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器学习方法通常针对固定的联合分布学习条件分布，缺乏泛化能力；作者旨在开发一种能够对联合分布与条件分布对进行“摊销化（amortized）”处理的通用求解方法。

Method: 提出了一种映射算子，该算子能将任意联合概率密度直接映射为其条件概率密度；从理论上证明了该算子可以被神经算子以任意精度逼近；并通过高斯混合模型进行了实验验证。

Result: 通过理论证明了条件化算子在特定密度类上的连续性，并成功利用神经算子实现了针对高斯混合模型的条件映射学习，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种通用的、摊销化的概率条件化求解框架，证明了利用神经算子实现这一目标的理论可行性，为构建贝叶斯推理的“基础模型”提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Probabilistic conditioning is concerned with the identification of a distribution of a random variable $X$ given a random variable $Y$. It is a cornerstone of scientific and engineering applications where modeling uncertainty is key. This problem has traditionally been addressed in machine learning by directly learning the conditional distribution of a fixed joint distribution. This paper introduces a novel perspective: we propose to solve the conditioning problem by identifying a single operator that maps any joint density to its conditional, thus amortizing over joint-conditional pairs. We establish that the conditioning operator can be approximated to arbitrary accuracy by neural operators. Our proof relies on new results establishing continuity of the conditioning operator over suitable classes of densities. Finally, we learn the conditioning map for a class of Gaussian mixtures using neural operators, illustrating the promise of our framework. This work provides the theoretical underpinnings for general-purpose, amortized methods for probabilistic conditioning, such as foundation models for Bayesian inference.

</details>


### [238] [Locally Near Optimal Piecewise Linear Regression in High Dimensions via Difference of Max-Affine Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06959)
*Haitham Kanj,Kiryung Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“最大仿射之差”（DoMA）参数化的分段线性回归求解方法——自适应块梯度下降（ABGD）算法，在保证理论最优收敛速率的同时，在合成及真实数据集上均表现出卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决分段线性回归（PLR）的参数化求解难题，并为非凸优化过程提供强有力的局部收敛性理论保证。

Method: 将分段线性函数参数化为两个最大仿射函数之差（DoMA），并提出自适应块梯度下降（ABGD）算法进行优化，同时采用改进的初始化策略。

Result: 在次高斯特征和噪声分布下，ABGD 实现了线性收敛；在含噪情况下样本复杂度达到 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\max(σ_z/ε,1)^2)$，无噪情况下达到 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$，且被证明是极小极大最优的。

Conclusion: ABGD 算法在分段线性回归问题中表现优异，实现了样本复杂度的极小极大最优性，且在合成数据和真实数据集上均验证了其理论保证与竞赛性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a parametric solution to piecewise linear regression through the Adaptive Block Gradient Descent (ABGD) algorithm. The heart of the method is the parametrization of piecewise linear functions as the difference of max-affine (DoMA) functions. A non-asymptotic local convergence analysis for ABGD is provided under sub-Gaussian covariate and noise distributions. To initialize ABGD, we adapt a prior algorithm originally developed for the simpler setting of max-affine functions. When suitably initialized, ABGD converges linearly to an $ε$-accurate estimate given $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\max(σ_z/ε,1)^2)$ observations where $σ_z^2$ denotes the noise variance. This implies exact recovery given $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$ samples in the noiseless case. Also, such a rate is shown to be minimax optimal up to logarithmic factors. Synthetic numerical results corroborate the theoretical guarantees for ABGD. We also observe competitive performance compared to the state-of-the-art methods on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [239] [BGM-IV: an AI-powered Bayesian generative modeling approach for instrumental variable analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07029)
*Guyue Luo,Qiao Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对高维协变量下的非线性工具变量估计难题，BGM-IV通过潜空间中的贝叶斯生成建模与伪似然推断，实现了更稳健的因果估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性工具变量（IV）方法在面对高维协变量和嵌入在复杂高维表示中的因果信息时表现不佳。

Method: 提出BGM-IV，一种基于潜变量的贝叶斯生成模型。该方法通过推断涵盖混淆结构、结果特异性、处理特异性及干扰信息的潜变量组件，将工具变量回归问题转化为潜空间中的后验推断，并利用工具变量集成的伪似然函数来处理内生性问题。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行实验，结果显示BGM-IV在低维场景下保持竞争力，并显著优于高维协变量场景下的现有方法。

Conclusion: 结构化潜变量生成模型为处理高维协变量下的非线性工具变量估计提供了一种具有原则且有效的策略，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Instrumental-variable (IV) regression enables causal estimation under endogeneity, but modern IV problems often involve nonlinear structural effects and high-dimensional covariates. Existing nonlinear IV methods directly learn the causal relation in observed feature space or rely on learned representations within two-stage or moment-based procedures, which can struggle when the causal information is embedded in a high-dimensional representation. We propose BGM-IV, a latent Bayesian generative modeling approach that reframes nonlinear IV regression as posterior inference in a causally structured latent space. BGM-IV infers latent components that separately capture shared confounding structure, outcome-specific variation, treatment-specific variation, and covariate-only nuisance information. To account for endogeneity, BGM-IV replaces the confounded outcome likelihood with an IV-integrated pseudo-likelihood that averages over instrument-induced treatment values within the latent model. Across various benchmark datasets, BGM-IV remains competitive in the classical low-dimensional regime and performs best in high-dimensional covariate regimes. Together, these results show that structured latent generative modeling provides a principled and effective strategy to nonlinear IV estimation with rich covariates. The code of BGM-IV is available at https://github.com/liuq-lab/BGM-IV.

</details>


### [240] [TRACE: Transport Alignment Conformal Prediction via Diffusion and Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07100)
*Zhenhan Fang,Aixin Tan,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TRACE 提出了一种基于扩散和流匹配模型的保形预测框架，通过计算运输轨迹上的匹配误差来衡量输出的不确定性，从而在无需似然估计的情况下实现了对多维输出的有效保形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保形预测方法在处理多维输出时，常受限于严格的几何假设、需要显式似然估计或可逆变换，难以直接应用于复杂的生成建模场景。

Method: 提出 TRACE (TRansport Alignment Conformal Estimation) 框架。该方法通过在扩散或流匹配模型中计算候选输出沿随机运输轨迹的去噪误差或速度匹配误差来定义非一致性评分，并结合分割保形预测（split conformal prediction）进行校准。

Result: 实验表明，TRACE 方法在保证有效边缘覆盖率的前提下，能够自然地适应多模态和非凸的条件分布，且表现优于传统方法。

Conclusion: TRACE 利用扩散或流匹配模型中的运输轨迹（transport trajectories）构建非一致性评分，为多维输出提供了一种灵活、无需似然评估的保形预测方法，在复杂条件分布下展现了良好的适应性和覆盖保证。

Abstract: Constructing valid and informative conformal prediction regions for multi-dimensional outputs remains a fundamental challenge. While conformal prediction provides finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees, its practical performance critically depends on the choice of nonconformity score. Existing approaches often rely on restrictive geometric assumptions or require explicit likelihood evaluation and invertible transformations, limiting their applicability in complex generative settings.
  In this work, we introduce TRACE (TRansport Alignment Conformal Estimation), a conformal prediction framework that defines nonconformity through transport alignment in diffusion and flow matching models. Rather than evaluating likelihoods, we measure how well a candidate output aligns with the learned generative dynamics by averaging denoising or velocity-matching errors along stochastic transport trajectories. The resulting transport-based scores are scalar-valued and can be calibrated using split conformal prediction, yielding valid marginal coverage under exchangeability. We further analyze the statistical properties of the proposed scores and their sensitivity to computational budget. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate valid coverage and show that the resulting regions adapt naturally to multimodal and non-convex conditional distributions.

</details>


### [241] [A Refined Generalization Analysis for Extreme Multi-class Supervised Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07596)
*Nong Minh Hieu,Antoine Ledent*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了对比表示学习（CRL）在非独立同分布场景下的理论样本复杂度问题，通过改进估计方法，消除了对长尾类别分布的过度依赖，提供了更紧致的理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 CRL 样本复杂度理论通常假设数据独立同分布（i.i.d.），但在实际对比学习应用中，元组往往由有限池中构建，导致依赖性；且已有针对此设置的分析（基于 U-Statistics）在极端多分类场景下受限于稀有类别频率，导致界过于悲观。

Method: 利用统计学习理论框架，改进了此前基于 U-Statistics 的分析方法；通过设计新的风险估计器（estimator），实现了对跨类别风险集中度的捕捉，从而消除了对稀有类别概率的过分依赖。

Result: 证明了样本复杂度仅与类别数 $R$ 的阶数相关，不再依赖于稀有类别的最小概率 $ρ_{\min}$；特别是在长尾分布下，新提出的估计器实现了 $\mathcal{O}(k)$ 的样本复杂度（$k$ 为每个元组的样本数）。

Conclusion: 本文改进了对比表示学习（CRL）在存在类别依赖的有限数据集上的样本复杂度理论界，提出了与类别分布无关的界，并针对长尾多分类场景给出了更紧致的 $\mathcal{O}(k)$ 复杂性分析。

Abstract: Contrastive Representation Learning (CRL) has achieved strong empirical success in multiple machine learning disciplines, yet its theoretical sample complexity remains poorly understood. Existing analyses usually assume that input tuples are identically and independently distributed, an assumption violated in most practical settings where contrastive tuples are constructed from a finite pool of labeled data, inducing dependencies among tuples. While one recent work analyzed this learning setting using U-Statistics to estimate the population risk, the techniques used therein require the risk of each class to concentrate uniformly, making excess risk bounds scale in the order of $ρ_{\min}^{-{1}/{2}}$ where $ρ_{\min}$ denotes the probability of the rarest class. Such a dependency can be overly pessimistic in the extreme multiclass settings where there are many tail classes which contribute minimally to the overall population risk. Our contributions are two-fold. Firstly, we improve upon the previous work and prove a bound with a sample complexity of the same order as the number of classes $R$, regardless of the distribution over classes. Furthermore, we formulate a different estimator that captures the concentration of the risk \textit{across classes}, enabling sharper bounds in extreme multi-class learning scenarios, especially where class distributions are long-tailed. Under mild assumptions on the class distributions, the resulting sample complexity is $\mathcal{O}(k)$ where $k$ is the number of samples per tuple.

</details>


### [242] [Debiased Counterfactual Generation via Flow Matching from Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07665)
*Hugh Dance,Johnny Xi,Peter Orbanz,Benjamin Bloem-Reddy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用观察分布与逆事实分布内在统计联系的“去混杂流”方法，通过流匹配和高效影响函数优化，高效且准确地估计逆事实分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆事实分布建模方法将其视为独立的生成目标，忽视了其与观测数据之间的内在联系（如共享支持集、尾部行为和不变特征），导致估计效率低下或存在偏差。

Method: 提出了一种基于流匹配（flow-matching）的方法，通过从观察分布到逆事实分布的“去混杂流”进行学习，并引入了基于高效影响函数（efficient influence function）校正的半参数有效估计器，同时扩展用于最小能量流计算。

Result: 提出的方法在处理高维逆事实分布估计任务时表现出色，能够有效纠正混杂带来的偏差，且在实验中超越了当前的先进基准。

Conclusion: 通过去混杂流（deconfounding flows）学习逆事实分布优于现有的去偏估计器，并缓解了传统基于流的方法的缺陷。

Abstract: Estimating counterfactual distributions under interventions is central to treatment risk assessment and counterfactual generation tasks. Existing approaches model the counterfactual distribution as a standalone generative target, without exploiting its relationship to the observational data. In this work, we show that under standard assumptions, observational and counterfactual outcome distributions are tightly linked: they have identical support and tail behavior, remain statistically close under weak confounding, and share any features of high-dimensional outcomes which are invariant to confounders. These properties motivate learning counterfactual distributions not from scratch, but via a deconfounding flow from the observational distribution. We formulate this problem via flow-matching and derive a semiparametrically efficient estimator based on a novel efficient influence function correction. We subsequently extend our estimator to target minimal-energy flows in high-dimensions, which we show can be especially simple targets between observational and counterfactual distributions. In experiments, deconfounding flows outperform existing debiased counterfactual distribution estimators, while also mitigating known failure modes of flow-based methods.

</details>


### [243] [Flow Matching for Count Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07746)
*Ganchao Wei,John Pearson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Count-FM 是一种针对计数数据的高效生成建模框架，通过连续时间生灭过程实现概率分布间的平滑转换，兼具计算效率与建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高维计数数据（如 scRNA-seq、神经脉冲）生成模型通常将计数视为分类状态或将其映射到连续空间，这些方法在计数范围较大时不够自然且低效。

Method: 提出名为 count-FM 的流匹配（flow-matching）框架，基于连续时间生灭过程（birth-death process）并采用局部单位跳跃（local unit jumps），通过仿真无关（simulation-free）的训练方式学习条件转移速率。

Result: 实验证明 count-FM 在样本质量上优于现有基准模型，且参数量显著减少；在 scRNA-seq 和神经脉冲数据的无条件生成、分布迁移及条件生成任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: Count-FM 提出了一种基于连续时间生灭过程的流匹配框架，有效解决了大规模计数数据的生成与建模问题，在采样质量、模型效率和可解释性方面均优于现有基准。

Abstract: High-dimensional count data arise in applications such as single-cell RNA sequencing and neural spike trains, where mapping between distributions across successive batches or time points form critical components of data analysis. The recent success of diffusion- and flow-based deep generative models for images, video, and text motivates extending these ideas to count-valued settings, but many existing methods either treat each count as a categorical state or transform counts into a continuous space, neither of which is natural or efficient when the count range is large. We propose count-FM, a flow-matching framework for count data based on a continuous-time birth-death process with local unit jumps. Count-FM learns marginal transitions efficiently in count space through simulation-free training of conditional transition rates, allowing transport between arbitrary count-distributed source and target populations. In simulation, count-FM achieves better sample quality than representative baselines while using substantially fewer parameters. We further apply count-FM to scRNA-seq and neural spike-train data for unconditional generation, transport, and conditional generation. Across these tasks, count-FM yields improved sample quality, greater modeling efficiency, and interpretable transport paths.

</details>


### [244] [Expectation-Maximization as a Spectrally Governed Relaxation Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07818)
*Qiao Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文建立了EM算法的统一动力学框架，通过潜变量算子将全局单调下降与局部线性收敛联系在一起，并提供了局部加速的最优控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM算法在全局单调性、局部收敛速度和稳健性方面的特性通常被分开讨论。本文旨在揭示全局非线性下降与局部线性收敛之间如何通过一个动态框架相互关联。

Method: 本文提出了一种连接全局EM轨迹与局部线性算子的潜变量算子方法，通过对后验相对熵进行全局能量分解，并在非退化极大值点处对EM映射进行线性化分析，导出了缺失信息比率（missing-information ratio）。

Result: 研究表明，导出的局部算子与缺失信息比率及观测似然的几何Hessian矩阵一致，能够统一描述局部收缩、后验刚性（posterior rigidity）和几何曲率，并推导出了EM算法的局部加速优化松弛规则。

Conclusion: 该论文将全局EM下降与局部线性收敛统一进了一个共同的动力学框架中。通过引入潜变量算子，不仅深化了对EM算法收敛行为的理论理解，还为局部加速提供了最优的标量松弛规则。

Abstract: The expectation--maximization (EM) algorithm combines global monotonicity, local linear convergence, and strong practical robustness, but these features are usually analyzed separately. Global descent is nonlinear, whereas local convergence is governed by the spectrum of the linearized EM map. How these two levels fit into a single dynamical picture has remained less transparent.
  We make explicit the latent-variable operator that connects them. Along the EM trajectory, the likelihood increment admits a global energy decomposition in terms of posterior-relative entropy. Linearization at a nondegenerate maximizer $θ^\ast$ then reveals the local operator \[ \mathcal G_{θ^\ast}=I-DT(θ^\ast), \] which coincides with both the missing-information ratio and the information-geometric Hessian of the observed likelihood.
  This operator provides a unified description of local contraction, posterior rigidity, and geometric curvature. Its spectrum yields a sharp characterization of local convergence and naturally leads to an optimal scalar relaxation rule for locally accelerated EM. These results place global descent, local spectral behavior, and optimal local relaxation within a common dynamical framework.

</details>


### [245] [Characterizing and Correcting Effective Target Shift in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07886)
*Ziyan Li,Naoki Hiratani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在线核回归等价于带偏移目标的离线回归，并通过提出一种目标修正方法，使在线模型能够匹配离线学习性能，在图像分类任务中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统在处理数据流和分布偏移（distributional shift）时的性能往往表现不佳，该研究旨在通过核回归理论揭示在线学习与离线学习之间的联系，以解决非平稳环境下的在线学习难题。

Method: 1. 分析在线核回归与离线回归的数学关系，推导其闭式表达式；2. 识别在线学习中由于分布偏移导致的“目标偏移”现象；3. 推导目标修正的闭式解及迭代计算公式；4. 在CIFAR-10和CORe50数据集上通过在线随机梯度下降（SGD）验证方法有效性。

Result: 在该框架下，使用迭代修正目标的在线SGD在持续学习任务中表现优于直接使用真实目标的基准方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入“目标修正”（target correction）机制，在线核回归能够学习到与离线学习一致的预测器，从而有效提升非平稳环境下的在线学习性能。

Abstract: Online learning from a stream of data is a defining feature of intelligence, yet modern machine learning systems often struggle in this setting, especially under distributional shift. To understand its basic properties, we study the relationship between online and offline learning in the context of kernel regression. We derive a closed-form expression for the function learned by online kernel regression, revealing that online kernel regression is equivalent to offline regression with shifted, inaccurate target outputs. Conversely, we show that by compensating for this effective shift in the teaching signal through target correction, online kernel-based learning can provably learn the same predictor as its offline counterpart. We derive both a closed-form expression for this target correction and an iterative form that can be applied sequentially. Applying this framework to image classification tasks on CIFAR-10 and CORe50, we show that online stochastic gradient descent with iteratively corrected targets outperforms learning with the true targets in continual learning settings. This work therefore provides a basic framework for analyzing and improving online learning in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [246] [Consistency Regularised Gradient Flows for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07907)
*Alessio Spagnoletti,Tim Y. J. Wang,Marcelo Pereyra,O. Deniz Akyildiz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型梯度流框架，通过在潜在空间联合优化采样与提示词，在无需反向传播的情况下实现了快速、高质量的图像反问题重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型（LDM）的反问题求解器通常需要大量的神经函数评估（NFE）以及对大型预训练组件进行复杂的反向传播，导致计算开销过大且重建质量受损。

Method: 提出了一个统一的欧几里得-Wasserstein-2（Euclidean-Wasserstein-2）梯度流框架，在潜在空间内联合执行后验采样和提示优化，并结合了少步（few-step）潜在文生图模型，避免了对自动编码器的反向传播。

Result: 在多个典型的成像反问题实验中，该方法实现了SOTA性能，且显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于欧几里得-Wasserstein-2梯度流的统一框架，通过在潜在空间中同时进行后验采样和提示优化，有效解决了现有扩散模型求解反问题时计算成本高和性能受限的问题，实现了低NFE推理。

Abstract: Vision-Language Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) (Rombach et al., 2022) provide powerful generative priors for inverse problems. However, existing LDM-based inverse solvers typically require a large number of neural function evaluations (NFEs) and backpropagation through large pretrained components, leading to substantial computational costs and, in some cases, degraded reconstruction quality. We propose a unified Euclidean-Wasserstein-2 gradient-flow framework that jointly performs posterior sampling and prompt optimization in the latent space through a single flow that aligns the prior and posterior with the observed data. Combined with few-step latent text-to-image models, this formulation enables low-NFE inference without backpropagation through autoencoders. Experiments across several canonical imaging inverse problems show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with significantly reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [247] [Asymptotically Log-Optimal Bayes-Assisted Confidence Sequences for Bounded Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07964)
*Valentin Kilian,Stefano Cortinovis,François Caron*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯预测因子的自适应置信序列构建方法，在保持严谨的随时有效覆盖保证的同时，利用先验知识大幅提高了估计的统计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于鞅的置信序列在处理有界独立同分布（IID）数据时，往往未能充分利用先验信息，导致实践中的效率较低。

Method: 提出“贝叶斯辅助（Bayes-assisted）”框架：利用贝叶斯预测模型在每一步选择使预测期望对数增长最大化的鞅因子（martingale factor）。具体实例化方案包括狄利克雷过程混合模型（DP mixtures）和贝叶斯指数倾斜经验似然（BETEL）。

Result: 若预测分布满足Wasserstein一致性，该方法在渐近意义上达到对数最优；实验表明，该方法能有效降低置信宽度并减少采样需求，且在模型误设时仍保持严格的有效性 coverage。

Conclusion: 提出了一种贝叶斯辅助的置信序列构建框架，在保证任意时刻有效性（随时有效）的前提下，通过先验信息显著提升了置信区间的收敛性能，并证明了其渐近最优性。

Abstract: Confidence sequences based on test martingales provide time-uniform uncertainty quantification for the mean of bounded IID observations without parametric distributional assumptions. Their practical efficiency, however, depends strongly on the choice of martingale updates, and many existing constructions do not exploit prior information about plausible data-generating distributions or mean values. We propose a Bayes-assisted framework that uses a Bayesian working predictive model to adaptively construct confidence sequences. For each candidate mean and time point, the predictive distribution selects, among valid one-step martingale factors, the update maximising predictive expected log-growth; validity is therefore preserved even when the prior or working model is misspecified. We prove that if the predictive distribution is Wasserstein-consistent, the resulting procedure is asymptotically log-optimal, matching the per-sample log-growth of an oracle procedure with access to the true distribution. We instantiate the framework using robust predictives based on Dirichlet-process mixtures and Bayesian exponentially tilted empirical likelihood. Experiments on synthetic data, sequential best-arm identification for LLM evaluation, and prediction-powered inference show that informative priors can substantially reduce confidence-sequence width and sampling effort while retaining anytime-valid coverage.

</details>


### [248] [Semiparametric Efficient Test for Interpretable Distributional Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08034)
*Houssam Zenati,Arthur Gretton*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DR-ME 是一种新型的半参数因果检验框架，它不仅能识别处理效应导致的分布变化，还能通过学习特定数据点定位这些差异的具体坐标，克服了传统全局检验缺乏可解释性的缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布处理效应检验（如全局核检验）虽然能检测分布差异，但无法说明差异具体出现在分布的哪个部分（如尾部、众数或离散度），且容易掩盖“均值不变但分布改变”的效应。

Method: 提出 DR-ME 方法：一种基于核判别（kernel witness）的半参数高效有限位置检验。使用正交双稳健（doubly robust）核特征，通过特定的位置学习准则确定观察点，并利用样本拆分（sample splitting）解决后选择推断问题。

Result: 实验表明 DR-ME 具备近乎名义的第一类错误率，在统计功效上与全局双稳健核检验相当，且能成功定位医疗影像研究中的分布效应。

Conclusion: DR-ME 是一种有效的、可解释的因果分布处理效应检验方法，通过学习特定位置来定位分布差异，在保证统计有效性的同时提供了比全局检验更深入的见解。

Abstract: Distributional treatment effects can be invisible to means: a treatment may preserve average outcomes while changing tails, modes, dispersion, or rare-event probabilities. Kernel tests can detect discrepancies between interventional outcome laws, but global tests do not reveal where the laws differ. We propose DR-ME, to our knowledge the first semiparametrically efficient finite-location test for interpretable distributional treatment effects. DR-ME evaluates an interventional kernel witness at learned outcome locations, returning causal-discrepancy coordinates rather than only a global rejection. From observational data, we derive orthogonal doubly robust kernel features whose centered oracle form is the canonical gradient of this finite witness. For fixed locations, we characterize the local testing limit: DR-ME is chi-square calibrated under the null, has noncentral chi-square local power, and uses the covariance whitening that optimizes local signal-to-noise for discrepancies visible through the selected coordinates. This efficient local-power geometry yields a principled location-learning criterion, with sample splitting preserving post-selection validity. Experiments show near-nominal type-I error, competitive power against global doubly robust kernel tests, and interpretable learned locations that localize distributional effects in a semi-synthetic medical-imaging study.

</details>


### [249] [A Note on Non-Negative $L_1$-Approximating Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08072)
*Jane H. Lee,Anay Mehrotra,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有有限高斯表面积（GSA）的集合类，其指示函数存在度数为 $\tilde{O}(\Gamma^2/\varepsilon^2)$ 的非负 $L_1$ 近似多项式，达到了最优近似阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在标准高斯分布下非负 $L_1$ 近似多项式的存在性，以弥补传统非约束 $L_1$ 近似与更强的“夹心”多项式（sandwiching polynomials）之间的理论差距，并为平滑学习（smoothed learning）提供理论支撑。

Method: 通过理论推导证明了高斯表面积（GSA）与指示函数非负 $L_1$ 近似多项式的存在性之间的关系，并给出了具体的度数界限。

Result: 对于高斯表面积最多为 $\Gamma$ 的任何集合类，存在度数为 $k=\tilde{O}(\Gamma^2/\varepsilon^2)$ 的非负多项式，其在 $L_1$ 范数下以 $\varepsilon$ 的误差逼近该集合的指示函数。该界限在常数因子内与已知的无非负约束的 $L_1$ 近似最优界限相匹配。

Conclusion: 证明了具有有限高斯表面积（GSA）的集合类，其指示函数存在度数为 $\tilde{O}(\Gamma^2/\varepsilon^2)$ 的非负 $L_1$ 近似多项式。这一结果在保持最优近似阶数的同时，满足了更强的非负性限制。

Abstract: $L_1$-Approximating polynomials, i.e., polynomials that approximate indicator functions in $L_1$-norm under certain distributions, are widely used in computational learning theory. We study the existence of \textit{non-negative} $L_1$-approximating polynomials with respect to Gaussian distributions. This is a stronger requirement than $L_1$-approximation but weaker than sandwiching polynomials (which themselves have many applications). These non-negative approximating polynomials have recently found uses in smoothed learning from positive-only examples.
  In this short note, we prove that every class of sets with Gaussian surface area (GSA) at most $Γ$ under the standard Gaussian admits degree-$k$ non-negative polynomials that $\eps$-approximate its indicator functions in $L_1$-norm, for $k=\tilde{O}(Γ^2/\varepsilon^2)$. Equivalently, finite GSA implies $L_1$-approximation with the stronger pointwise guarantee that the approximating polynomial has range contained in $[0,\infty)$. Up to a constant-factor, this matches the degree of the best currently known Gaussian $L_1$-approximation degree bound without the non-negativity constraint.

</details>


### [250] [An Interpretable and Scalable Framework for Evaluating Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07046)
*Xinhao Qu,Qiang Heng,Hao Zeng,Xiaoqian Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对传统大模型评估方法忽视题目异质性的问题，本文提出了一种基于MM优化算法的、高效可扩展的IRT评估框架，实现了对模型能力与试题特性的精确建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型评估主要依赖平均准确率，忽视了输出随机性和题目异质性；而传统的项目反应理论（IRT）计算复杂且数值不稳定，难以在大规模基准测试中应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于主要化-最小化（majorization-minimization）原则的框架，将IRT参数估计问题重构为一系列受约束的矩阵分解子问题，实现了稳定、高效的参数求解。

Result: 在合成数据集及MATH-500、Open LLM Leaderboard等真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持高估计精度的同时，比现有IRT实现方式快几个数量级，并能揭示题目的难度与区分度。

Conclusion: 基于主要化-最小化（MM）原则的IRT框架为大模型评估提供了一种可扩展且具有可解释性的替代方案，能够有效分析模型真实能力及题目特征，从而指导更科学的基准测试设计。

Abstract: Evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is increasingly critical, yet standard benchmarking methods rely on average accuracy, overlooking both the inherent stochasticity of LLM outputs and the heterogeneity of benchmark items. Item Response Theory (IRT) offers a principled framework for modeling latent model abilities and item characteristics, but conventional methods are computationally expensive and numerically unstable, limiting large-scale implementations. To address these challenges, we propose an interpretable and scalable framework for LLM evaluation based on the majorization-minimization principle. Our approach reformulates the problem as a sequence of constrained matrix factorization subproblems, enabling stable and efficient parameter estimation with theoretical guarantees for identifiability and convergence. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including MATH-500 and six Open LLM Leaderboard benchmarks, demonstrate that our method achieves superior scalability and interpretability. It delivers orders-of-magnitude speedups over competing methods while maintaining comparable or even higher estimation accuracy. Our results align with established scaling laws and offer insights into item difficulty and discrimination, informing more principled benchmark design.

</details>


### [251] [Reliable Chain-of-Thought via Prefix Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07654)
*Naoto Iwase,Yuki Ichihara,Mohammad Atif Quamar,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 通过“截断后再生”并计算一致性权重，实现低成本、高准确率的推理答案聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽性（Self-Consistency）中的多数投票（MV）需要大量采样，计算成本较高。研究人员发现正确答案的思维链在截断再生时更倾向于保持一致，因此试图利用这一特性寻找比标准MV更高效的答案验证机制。

Method: 提出“前缀一致性”（Prefix Consistency）评估方法：在生成思维链（CoT）过程中，对思维链进行截断并多次再生（Regeneration），通过统计各候选项在该过程中再次出现的频率作为置信度权重，用于加权聚合最终答案。

Result: 在5个模型和4个数学/科学数据集上验证：前缀一致性是最佳的正确性预测指标；通过该指标加权，在达到标准MV性能时，Token消耗减少了4.6倍至21倍。

Conclusion: 通过“前缀一致性”加权（Prefix Consistency scoring）可以有效替代传统多数投票，在大幅减少推理计算成本（Token消耗）的同时，达到与标准多数投票相当甚至更好的准确率，且无需模型内部概率或外部评分提示。

Abstract: Large Language Models often improve accuracy on reasoning tasks by sampling multiple Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces and aggregating them with majority voting (MV), a test-time technique called self-consistency. When we truncate a CoT partway through and regenerate the remainder, we observe that traces with correct answers reproduce their original answer more often than traces with wrong answers. We use this difference as a reliability signal, prefix consistency, that weights each candidate answer by how often it reappears under regeneration. It requires no access to token log-probabilities or self-rating prompts. Across five reasoning models and four math and science benchmarks, prefix consistency is the best correctness predictor in most settings, and reweighting votes by it reaches Standard MV plateau accuracy at up to 21x fewer tokens (median 4.6x). Our code is available at https://github.com/naoto-iwase/prefix-consistency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [CARMEN: CORDIC-Accelerated Resource-Efficient Multi-Precision Inference Engine for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06878)
*Sonu Kumar,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma,Adam Teman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CARMEN 是一款创新的自适应向量引擎，利用 CORDIC 迭代深度动态调节计算精度，显著提升了深度学习推理在功耗、效率和计算密度方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习硬件往往难以在资源受限的环境下同时兼顾推理精度、计算效率与功耗，旨在通过硬件架构创新实现资源高效的推理。

Method: 提出了一种运行时自适应的 CORDIC 加速多精度向量引擎（CARMEN），核心在于利用 CORDIC 迭代深度控制计算精度，集成低资源迭代 CORDIC 乘加单元（MAC）及时间复用多激活函数模块，支持 8/16 位精度切换。

Result: 在 28nm CMOS 工艺下，计算周期减少 33%，每 MAC 级功耗降低 21%；在 256-PE 配置下实现 4.83 TOPS/mm² 的计算密度和 11.67 TOPS/W 的能效；FPGA 部署验证了其在实时目标检测任务中的实用性（154.6ms 延迟，0.43W 功耗）。

Conclusion: CARMEN 通过基于 CORDIC 的动态精度调节架构，成功实现了在不修改硬件的前提下平衡深度学习推理的能效与计算精度，为资源受限场景下的边缘计算提供了一种高效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents CARMEN, a runtime-adaptive, CORDIC-accelerated multi-precision vector engine for resource-efficient deep learning inference. The key insight is that CORDIC iteration depth directly governs computational accuracy, enabling dynamic switching between approximate and accurate execution modes without hardware modification. The architecture integrates a low-resource iterative CORDIC-based MAC unit with a time-multiplexed multi-activation function block, supporting flexible 8/16-bit precision and high hardware utilization. ASIC implementation in 28 nm CMOS achieves up to 33% reduction in computation cycles and 21% power savings per MAC stage; a 256-PE configuration delivers 4.83 TOPS/mm2 compute density and 11.67 TOPS/W energy efficiency. FPGA deployment on PynqZ2 validates 154.6 ms latency at 0.43 W for real-time object detection.

</details>


### [253] [TransDot: An Area-efficient Reconfigurable Floating-Point Unit for Trans-Precision Dot-Product Accumulation for FPGA AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07245)
*Jiayi Wang,Maohua Nie,Sin-Chen Lin,C. -J. Richard Shi,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 为了解决现有 FPU 难以高效执行跨精度点积累加（DPA）的问题，本文提出了 TransDot，一种可在同一数据通路中统一支持多精度 SIMD FMA 和 DPA 的可重构 FPU，显著提高了 AI 运算的吞吐量与面积效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源 FPU（如 FPnew）无法支持跨精度点积累加（DPA），且通过重复 SIMD FMA 通道实现低精度计算会导致面积浪费和资源利用率低下，无法充分利用现代 FPGA AI 引擎的计算潜力。

Method: 提出 TransDot，一种可重构浮点单元（FPU），通过共享的可重构数据通路集成多精度 SIMD FMA 和跨精度 DPA（支持从 FP16、FP8 到 FP4 的点积并累加至 FP32）。

Result: TransDot 实现了 2 倍 FP16、4 倍 FP8 和 8 倍 FP4 的 DPA 吞吐量；在面积效率方面，FP16 DPA 提升了 1.46 倍，FP8 DPA 提升了 2.92 倍；虽然相比基准设计面积增加了 37.3% 并增加了一级流水线，但整体性能提升显著。

Conclusion: TransDot 通过一种创新的可重构数据通路实现了多精度 SIMD FMA 和跨精度点积累加（DPA）的统一，显著提升了在 AI 引擎中的计算吞吐量和面积效率，为下一代 AMD Versal 设备提供了一种高效的算术单元设计方案。

Abstract: Commercial FPGAs, such as AMD Versal devices, increasingly incorporate AI engines that exploit low-precision packed-SIMD fused multiply-accumulate (FMA) to achieve proportional throughput gains. However, trans-precision FMA (e.g., multiplying two FP16 numbers and adding their result to an FP32 accumulator), which preserves numerical stability by accumulating in higher precision, remains bottlenecked by the highest-precision, lowest-throughput operation. Dot-product accumulation (DPA) (e.g., performing a dot-product on two 4-element FP8 vectors and adding its result to an FP32 accumulator) can fully utilize the input/output bandwidth and computational resources. Existing flexible open-source FPUs, such as FPnew, do not support DPA and implement SIMD FMA on low-precision formats by replicating independent FMA lanes, which increases area, underutilizes shared arithmetic resources, and complicates the integration of DPA operations. This paper presents TransDot, a reconfigurable FPU that unifies multi-precision SIMD FMA and trans-precision DPA within a shared, reconfigurable datapath. TransDot extends the baseline design with 2-term FP16, 4-term FP8, and 8-term FP4 dot-product accumulation into FP32 using reconfigurable subcomponents. Evaluation shows that TransDot delivers 2$\times$ FP16, 4$\times$ FP8, and 8$\times$ FP4 throughput via DPA with FP32 accumulation, and 1.46$\times$ area efficiency in FP16 DPA and 2.92$\times$ area efficiency in FP8 DPA, at the cost of 37.3% larger area on average and an additional pipeline stage in dot-product mode compared to the FPnew baseline. These results demonstrate that TransDot's area-efficient design enables scalable deployment in next-generation AMD Versal AI engines.

</details>


### [254] [TREA: Low-precision Time-Multiplexed, Resource-Efficient Edge Accelerator for Object Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07321)
*Vijay Pratap Sharma,Mukul Lokhande,Ratko Pilipovic,Omkar Kokane,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TREA是一种针对边缘AI视觉任务的资源高效型加速器，通过双精度MSDF计算架构、结构化稀疏训练协同设计以及可重构激活函数，在严苛的能效与延迟约束下实现了显著的性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流边缘视觉平台在进行目标检测与分类任务时，面临着极其严格的面积、功耗和延迟限制，传统加速器方案难以兼顾高效率与低开销。

Method: 1. 提出双精度(4/8-bit)基于MSDF的DQ-MAC单元，支持动态位截断与时间复用；2. 结合SHARP结构化剪枝策略，实现近50%的结构化稀疏度并保持高MAC利用率；3. 采用基于CORDIC的重构化非线性激活函数核(RQ-NAF)，通过流水线和时间复用实现硬件重用；4. 采用1D阵列架构和逐层硬件重用设计，减少面积与控制复杂度。

Result: 硬件层级实现最高9倍的卷积核延迟缩减，支持4倍吞吐量提升（4x FxP4或1x FxP8 operations per cycle），在资源利用率、延迟及能效方面显著优于传统固定精度加速器。

Conclusion: TREA 通过创新的位宽可配置性和结构化剪枝技术，在保持高硬件利用率的同时，显著提升了边缘视觉任务的吞吐量、能效和延迟表现，是边缘AI加速器的有效解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents TREA, a low-precision time-multiplexed and resource-efficient edge-AI accelerator for object detection and classification, targeting stringent area-power-latency constraints of edge vision platforms. The proposed architecture integrates a dual-precision (4/8-bit) SIMD multiply-accumulate (DQ-MAC) unit based on most-significant-digit-first (MSDF) shift-and-add computation with run-time bit truncation, eliminating conventional multiplier overhead and reducing accumulator bit-width. The DQ-MAC supports 4x FxP4 or 1x FxP8 operations per cycle, achieving up to 4x throughput improvement without hardware duplication. A structured hardware-aware reductive pruning (SHARP) strategy is co-designed with the SIMD datapath, enabling near 50% structured sparsity while maintaining full MAC utilization. This allows a 3x3 convolution kernel to be computed in 1 cycle in FxP4 mode compared to 9 cycles in FxP8, and a 5x5 kernel in 3 cycles versus 25 cycles, yielding up to 9x latency reduction at the kernel level. The accelerator further incorporates a reconfigurable CORDIC-based nonlinear activation function (RQ-NAF) core with a 9-stage pipeline, supporting Sigmoid, Tanh, and ReLU at one output per cycle after pipeline fill, while enabling (N-1) hardware reuse through time-multiplexing. The complete TREA architecture employs a 1D array of 100 SIMD DQ-MAC units with layer-wise hardware reuse, significantly reducing area and control complexity. Experimental results demonstrate substantial improvements in latency, hardware utilization, and energy efficiency compared to conventional fixed-precision and non-reconfigurable accelerators, validating TREA as an effective solution for real-time edge vision workloads.

</details>


### [255] [Accelerating Precise End-to-End Simulation: Latency-Sensitive Many-core System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07750)
*Yinrong Li,Zexin Fu,Yichao Zhang,Germain Haugou,Chi Zhang,Marco Bertuletti,Bowen Wang,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文设计了一套针对大规模众核架构（含SPM）的快速高精度建模框架，在性能误差低于7%的情况下，实现了比RTL仿真快115倍的仿真速度，为硬件架构探索与算法优化提供了高效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型工作负载对计算和内存提出了更高要求，促使许多设计转向具有紧密耦合暂存器内存（SPM）的众核加速器，但随之带来的系统复杂性导致传统RTL仿真速度过慢，难以满足复杂的性能评估需求。

Method: 提出了一种针对延迟敏感型众核架构（如TeraNoC）的端到端建模方案。该方法在保留对SPM访问时序影响的核心行为（跨多个互连尺度）的基础上，抽象掉非必要的硬件细节，从而实现仿真加速。

Result: 在多样化基准测试中，该模型与RTL基准相比误差低于7%，仿真速度提升高达115倍，并支持对处理单元和互连的详细分析。

Conclusion: 本文提出的建模方法在保持高精度的前提下，显著提升了大规模众核架构的仿真速度，有效解决了硬件设计验证与软件开发中的性能评估瓶颈，通过FlashAttention-2优化和NoC重映射案例验证了其在架构探索中的实用性。

Abstract: Modern large language model workloads put increasing demands on parallel compute capability and on-chip memory capacity, while also stressing fine-grained data movement and synchronization. These trends motivate exploring and designing many-core accelerators with tightly coupled scratchpad memory (SPM) for scalable compute and predictable, explicitly managed data access. However, this architectural shift raises two challenges: cycle-accurate register-transfer level (RTL) simulation becomes prohibitively slow as system complexity grows, and performance estimation requires precise modeling of latency-sensitive interconnect behavior. This paper presents a fast yet accurate end-to-end modeling approach for latency-sensitive many-core architectures, targeting large-scale instances such as TeraNoC with 1024 cores and a 4MiB globally shared L1 SPM. The approach captures timing behavior of latency-sensitive SPM accesses across multiple interconnect scales, while abstracting non-essential hardware details. Across diverse benchmarks, the model tracks a cycle-accurate RTL golden model with errors below 7%, while delivering up to 115x faster simulation. The framework also provides detailed profiling across processing elements and interconnect, enabling efficient end-to-end software development and hardware design exploration. Two case studies demonstrate its practicality: profiling-guided optimization of FlashAttention-2 to reduce interconnect stalls and synchronization overhead, and design space exploration of network-on-chip (NoC) router remapping to alleviate traffic imbalance and improve throughput.

</details>


### [256] [Bridging the Last Mile of Circuit Design: PostEDA-Bench, a Hierarchical Benchmark for PPA Convergence and DRC Fixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06936)
*Pengju Liu,Nuo Xu,Jinwei Tang,Yu Cao,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PostEDA-Bench基准测试，分析发现LLM在处理复杂EDA设计收敛任务（DRC修复与多目标PPA优化）时能力严重受限，且核心瓶颈在于缺乏有效的权衡推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDA-LLM基准测试不仅忽略了EDA流程中关键的DRC（设计规则检查）修复环节，且多依赖单一工具链的扁平化结构，无法真实反映工业界“最后一公里”的设计收敛需求。

Method: 构建了一个包含145个任务的层次化基准测试集PostEDA-Bench，涵盖DRC-Essential、DRC-Reasoning、PPA-Mono和PPA-Multi四类任务；并在多种智能体框架下测试了8种商用及开源LLM，同时探究了视觉增强对性能的影响。

Result: LLM在简单任务上表现尚可，但在实际DRC推理任务中最高成功率仅为36.66%，在多目标PPA优化中仅为20.00%；视觉增强对DRC任务有明显提升作用；多目标PPA的瓶颈在于权衡推理能力，而非单纯的参数知识。

Conclusion: PostEDA-Bench揭示了当前LLM智能体在处理实际电子设计自动化（EDA）任务时存在显著局限性，特别是复杂DRC修复和多目标PPA优化能力不足。研究强调，未来的研究重点应从单一参数调整转向复杂的权衡推理。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly applied to the "last mile" of Electronic Design Automation (EDA): repairing residual sign-off Design Rule Check (DRC) violations and converging Power-Performance-Area (PPA) targets after tool runs. Existing EDA-LLM benchmarks, however, omit DRC fixing entirely and rely on flat hierarchies tied to a single toolchain. We introduce PostEDA-Bench, a hierarchical benchmark with 145 tasks across DRC-Essential, DRC-Reasoning, PPA-Mono, and PPA-Multi, supported by EDA toolchains with machine-checkable evaluation. Across eight commercial and open-source LLMs under multiple agent scaffolds, we find that agents handle synthetic DRC-Essential and single-objective PPA-Mono reasonably well but degrade sharply on the more practical DRC-Reasoning, where the best success rate is 36.66%, and PPA-Multi, where the best success rate is 20.00%; vision augmentation consistently enhances DRC-Bench; and trade-off reasoning, rather than knob knowledge, is the dominant PPA-Multi bottleneck.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [More Thinking, More Bias: Length-Driven Position Bias in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06672)
*Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现思维链推理并不解决位置偏见，推理过程越长，模型产生位置偏见（倾向于选择特定位置选项）的可能性就越大，这种“长度驱动”的偏见在不同规模模型中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界普遍认为思维链（CoT）及专用推理模型通过深思熟虑可以减少浅层启发式偏见，但该假设尚未在位置偏见（Position Bias）方面得到验证。本研究旨在探讨推理轨迹长度与多项选择题中位置偏见之间的关系。

Method: 通过对13种推理模式配置（包括R1蒸馏模型、Base+CoT模型及DeepSeek-R1）在MMLU、ARC-Challenge和GPQA数据集上的实验，分析推理轨迹长度与位置偏见分数（PBS）的相关性；采用轨迹截断干预（Truncation Intervention）提供因果证据，并对比CoT偏见与直接回答偏见的不同特征。

Result: 1. 在绝大多数（12/13）配置中，控制准确率后，推理轨迹长度与PBS呈现正相关（0.11-0.41）；2. 随着推理长度四分位数的增加，PBS呈单调递增；3. 截断干预证实了随轨迹延长，模型向偏好位置选项转移的概率增加；4. 尽管大参数模型整体PBS较低，但长轨迹仍会触发偏见，表明准确率仅掩盖而非消除了偏见机制；5. CoT引发的偏见与直接回答时的偏见属于不同现象。

Conclusion: 思维链（CoT）推理并未消除位置偏见，反而引入了与推理轨迹长度成正比的“累计位置偏见”。在多项选择题评估中，不能默认推理模型具有顺序鲁棒性，研究者应利用文中提出的诊断工具包进行偏见审计。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and reasoning-tuned models such as DeepSeek-R1 are commonly assumed to reduce shallow heuristic biases by thinking carefully. We test this on position bias in multiple-choice QA and find a different story: within any reasoning-capable model, per-question position bias scales with the length of the reasoning trajectory.
  Across thirteen reasoning-mode configurations (two R1-distilled 7-8B models, two base models prompted with CoT, and DeepSeek-R1 at 671B) on MMLU, ARC-Challenge, and GPQA, twelve show a positive partial correlation between trajectory length and Position Bias Score (PBS) after controlling for accuracy, ranging from 0.11 to 0.41 (all p < 0.05). All twelve open-weight reasoning-mode configurations show monotonically increasing PBS across length quartiles. A truncation intervention provides causal evidence: continuations resumed from later points in the trajectory are increasingly likely to shift toward position-preferred options (16% to 32% for R1-Qwen-7B across absolute-position buckets).
  At 671B, aggregate PBS collapses to 0.019, but the length effect still manifests in the longest quartile (PBS = 0.071), suggesting that accuracy gates the expression of length-driven bias rather than eliminating the underlying mechanism. We additionally find that direct-answer position bias is a distinct phenomenon with a different footprint (strong in Llama-Instruct-direct, weak in Qwen-Instruct-direct, and uncorrelated with trajectory length): CoT reasoning replaces this baseline bias with length-accumulated bias.
  Our results argue that reasoning-capable models should not be treated as order-robust by default in MCQ evaluation pipelines, and offer a diagnostic toolkit (PBS, commitment change point, effective switching, truncation probes) for auditing position bias in reasoning models.

</details>


### [258] [State Representation and Termination for Recursive Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06690)
*Debashis Guha,Amritendu Mukherjee,Sanjay Kukreja,Tarun Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过定义“状态图”来表示递归推理过程，并引入“order-gap”度量作为停止迭代的准则，为判断推理系统何时达到有效平衡提供了一个理论分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理系统在扩展（获取新证据）和合并（优化理解）的交替过程中，缺乏通用的状态表示方法，且对何时停止迭代往往缺乏严谨的理论依据。

Method: 1. 使用“认识状态图（epistemic state graph）”形式化表示包含推理声明、证据关系、开放问题及置信权重的推理状态。2. 定义“order-gap（顺序间隙）”：比较“先扩展后合并”与“先合并后扩展”两种路径所得状态的距离。3. 通过对线性化 order-gap 在不动点附近的分析，推导出该停止判定标准的非退化性条件。

Result: 证明了线性化 order-gap 在不动点附近非退化的充要条件。该条件作为一个局部指标，能够有效判断停止准则是否具有信息量（而非代数上的无效），并将其应用于 Agent 循环、思维树（ToT）、定理证明及持续学习等场景。

Conclusion: 提出了一个关于递归推理系统何时停止迭代的理论框架。通过“order-gap”量化推理状态的收敛一致性，提供了一个局部条件来判断推理是否达到有效平衡点。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning systems alternate between acquiring new evidence and refining an accumulated understanding. Two design choices are typically left implicit: how to represent the evolving reasoning state, and when to stop iterating. This paper addresses both. We represent the reasoning state as an epistemic state graph encoding extracted claims, evidential relations, open questions, and confidence weights. We define the order-gap as the distance between the states reached by expand-then-consolidate versus consolidate-then-expand; a small order-gap suggests that the two orderings agree and further iteration is unlikely to help. Our main result gives a necessary and sufficient condition for the linearised order-gap to be non-degenerate near the fixed point, showing when the criterion is informative rather than algebraically vacuous. This is a local condition, not a global convergence guarantee. We apply the framework to recursive reasoning systems and sketch its application to agent loops, tree-of-thought reasoning, theorem proving, and continual learning.

</details>


### [259] [Weblica: Scalable and Reproducible Training Environments for Visual Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06761)
*Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Yuanzheng Gong,Anders Boesen Lindbo Larsen,Afshin Dehghan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Weblica是一个通过HTTP缓存和LLM合成实现可扩展Web环境构建的框架，显著提升了视觉Web智能体的训练效能与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前Web智能体训练数据规模受限、难以覆盖Web多样性（复杂、开放、动态）的问题，特别是克服离线轨迹监督微调和模拟环境数量不足的局限。

Method: 提出Weblica框架，包含两个核心技术：1) 基于HTTP级别的缓存技术，用于捕获和重放稳定的视觉状态并保持交互性；2) 基于LLM的环境合成技术，结合真实网站和核心导航技能生成多样化任务。

Result: 在数千个多样化环境和任务中进行RL训练，Weblica-8B在多项Web导航基准测试中表现卓越，推理步骤更少，且具备良好的测试时计算扩展性。

Conclusion: Weblica通过HTTP缓存和LLM环境合成提供了一种可扩展的视觉Web智能体训练范式，Weblica-8B在该框架下展示了优异的性能，不仅超越了同规模开源基线，还具备与API模型竞争的能力。

Abstract: The web is complex, open-ended, and constantly changing, making it challenging to scale training data for visual web agents. Existing data collection attempts remain limited to offline trajectories for supervised fine-tuning or a handful of simulated environments for RL training, thus failing to capture web diversity. We propose Weblica (Web Replica), a framework for constructing reproducible and scalable web environments. Our framework leverages 1) HTTP-level caching to capture and replay stable visual states while preserving interactive behavior and 2) LLM-based environment synthesis grounded in real-world websites and core web navigation skills. Using this framework, we scale RL training to thousands of diverse environments and tasks. Our best model, Weblica-8B, outperforms open-weight baselines of similar size across multiple web navigation benchmarks while using fewer inference steps, scales favorably with additional test-time compute, and is competitive with API models.

</details>


### [260] [Towards Security-Auditable LLM Agents: A Unified Graph Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06812)
*Chaofan Li,Lyuye Zhang,Jintao Zhai,Siyue Feng,Xichun Yang,Huahao Wang,Shihan Dou,Yu Ji,Yutao Hu,Yueming Wu,Yang Liu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 AI 智能体系统缺乏有效安全审计手段的问题，本文提出了 Agent-BOM 图结构表示法，通过关联动静态语义信息，实现了对复杂智能体攻击链的精准重构与风险审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的智能体系统在执行动态任务时存在严重的“语义鸿沟”，导致现有的审计工具（如 SBOM 和日志）无法捕获认知状态演变、权限绑定、内存污染及复杂的攻击链传播，使得事后安全审计极为困难。

Method: 提出了一种基于分层属性有向图（hierarchical attributed directed graph）的 Agent-BOM 表示模型，将静态能力库与动态语义状态分离，并通过语义边关联；开发了基于图查询的风险评估范式，并在 OpenClaw 环境中实现了审计插件。

Result: 实验表明，Agent-BOM 能够成功重构隐蔽的攻击链，包括跨会话内存投毒、工具滥用、能力供应链劫持、多智能体生态劫持以及权限与信任滥用等。

Conclusion: Agent-BOM 提供了一种统一且可审计的框架，有效地解决了复杂智能体系统中安全审计的难题，为根因分析和安全判定奠定了基础。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems are rapidly evolving to perform complex autonomous tasks through dynamic tool invocation, stateful memory management, and multi-agent collaboration. However, this semantics-driven execution paradigm creates a severe semantic gap between low-level physical events and high-level execution intent, making post-hoc security auditing fundamentally difficult. Existing representation mechanisms, including static SBOMs and runtime logs, provide only fragmented evidence and fail to capture cognitive-state evolution, capability bindings, persistent memory contamination, and cascading risk propagation across interacting agents. To bridge this gap, we propose Agent-BOM, a unified structural representation for agent security auditing. Agent-BOM models an agentic system as a hierarchical attributed directed graph that separates static capability bases, such as models, tools, and long-term memory, from dynamic runtime semantic states, such as goals, reasoning trajectories, and actions. These layers are connected through semantic edges and security attributes, transforming fragmented execution traces into queryable audit paths. Building on Agent-BOM, we develop a graph-query-based paradigm for path-level risk assessment and instantiate it with the OWASP Agentic Top 10. We further implement an auditing plugin in the OpenClaw environment to construct Agent-BOM from live executions. Evaluation on representative real-world agentic attack scenarios shows that Agent-BOM can reconstruct stealthy attack chains, including cross-session memory poisoning and tool misuse, capability supply-chain hijacking and unexpected code execution, multi-agent ecosystem hijacking, and privilege and trust abuse. These results demonstrate that Agent-BOM provides a unified and auditable foundation for root-cause analysis and security adjudication in complex agentic ecosystems.

</details>


### [261] [Uneven Evolution of Cognition Across Generations of Generative AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06815)
*Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Daniel McDuff,Xin Liu,Jed McGiffin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过心理测量框架评估AI认知能力，发现生成式模型表现出显著的“语言能力优越、视觉推理解析缺失”的非均衡架构，且单纯依靠模型规模扩张难以弥补这种架构偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法多侧重于窄域任务性能，难以衡量模型在迈向通用人工智能（AGI）过程中的认知能力与演进规律，迫切需要更科学的心理测量框架来揭示其认知架构。

Method: 1. 借鉴韦氏成人智力量表（WAIS）构建心理测量框架，评估AI的认知维度；2. 开发人工智商（AIQ）基准测试，跨世代、跨模型系列分析认知进化轨迹；3. 对比语言与视觉格式下同一任务的表现，分析模式偏好。

Result: 1. 模型认知架构极度不均衡：语言理解/工作记忆位列人类98百分位以上，但感知推理处于1百分位以下；2. 揭示了模态解离（modal dissociation）：模型在语言呈现的量化推理上表现远好于视觉呈现，呈现显著的语言偏好；3. 视觉感知重组能力改进缓慢，存在演进停滞现象。

Conclusion: 当前生成式AI的认知能力呈现不均衡发展且存在架构性限制。仅靠模型缩放（scaling）和优化不足以实现类似于人类的平衡通用智能，需要解决其视觉感知与符号/语言推理之间的深层架构偏差。

Abstract: The pursuit of artificial general intelligence necessitates robust methods for evaluating the cognitive capabilities of models beyond narrow task performance. Here, we introduce a psychometric framework to assess the cognitive profiles of generative AI, comparing them to human norms and tracking their evolution across generations. Initial evaluation of leading multimodal models using tasks adapted from the Wechsler Adult Intelligence Scale revealed a profoundly uneven cognitive architecture: near-ceiling performance in verbal comprehension and working memory (>$98^{\text{th}}$ percentile) contrasted with near-floor performance in perceptual reasoning (<$1^{\text{st}}$ percentile). To track developmental trajectories beyond human-normed limits, we developed the Artificial Intelligence Quotient (AIQ) Benchmark and applied it to six generations and two model families, revealing significant but asymmetric performance gains. Notably, we uncovered a sharp dissociation between modalities; abstract quantitative reasoning matured far more rapidly when presented linguistically compared to a visually analogous format, indicating an architectural bias towards language-based symbolic manipulation. While abstract visual reasoning improved, visual-perceptual organization remained largely stagnant. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that the cognitive abilities of generative models are evolving unevenly, suggesting that scaling and optimization approaches to AGI development alone may be insufficient to overcome fundamental architectural limitations in achieving balanced, human-like general intelligence.

</details>


### [262] [Extracting Search Trees from LLM Reasoning Traces Reveals Myopic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06840)
*Sixing Chen,Ji-An Li,Saner Cakir,Sinan Akcali,Kayla Lee,Marcelo G. Mattar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大模型（LLM）虽然能生成深度的思维链，但其决策实质上是“短视”的。通过在四子棋实验中量化搜索树，发现大模型性能依赖宽度而非深度，且其深层推理路径并未真正驱动最终决策，这与人类依赖深度搜索的精细规划形成了鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽知大模型能生成长思维链（CoT），但缺乏对其内部是否进行真实规划、如何结构化以及如何驱动决策的系统理解。

Method: 以“四子棋”（four-in-a-row）为实验环境，通过提取和量化推理过程中的搜索树，拟合计算模型分析规划结构，并结合因果干预实验（修剪思维链段落）来验证不同深度节点对决策的影响。

Result: 1. 大模型的搜索深度普遍浅于人类；2. 大模型性能主要由搜索宽度而非深度决定；3. 大模型推理中虽有深度节点，但其决策更多被“近视”模型解释（即忽略了深层节点）；4. 因果干预实验证实，大模型的动作选择主要由浅层节点而非深层节点驱动。

Conclusion: 大模型与人类在规划决策上存在根本差异：人类专家依赖于深度的搜索规划，而大模型虽然能生成深度推理路径，但其决策实际更多受制于短效（myopic）的浅层决策逻辑，并未真正利用深度前瞻。该研究为对齐人类与大模型的规划能力提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), especially reasoning models, generate extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that often contains explicit deliberation over future outcomes. Yet whether this deliberation constitutes genuine planning, how it is structured, and what aspects of it drive performance remain poorly understood. In this work, we introduce a new method to characterize LLM planning by extracting and quantifying search trees from reasoning traces in the four-in-a-row board game. By fitting computational models on the extracted search trees, we characterize how plans are structured and how they influence move decisions. We find that LLMs' search is shallower than humans', and that performance is predicted by search breadth rather than depth. Most strikingly, although LLMs expand deep nodes in their traces, their move choices are best explained by a myopic model that ignores those nodes entirely. A causal intervention study where we selectively prune CoT paragraphs further suggests that move selection is driven predominantly by shallow rather than deep nodes. These patterns contrast with human planning, where performance is driven primarily by deep search. Together, our findings reveal a key difference between LLM and human planning: while human expertise is driven by deeper search, LLMs do not act on deep lookahead. This dissociation offers targeted guidance for aligning LLM and human planning. More broadly, our framework provides a generalizable approach for interpreting the structure of LLM planning across strategic domains.

</details>


### [263] [AGWM: Affordance-Grounded World Models for Environments with Compositional Prerequisites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06841)
*Qinshi Zhang,Weipeng Deng,Zhihan Jiang,Jiaming Qu,Qianren Li,Weitao Xu,Ray LC*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统世界模型在交互环境中无法处理动作执行先决条件导致预测误差累计的问题，本文提出了 AGWM，通过图结构显式建模动作的可执行性，提高了长程预测的稳定性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型倾向于将频繁共现的动作与结果误判为因果关系，忽视了动作的前置条件。在交互式环境中，动作会带来“结构改变事件（SC Events）”，导致传统模型在长程预测中出现累计误差。

Method: 提出了一种“基于可供性（Affordance）的世界模型”（AGWM），通过学习一个有向无环图（DAG）形式的抽象可供性结构，显式跟踪动作的动态可执行性。

Result: 在游戏模拟环境中的实验表明，AGWM 显著降低了多步预测误差，在处理未知环境配置时表现出更强的泛化能力，并增强了模型行为的可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过引入对动作前提条件的显式建模，AGWM 有效解决了传统世界模型在处理结构变化事件时的预测漂移问题，提升了模型的泛化能力和可解释性。

Abstract: In model-based learning, the agent learns behaviors by simulating trajectories based on world model predictions. Standard world models typically learn a stationary transition function that maps states and actions to next states, when an action and an outcome frequently co-occur in training data, the model tends to internalize this correlation as a general causal rule while ignoring action preconditions. In interactive environments, however, agent actions can reshape the future affordance space. At each timestep, an action may becomes executable only after its prerequisites are met, or non-executable when they are destroyed. We term such events structure-changing events (SC events). As a result, a conventional world model often fails to determine whether a given action is executable in the current state, especially in multi-step predictions. Each imagined step is conditioned on an incorrect affordance state, and therefore the prediction error compounds over the rollout horizon. In this paper, we propose AGWM (Affordance-Grounded World Model), which learns an abstract affordance structure represented as a DAG of prerequisite dependencies to explicitly track the dynamic executability of actions. Experiments on game-based simulated environments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by achieving lower multi-step prediction error, better generalization to novel configurations, and improved interpretability.

</details>


### [264] [Agentick: A Unified Benchmark for General Sequential Decision-Making Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06869)
*Roger Creus Castanyer,Pablo Samuel Castro,Glen Berseth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentick 是一个全新的多模态序列决策基准，旨在统一评估不同 AI 智能体架构（RL、LLM、VLM 等），通过 37 个多样化任务发现当前各类模型在不同能力上各有所长，且均存在较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的 AI 智能体研究缺乏一个能够涵盖从训练（RL）到预训练模型利用（Foundation Model Agents）等不同范式的统一基准，无法公平地比较不同方法在序列决策任务上的表现。

Method: 构建了一个包含37个程序生成任务的 Gymnasium 兼容基准测试平台。提供了统一的 Coding API、Oracle 参考策略、预构建的 SFT 数据集以及可组合的智能体评估套件（harness），支持包括 RL、LLM、VLM、混合及人类智能体的多范式评估。

Result: 在对27种配置和9万多集测试中发现：GPT-4o mini 综合性能领先（Oracle 归一化得分 0.309），但在规划和多智能体任务中 PPO 表现更好；推理框架可将 LLM 性能提升 3-10 倍；ASCII 形式的观测表现持续优于自然语言描述。

Conclusion: Agentick 提供了一个能力分解、多模态的评估框架，填补了现有通用智能体基准的空白。实证研究表明当前没有单一范式占据绝对主导地位，各方法在不同任务类型上表现各异，强调了在规划、推理和观测模态方面仍有巨大的研究提升空间。

Abstract: AI agent research spans a wide spectrum: from RL agents that learn from scratch to foundation model agents that leverage pre-trained knowledge, yet no unified benchmark enables fair comparison across these approaches. We present Agentick, a benchmark for sequential decision-making agents designed to evaluate RL, LLM, VLM, hybrid, and human agents on common ground and to power research on the fundamental challenges of sequential decision-making. Agentick provides 37 procedurally generated tasks across six capability categories, four difficulty levels, and five observation modalities, all exposed through a single Gymnasium-compatible interface.
