To address this, we formulate synthesis planning as a multi-objective search problem and introduce MORetro*, an algorithm that generates a Pareto front of synthesis routes to explicitly capture trade-offs among user-defined criteria. MORetro* uses weighted scalarization and BO-informed sampling to efficiently navigate the combinatorial search space and prioritize promising trade-offs. Building on multi-objective A*-search, we provide optimality guarantees showing that, for a fixed single-step model, MORetro* recovers the true Pareto front. Across multiple retrosynthesis benchmarks, MORetro* produces diverse, high-quality Pareto fronts, uncovering solutions overlooked by single-objective approaches and better aligning CASP outputs with industrial decision-making.

</details>


### [301] [Multi-Environment POMDPs with Finite-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07537)
*Léonard Brice,Filip Cano,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Thomas A. Henzinger,Stefanie Muroya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多环境POMDPs（MEPOMDPs），证明其有限视界下的最优策略计算问题属于PSPACE-complete，并提出了一种超越现有技术的实用求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多环境部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（MEPOMDPs），解决在初始状态未知且为对抗性选择条件下的最优策略计算难题。

Method: 建立计算复杂性理论证明，并提出了一种新的实用求解算法，通过经典基准测试进行验证。

Result: 1. 证明MEPOMDPs在有限视界下是PSPACE-complete的；2. 提出的新算法在经典基准测试中表现显著优于之前唯一的已知算法。

Conclusion: MEPOMDPs在有限视界目标下的最优策略计算与POMDPs复杂度相同（均为PSPACE-complete），且本文提出的算法在实际应用中显著优于现有基准方案。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) are systems in which one agent interacts with a stochastic environment, and receives only partial information about the current state. In a multi-environment POMDP (MEPOMDP), the initial state is unknown, and assumed to be adversarially chosen. In this work we focus on computing the optimal value and policy in MEPOMDPs with finite-horizon objectives. That problem is known to be PSPACE-complete in POMDPs. Our main results are as follows: (1) we establish that it is also PSPACE-complete in the more general setting of MEPOMDPs; (2) we present a practical algorithm and evaluate it on classical benchmarks, significantly outperforming the only previously known algorithm.

</details>


### [302] [From Pixels to Prompts: Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07544)
*Khang Hoang Nhat Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）领域迭代过快、理解门槛高的问题，本书通过构建系统化的认知框架，帮助读者掌握模型底层逻辑，提升论文解读能力与系统设计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型领域发展极快，新概念与模型层出不穷。作者观察到领域内存在“知其然但不知其所以然”的痛点，希望填补初学者与专业理解之间的鸿沟，降低理解门槛。

Method: 通过梳理VLM领域的底层逻辑与结构化思维，而非进行简单的文献堆砌，旨在建立读者对该领域的直觉与系统性认知。

Result: 提出了一种结构化的视觉-语言模型学习框架，帮助读者建立长效的“心理地图”，以应对日新月异的技术迭代。

Conclusion: 本书旨在通过提供清晰的视觉-语言模型（VLM）知识框架，帮助读者构建持久的认知地图，从而能够自如地解读前沿论文并进行系统设计，而非仅仅罗列瞬息万变的技术细节。

Abstract: When you read a paper about a new Vision-Language Model today, it can be easy to forget how strange this idea would have sounded not so long ago. Teaching machines to see was already hard. Teaching them to read and generate language was already hard. Asking them to do both at once - and then to reason, answer questions, follow instructions, and sometimes even surprise us - still carries a quiet trace of science fiction, even as it becomes routine. This book was born from a simple feeling: \emph{it is too easy to get lost}. The field moves quickly, new model names appear constantly, and the gap between ``I know the buzzwords'' and ``I actually understand how this works'' can feel uncomfortably wide. I have felt that gap many times. If you are holding this book, you probably have too. My goal is not to provide an exhaustive catalog of every dataset, benchmark, and new model variant. Instead, I want to offer something more modest - and, I hope, more durable: a clear mental map of Vision-Language Models. Enough structure that you can read new papers with confidence; enough intuition that you can design your own systems without feeling as if you are assembling LEGO bricks blindly.

</details>


### [303] [Parallel Lifted Planning via Semi-Naive Datalog Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07584)
*Dominik Drexler,Oliver Joergensen,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过双层并行化执行模型和优化的 Datalog 求值器，显著提升了提升式经典规划的求解性能，特别是在面对复杂接地任务时，实现了显著的并行加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升式规划避免了繁琐的预接地（grounding）步骤，但在搜索过程中频繁的实例化操作导致性能较低。现有工作通过 Datalog 视角进行研究，但仍需进一步提升计算效率和并行化程度。

Method: 1. 构建包含规则级（rule-level）和接地级（grounding-level）的双层并行执行模型；2. 开发针对规划任务优化的 Datalog 求值器，利用团枚举算法提升接地效率，并支持半朴素（semi-naive）评估。

Result: 在单核上求解的规划任务数超过基准；在难以接地的任务上，证明了并行模型具有 92.4% 的平均并行分数；在 8 核环境下实现了最高 6 倍的性能提速。

Conclusion: 通过引入双层并行执行模型和基于团枚举（clique enumeration）的半朴素 Datalog 求值器，成功克服了提升式规划（lifted planning）在搜索过程中的计算瓶颈，在单核和多核环境下均展现出优于现有基准的性能。

Abstract: Lifted classical planners operate directly on first-order planning tasks to avoid the computationally demanding grounding step. However, lifted planning is typically slower, as planners must repeatedly instantiate ground structures during search. Many core components of lifted classical planning, such as successor generation, axiom evaluation, task grounding, and delete-relaxed heuristics, have previously been studied through the lens of Datalog evaluation. We build upon this line of work and extend it by developing and analyzing an execution model with two levels of parallelism: rule-level parallelism and grounding parallelism. We further specialize this solver for planning-specific workloads with a grounder based on clique enumeration, which we extend to support semi-naive Datalog evaluation. Our experimental evaluation using greedy best-first search with the FF heuristic shows that our implementation already solves more tasks than the baselines on a single core, and the gap widens as additional cores are used. Moreover, on hard-to-ground tasks where on average 97.6% of the total runtime is spent in Datalog execution, the proposed execution model exhibits an average parallel fraction of 92.4%, while achieving up to a 6-fold speedup on 8 cores in practice.

</details>


### [304] [Inference Time Causal Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07631)
*Sadegh Khorasani,Saber Salehkaleybar,Negar Kiyavash,Matthias Grossglauser*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需分类器探针的因果干预技术（HDMI），通过在隐层空间应用裕度目标直接操控生成模型，有效提升了干预行为的可靠性与选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果探测（Causal probing）方法过度依赖辅助探针分类器（auxiliary probe classifiers），这导致方法与模型特定任务绑定，且存在与模型内置表征几何结构失调（misalignment）的风险。

Method: 提出“隐层导向裕度干预”（Hidden-state Driven Margin Intervention, HDMI）：利用模型原生输出，通过裕度目标（margin objective）引导隐层状态；引入Lookahead变体（LA-HDMI）进行文本编辑，通过反向传播至softmax嵌入层调整当前隐层状态，在保持文本流畅度的同时提升目标Token概率。

Result: 在LGD一致性语料库和CausalGym基准测试中，基于Meta-Llama-3-8B及Pythia-70M模型，HDMI在可靠性指标（完备性与选择性的调和平均数）上始终超过了现有的探测方法。

Conclusion: HDMI提出了一种无需探针（probe-free）且基于梯度的干预方法，通过直接操作模型隐层状态实现高效的因果控制，在可靠性（完备性与选择性的平衡）方面显著优于现有的依赖探针的方法。

Abstract: Causal probing methods aim to test and control how internal representations influence the behavior of generative models. In causal probing, an intervention modifies hidden states so that a property takes on a different value. Most existing approaches define such interventions by training an auxiliary probe classifier, which ties the method to a specific task or model and risks misalignment with the model's predictive geometry. We propose Hidden-state Driven Margin Intervention (HDMI), a probe-free, gradient-based technique that directly steers hidden states using the model's native output. HDMI applies a margin objective that increases the probability of a target continuation while decreasing that of the source, without relying on probe classifiers. We further introduce a lookahead variant (LA-HDMI) for text editing that backpropagates through the softmax embeddings, modifying the current hidden state so that the likelihood of user-specified tokens increases in next token generations while preserving fluency. To evaluate interventions, we measure completeness (whether the targeted property changes as intended) and selectivity (whether unrelated properties are preserved), and report their harmonic mean as an overall measure of reliability. HDMI consistently achieves higher reliability than prior methods on the LGD agreement corpus and the CausalGym benchmark, across Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct, and Pythia-70M.

</details>


### [305] [Learning to Communicate Locally for Large-Scale Multi-Agent Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07637)
*Valeriy Vyaltsev,Alsu Sagirova,Anton Andreychuk,Yuri Kuratov,Konstantin Yakovlev,Aleksandr Panov,Alexey Skrynnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LC-MAPF的去中心化多智能体路径规划框架，通过引入可学习的局部通信模块提升智能体协作能力，在超越现有学习型方法的同时，保持了优良的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有基于机器学习的去中心化MAPF方法中，智能体缺乏有效合作与信息交流导致的路径规划受限问题，同时克服以往通信机制带来的可扩展性差的瓶颈。

Method: 提出LC-MAPF（多智能体路径规划中的局部通信）框架，通过引入一个可学习的通信模块，使智能体能够在环境决策中与邻近智能体进行多轮信息交换与特征共享。

Result: 实验证明，LC-MAPF在多种测试场景及各评估指标上均优于现有的基于模仿学习（IL）和强化学习（RL）的MAPF求解器，且该通信机制未造成额外的扩展性压力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的LC-MAPF框架结合了多轮通信机制与深度强化学习，在保持高可扩展性的前提下，显著提升了多智能体路径规划的协同效率与决策质量，优于现有的单智能体视角学习方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) is a widely used abstraction for multi-robot trajectory planning problems, where multiple homogeneous agents move simultaneously within a shared environment. Although solving MAPF optimally is NP-hard, scalable and efficient solvers are critical for real-world applications such as logistics and search-and-rescue. To this end, the research community has proposed various decentralized suboptimal MAPF solvers that leverage machine learning. Such methods frame MAPF (from a single agent perspective) as a Dec-POMDP where at each time step an agent has to decide an action based on the local observation and typically solve the problem via reinforcement learning or imitation learning. We follow the same approach but additionally introduce a learnable communication module tailored to enhance cooperation between agents via efficient feature sharing. We present the Local Communication for Multi-agent Pathfinding (LC-MAPF), a generalizable pre-trained model that applies multi-round communication between neighboring agents to exchange information and improve their coordination. Our experiments show that the introduced method outperforms the existing learning-based MAPF solvers, including IL and RL-based approaches, across diverse metrics in a diverse range of (unseen) test scenarios. Remarkably, the introduced communication mechanism does not compromise LC-MAPF's scalability, a common bottleneck for communication-based MAPF solvers.

</details>


### [306] [Tacit Knowledge Extraction via Logic Augmented Generation and Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07639)
*Lorenzo Lamazzi,Aldo Gangemi,Alessio Giberti,Andrea Giovanni Nuzzolese,Vittorio Andrea Rocca,Mattia Torta,Francesco Poggi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合逻辑增强生成与主动推理的神经符号框架，成功实现了从教学视频中提取并形式化隐性知识，显著提升了工业知识工程中知识图谱的构建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决隐性知识（如经验、上下文约束、隐含假设）难以被捕获、形式化及机器可解释化的问题，弥补当前知识工程应对过程导向知识时的不足。

Method: 提出了一种结合“逻辑增强生成（Logic-Augmented Generation）”与“主动推理（Active-Inference）”机制的神经符号框架，用于以本体论为基础的知识图谱构建。以教学视频中的装配类维修程序为研究对象进行案例验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提高了知识图谱的完备性（completeness）和语义质量（semantic quality）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了所提出的神经符号框架能够有效提升工业领域隐性知识的获取、规范化与知识图谱构建质量，为隐性知识工程提供了一种可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Tacit knowledge plays a central role in human expertise, yet it remains difficult to capture, formalize, and reuse in machine-interpretable form. This challenge is especially relevant in procedural domains, where successful execution depends not only on explicit instructions, but also on implicit assumptions, contextual constraints, embodied skills, and experience-based judgments rarely documented. As a result, current knowledge engineering pipelines struggle to transform tacit and process-centric knowledge into formally specified, machine-interpretable representations that can be queried, validated, reasoned over, and reused. In this paper, we introduce a neuro-symbolic framework that combines Logic-Augmented Generation and an Active-Inference-inspired approach for ontology-grounded Knowledge Graph construction. We evaluate the approach in a knowledge transfer case study in manufacturing, using assembly-like repair procedures from instructional videos as a reproducible proxy domain. Results show that the proposed solution improves completeness and semantic quality, advancing neuro-symbolic knowledge engineering for industrial domains.

</details>


### [307] [FactoryBench: Evaluating Industrial Machine Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07675)
*Yanis Merzouki,Coral Izquierdo,Matei Ignuta-Ciuncanu,Marcos Gomez-Bracamonte,Riccardo Maggioni,Alessandro Lombardi,Camilla Mazzoleni,Federico Martelli,Balazs Gunther,Jonas Petersen,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FactoryBench，一个针对工业机器人遥测数据的机器理解基准，通过构建涵盖因果阶梯的7万多条问答数据，评估发现当前大模型在处理结构化工业逻辑和决策任务上表现能力极度受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人遥测数据的机器理解能力评估缺乏统一规范，现有的模型在理解复杂工业流程及其因果逻辑方面能力严重受限。

Method: 1. 提出FactoryBench基准，基于Pearl因果阶梯构建四个层级的问答；2. 建立可扩展的Q&A自动化生成框架；3. 发布FactoryWave数据集（多变量传感器数据）；4. 构建包含超过7万个问答对的基准，并采用确定性评分与LLM-as-judge结合的评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含FactoryWave及其他数据集的7万+问答项，零样本评估表明主流大模型在处理工业遥测任务时面临巨大挑战。

Conclusion: 当前前沿大模型在处理工业机器人遥测数据方面表现远未达标（结构化问题准确率<50%，决策任务准确率<18%），揭示了通用模型在工业领域机器理解能力上的巨大不足。

Abstract: We introduce FactoryBench, a benchmark for evaluating time-series models and LLMs on machine understanding over industrial robotic telemetry. Q&A pairs are organized along four causal levels (state, intervention, counterfactual, decision) instantiating Pearl's ladder of causation, and span five answer formats: four structured formats are scored deterministically and free-form answers are scored by an LLM-as-judge voting protocol. We propose a scalable Q&A generation framework built around structured question templates, present FactoryWave (a dense, multitask, multivariate sensor dataset collected from a UR3 cobot and a KUKA KR10 industrial arm), and construct FactoryBench as a large-scale benchmark of over 70k Q&A items grounded in roughly 15k normalized episodes from FactoryWave, AURSAD, and voraus-AD. Zero-shot evaluation of six frontier LLMs shows that no model exceeds 50% on structured levels or 18% on decision-making, revealing a wide gap between current models and operational machine understanding.

</details>


### [308] [Online Goal Recognition using Path Signature and Dynamic Time Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07736)
*Douglas Tesch,Nathan Gavenski,Leonardo Amado,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用路径签名进行在线目标识别的新方法，通过高效捕捉轨迹语义特征，显著提升了在线任务中的预测精度与分析效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线目标识别方法在处理连续域轨迹时，存在轨迹编码效率低和比较机制不完善的问题，且未能充分利用其他领域成熟的编码技术（如路径签名）。

Method: 引入“路径签名”（path signatures）技术对轨迹进行编码。利用粗糙路径理论（rough path theory）中的这一工具捕捉轨迹的关键语义特征，从而实现对在线轨迹的高效编码与准确比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在预测准确率和在线规划效率方面均优于现有的前沿（SOTA）方法，同时具备良好的计算性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于路径签名的方法在在线目标识别任务中表现优异，不仅显著提升了预测准确率和在线规划效率，且在离线场景下也保持了极具竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Online goal recognition in continuous domains poses two central challenges: efficiently encoding large trajectories and effectively comparing them. Recent work addresses these challenges by using custom state-space representations and metrics to compare observations against hypotheses. However, these approaches often overlook well-established encoding techniques used in other domains that offer substantial advantages. This paper introduces a novel method for online goal recognition that leverages path signatures, a compact, expressive representation of rough path theory that efficiently captures key semantic features of trajectories, enabling more meaningful comparisons between them. Experiments show that our method consistently outperforms the state of the art in predictive accuracy and online planning efficiency, while remaining competitive offline.

</details>


### [309] [RuleSafe-VL: Evaluating Rule-Conditioned Decision Reasoning in Vision-Language Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07760)
*Zhifeng Lu,Dianyuan Wang,Yuhu Shang,Zhenbo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入 RuleSafe-VL 基准测试，通过分解内容审核的规则推理过程，揭示了主流多模态模型在处理规则依赖与上下文逻辑时的重大缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅关注最终标签匹配，忽略了内容审核中核心的规则执行逻辑和因果推理过程，导致模型可能依赖表面特征而非遵循策略规则进行决策。

Method: 提出 RuleSafe-VL 基准测试，包含 93 条原子规则和 92 种规则关系，涵盖 2,166 个上下文敏感的多模态案例，并设计了识别规则、恢复规则关系、判断决策充分性及补全上下文得出结果等四项诊断任务。

Result: 在 10 个前沿模型上的实验表明，规则关系恢复是主要瓶颈（最高仅 64.8 Macro-F1），部分安全导向模型表现极差（低于 7 Macro-F1）；决策状态预测同样不可靠（约 64.5 Macro-F1）。

Conclusion: RuleSafe-VL 将内容审核评估从单纯的终局标签匹配转向基于规则的诊断推理评估，揭示了当前多模态大模型在处理复杂策略逻辑时的显著局限性。

Abstract: Platform content moderation applies explicit policy rules and context-dependent conditions to decide whether user content is allowed, restricted, or removed. A correct moderation outcome must therefore depend on which rules a case activates, how those rules interact, and whether the available evidence is sufficient. Current multimodal safety benchmarks largely reduce moderation to matching predefined final labels, leaving this underlying rule structure untested. As a result, a high benchmark score reveals little about whether a model applies the policy correctly or arrives at the correct label through superficial cues. To evaluate this rule-governed process, we introduce RuleSafe-VL, a benchmark for rule-conditioned decision reasoning in vision-language content moderation. Derived from publicly available platform moderation policies, RuleSafe-VL formalizes 93 atomic rules and 92 typed rule relations, yielding 2,166 context-sensitive image-text cases across three high-risk policy families. Its four diagnostic tasks decompose moderation into a rule-conditioned decision chain. They identify activated rules, recover rule interactions, judge decision sufficiency, and resolve outcomes once missing context is supplied. Experiments on 10 frontier, open-source, and safety-oriented VLMs reveal rule-relation recovery as the dominant bottleneck, where the best model reaches only 64.8 Macro-F1 and some safety-oriented models fall below 7 Macro-F1. Decision-state prediction also remains unreliable, peaking at 64.5 Macro-F1. RuleSafe-VL shifts moderation evaluation from final-label scoring toward diagnostic assessment of rule-conditioned decision reasoning.

</details>


### [310] [Exact Regular-Constrained Variable-Order Markov Generation via Sparse Context-State Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07839)
*François Pachet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在变阶马尔可夫模型中应用正则约束进行精确推理的算法，通过构造稀疏乘积自动机解决了高阶信息保存与复杂约束计算之间的矛盾，实现了高效且精确的序列模型推断与数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正则约束下的置信传播方法仅适用于一阶马尔可夫链。而变阶马尔可夫模型在生成序列时利用长短不一的上下文，简单的降阶处理会导致历史信息丢失，因此需要一种能维持变阶特性并同时满足正则约束的精确推断机制。

Method: 1. 定义了变阶马尔可夫模型状态空间与正则约束自动机的乘积结构；2. 构建了基于观测上下文状态的稀疏模型，取代传统的固定阶马尔可夫状态以维持上下文区分；3. 应用置信传播（BP）算法计算有限视野内的推断；4. 利用逆计数查找技术支持可逆数据增强。

Result: 该方法实现了一次遍历即可得到满足正则约束的变阶分布，且复杂度与序列视界呈线性关系（在可达乘积边数量上为多项式关系）；无需显式展开 K-tuple，且支持高效的数据增强及对生成时回退策略的精确数学建模。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对变阶马尔可夫模型（VOMM）在满足正则约束条件下的精确推理方法，通过构建包含可变上下文状态的乘积自动机，有效解决了高阶历史区分与正则约束之间的不匹配问题，且推理复杂度保持在多项式级别。

Abstract: Variable-order Markov models generate sequences over a finite alphabet by conditioning each symbol on the longest available suffix of the generated history. Regular constraints, by contrast, describe finite-horizon control requirements by an automaton: fixed positions, forced endings, metrical patterns, and forbidden copied fragments are all special cases. Existing exact methods already handle regular constraints with belief propagation for first-order Markov chains. The contribution here is the variable-order extension: identifying the state space on which the existing BP-regular machinery must be run when the generator is a variable-order/backoff model. A first-order constraint layer can enforce useful support conditions, but it computes future mass after merging histories that a variable-order generator deliberately keeps distinct. We formalize this mismatch and give the sparse construction obtained by replacing the first-order Markov state with the observed context state, then taking the standard product with the regular constraint automaton. For a fixed trained context graph and automaton, inference is linear in the sequence horizon; in general it is polynomial in the number of reachable product edges. This gives the correct variable-order distribution conditioned on regular constraints without expanding to all K-tuples. The same finite-source interface supports reversible data augmentation by inverse count lookup, matching materialized transposition augmentation without storing transformed corpora. We also separate exact BP inference from generation-time backoff policies, such as singleton avoidance, whose stochastic semantics must be made explicit if exactness is claimed.

</details>


### [311] [TraceFix: Repairing Agent Coordination Protocols with TLA+ Counterexamples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07935)
*Shuren Xia,Qiwei Li,Taqiya Ehsan,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceFix 是一种首个基于 TLA+ 模型检查的 LLM 多智能体协作验证框架，通过协同协议的自动化验证与运行时监控，显著提升了复杂多智能体系统的逻辑稳健性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体协作方法缺乏对复杂交互协议的严格逻辑核验，容易出现死锁、活锁及协作失效等问题，可靠性不足。

Method: 1. 使用 LLM 将任务描述解析为结构化协议拓扑（IR）；2. 生成 PlusCal 协调逻辑；3. 利用 TLA+ 模型检查器 (TLC) 进行循环验证并在出现反例时进行修复；4. 将验证后的逻辑编译为系统提示词，并在运行监控器管控下执行。

Result: 1. 48 个测试任务全部通过 TLA+ 验证；2. 验证过程耗时均在 60 秒内；3. 运行成功率达到 89.4%；4. 错误率（死锁/活锁）从 31.1% 降至 14.1%，且在大模型能力受限或故障注入场景下表现显著优于基准方案。

Conclusion: TraceFix 通过将形式化验证（TLA+）引入多智能体协作，有效解决了 LLM 在复杂交互中的逻辑错误和可靠性问题，证明了“验证优先”范式在保障自动化系统运行稳定性方面的优越性。

Abstract: We present TraceFix, a verification-first pipeline for Large Language Model (LLM) multi-agent coordination. An agent synthesizes a protocol topology as a structured intermediate representation (IR) from a task description, generates PlusCal coordination logic, and iteratively repairs the protocol using counterexamples from the TLA+ model checker (TLC) until verification succeeds. Verified process bodies are compiled into per-agent system prompts and executed under a runtime monitor that rejects out-of-topology coordination operations. On 48 tasks spanning 16 scenario families, all tasks reach full TLC verification; 62.5% pass on the first attempt and none requires more than four repair iterations. State spaces span six orders of magnitude yet verification completes in under 60 s for every task. A 3,456-run runtime comparison shows that topology-monitored execution achieves the highest task completion (89.4% average, 81.5% full) and that runtimes using the verified protocol degrade at roughly half the rate of prompt-only and chat-only baselines when model capability is reduced. A paired ablation under a fixed runtime shows that TLC-verified protocols cut deadlock/livelock (DL/LL) from 31.1% to 14.1%, with the largest separation under fault injection.

</details>


### [312] [The Limits of AI-Driven Allocation: Optimal Screening under Aleatoric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07979)
*Santiago Cortes-Gomez,Mateo Dulce Rubio,Carlos Patino,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合“精准筛选”与“算法预测”的两阶段资源分配策略，旨在解决仅靠算法进行人道主义或政策资源分配时的固有误分配问题，提高整体配置效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解仅依赖预测风险分数的算法定位在处理个体脆弱性差异（即偶然性不确定性）时的局限性，克服完全依赖算法可能导致的资源误分配问题。

Method: 构建了一个两阶段资源分配框架：在最终分配资源前，先对部分单元进行筛选以获取真实状态，再根据筛选数据与算法预测结果共同决策。通过数学推导确定了“筛选边缘群体、定位高风险群体”的最优策略，并利用哥伦比亚的社会保障和人道主义排雷数据进行了实证分析。

Result: 提出了一个最优分配策略，指出应优先对算法评分处于“临界状态”的个体进行筛选，同时直接为高风险个体分配资源。实证结果证明，该策略能显著提高资源配置效率。

Conclusion: 通过将筛选与算法定位结合，可以有效缓解仅依赖算法预测带来的错误分配问题。当总体中存在较高的偶然性不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）时，筛选机制价值更高，二者呈现互补关系。该框架实现了资源效率与成本之间的最优平衡。

Abstract: The rise of machine learning has shifted targeted resource allocation in policy and humanitarian settings toward algorithmic targeting based on predicted risk scores. This approach is typically cheaper and faster than traditional screening procedures that directly observe the latent vulnerability status through physical verification. Yet, even access to the true conditional vulnerability probability cannot eliminate misallocation: aleatoric uncertainty over individual vulnerability status is irreducible, and probabilistic targeting inevitably misallocates some resources. In this work we study how screening and algorithmic targeting should be optimally combined in a two-stage allocation framework where a screening stage observes true outcomes for a subset of units before a final allocation stage assigns the resource under a fixed coverage budget. We show that the optimal strategy screens units at the margin of algorithmic allocation, while directly targeting the highest-risk units. Furthermore, we empirically characterize when screening and algorithmic targeting act as complements or substitutes: efficiency gains from screening grow as the aleatoric uncertainty in the population increases. We illustrate our framework with applications in income-based social protection programs and humanitarian demining in Colombia, where the tension between screening costs and allocation efficiency is operationally consequential.

</details>


### [313] [Abductive Reasoning with Probabilistic Commonsense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08011)
*Joseph Cotnareanu,Chiara Roverato,Han Zhou,Didier Chetelat,Yingxue Zhang,Mark Coates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有推理模型忽略常识主观差异的问题，本文提出了 PACS 框架，通过采样和聚合不同个体常识信念的方式，提升了 LLM 在常识推理任务中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号推理框架依赖于形式化求解器，但求解器缺乏常识；现有的 LLM 辅助方法忽略了常识在不同个体间存在差异这一事实，错误地假设了对常识事实的普遍共识。

Method: 提出了一种名为 PACS（Probabilistic Abductive CommonSense）的算法。该方法利用 LLM 和形式化求解器对个体的常识信念进行采样，将其视为观测值，并整合这些采样结果以得出结论，从而显式地建模常识认识上的个体差异。

Result: 实验表明，PACS 在多个基准测试中均优于思维链（CoT）推理、传统的神经符号方法以及基于搜索的推理方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模 commonsense 的个体差异性，PACS 框架有效地结合了 LLM 的常识推理能力与形式化求解器的逻辑严谨性，在处理需要常识判断的任务中表现优于现有的思维链和神经符号推理方法。

Abstract: Recent efforts to improve the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) have focused on integrating formal logic solvers within neurosymbolic frameworks. A key challenge is that formal solvers lack commonsense world knowledge, preventing them from making reasoning steps that humans find obvious. Prior methods address this by using LLMs to supply missing commonsense assumptions, but these approaches implicitly assume universal agreement on such commonsense facts. In reality, commonsense beliefs vary across individuals. We propose a probabilistic framework for abductive commonsense reasoning that explicitly models this variation, aiming to determine whether most people would judge a statement as true or false. We introduce Probabilistic Abductive CommonSense (PACS), a novel algorithm that uses an LLM and a formal solver to sample proofs as observations of individuals' distinct commonsense beliefs, and aggregates conclusions across these samples. Empirically, PACS outperforms chain-of-thought reasoning, prior neurosymbolic methods, and search-based approaches across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [314] [Reason to Play: Behavioral and Brain Alignment Between Frontier LRMs and Human Game Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08019)
*Botos Csaba,Sreejan Kumar,Austin Tudor David Andrews,Laurence Hunt,Chris Summerfield,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Rui Ponte Costa,Marcelo G. Mattar,Momchil Tomov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证明大语言推理模型（LRMs）能够有效地模拟人类在复杂游戏任务中的学习行为，且其内部模型对游戏状态的上下文表示与人脑生物学特征具有高度关联性，优于传统强化学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探讨现代人工智能系统（特别是大语言模型）是否能够像人类一样，通过规则发现、假设修正和多步规划在复杂新颖环境中进行抽象知识学习与决策。

Method: 利用包含人类玩家游戏行为数据及同步fMRI脑成像的大规模数据集，对比了LRMs、免模型/基于模型的深度强化学习（DRL）算法及贝叶斯推理模型；通过任务表现、行为模式匹配及fMRI脑活动预测（表征一致性）进行综合评估及消融实验。

Result: LRMs在行为表现上最接近人类探索模式；在脑活动预测方面，LRMs在皮层及皮层下区域的预测准确度比传统深度强化学习模型高出一个数量级，且具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 前沿大语言推理模型（LRMs）是解释人类在复杂环境中的学习与决策行为的强有力计算模型，其脑活动预测能力显著优于传统的强化学习模型。大脑对齐效应主要源于模型对游戏状态的上下文表示，而非后续的规划任务。

Abstract: Humans rapidly learn abstract knowledge when encountering novel environments and flexibly deploy this knowledge to guide efficient and intelligent action. Can modern AI systems learn and plan in a similar way? We study this question using a dataset of complex human gameplay with concurrent fMRI recordings, in which participants learn novel video games that require rule discovery, hypothesis revision, and multi-step planning. We jointly evaluate models by their ability to play the games, match human learning behavior, and predict brain activity during the same task, comparing a suite of frontier Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) against model-free and model-based deep reinforcement learning agents and a Bayesian theory-based agent. We find that frontier LRMs most closely match human behavioral patterns during game discovery and predict brain activity an order of magnitude better than both reinforcement learning alternatives across cortical and subcortical regions, with effects robust to permutation controls. Through targeted manipulations, we further show that brain alignment reflects the model's in-context representation of the game state rather than its downstream planning or reasoning. Our results establish LRMs as compelling computational accounts of human learning and decision making in complex, naturalistic environments. Project page with interactive replays: https://botcs.github.io/reason-to-play/

</details>


### [315] [VecCISC: Improving Confidence-Informed Self-Consistency with Reasoning Trace Clustering and Candidate Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08070)
*James Petullo,Sonny George,Dylan Cashman,Nianwen Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了 VecCISC，通过语义相似度过滤冗余或低质量的推理路径，在不损失性能的情况下，将加权多数投票的推理成本降低了近一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加权多数投票方法（如 CISC）虽能提升推理准确率，但依赖评论员模型对每个候选答案进行评分，导致推理阶段的额外开销和成本巨大。

Method: 提出 VecCISC 框架，利用语义相似度度量（Semantic Similarity）对候选推理路径进行预筛选。该方法通过过滤掉语义等价、退化或幻觉的推理路径，减少调用评论员模型（Critic LLM）对每个样本进行置信度评分的次数。

Result: 在数学、化学、生物、常识推理和人文科学等五个基准测试集上进行实验，结果表明 VecCISC 在保持甚至超越 CISC 准确率的前提下，减少了 47% 的 Token 消耗。

Conclusion: VecCISC 是一种有效的成本优化策略，能够在保持或提升加权多数投票性能的同时，显著降低计算资源消耗，适合在大规模推理场景中替代传统的全量评估方法。

Abstract: A standard technique for scaling inference-time reasoning is Self-Consistency, whereby multiple candidate answers are sampled from an LLM and the most common answer is selected. More recently, it has been shown that weighted majority voting (e.g. Confidence-Informed Self Consistency (CISC)), which assigns a confidence value to each candidate answer and chooses the answer with the largest accumulated score, tends to be more accurate on a wide range of popular benchmarks. In practice, weighted majority voting necessitates calling a critic LLM on each candidate's reasoning trace to produce the answer's confidence score. This secondary series of LLM calls greatly increases the overhead and cost of weighted majority voting, despite its potential performance benefits. To reduce this expense, we propose VecCISC, a lightweight, adaptive framework that uses a measure of semantic similarity to filter reasoning traces that are semantically equivalent to others, degenerate, or hallucinated, thus decreasing the number of candidate answers that must be evaluated by the critic. To ensure adequate experimental thoroughness, we evaluate VecCISC on five challenging, widely-adopted datasets spanning the domains of mathematics, chemistry, biology, commonsense reasoning, and the humanities. Our results demonstrate that VecCISC reduces the total token usage by 47%, while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of CISC.

</details>


### [316] [From Storage to Experience: A Survey on the Evolution of LLM Agent Memory Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06716)
*Jinghao Luo,Yuchen Tian,Chuxue Cao,Ziyang Luo,Hongzhan Lin,Kaixin Li,Chuyi Kong,Ruichao Yang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体记忆研究碎片化的问题，提出了一种包含“存储、反思、经验”三阶段的统一演进框架，旨在通过明确的设计原则和发展路径，指导下一代具备持续学习能力的智能体研发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于LLM智能体记忆机制的研究较为碎片化，学术界在操作系统工程视角与认知科学视角之间存在理论鸿沟，缺乏统一的演进视角和技术合成框架。

Method: 提出了一种全新的演进框架，将记忆机制的形式化发展过程划分为三个阶段：存储（轨迹保存）、反思（轨迹细化）和经验（轨迹抽象）；并深入分析了推动这一演进的三个核心驱动因素：长程一致性需求、动态环境挑战及持续学习目标。

Result: 构建了一个系统化的理论模型，梳理了记忆机制的演进脉络，同时识别并探索了“经验”阶段的两个变革性机制：主动探索（proactive exploration）与跨轨迹抽象（cross-trajectory abstraction）。

Conclusion: 本文为大模型智能体记忆机制提供了一个统一的演进理论框架（存储、反思、经验），并提出了指导下一代智能体设计的路线图，强调了持续学习和跨轨迹抽象是未来的关键方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents have fundamentally reshaped artificial intelligence by integrating external tools and planning capabilities. While memory mechanisms have emerged as the architectural cornerstone of these systems, current research remains fragmented, oscillating between operating system engineering and cognitive science. This theoretical divide prevents a unified view of technological synthesis and a coherent evolutionary perspective. To bridge this gap, this survey proposes a novel evolutionary framework for LLM agent memory mechanisms, formalizing the development process into three stages: Storage (trajectory preservation), Reflection (trajectory refinement), and Experience (trajectory abstraction). We first formally define these three stages before analyzing the three core drivers of this evolution: the necessity for long-range consistency, the challenges in dynamic environments, and the ultimate goal of continual learning. Furthermore, we specifically explore two transformative mechanisms in the frontier Experience stage: proactive exploration and cross-trajectory abstraction. By synthesizing these disparate views, this work offers robust design principles and a clear roadmap for the development of next-generation LLM agents.

</details>


### [317] [When Does a Language Model Commit? A Finite-Answer Theory of Pre-Verbalization Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06723)
*Long Zhang,Wei-neng Chen,Feng-feng Wei,Zi-bo Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种量化LLM内部答案形成时间的方法。发现模型在输出答案前很久就已经在内部形成了稳定的偏好目标，这种内部偏好领先于外部观测到的文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究难以明确衡量大型语言模型在生成内容时，内部“最终答案”是在何时形成的（即偏好何时趋于稳定），因此需要一种无需依赖额外探测器或采样策略的、能够反映内部状态的方法。

Method: 引入“有限答案偏好稳定”（finite-answer preference stabilization）概念；通过将模型内部的延续概率投射到有限答案集合上，构建对数几率差（log-odds code, δ(ξ)）作为研究指标；通过控制性延迟判定任务，在不依赖贪婪采样或外部探测器的情况下，量化模型输出的解析起点、回顾性稳定时间和提前量。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct模型上的实验显示，模型内部的答案偏好在最终答案被正式输出前即可稳定（领先17-31个token）；该信号线性可恢复，并能从隐藏状态中捕捉到，具有跨任务的一致性；诊断实验验证了该方法与在线停顿、无言语信念和因果控制的区分度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，语言模型在生成最终答案之前，其内部对答案的偏好倾向就已经趋于稳定（存在明显的“提前量”）。这种稳定状态可以被测量，且与模型的预测输出高度相关，而非事实本身。虽然可以通过该信号识别模型的内部偏差，但单纯利用这些信号进行因果控制（steering）的效果有限。

Abstract: Language models often generate reasoning before giving a final answer, but the visible answer does not reveal when the model's answer preference became stable. We study this question through a narrow computable object: \emph{finite-answer preference stabilization}. For a model state and specified answer verbalizers, we project the model's own continuation probabilities onto a finite answer set; in binary tasks this yields an exact log-odds code, $δ(ξ)=S_θ(\mathrm{yes}\midξ)-S_θ(\mathrm{no}\midξ)$. This target defines parser-based answer onset, retrospective stabilization time, and lead without relying on greedy rollouts or learned probes. In controlled delayed-verdict tasks with Qwen3-4B-Instruct, the contextual finite-answer projection stabilizes before the answer is parseable, with 17--31 token mean lead in the main templates and positive, shorter lead in a parser-clean replication. The signal tracks the model's eventual output rather than truth, is linearly recoverable from compact hidden summaries, is partly separable from cursor progress, and transfers as shared information without a single invariant coordinate. Diagnostics separate the measurement from online stopping, verbalizer-free belief, and causal answer control; exact steering shows local sensitivity of $δ$ but not reliable generation control.

</details>


### [318] [XDecomposer: Learning Prior-Free Set Decomposition for Multiphase X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05866)
*Hanyu Gao,Bin Cao,Yunyue Su,Tong-Yi Zhang,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XDecomposer 是一种无需先验知识的深度学习框架，通过集合预测和物理约束分解，解决了复杂多相 XRD 图谱的自动化解构与结构识别难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相粉末 X 射线衍射（PXRD）分析是结构鉴定的瓶颈，现有方法多局限于单相输入，难以处理真实合成中常见的复杂多相混合物。

Method: 提出 XDecomposer 框架，将多相衍射分析建模为集合预测问题；采用相查询驱动（phase-query-driven）的分解机制和衍射一致性物理重建方法，无需候选列表或先验模板即可直接推理出物相及其比例和结构特征。

Result: 在模拟和实验数据集上均表现优异，显著提升了重建精度和物相识别能力，并表现出对未见混合物的强大泛化性。

Conclusion: XDecomposer 实现了无需先验知识的多相 XRD 模式分解与识别，成功解决了复杂混合物中物相难以分辨的长期难题，为数据驱动的晶体学分析提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Multiphase powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) analysis remains a fundamental bottleneck in structure identification, as real-world synthesis often produces complex mixtures whose constituent phases (components) cannot be reliably disentangled. While recent advances in representation-based crystal retrieval and generation suggest the possibility of inferring structures directly from PXRD, existing approaches largely assume single-phase inputs and break down in multiphase settings. Here, we present XDecomposer, a prior-free framework for joint decomposition and identification of multiphase XRD patterns without requiring candidate phase lists, structural templates, or prior knowledge of phase number. We formulate multiphase diffraction analysis as a set prediction problem, where the model infers an unordered set of phase-resolved components, their mixture proportions, and corresponding structural representations within a unified architecture. A phase-query-driven decomposition mechanism, together with diffraction-consistent physical reconstruction, enables accurate source separation while preserving crystallographic fidelity. Extensive experiments on both simulated and experimental datasets show that XDecomposer substantially improves reconstruction accuracy and phase identification across diverse chemical systems, while maintaining strong generalization to unseen mixtures. These results provide a practical route toward data-driven, source-resolved multiphase XRD analysis and reduce long-standing dependence on prior-guided iteratively phase matching. The code is openly available at https://github.com/Licht0812/XDecomposer

</details>


### [319] [MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623)
*Zhexuan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Li Wang,Zifei Shan,Yutong Wang,Zhenxi Song,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASPO 是一种多智能体系统提示词联合优化框架，通过评估提示词对全局任务的贡献并利用演化搜索，高效提升了多智能体协作的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统（MAS）依赖人工编写特定角色提示词，但由于局部优化与全局目标不匹配，缺乏高效的联合优化方法。

Method: 提出 MASPO 框架，包含：1. 联合评估机制（Joint Evaluation），评估提示词对后续智能体下游任务成功率的影响；2. 基于数据驱动的演化束搜索（Evolutionary Beam Search），在提示词空间中高效搜索优化路径。

Result: 在 6 个不同任务上进行了评估，MASPO 表现优于主流提示词优化方法，平均准确率提升了 2.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: MASPO 证明了通过关节评估和演化搜索来联合优化MAS中的提示词，无需标注即可有效提升系统整体协作性能，优于现有的提示词优化方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [320] [Modular Lie Algebraic PDE Control of Multibody Flexible Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06709)
*Sadeq Yaqubi,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于螺旋理论和偏微分方程（PDE）的柔性多体机器人控制框架，通过精确的动力学建模和非线性控制算法，实现了考虑链路形变下的全局指数收敛和系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决柔性多体机器人系统在复杂3D空间运动中的精确控制问题，特别是针对串联机械臂在处理链路形变与动力学控制耦合时的实时性和稳定控制挑战。

Method: 引入了基于螺旋论（screw-theoretic）的李代数动态模型；利用应变基（strain-based）PDE消除分布式弹性加速度；采用了考虑形变补偿的逆运动学生成轨迹；通过设计基于李雅普诺夫函数（Lyapunov function）的各链路标称控制器及自适应参数估计，并利用牛顿第三定律实现了链路间交互作用力的抵消（telescoping cancellation）。

Result: 实现了跟踪误差的全局指数收敛，保证了弹性形变的有界性，且该稳定性证明具有模块化扩展性，适用于任意长度的链式结构。

Conclusion: 提出了一个具有模块化和可扩展性的偏微分方程（PDE）驱动的控制框架，通过基于螺旋理论（screw-theoretic）的李代数建模实现了系统全局指数稳定性，并通过数值仿真验证了物理一致性。

Abstract: This paper addresses PDE-based control for flexible multibody robotic systems, presenting a subsystem-based framework for serial manipulators with arbitrary links in 3D space. The approach uses a screw-theoretic Lie-algebraic model where motion, deformation, and forces are expressed as body-fixed twists and wrenches in se(3). By substituting a strain-based deformation PDE into the dynamics, distributed elastic acceleration is eliminated, yielding a model governed by twist acceleration and the deformation field. Subsystem twist trajectories are generated from task-space endpoints via deflection-compensating inverse kinematics, providing real-time correction for tip deformation. A nominal controller for each link ensures exponential decay of twist errors via a Lyapunov function nu_i. An adaptive modification replaces physical parameters with online estimates, establishing exponential convergence of both twist and parameter errors. Summing over all links, composite Lyapunov functions V = sum(nu_i) and V^a = sum(nu_i^a) yield time derivatives where inter-link interaction power terms telescope to zero. This cancellation is ensured by Newton's third law and the frame invariance of the power pairing on se(3) x se*(3), establishing global exponential convergence of tracking errors. Bounded elastic deformation is guaranteed by an Euler-Bernoulli energy argument. The screw-theoretic structure renders interaction cancellation exact, making the stability certificate modular and scalable to chains of arbitrary length. Numerical simulations demonstrate the scheme's physical consistency.

</details>


### [321] [An Aerial Manipulator for Perception-Driven Flower Targeting Toward Contactless Pollination in Vertical Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06759)
*Chenzhe Jin,Zhuohang Wu,Yifan Cai,Xiangqi Li,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研制了一种用于垂直农场的空中机械臂平台，通过融合RGBD感知、MPPI路径控制与轻量化机械臂，实现了厘米级的花朵精准定位与对齐，为自动化非接触式授粉提供了可行的技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然授粉媒介的减少给受控室内农业（特别是垂直农场）的作物生产带来了巨大挑战，亟需开发能够在不损伤花朵的前提下进行精准授粉任务的机器人系统。

Method: 提出了一种集成了基于RGBD的感知系统、运行在PX4平台上的MPPI（模型预测路径积分）控制算法以及轻量化2自由度（2DoF）机械臂的空中操作平台；通过MuJoCo仿真环境与实验室实物测试台验证系统性能。

Result: 仿真结果显示系统具备稳定的轨迹收敛性和目标对齐能力；实验室环境下实现了稳定的飞行、可靠的花朵定位，以及厘米级的末端执行器定位精度。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发并验证了一套用于垂直农场环境的空中机械臂系统，证明了其在受限空间内进行精准花朵定位与对齐的可行性，为未来开发非接触式授粉技术（如声学授粉模块）提供了坚实的基础。

Abstract: The decline of natural pollinators has created a major challenge for crop production in controlled indoor agriculture, particularly in vertical farming environments where natural insect pollination is absent. This motivates the development of robotic systems capable of performing precise flower targeting tasks while minimizing physical interference with delicate floral structures. This paper presents an aerial manipulator platform for perception driven flower detection, localization, and approach in vertical farming environments. The proposed system integrates onboard RGBD based perception, model predictive path integral (MPPI) based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) control on a PX4 platform, and a lightweight 2DoF manipulator for precise end effector positioning. The platform is evaluated in both MuJoCo simulation and UAV lab experiments using a flower targeting testbed. The experimental results demonstrate stable UAV flight, reliable flower localization, and centimeter level end effector positioning accuracy. In simulation, the proposed controller achieves consistent trajectory convergence and accurate target alignment. In the real world UAV lab environment, the integrated perception control manipulation framework enables stable flower targeted positioning and end effector alignment under constrained aerial operation. These results validate the proposed aerial manipulator as a robust robotic carrier and positioning framework for future contactless pollination systems. While the current study focuses on perception guided targeting and positioning, the developed platform provides a practical foundation for integrating advanced contactless end effectors, including acoustic based pollen manipulation modules, in future work.

</details>


### [322] [Traffic Scenario Orchestration from Language via Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06966)
*Frieda Rong,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于基础模型的自动驾驶测试场景生成 orchestrator，通过将自然语言描述转化为约束求解问题，实现了对闭环自动驾驶测试场景的高效、可控生成，解决了传统方法在扩展性与精确性上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真测试面临挑战：手动编程场景难以扩展，而传统的统计建模方法难以在分布外（out-of-distribution）场景中实现精确控制。研究旨在平衡场景生成的可控性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种基于语言输入到仿真输出的场景编排器（Scenario Orchestrator）。利用基础模型（Foundation Model）将自然语言转化为约束条件，并结合现成的约束求解器，在仿真环境中求解出符合测试意图的参与者（actor）行为路径。

Result: 在多样化的测试场景基准下，该方法的编排成功率显著优于基准方法，且在需要自动驾驶车辆进行交互反应（ego-reactive）的复杂场景中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将生成式AI的语义推理能力与约束求解器的精确控制能力相结合，有效解决了自动驾驶测试中场景生成的可扩展性与可控性矛盾，特别适用于生成具备闭环响应特征的测试场景。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) require extensive testing in simulation, but test case generation for driving scenarios is laborious. The desired scenarios are often out-of-distribution and have precise requirements on interactions with the AV policy under test. Manually programming scenarios allows for precise controllability but is difficult to scale. On the other hand, statistical models can leverage compute and data, but struggle with precise controllability when out-of-distribution. We cast scenario orchestration as a constraint-solving problem and present a language-in, simulation-out scenario orchestrator for closed-loop testing AVs. Our approach leverages foundation model reasoning to translate general, natural language descriptions into a set of constraints as a scenario representation. This then allows us to leverage off the shelf solvers to solve for actor behaviors which meet precise testing intentions in closed-loop. Under a benchmark of carefully crafted and diverse scenario descriptions, our approach greatly outperforms our baselines in orchestration success rate. We further show that our closed-loop approach is especially important for scenarios which require ego-reactive specifications.

</details>


### [323] [Dr-BA: Separable Optimization for Direct Radar Bundle Adjustment & Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07041)
*Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dr-BA 是首个直接对 2D 旋转雷达强度原始图像进行处理的束调整框架，通过解耦位姿与建图的优化策略，实现了更高精度的全天候雷达定位与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达状态估计方法多依赖于从原始数据中提取稀疏点云，导致信息丢失且对动态场景适应性有限；雷达具备全天候特性，迫切需要能更充分利用雷达原始信息（全返回数据）的位姿估计与地图构建方法，以提高自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 Dr-BA 框架：直接在 2D 旋转雷达强度图像上操作，摒弃了传统的稀疏点云提取；采用可分离优化（separable optimization）策略，将位姿估计与映射解耦，从而实现高效求解；该框架可自然扩展至直接雷达定位（DRL）。

Result: 在超过 200 公里的五条不同道路路测数据上进行了验证；实现了雷达束调整（BA）和跨会话定位的最新性能（SOTA）；证明了该方法在处理全天候雷达数据上的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: Dr-BA 提出了一种高效的雷达束调整框架，能够直接利用原始强度图像进行联合位姿估计与映射，并在各种天气条件下展现出卓越的鲁棒性和精度，为雷达感知提供了新的技术范式。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-BA, a first-of-its-kind radar bundle adjustment (BA) framework that operates directly on 2D spinning radar intensity images. Unlike camera or lidar sensors, radar is largely unaffected by precipitation, making it a critical modality for autonomous systems that require all-weather robustness. Existing state estimation approaches using spinning radar typically extract sparse point clouds from range-azimuth-intensity measurements and apply point cloud alignment techniques to estimate vehicle motion, scene structure, or to localize within an existing map. In contrast, Dr-BA uses the full radar returns from multiple scans to jointly estimate dense maps and sensor poses. By formulating the problem as a separable optimization, we derive an efficient and general solution that decouples pose estimation from mapping. In addition to solving the BA problem, this formulation naturally extends to direct radar-only localization (DRL) within a previously built map. Dr-BA achieves state-of-the-art radar-based BA and cross-session localization performance, demonstrated on more than 200 km of on-road data across five distinct routes. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_ba.

</details>


### [324] [Palm-sized Omnidirectional Vision-Based UAV Exploration with Sparse Topological Map Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07275)
*Zirui Wang,Xinjia Luo,Haotian Sun,Jun Ma,Jian Guo,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为资源受限微型无人机设计的轻量级自主探索系统，通过全向视觉和稀疏拓扑地图取代传统的密集点云和占用栅格，显著降低了计算与内存开销，实现了高效的探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索方法依赖占用栅格或点云，计算与内存开销大；微型无人机受限于尺寸、重量和功耗（SWaP），难以搭载激光雷达等高精度传感器。

Method: 1. 采用多鱼眼相机方案实现全向视场（FoV）及深度估计。
2. 提出基于拓扑节点而非显式边界的边界表示法，无需维护实时占用栅格或全局点云。
3. 利用包含关键节点及其描述符的稀疏拓扑地图来抽象环境。
4. 直接在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划。

Result: 在模拟环境和实际手掌大小（11cm轴距、400g）的视觉无人机上进行了验证，结果表明该方法在保持低计算资源消耗的同时，能够实现高效的自主探索。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于资源受限的微型无人机（SWaP受限）的高效自主探索系统，证明了仅利用全向视觉和稀疏拓扑地图即可实现高效环境探索，摆脱了对高性能激光雷达和密集地图的依赖。

Abstract: Classic exploration methods often rely on dense occupancy maps or high-resolution point clouds for frontier detection and path planning, resulting in substantial memory consumption and computational overhead. Moreover, micro UAVs under size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints are not practical to be equipped with sensors like LiDAR to obtain accurate environmental geometric measurements. This paper presents a lightweight autonomous exploration system that leverages omnidirectional vision and sparse topological map guidance. Specifically, we utilize a multi-fisheye camera setup to achieve omnidirectional Field of View (FoV) and perform depth estimation. To address the limited depth estimation accuracy, frontiers are represented as potential unexplored regions characterized by topological nodes instead of explicit boundaries, enabling efficient identification of frontier regions without maintaining occupancy grids or global point clouds. Unlike classic dense representations, our approach abstracts the environment using a sparse topological map composed of key nodes and their descriptors, reducing memory consumption and computational demands. Global path planning is performed directly on the sparse graph. The proposed method is validated in both simulation and on a palm-sized vision-based UAV with an 11 cm wheelbase and a 400 g weight in real-world experiments, demonstrating that our method can achieve efficient exploration with extremely low computational consumption.

</details>


### [325] [BioProVLA-Agent: An Affordable, Protocol-Driven, Vision-Enhanced VLA-Enabled Embodied Multi-Agent System with Closed-Loop-Capable Reasoning for Biological Laboratory Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07306)
*Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Hongmei Fei,Xiwen Cao,Ting Xiao,Qi Wang,Huanbo Jin,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BioProVLA-Agent是一个低成本的协议驱动型多智能体系统，利用VLA模型和针对性的数据增强策略，有效解决了实验室环境下针对透明/反射物体的稳健视觉感知与自动化操作难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验室自动化系统成本高昂且依赖固定流程，难以处理非结构化环境、透明/反射物体以及需要复杂状态感知的多步骤生物实验任务。

Method: 1. 协议解析（Protocol Agent）：将协议转化为可验证的子任务；2. 视觉验证（VLM-RAG Agent）：基于观察、机器人状态和检索知识进行任务状态评估；3. 具身执行（VLA Embodied Agent）：基于轻量级策略执行子任务；4. 数据增强（AugSmolVLA）：针对透明容器、反射、光照变化等湿实验室视觉挑战进行在线增强训练。

Result: 在包含15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双臂任务的基准测试中，AugSmolVLA在常态及高曝光等视觉受干扰环境下，表现优于ACT、X-VLA和基准SmolVLA，显著提升了透明物体处理和复杂工作流的执行稳定性。

Conclusion: BioProVLA-Agent通过引入协议驱动、视觉增强和闭环验证机制，证明了利用轻量级VLA模型实现低成本、高鲁棒性生物实验室自动化的可行性，为生物操作中的具身智能提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Biological laboratory automation can reduce repetitive manual work and improve reproducibility, but reliable embodied execution in wet-lab environments remains challenging. Protocols are often unstructured, labware is frequently transparent or reflective, and multi-step procedures require state-aware execution beyond one-shot instruction following. Existing robotic systems often rely on costly hardware, fixed workflows, dedicated instruments, or robotics-oriented interfaces. Here, we introduce BioProVLA-Agent, an affordable, protocol-driven, vision-enhanced embodied multi-agent system enabled by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for biological manipulation. The system uses protocols as the task interface and integrates protocol parsing, visual state verification, and embodied execution in a closed-loop workflow. A Tailored LLM Protocol Agent converts protocols into verifiable subtasks; a VLM-RAG Verification Agent assesses readiness and completion using observations, robot states, retrieved knowledge, and success/failure examples; and a VLA Embodied Agent executes verified subtasks through a lightweight policy. To improve robustness under wet-lab visual perturbations, we develop AugSmolVLA, an online augmentation strategy targeting transparent labware, reflections, illumination shifts, and overexposure. We evaluate the system on a hierarchical benchmark covering 15 atomic tasks, 6 composite workflows, and 3 bimanual tasks, including tube loading, sorting, waste disposal, cap twisting, and liquid pouring. Across normal and high-exposure settings, AugSmolVLA improves execution stability over ACT, X-VLA, and the original SmolVLA, especially for precise placement, transparent-object manipulation, composite workflows, and visually degraded scenes. These results suggest a practical route toward accessible, protocol-centered, and verification-capable embodied AI for biological manipulation.

</details>


### [326] [AT-VLA: Adaptive Tactile Injection for Enhanced Feedback Reaction in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07308)
*Xiaoqi Li,Muhe Cai,Jiadong Xu,Juan Zhu,Hongwei Fan,Yan Shen,Guangrui Ren,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AT-VLA 通过自适应触觉注入和双流控制架构，在不损害 VLA 预训练能力的前提下，实现了接触丰富任务所需的实时、高动态触觉反馈响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型在处理接触丰富的复杂操作任务时，面临触觉反馈融合导致的预训练能力退化以及推理速度缓慢（难以满足实时性）两大挑战。

Method: 1. 提出自适应触觉注入机制（Adaptive Tactile Injection）：动态决定触觉信号的注入时机与位置，避免对 VLA 预训练知识的干扰；2. 提出触觉反应双流机制（Tactile Reaction Dual-Stream）：将推理分为低频视觉语言流与高频触觉控制流，实现 0.04 秒内的实时闭环控制。

Result: 实验表明，AT-VLA 在接触丰富的机器人真实操作场景中能有效利用触觉反馈，实现高精度的实时交互调整。

Conclusion: AT-VLA 有效解决了接触丰富任务中 VLA 模型性能受限与推理延迟高的问题，实现了实时、高精度的触觉反馈控制，在实际机器人操作任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significantly advanced the capabilities of robotic agents in executing diverse tasks; however, they still face challenges in contact-rich manipulation scenarios that require precise physical interactions. To address this limitation, recent studies have attempted to incorporate tactile signals during downstream tasks, enabling pretrained VLAs to interpret tactile feedback. Nevertheless, introducing new modalities during finetuning, which are rarely present in the pretrain stage, may disrupt the pretrained capabilities of VLAs. In addition, the inherently slow inference speed of VLAs hampers real-time responsiveness and limits the effective utilization of tactile feedback for action adjustment. To overcome these challenges, we propose Adaptive Tactile Vision-Language-Action (AT-VLA), which introduces a novel Adaptive Tactile Injection mechanism. This mechanism dynamically determines the appropriate timing and locations for tactile injection, incorporating only when it significantly contributes to action generation, thereby minimizing interference with pretrained representations. Furthermore, to enable rapid and accurate tactile responses, we propose a Tactile Reaction Dual-Stream mechanism, which decouples sensory processing into a slow visual-language stream for low-frequency perceptual reasoning and a fast tactile control stream for high-frequency physical interaction understanding, achieving real-time close-loop responses within 0.04 s. Real-world experiments thoroughly validate the effectiveness of AT-VLA in contact-rich manipulation tasks. The project page is available at: https://sites.google.com/view/at-vla.

</details>


### [327] [CSR: Infinite-Horizon Real-Time Policies with Massive Cached State Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07325)
*Robin Karlsson,Go Suzui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了CSR架构和ASR算法，通过优化KV-Cache重用与异步内存管理，显著解决了LLM处理长历史状态时的延迟瓶颈，使机器人能够以高频实时运行大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模语言模型（LLM）驱动的机器人应用中，处理长历史记录导致的首字延迟（TTFT）过高，成为实现实时、持续性认知任务的严重瓶颈，而现有的RAG或滑动窗口方法在上下文完整性与计算成本之间无法达成平衡。

Method: 1. 理论基础：形式化任务结构，证明了前缀稳定性、增量可扩展性和异步状态协调是降低延迟的必要条件。2. CSR框架：通过优化KV-Cache的重用机制，实现对大规模历史上下文的高效处理。3. ASR算法：采用异步状态协调技术，将内存驱逐任务分流至并行计算资源，消除实时推理过程中的延迟抖动。

Result: 1. 延迟表现：在120K token上下文及235B参数模型下，TTFT从14.67秒降低至0.56秒，加速约26倍。2. 召回能力：在具身AI基准测试中，召回率达到SOTA水平（0.836 vs 0.459），同时保持了RAG级别的低延迟。3. 稳定性：ASR算法有效消除了长周期运行中的延迟尖峰，支持持续、高频且无感知的推理任务。

Conclusion: CSR框架通过利用缓存状态重用和异步状态协调机制，成功解决了大规模语言模型在机器人持续性认知任务中因处理大规模历史状态导致的TTFT高延迟瓶颈，实现了实时、高频（>2Hz）的具身智能策略部署。

Abstract: Deploying massive large language models (LLMs) as continuous cognitive engines for robotics is bottlenecked by the time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency required to process extensive state histories. Existing solutions like RAG or sliding windows compromise global context or incur prohibitive re-computation costs. We formalize the optimal task structure for minimizing latency and theoretically prove that prefix stability, incremental extensibility, and asynchronous state reconciliation are necessary conditions for real-time performance. Building on these proofs, we introduce the Cached State Representation (CSR) framework as the practical instantiation of these properties, ensuring optimal KV-cache reuse. To sustain these properties over infinite horizons, we further propose an Asynchronous State Reconciliation (ASR) algorithm that offloads state memory eviction to a parallel computational resource to eliminate latency spikes. On a physical robot wirelessly connected to an on-premise GPU server, CSR achieves a 26-fold latency reduction (14.67s to 0.56s) for 120K token contexts with a 235B parameter model compared to a standard baseline. On an embodied AI benchmark, we achieve SOTA recall (0.836 vs. 0.459) while maintaining RAG-level latency. ASR is validated to sustain bounded, spike-free TTFT over 10 eviction cycles in continuous real-world operation. Together, CSR and ASR enable massive LLMs to function as continuously operating, high-frequency (> 2 Hz) embodied policies.

</details>


### [328] [Weather-Robust Scene Semantics with Vision-Aligned 4D Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07367)
*Kali Hamilton,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种雷达-VLM对齐方法，通过简单的7M参数投影层及LayerNorm技术，使雷达能在极端天气下实现比摄像头更可靠的场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对摄像头和LiDAR在雨、雾、雪等极端恶劣天气下性能显著下降，而毫米波雷达不受影响的特性，旨在利用雷达数据提升场景理解能力。

Method: 提出了一个冻结SigLIP视觉嵌入与雷达编码器对齐的框架，并通过冻结的VLM解码结构化场景描述。核心方法包括引入训练约7M参数的投影层，并针对Token范数不匹配问题在投影输出端加入LayerNorm。

Result: 在K-RADAR数据集（含雾、轻雪、重雪场景）上，所有雷达配置的表现均显著优于摄像头基线（摄像头的幻觉率超过90%）。

Conclusion: 通过在雷达投影层使用LayerNorm解决了模态间的Token范数不匹配问题，并为雷达-视觉语言模型（Radar-VLM）的设计提供了架构权衡参考。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDAR degrade in rain, fog, and snow, while millimeter-wave radar remains largely unaffected. We align a radar encoder to frozen SigLIP vision
  embeddings and decode structured scene captions through a frozen vision-language model (VLM) with approximately 7M trainable parameters. On K-RADAR with
  held-out fog, light snow, and heavy snow sequences, all radar configurations outperform a camera baseline that collapses to over 90% hallucination. We
  identify a token-norm mismatch as the dominant failure mode when bridging radar to a frozen VLM and show that projector-output LayerNorm resolves it.
  Analysis of encoder complexity, caption format, and pooling strategy reveals tradeoffs that inform future radar-VLM pipeline design.

</details>


### [329] [Escaping the Diversity Trap in Robotic Manipulation via Anchor-Centric Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07381)
*Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qi Lv,Yiqi Lin,Zechen Bai,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人VLA模型自适应中的数据效率问题，本文提出了“锚点中心自适应（ACA）”策略，通过平衡“覆盖率”与“密度”以应对估计噪声，在有限数据下实现了更可靠的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在特定硬件上进行真实世界自适应时，由于采集机器人演示数据的成本高昂，传统的“最大化覆盖范围”策略因估计噪声问题容易陷入“多样性陷阱”，导致性能下降。

Method: 1. 形式化了“覆盖-密度权衡（Coverage–Density Trade-off）”：通过推导将策略误差分解为估计误差（密度相关）和外推误差（覆盖相关）；2. 提出了“锚点中心自适应（Anchor-Centric Adaptation, ACA）”两阶段框架：第一阶段在核心锚点进行重复演示以稳定策略骨架，第二阶段通过强制错误挖掘和约束残差更新来扩展覆盖范围。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，ACA框架在相同数据预算下，相比传统的多元化采样策略，显著提升了任务的可靠性和成功率。

Conclusion: 在有限的数据预算下，通过构建“锚点”进行重复训练以降低估计噪声（高密度），并结合针对性错误挖掘进行覆盖率扩展，比单纯追求广泛覆盖的样本采集策略更有效，证明了“覆盖-密度权衡”在实际机器人任务中的优越性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer broad general capabilities, deploying them on specific hardware requires real-world adaptation to bridge the embodiment gap. Since robot demonstrations are costly, this adaptation must often occur under a strict data budget. In this work, we identify a critical diversity trap: the standard heuristic of "maximizing coverage" by collecting diverse, single-shot demonstrations can be self-defeating due to non-vanishing estimation noise. We formalize this phenomenon as a Coverage--Density Trade-off. By decomposing the policy error into estimation (density) and extrapolation (coverage) terms, we characterize an interior optimal allocation of unique conditions for a fixed budget. Guided by this analysis, we propose Anchor-Centric Adaptation (ACA), a two-stage framework that first stabilizes a policy skeleton through repeated demonstrations at core anchors, then selectively expands coverage to high-risk boundaries via teacher-forced error mining and constrained residual updates. Real-robot experiments validate our trade-off framework and demonstrate that ACA significantly improves task reliability and success rates over standard diverse sampling strategies under the same budget.

</details>


### [330] [PathPainter: Transferring the Generalization Ability of Image Generation Models to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07496)
*Yijin Wang,Yuru Tian,Xijie Huang,Weiqi Gai,Mo Zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用BEV先验和生成式基础模型的机器人导航系统，通过语义理解生成通行掩码并利用跨视图定位修正漂移，实现了鲁棒的长距离导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面及近地机器人导航中，如何有效利用鸟瞰图（BEV）作为全局先验信息，并解决执行过程中的定位漂移与导航准确性问题。

Method: 1. 利用图像生成模型根据自然语言指令生成目标目的地及可通行掩码（traversability masks）；2. 引入跨视图定位技术将机器人里程计与BEV地图对齐，从而修正传统里程计产生的长距离漂移。

Result: 在基准实验中表现优异；通过无人机在160米户外长距离导航任务中验证了该系统，仅需简单的局部运动规划器即可完成任务。

Conclusion: 本研究成功验证了将视觉基础模型的生成能力应用于具身导航的可行性，通过利用BEV先验信息和跨视图定位，解决了长距离导航中的目标识别与里程计漂移问题。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [331] [Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07514)
*Bo-Kai Ruan,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Ling Lo,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出世界动作模型（WAMs）在“动作-状态一致性”上存在缺陷，通过分析这一指标并利用模型间多样本一致性，提出了一种无需额外训练即可提升规划性能的择优选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）生成的未来虽然视觉上合理，但往往缺乏动态上的真实性与可靠性，缺乏一种有效的度量指标来判断生成的未来轨迹是否真正反映了动作与状态转换之间的物理规律。

Method: 系统分析了联合预测模型和逆动力学模型在“动作-状态一致性”方面的表现；识别出背景坍塌（background collapse）现象；提出了一种基于多模型一致性判断（consensus strategy）的测试时择优方法。

Result: 实验表明，“动作-状态一致性”是衡量模型成功与否的有效指标；背景坍塌会导致低动力失败轨迹表现出虚假的一致性；所提的择优策略在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0任务中提升了成功率。

Conclusion: 一致性是评估世界模型可靠性的关键维度，其提出的无需价值函数（value-free）的择优策略能够有效提升规划成功率，无需额外训练或奖励模型。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable decision-making through imagined rollouts by predicting future observations and actions. However, the reliability of these imagined futures remains under-examined: is a generated future merely visually plausible, or is it dynamically compatible with the action sequence it claims to model? In this work, we identify action-state consistency, the alignment between predicted actions and induced state transitions, as a missing reliability axis for WAMs. Through a systematic study across representative joint-prediction and inverse-dynamics models, we find that action-state consistency systematically separates successful and failed rollouts across many tasks and follows similar success-failure trends as learned value estimates. These results suggest that consistency captures decision-relevant structure beyond visual realism. We further identify background collapse as an important boundary condition, where low-dynamics failed trajectories can become deceptively consistent because static futures are easier to predict. Building on these findings, we introduce a value-free consensus strategy for test-time selection, which ranks candidate rollouts by agreement among predicted futures. This strategy improves success rates on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 without additional training or reward modeling. Taken together, our findings establish action-state consistency as both a diagnostic tool for evaluating WAM reliability and a practical signal for value-free planning.

</details>


### [332] [Search-based Robustness Testing of Laptop Refurbishing Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07530)
*Erblin Isaku,Hassan Sartaj,Shaukat Ali,Malaika Din Hashmi,Francois Picard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 PROBE，一种利用多目标优化（NSGA-II）来系统检测笔记本翻新机器人对象检测模型稳健性的搜索测试方法，该方法能高效找到最小化扰动以诱导出模型故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持欧洲循环经济行动计划，丹麦技术研究所（DTI）开发了用于笔记本电脑翻新的机器人。这些机器人的对象检测模型对输入微小变化极其敏感，若不稳健可能会导致笔记本电脑在拆卸过程中受损。因此，需要一种能够系统性检测这些模型稳健性的方法，以确保翻新过程的安全性。

Method: 提出了 PROBE，一种基于搜索的稳健性测试方法。该方法利用多目标优化算法（NSGA-II），通过在限制扰动幅度的同时优化故障诱导（结合定位精度和置信度），系统地探索扰动空间以发现故障。并引入变异关系（metamorphic relations）来评估非失败案例的稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明：1. PROBE 在生成故障诱导扰动方面的效能比随机搜索高出 3 到 7 倍；2. PROBE 能够以更小的扰动幅度诱导模型失败；3. 生成的扰动在不同模型间具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: PROBE 是一种高效的、基于搜索的稳健性测试方法，能够有效地暴露对象检测模型中的故障。研究证明了该方法在生成故障诱导扰动方面优于随机搜索，且生成的扰动具有模型迁移性。此外，变异关系（metamorphic relations）为评估模型的稳定性提供了额外的视角。

Abstract: The Danish Technological Institute (DTI) focuses on transferring advanced technologies (including robots) to the industry and the public sector. One key application is laptop refurbishment using specialized robots, aimed at promoting reuse, reducing electronic waste, and supporting the European Circular Economy Action Plan. The software of such robots often includes features that use object detection models to detect objects for various purposes, such as identifying screws for laptop disassembly or detecting stickers to remove them. Ensuring the robustness of such models to small input variations remains a critical challenge, and addressing it is important to avoid potential damage to laptops during refurbishment. In this paper, we propose PROBE, a search-based robustness testing approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to identify minimal, localized perturbations that expose failures in object detection models used in the software of laptop refurbishing robots. PROBE employs NSGA-II to systematically explore the perturbation space, optimizing for failure induction considering both localization and confidence, and perturbation magnitude, while enabling the discovery of diverse failure cases. Results show that PROBE is 3$\times$ to 7$\times$ more effective than random search in generating failure-inducing perturbations, while requiring smaller perturbation magnitudes, and that the generated perturbations transfer across models. We further show that metamorphic relations provide additional insights into model robustness, enabling the assessment of stability even in non-failing cases.

</details>


### [333] [How to utilize failure demo data?: Effective data selection for imitation learning using distribution differences in attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07560)
*Kana Miyamoto,Kanata Suzuki,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人模仿学习中失败数据利用难的问题，提出了一种通过学习成功-失败潜在表示差异并结合注意力机制的方法，能够稳定、高效地从人工演示录制中挖掘失败样本的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习大多假设演示数据全是成功的，但实际收集数据时往往包含无法避免的失败尝试。现有利用失败数据的方法通常需要繁琐的预处理或昂贵的在线自主试错，缺乏一种直接且稳定利用这些失败数据的通用机制。

Method: 1. 学习成功与失败轨迹之间的潜在表征差异（latent representations of success-failure discrepancies）；2. 将这些差异整合到Transformer的注意机制（attention mechanism）中；3. 提出一种基于注意力差异的度量标准，用于自动筛选对学习有益的失败样本。

Result: 在仿真任务中，该方法能有效利用失败数据，提升任务成功率。提出的度量标准成功识别出了对模型训练具有正面引导作用的失败样本。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效利用包含失败样本的混合机器人演示数据，通过潜在空间的差异化表示和注意机制的协同，显著提升了任务的成功率和策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotic tasks has relied primarily on policies trained only on successful demonstrations, although failures are unavoidable during human data collection. Many existing approaches for exploiting failure data require additional data processing or iterative policy updates through autonomous rollouts, making it difficult to directly and stably utilize failure data accumulated during data collection. In this work, we propose a method that learns latent representations of success-failure discrepancies and incorporates them into the attention mechanism. During inference, an appropriate latent mode is selected from the initial observation to improve action stability. Furthermore, we introduce a post-training metric that quantifies the attention discrepancy between each failure sample and successful demonstrations to select failure data. Simulation results show that the proposed method improves task success rates when trained with failure data and that the proposed metric identifies failure samples that are beneficial for learning when combined with successful demonstrations. These results suggest that the proposed method can support more efficient use of collected demonstrations in robotic data collection pipelines.

</details>


### [334] [MemCompiler: Compile, Don't Inject -- State-Conditioned Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07594)
*Xin Ding,Xinrui Wang,Yifan Yang,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Qianxi Zhang,Liang Mi,Hanxin Zhu,Kun Li,Yunxin Liu,Zhibo Chen,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MemCompiler 系统，通过感知智能体当前状态动态“编译”相关记忆而非静态注入，显著提升了具身智能体的任务效能与运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“预先单体内存注入（AMMI）”模式存在静态上下文与智能体演化状态不匹配的问题，甚至会导致轻量级执行器性能不如无记忆基线。

Method: 引入 MemCompiler 系统，核心是“状态条件内存编译（State-Conditioned Memory Compilation）”。该模块读取智能体的“简要状态（Brief State）”，动态选取并编译相关记忆，通过文本通道和保持感知信息的潜在“软内存（Soft-Mem）”通道传达给执行器。

Result: 在 AlfWorld、EmbodiedBench 和 ScienceWorld 上表现优异，相比无记忆基线在开源大模型上性能提升高达 129%，并能媲美顶尖闭源系统，同时将每步延迟降低了 60%。

Conclusion: MemCompiler 通过将内存使用重构为“状态条件内存编译”，有效解决了智能体中静态内存注入导致的性能退化和效率低下问题，实现了更高效、更具动态适应性的智能体交互。

Abstract: Existing memory systems for embodied agents typically inject retrieved memory as static context at episode start, a paradigm we term Ahead-of-time Monolithic Memory Injection (AMMI). However, this static design quickly becomes misaligned with the agent's evolving state and may degrade lightweight executors below the no-memory baseline. To address this, we propose MemCompiler, which reframes memory utilization as State-Conditioned Memory Compilation. A learned Memory Compiler reads a structured Brief State capturing the agent's current execution state and dynamically selects and compiles only relevant memory into executable guidance. This guidance is delivered through a text channel and a latent Soft-Mem channel that preserves perceptual information not expressible in text. Across Alf World, EmbodiedBench, and ScienceWorld, MemCompiler consistently improves over no-memory across open-source backbones (up to +129%), matches or approaches frontier closed-source systems, and reduces per-step latency by 60%, demonstrating that state-aware memory compilation improves both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [335] [BrickCraft: Visuomotor Skill Composition with Situated Manual Guidance for Long-Horizon Interlocking Brick Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07605)
*Jichuan Yu,Bowei Li,Zhenran Tang,Guanxing Lu,Chuxiong Hu,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrickCraft 是一个用于积木组装的组合式机器人框架，通过相对定位配方、原始技能分解以及场景手册机制，实现了长序列任务的高效推理、空间对齐与泛化执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人装配互锁积木需要长序列任务推理、精准的空间定位以及精细的操控能力，现有的方法在处理长序列任务的泛化性和复杂结构装配上存在不足。

Method: 1. 采用相对定位配方（Relative Formulation），将每一步组装锚定在结构中的参考积木上；2. 将复杂任务分解为有限的复用原始技能；3. 引入“场景手册”（Situated Manuals），通过将组装意图投影至实时视觉观测，实现高层规划与底层视觉运动技能的对齐。

Result: 实验表明，BrickCraft 仅需少量演示即可习得熟练的装配技能，并对未见过的复杂结构展现出强大的组合式泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrickCraft 通过将复杂的组装任务拆解为可重复使用的原始技能，实现了对长序列、复杂互锁积木结构的高效且具有泛化能力的组装，证明了组合式框架在机器人任务规划与执行中的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic assembly of interlocking bricks demands seamless integration of long-horizon task reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained manipulation. This paper presents BrickCraft, a compositional framework designed for long-horizon and generalizable interlocking brick assembly. BrickCraft models the assembly process using a relative formulation, where each step is anchored to a reference brick within the partial structure, thereby decomposing complex tasks into a finite set of reusable primitive skills. BrickCraft bridges the gap between high-level assembly plans and physical execution through situated manuals, which provide explicit spatial guidance for learned visuomotor skills by projecting the assembly intent onto real-time robot observations. Finally, BrickCraft employs a compositional execution pipeline that chains these spatially grounded skills to accomplish long-horizon assembly tasks. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that BrickCraft acquires proficient assembly skills from a limited set of demonstrations and exhibits strong compositional generalization to unseen structures. The project website is available at https://intelligent-control-lab.github.io/BrickCraft.

</details>


### [336] [Offline-Online Hierarchical 3D Global Relocalization With Synthetic LiDAR Sensing and Descriptor-Space Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07741)
*Jiahua Ren,Kai Shen,Muhua Zhang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层全局重定位框架，通过离线索引构建与在线“粗检索+精配准”策略，在大规模场景下实现了高效、高精度的3D重定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D全局重定位方法在大规模场景下存在搜索空间庞大、计算开销高、在线重定位耗时长等问题，难以满足移动机器人的实际应用需求。

Method: 提出了一种离线-在线分层框架：离线阶段，通过在栅格地图中模拟激光雷达扫描，构建候选位置及其对应的几何描述符索引；在线阶段，首先通过全局检索获取粗略姿态估计，随后利用点云配准输出精确的6-DoF姿态。

Result: 在真实环境实验中，平均重定位耗时仅为3秒，平均定位精度达到8 cm，计算效率较现有方法提升了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的离线-在线分层框架有效解决了大规模空间下3D全局重定位计算开销大、耗时长的难题。该方法在保证重定位精度（8 cm）的前提下，将计算效率提升了一个数量级，具有极高的实用价值。

Abstract: 3D global relocalization is one of the key capabilities for mobile robots in practical applications. However, in large scale spaces, existing methods often suffer from prolonged online relocalization time due to factors such as the massive pose search space and high computational overhead. To address these issues, this paper proposes an offline-online hierarchical framework that decouples the search space. In the offline phase, candidate positions and their corresponding geometric descriptor indices are generated in the map by simulating LiDAR scans within the grid map. In the online phase, a coarse pose estimate is first obtained via global retrieval, followed by point cloud registration to output precise 6-DoF pose estimates. Real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an average relocalization time of 3 s and an average localization accuracy of 8 cm in 3D environments. Compared with existing global relocalization methods, the proposed method achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in computational efficiency while delivering comparable relocalization accuracy.

</details>


### [337] [Sensitivity-Based Robust NMPC for Close-Proximity Offshore Wind Turbine Inspection with a Tilted Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07771)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决海上风机近距离巡检中因模型失配和强风导致的安全违约问题，本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC算法，通过实时动态收紧约束，在不改变原有优化结构的前提下有效保障了飞行安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距离海上风机巡检面临强风、模型失配（质量、惯性、推力系数、阻力等）及严苛的避障要求，传统的名义NMPC在面对这些不确定性时，无法严格保证安全约束的满足。

Method: 提出了一种在线约束收紧（online constraint tightening）策略：利用一阶参数状态灵敏度（First-order parametric state sensitivities）表征结构化不确定性，并结合阶段相关的附加余量（stage-dependent additive margin）处理受限阵风。该方法将灵敏度传播和余量评估集成到名义NMPC框架中，无需改变滚动时域优化的基本结构。

Result: 通过500次蒙特卡洛仿真验证，结果表明该方法在复杂的螺旋巡检轨迹下，完全消除了名义NMPC中出现的碰撞（违反间隙约束）现象，且计算耗时仅有适度增加。

Conclusion: 提出了基于灵敏度的鲁棒非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）方法，通过实时约束收紧实现了复杂环境下的多旋翼近距离安全巡检，有效规避了名义模型下的碰撞风险。

Abstract: Close-proximity offshore wind turbine inspection requires strict clearance control around large cylindrical structures under wind and model mismatch. Nominal Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) may violate safety constraints when mass, inertia, thrust effectiveness, drag, or wind conditions differ from nominal assumptions. We propose a sensitivity-based robust NMPC for a tilted multirotor that robustifies the tower-clearance constraint via online constraint tightening. First-order parametric state sensitivities provide a structured-uncertainty margin, while bounded gusts are handled by a stage-dependent additive margin. The formulation augments the nominal NMPC with sensitivity propagation and margin evaluation only, leaving the receding-horizon optimization structure unchanged. Monte-Carlo evaluation over 500 uncertainty realizations on a boundary-critical helical inspection trajectory shows that the proposed controller eliminates the clearance violations observed under nominal NMPC at the cost of a moderate increase in solve time.

</details>


### [338] [Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07835)
*Ethan Schneider,Jingkai Chen,Tianyi Gu,Kunlei Lian,Seth Hutchinson,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动化仓储中的多对多多智能体拾取递送（MAPD）难题，M2M算法通过优化任务分配逻辑，显著提升了复杂仓储环境下的作业效率与产量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPD研究多集中于一对一任务，无法满足现实仓储中一个SKU对应多个存储/拾取位置的多对多（Many-to-Many）需求。

Method: 提出了名为M2M的新型算法及其变体（M2M和M2M-wSKU），通过优化任务持续时间估计及考虑SKU分布，解决四维指派难题。

Result: 在8小时模拟测试中，M2M算法在不同环境和库存密度下表现卓越，平均多完成22,000项任务。

Conclusion: M2M算法有效解决了多对多MAPD问题，在处理复杂仓库任务流时表现优于现有最优方案，显著提高了任务处理吞吐量。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems in automated warehouses must manage continuous streams of pickup-and-delivery tasks while ensuring efficiency and safety.
